# 仓颉后端完整功能清单

## 本轮校准依据

审计日期 2026-11-16，版本 v2。最终源码基线为远程 origin/main 的 `1e116f574a88bb009b618218df23f679adc7a5fe`，父提交为 `b7c684fa48a0c3df84eaacb9243e7138b05fcafa`。旧审计基线 `8dd7630fe88a566345baa67c91879d8edefb73e6` 与该主线分叉。本轮逐文件对比生产源码并复查改变的启动、部署、依赖和测试入口，校准证据见迁移审计校准报告。下文保留的源码摘录经字节对比后沿用。数据库实存 61 表属于上一轮本地环境的只读快照，远程初始化和升级代码定义 51 表，不能将本地快照视为每个部署的必备结构。

当前 main 仅保留两个 Python 测试文件，仓颉单元测试、前端测试目录和旧 smoke/verify 脚本已删除。下文涉及旧测试路径均为历史证据，应在隔离环境恢复或重建相应用例后再验收。迁移成功项仍为零。

统计口径为 src 下非测试 .cj 编译单元，共 87 个，包含业务、基础设施与启动入口。不能将这一数字等同于独立业务领域数量。业务领域按账号、题目入库、识别、诊断、辅导、学习任务、练习、复习、题库与练习场、知识与记忆、统计、试卷导出、媒体语音、管理系统共 14 组组织。

入口 main.cj 启动 Accounts 路由、识别任务恢复与两个 learningTaskWorker。另有 --question-bank、--create-local-user、--init-db、--upgrade-db、--seed-knowledge 命令，均纳入替代范围。

### algorithmCompute

源位置 `backend-cangjie/src/algorithm_client.cj:9`。直接调用 algorithmCompute, check, close, jsonText, read。

```cangjie
if (secret.size < 32 || !endpoint.startsWith("http://127.0.0.1:")) { throw ApiError(503, "内部算法服务未配置") }
try { UInt16.parse(endpoint[17..]) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(503, "内部算法地址无效") }
if (response.status == 502) { throw ApiError(502, "语音计算暂时不可用") }
if (response.status == 422) { throw ApiError(422, "图片或计算数据无效") }
throw ApiError(503, "内部算法计算暂时不可用")
if (bytes.size + count > 128 * 1024 * 1024) { throw ApiError(503, "算法结果过大") }
if (jsonText(payload, "operation") != jsonText(input, "operation")) { throw ApiError(503, "算法结果不匹配") }
return (payload["result"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(503, "内部算法服务暂时不可用") }
```

```cangjie
if (bytesMatch(bytes,[0x89,0x50,0x4e,0x47,0x0d,0x0a,0x1a,0x0a],0)) { return "png" }
if (bytesMatch(bytes,[0xff,0xd8,0xff],0)) { return "jpg" }
if (bytesMatch(bytes,"RIFF".toArray(),0) && bytesMatch(bytes,"WEBP".toArray(),8)) { return "webp" }
if (bytesMatch(bytes,"GIF87a".toArray(),0) || bytesMatch(bytes,"GIF89a".toArray(),0)) { return "gif" }
if (bytesMatch(bytes,"BM".toArray(),0)) { return "bmp" }
if (bytesMatch(bytes,[0x49,0x49,0x2a,0],0) || bytesMatch(bytes,[0x4d,0x4d,0,0x2a],0)) { return "tif" }
return "bin"
```

```cangjie
tx.query("INSERT INTO knowledge_relations (id,source_id,target_id,relation_type,created_at,updated_at) SELECT ?,s.id,t.id,'prerequisite',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP() FROM knowledge_points s JOIN knowledge_points t ON t.code=? WHERE s.code=? AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM knowledge_relations r WHERE r.source_id=s.id AND r.target_id=t.id AND r.relation_type='prerequisite')",[Some(newId()),sqlValue(relation["target"]),sqlValue(relation["source"])])
```

```cangjie
let now=rowText(tx.query("SELECT UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) AS now").rows[0],"now").replace(" ","T")+"+00:00"

```

```cangjie
let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_practice_attempts WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND mistake_id=? AND dataset_question_id=?",[Some(tutorText(evidence,"practice_attempt_id")),Some(owner),sqlValue(question["id"]),Some(dataset)]).rows
if (rows.isEmpty()) { return false }
let candidates=db.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=?",[Some(dataset)]).rows
return !candidates.isEmpty() && tutorFlag(details,"can_update_learning") && practiceProofValid(db,question,preparation,rowJson("dataset_questions",candidates[0]),trace)
return !db.query("SELECT id FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND question_id=? AND status='completed'",[Some(tutorText(evidence,"tutor_session_id")),Some(owner),sqlValue(question["id"])]).rows.isEmpty()
return false
```

```cangjie
tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_self_checks (id,user_id,question_id,task_id,input_fingerprint,text,version,deleted,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,1,0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(owner),sqlValue(task["question_id"]),Some(id),sqlValue(task["input_fingerprint"]),Some(text)])

return selfCheckPublic(saved,current,canSave)
```

```cangjie
tx.query("UPDATE learning_task_actions SET status='failed',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE task_id=? AND status='pending'",[Some(id)])
return None
taskPreparationFailure(tx,task,"prepare_database_"+error.code.toString()); return None
taskPreparationFailure(tx,task,"prepare_context_invalid"); return None
stopTaskAtLimit(tx,task,"prepare_budget"); return None
tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='running',lease_token=?,lease_until=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)+INTERVAL 90 SECOND,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(lease),Some(id)])

return None
```

```cangjie
let method=context.request.method
if (method!="GET" && method!="PATCH" && method!="DELETE") { throw ApiError(405,"请求方法不支持") }
return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
let rows=tx.query("SELECT id FROM papers WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
if (rows.isEmpty()) { return result }
tx.query("DELETE FROM export_jobs WHERE paper_id=?",[Some(id)]); tx.query("DELETE FROM paper_items WHERE paper_id=?",[Some(id)]); tx.query("DELETE FROM papers WHERE id=?",[Some(id)]); return result
if (let Some(title) <- body.get("title")) { tx.query("UPDATE papers SET title=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(title),Some(id)]) }
if (let Some(filters) <- body.get("filters")) { tx.query("UPDATE papers SET filters=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(filters.toString()),Some(id)]) }
if (body.get("items").isSome()) { tx.query("DELETE FROM paper_items WHERE paper_id=?",[Some(id)]); insertPaperItems(tx,id,rowText(user,"id"),tutorArray(body,"items")) }
return paperDetail(tx,accounts,ownedPaper(tx,id,rowText(user,"id")))
throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
```

```cangjie
let knowledgeRows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE id=? AND subject=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(target),sqlValue(mistake["subject"])]).rows
if (knowledgeRows.isEmpty() || rowText(knowledgeRows[0],"name")!=tutorText(preparation,"target_knowledge_name")) { throw ApiError(409,"练习知识点已变化，请重新获取练习") }
let old=tx.query("SELECT mastery_score FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? AND subject=? AND knowledge_point=? LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(stripWhitespace(jsonText(mistake,"subject"))),Some(point)]).rows
let sources=tx.query("SELECT `key` FROM dataset_sources WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(question["dataset_source_id"])]).rows
tx.query("INSERT INTO dataset_practice_attempts (id,user_id,mistake_id,dataset_question_id,source_dataset,knowledge_id,mode,student_answer,correct,duration_seconds,grading_details,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(practiceAttemptId),Some(owner),Some(mistakeId),Some(id),Some(source),if (knowledgeRows.isEmpty()) { None } else { sqlValue(knowledge["id"]) },Some(mode),Some(answer.toString()),sqlValue(grading["correct"]),Some(duration.toString()),Some(details.toString())])
```

## 逐文件完整实现证据

以下源码用于核对职责、helper 参数、返回结构与动态 SQL，迁移实现以本轮远程 main 校准版本为基线。

### backend-cangjie/src/account_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.net.http.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.encoding.hex.*
import stdx.encoding.url.*

public class Accounts {
    private let secret: String
    public init(secret: String) {
        if (secret.size < 32) { throw IllegalArgumentException("CANGJIE_AUTH_SECRET 至少需要 32 个字符") }
        this.secret = secret
    }

    public func mediaUrl(path: String): String {
        if (!path.startsWith("/uploads/") && !path.startsWith("/reports/") && !path.startsWith("/api/v1/practice/questions/")) { return path }
        let expires = unixNow() + 14400
        let signature = toHexString(hmac256(secret, "media:v1:${expires}:${path}")).toAsciiLower()
        let encoded = StringBuilder()
        for (byte in path.toArray()) {
            if ((byte >= 65 && byte <= 90) || (byte >= 97 && byte <= 122) ||
                (byte >= 48 && byte <= 57) || byte == 45 || byte == 46 || byte == 95 || byte == 126 || byte == 47) {
                encoded.append(String.fromUtf8([byte]))
            } else { encoded.append("%" + toHexString([byte]).toAsciiUpper()) }
        }
        return encoded.toString() + "?expires=${expires}&signature=${signature}"
    }

    public func validMedia(context: HttpContext): Bool {
        try {
            let form = Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? "")
            let expires = Int64.parse(form.get("expires") ?? "")
            let now = unixNow()
            if (expires <= now || expires > now + 14400) { return false }
            let expected = toHexString(hmac256(secret, "media:v1:${expires}:${context.request.url.path}")).toAsciiLower()
            return constantEqual(expected.toArray(), (form.get("signature") ?? "").toArray())
        } catch (_: Exception) { return false }
    }

    public func currentUser(context: HttpContext, db: Database): HashMap<String, Option<String>> {
        let authorization = context.request.headers.getFirst("Authorization") ?? ""
        if (!authorization.startsWith("Bearer ")) { throw ApiError(401, "请先使用账号和密码登录") }
        let token = authorization[7..].trimAscii()
        if (let Some(id) <- readToken(token, secret, None)) {
            let rows = db.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)]).rows
            if (rows.size == 1 && ["student","admin"].contains(rowText(rows[0],"role")) && readToken(token, secret, rows[0]["password_hash"]) == Some(id)) { return rows[0] }
        }
        throw ApiError(401, "请先使用账号和密码登录")
    }

    private func session(row: HashMap<String, Option<String>>): JsonObject {
        let result = userJson(row)
        result.put("access_token", JsonString(createToken(rowText(row, "id"), secret,
            rowText(row, "password_hash"), unixNow() + 2592000)))
        return result
    }

    public func register(context: HttpContext): JsonValue {
        requireMethod(context, "POST")
        let body = readJson(context)
        let email = emailField(body)
        let password = textField(body, "password", 256, trim: false)
        let name = textField(body, "name", 100, fallback: Some("智维同学"))
        let grade = textField(body, "grade", 30, fallback: Some("初二"))
        let hash = hashPassword(password)
        let id = newId()
        try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            try {
                db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
                    tx.query("INSERT INTO users (id,email,name,role,password_hash,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,'student',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                        [Some(id), Some(email), Some(name), Some(hash)])
                    tx.query("INSERT INTO student_profiles (id,user_id,grade,learning_goal,preferences,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,'修复错误思维',JSON_OBJECT(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                        [Some(newId()), Some(id), Some(grade)])
                    ()
                })
            } catch (error: DatabaseException) {
                if (error.code == 1062) { throw ApiError(409, "这个邮箱已经注册，请直接登录") }
                throw error
            }
            return session(db.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)]).rows[0])
        }
        throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
    }

    public func login(context: HttpContext): JsonValue {
        requireMethod(context, "POST")
        let body = readJson(context)
        let email = emailField(body)
        let password = textField(body, "password", 256, trim: false)
        try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            let rows = db.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?", [Some(email)]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty() || !["student","admin"].contains(rowText(rows[0],"role")) || !verifyPassword(password, rowText(rows[0], "password_hash"))) {
                throw ApiError(401, "账号或密码不正确")
            }
            return session(rows[0])
        }
        throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
    }

    public func resetPassword(context: HttpContext): JsonValue {
        requireMethod(context, "POST")
        let body = readJson(context)
        let email = emailField(body)
        let current = textField(body, "current_password", 256, trim: false)
        let replacement = textField(body, "new_password", 256, trim: false)
        let hash = hashPassword(replacement)
        try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
                let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(email)]).rows
                if (rows.isEmpty() || !verifyPassword(current, rowText(rows[0], "password_hash"))) {
                    throw ApiError(401, "账号或当前密码不正确，无法修改密码")
                }
                tx.query("UPDATE users SET password_hash = ?, updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",
                    [Some(hash), rows[0]["id"]])
                ()
            })
        }
        let result = JsonObject()
        result.put("email", JsonString(email))
        result.put("reset", JsonBool(true))
        return result
    }

    public func me(context: HttpContext): JsonValue {
        if (context.request.method != "GET" && context.request.method != "PATCH") {
            context.responseBuilder.header("Allow", "GET, PATCH")
            throw ApiError(405, "请求方法不支持")
        }
        try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            let user = currentUser(context, db)
            if (context.request.method == "PATCH") {
                let body = readJson(context)
                taskStrict(body,["name","email","grade","current_password"])
                let name=if (body.get("name").isSome()) { textField(body,"name",100) } else { rowText(user,"name") }
                let email=if (body.get("email").isSome()) { emailField(body) } else { rowText(user,"email") }
                let grade=if (body.get("grade").isSome()) { textField(body,"grade",30) } else { "" }
                if (!grade.isEmpty() && (rowText(user,"role")!="student" || !["初一","初二","初三","高一","高二","高三"].contains(grade))) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择有效年级") }
                try {
                    return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
                        let current=tx.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE",[user["id"]]).rows[0]
                        if (email!=rowText(current,"email")) {
                            let password=textField(body,"current_password",256,trim:false)
                            if (!verifyPassword(password,rowText(current,"password_hash"))) { throw ApiError(422,"当前密码不正确，邮箱未修改") }
                        }
                        tx.query("UPDATE users SET name=?,email=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(name),Some(email),user["id"]])
                        if (!grade.isEmpty()) {
                            tx.query("UPDATE student_profiles SET grade=?,preferences=JSON_SET(COALESCE(preferences,JSON_OBJECT()),'$.experience_version',COALESCE(JSON_EXTRACT(preferences,'$.experience_version'),0)+1),updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE user_id=?",[Some(grade),user["id"]])
                        }
                        userJson(tx.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=?",[user["id"]]).rows[0])
                    })
                } catch (error: DatabaseException) {
                    if (error.code==1062) { throw ApiError(409,"这个邮箱已被使用，请换一个邮箱") }
                    throw error
                }
            }
            return userJson(user)
        }
        throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
    }

    public func routes(server: Server): Unit {
        server.distributor.register("/api/v1/auth/register", { ctx => guarded(ctx, { => register(ctx) }) })
        server.distributor.register("/api/v1/auth/login", { ctx => guarded(ctx, { => login(ctx) }) })
        server.distributor.register("/api/v1/auth/reset-password", { ctx => guarded(ctx, { => resetPassword(ctx) }) })
        server.distributor.register("/api/v1/auth/me", { ctx => guarded(ctx, { => me(ctx) }) })
    }
}

```

### backend-cangjie/src/adaptive_practice.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

func adaptivePracticeSelection(db: Database, owner: String, subject: String, band: String, source: String, search: String, count: Int64, grade!: String): JsonObject {
    let candidates=JsonArray(); let allowed=HashSet<String>()
    for (row in db.query("SELECT q.*"+bankScopedWhere(grade)+" ORDER BY RAND() LIMIT 200",bankFilter(subject,band,source,search,grade:grade)).rows) {
        let q=rowJson("dataset_questions",row); let item=JsonObject()
        for (key in ["id","subject","grade_band","question_type","knowledge_points","options"]) { item.put(key,q[key]) }
        candidates.add(item); allowed.add(jsonText(q,"id"))
    }
    if (candidates.getItems().size<count) { throw ApiError(422,"当前范围可用题目不足，请减少题数或更换学科") }
    let observations=JsonArray()
    let history=db.query("SELECT i.snapshot,i.grading FROM study_session_items i JOIN study_sessions s ON s.id=i.session_id JOIN dataset_questions q ON q.id=JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(i.snapshot,'$.id')) WHERE s.user_id=? AND s.status='submitted' AND q.usable_for_practice=1 AND q.usable_for_auto_grade=1 AND (?='' OR q.subject=?) ORDER BY s.created_at DESC,i.position DESC LIMIT 400",[Some(owner),Some(subject),Some(subject)]).rows
    for (offset in 0..history.size) {
        let row=history[history.size-1-offset]
        let grade=(JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(row,"grading")) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let correctness=grade.get("correct") ?? JsonNull()
        if (!tutorFlag(grade,"can_update_learning") || !(correctness is JsonBool)) { continue }
        let q=(JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(row,"snapshot")) as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let item=JsonObject()
        for (key in ["id","subject","grade_band","question_type","knowledge_points","options"]) { item.put(key,q[key]) }
        item.put("correct",grade["correct"]); item.put("confirmed",JsonBool(true)); item.put("independent",JsonBool(true)); observations.add(item)
    }
    let payload=JsonObject(); payload.put("candidates",candidates); payload.put("observations",observations); payload.put("count",JsonInt(count))
    let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("adaptive_practice")); input.put("adaptive_practice",payload)
    let result=algorithmCompute(input); let ids=tutorArray(result,"question_ids"); let seen=HashSet<String>()
    if (ids.getItems().size!=count) { throw ApiError(503,"选题未完成，请重试") }
    for (id in ids.getItems()) {
        let value=(id as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        if (!allowed.contains(value) || seen.contains(value)) { throw ApiError(503,"选题结果无效，请重试") }; seen.add(value)
    }
    return result
}

```

### backend-cangjie/src/admin_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func adminMetrics(db: Database, owner: String): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject()
    for ((key,table,extra) in [("questions","questions"," AND status<>'archived'"),("sessions","tutor_sessions",""),("pending_reviews","review_plans"," AND status='pending'")]) {
        result.put(key,JsonInt(queryCount(db,"SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM ${table} WHERE user_id=?${extra}",[Some(owner)])))
    }
    return result
}

func adminStudentData(db: Database, id: String): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=? AND role='student'",[Some(id)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"未找到学生") }
    let user=rowJson("users",rows[0]); let profiles=db.query("SELECT * FROM student_profiles WHERE user_id=?",[Some(id)]).rows
    let profile=if (profiles.isEmpty()) { JsonObject() } else { rowJson("student_profiles",profiles[0]) }
    let result=JsonObject(); let student=adminFields(user,["id","name","created_at"])
    student.put("grade",JsonString(if (profiles.isEmpty()) { "未设置" } else { tutorText(profile,"grade") })); student.put("learning_goal",JsonString(tutorText(profile,"learning_goal")))
    result.put("student",student); result.put("metrics",adminMetrics(db,id)); let mastery=JsonArray(); let misconceptions=JsonArray(); let reviews=JsonArray()
    for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 20",[Some(id)]).rows) {
        let value=rowJson("mastery_states",row); let item=adminFields(value,["subject","knowledge_point","confidence","forgetting_risk","evidence_count"]); item.put("score",value["mastery_score"]); mastery.add(item)
    }
    for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM misconception_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY probability DESC,id DESC LIMIT 20",[Some(id)]).rows) { misconceptions.add(adminFields(rowJson("misconception_states",row),["code","label","probability","status","evidence_count"])) }
    for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY due_at,id LIMIT 20",[Some(id)]).rows) { reviews.add(adminFields(rowJson("review_plans",row),["id","question_id","due_at","status","reason"])) }
    result.put("mastery",mastery); result.put("misconceptions",misconceptions); result.put("reviews",reviews); return result
}

func adminSessionData(db: Database, id: String): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=?",[Some(id)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"未找到教学会话") }
    let session=rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let owner=jsonText(session,"user_id"); let questionId=jsonText(session,"question_id")
    let users=db.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=?",[Some(owner)]).rows; let questions=db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=?",[Some(questionId)]).rows
    let diagnoses=db.query("SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(questionId)]).rows
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("session",adminFields(session,["id","stage","status","turn_count","hint_level","mastery_score","created_at","updated_at"]))
    result.put("student",if (users.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { adminFields(rowJson("users",users[0]),["id","name"]) })
    result.put("question",if (questions.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { adminFields(rowJson("questions",questions[0]),["id","title","subject","question_text","student_answer","knowledge_points"]) })
    result.put("diagnosis",if (diagnoses.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { adminFields(rowJson("diagnoses",diagnoses[0]),["first_error_step","first_error_text","error_type","root_misconception","confidence","recommended_strategy"]) })
    let turns=JsonArray()
    for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_turns WHERE session_id=? ORDER BY created_at,id",[Some(id)]).rows) {
        let value=rowJson("tutor_turns",row); let item=adminFields(value,["id","role","content","stage"]); item.put("time",adminTimestamp(value["created_at"])); item.put("decision",adminSanitize(value.get("metadata_json") ?? JsonObject())); turns.add(item)
    }
    let updates=JsonArray()
    for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM cognitive_updates WHERE user_id=? AND question_id=? ORDER BY created_at,id",[Some(owner),Some(questionId)]).rows) {
        let value=rowJson("cognitive_updates",row); let item=adminFields(value,["id","entity_type","entity_key","old_value","new_value","reason"]); item.put("time",adminTimestamp(value["created_at"])); updates.add(item)
    }
    result.put("turns",turns); result.put("trace",adminTrace(db,id)); result.put("cognitive_updates",updates); return result
}

func adminWorkbenchData(db: Database): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("generated_at",JsonString(rowText(db.query("SELECT DATE_FORMAT(UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),'%Y-%m-%dT%H:%i:%s.%f+00:00') AS generated_at").rows[0],"generated_at")))
    let metrics=JsonObject()
    for ((key,table,condition) in [("students","users","role='student'"),("questions","questions","status<>'archived'"),("diagnoses","diagnoses","1=1"),("sessions","tutor_sessions","1=1"),("active_sessions","tutor_sessions","status='active'"),("agent_runs","agent_runs","1=1"),("cognitive_updates","cognitive_updates","1=1"),("pending_reviews","review_plans","status='pending'")]) {
        metrics.put(key,JsonInt(queryCount(db,"SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM ${table} WHERE ${condition}",[])))
    }
    result.put("metrics",metrics); let students=JsonArray(); let sessions=JsonArray()
    for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE role='student' ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 50").rows) {
        let user=rowJson("users",row); let id=jsonText(user,"id"); let item=adminFields(user,["id","name","updated_at"])
        let profiles=db.query("SELECT grade FROM student_profiles WHERE user_id=?",[Some(id)]).rows
        item.put("grade",JsonString(if (profiles.isEmpty()) { "未设置" } else { rowText(profiles[0],"grade") }))
        let counts=adminMetrics(db,id); item.put("question_count",counts["questions"]); item.put("session_count",counts["sessions"]); students.add(item)
    }
    for (row in db.query("SELECT s.*,u.name AS student_name,q.title AS question_title,q.subject AS question_subject FROM tutor_sessions s JOIN users u ON u.id=s.user_id JOIN questions q ON q.id=s.question_id ORDER BY s.updated_at DESC,s.id DESC LIMIT 50").rows) {
        let item=JsonObject()
        for (key in ["id","stage","status","updated_at"]) { item.put(key,if (key=="updated_at") { JsonString(rowText(row,key).replace(" ","T")) } else { JsonString(rowText(row,key)) }) }
        for ((key,source) in [("student_id","user_id"),("student_name","student_name"),("question_id","question_id"),("question_title","question_title"),("subject","question_subject")]) { item.put(key,JsonString(rowText(row,source))) }
        item.put("turn_count",JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(row,"turn_count"))); item.put("mastery_score",JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(row,"mastery_score"))); sessions.add(item)
    }
    result.put("students",students); result.put("sessions",sessions)
    let state=systemState(); let provider=tutorObject(state,"provider"); let voice=tutorObject(state,"voice"); let providerPublic=adminFields(provider,["logical_model","total_entries","text_entries","vision_entries"]); providerPublic.put("status",provider["last_status"])
    let system=JsonObject(); system.put("database",JsonString("mysql")); system.put("provider",providerPublic); system.put("voice",adminFields(voice,["status","loaded","stt_model","tts_model"])); result.put("system",system); return result
}

func adminApi(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, kind: String): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        if (rowText(user,"role")!="admin") { throw ApiError(403,"请使用管理员账号") }
        let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-1]
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            if (kind=="student") { return adminStudentData(tx,id) }
            if (kind=="session") { return adminSessionData(tx,id) }
            return adminWorkbenchData(tx)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*
import std.unicode.UnicodeStringExtension

func adminSanitize(value: JsonValue): JsonValue {
    if (let Some(object) <- (value as JsonObject)) {
        let result=JsonObject()
        for ((key,item) in object.getFields()) {
            var sensitive=false; let lower=key.toLower()
            for (part in ["credential","password","secret","api_key","apikey","access_token","refresh_token","authorization","chain_of_thought","reasoning_content","raw_prompt","system_prompt"]) { if (lower.contains(part)) { sensitive=true } }
            if (!sensitive) { result.put(key,adminSanitize(item)) }
        }
        return result
    }
    if (let Some(array) <- (value as JsonArray)) { let result=JsonArray(); for (item in array.getItems()) { result.add(adminSanitize(item)) }; return result }
    return value
}

func adminTimestamp(value: JsonValue): JsonValue {
    if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) {
        var raw=text.getValue()
        if (raw.endsWith("+00:00")) { raw=raw[..raw.size-6] }
        if (raw.endsWith(".000000")) { raw=raw[..raw.size-7] }
        return JsonString(raw)
    }
    return value
}

func adminFields(source: JsonObject, keys: Array<String>): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject(); for (key in keys) { result.put(key,if (key=="created_at" || key=="updated_at" || key=="due_at") { adminTimestamp(source.get(key) ?? JsonNull()) } else { source.get(key) ?? JsonNull() }) }; return result
}

func adminLabel(kind: String, key: String): String {
    let labels=(JsonValue.fromStr(##"{"agent":{"mindtrace_tutor":"教学决策智能体","diagnosis":"错因诊断智能体","cognition":"认知建模智能体"},"branch":{"guided_question":"引导式提问","direct_explanation":"直接讲解","worked_example":"例题示范","visualization":"可视化讲解","teach_back":"学生复述","near_transfer":"近迁移检验","far_transfer":"远迁移检验","review_schedule":"安排复习"},"tool":{"knowledge_search":"知识检索","step_alignment":"步骤对齐","visualization":"生成教学图示","rediagnose":"动态再诊断","answer_reveal":"答案与步骤核对","answer_check":"数学结论验证","variant_generator":"生成迁移题","cognition_update":"更新认知状态","review_scheduler":"生成复习计划"}}"##) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    let value=tutorObject(labels,kind).get(key) ?? JsonString(key); return (value as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
}

func adminTrace(db: Database, sessionId: String): JsonArray {
    let trace=JsonArray()
    for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM agent_runs WHERE session_id=? ORDER BY created_at,id",[Some(sessionId)]).rows) {
        let run=rowJson("agent_runs",row); let result=tutorObject(run,"result"); let item=JsonObject(); let branch=tutorText(result,"branch")
        item.put("id",run["id"]); item.put("time",adminTimestamp(run["created_at"])); item.put("agent",JsonString(adminLabel("agent",jsonText(run,"agent_name")))); item.put("status",run["status"])
        item.put("decision",JsonString(if (branch.isEmpty()) { "完成一次决策" } else { adminLabel("branch",branch) }))
        item.put("goal",JsonString(if (tutorText(result,"next_goal").isEmpty()) { tutorText(result,"goal") } else { tutorText(result,"next_goal") }))
        for (key in ["observation","plan","verification"]) { item.put(key,adminSanitize(result.get(key) ?? JsonObject())) }
        let actions=JsonArray()
        for (callRow in db.query("SELECT * FROM agent_tool_calls WHERE agent_run_id=? ORDER BY created_at,id",[row["id"]]).rows) {
            let call=rowJson("agent_tool_calls",callRow); let action=JsonObject(); action.put("id",call["id"]); action.put("tool",JsonString(adminLabel("tool",jsonText(call,"tool_name"))))
            action.put("input",adminSanitize(call.get("input_json") ?? JsonObject())); action.put("output",adminSanitize(call.get("output_json") ?? JsonObject())); actions.add(action)
        }
        item.put("actions",actions); trace.add(item)
    }
    return trace
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/algorithm_client.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.env.*
import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.log.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

public func algorithmCompute(input: JsonObject, timeoutSeconds!: Int64 = 200, taskBudget!: Option<TaskBudget> = None): JsonObject {
    if (let Some(budget) <- taskBudget) { budget.check("algorithm_start") }
    let endpoint = getVariable("CANGJIE_ALGORITHM_URL") ?? "http://127.0.0.1:8012"
    let secret = getVariable("CANGJIE_ALGORITHM_SECRET") ?? ""
    if (secret.size < 32 || !endpoint.startsWith("http://127.0.0.1:")) { throw ApiError(503, "内部算法服务未配置") }
    try { UInt16.parse(endpoint[17..]) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(503, "内部算法地址无效") }
    let client = ClientBuilder().noProxy().readTimeout(Duration.second * timeoutSeconds).build()
    client.logger.level = LogLevel.OFF
    try {
        let request = HttpRequestBuilder().method("POST").url(endpoint + "/v1/compute")
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json").header("Authorization", "Bearer " + secret).body(input.toString()).build()
        let response = client.send(request)
        try {
            if (let Some(budget) <- taskBudget) { budget.check("algorithm_response") }
            if (response.status != 200) {
                if (response.status == 502) { throw ApiError(502, "语音计算暂时不可用") }
                if (response.status == 422) { throw ApiError(422, "图片或计算数据无效") }
                throw ApiError(503, "内部算法计算暂时不可用")
            }
            let buffer = Array<UInt8>(65536, repeat: 0)
            // Decode only after all UTF-8 bytes arrive: chunk boundaries may split a rune.
            let bytes = ArrayList<UInt8>()
            while (true) {
                if (let Some(budget) <- taskBudget) { budget.check("algorithm_read") }
                let count = response.body.read(buffer)
                if (let Some(budget) <- taskBudget) { budget.check("algorithm_read") }
                if (count == 0) { break }
                if (bytes.size + count > 128 * 1024 * 1024) { throw ApiError(503, "算法结果过大") }
                bytes.add(all: buffer[..count])
            }
            let payload = (JsonValue.fromStr(String.fromUtf8(bytes.toArray())) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (jsonText(payload, "operation") != jsonText(input, "operation")) { throw ApiError(503, "算法结果不匹配") }
            return (payload["result"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        } finally { response.close() }
    } catch (error: TaskBudgetExceeded) { throw error }
    catch (error: ApiError) { throw error }
    catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(503, "内部算法服务暂时不可用") }
    finally { client.close() }
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/analysis_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.base64.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func analysisSnapshot(db: Database, id: String, owner: Option<String>): JsonObject {
    let rows = db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(id),owner]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty() || rowText(rows[0],"status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(404,"题目不存在或已归档") }
    let question = rowJson("questions",rows[0])
    if (question["confirmed"].toString() != "true" || stripWhitespace(jsonText(question,"question_text")).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(409,"请先核对并确认题目文字，再开始分析") }
    let result = JsonObject()
    result.put("question",question)
    result.put("work",firstJson(queryArray(db,"student_works","SELECT * FROM student_works WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",id)))
    result.put("steps",queryArray(db,"student_steps","SELECT s.* FROM student_steps s WHERE work_id = (SELECT id FROM student_works WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1) ORDER BY step_index,id",id))
    result.put("assets",queryArray(db,"question_assets","SELECT * FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND kind IN ('crop','question_figure') ORDER BY created_at,id",id))
    result.put("analysis",firstJson(queryArray(db,"problem_analyses","SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id = ?",id)))
    return result
}

func analysisFingerprint(snapshot: JsonObject): JsonArray {
    let result = JsonArray()
    let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    for (key in ["question_text","subject","grade","question_type","student_answer","options","knowledge_points","confirmed","status"]) { result.getItems().add(question[key]) }
    // These values are constructed from the same ordered SQL columns on each read.
    for (key in ["work","steps","assets","analysis"]) { result.getItems().add(snapshot[key]) }
    return result
}

public func analyzeQuestion(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let snapshot = db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx => analysisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"]) })
        let expected = analysisFingerprint(snapshot)
        let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let input = JsonObject()
        input.put("operation",JsonString("analyze")); input.put("text",question["question_text"])
        for (key in ["subject","question_type","student_answer","options"]) { input.put(key,question[key]) }
        input.put("grade",if (question["grade"] is JsonNull) { JsonString("") } else { question["grade"] })
        let steps = JsonArray()
        for (item in (snapshot["steps"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) { steps.getItems().add((item as JsonObject).getOrThrow()["text"]) }
        input.put("student_steps",steps)
        let images = JsonArray(); let seen = HashSet<String>()
        for (item in (snapshot["assets"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) {
            let path = jsonText((item as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"path")
            if (!seen.contains(path)) { images.getItems().add(JsonString(toBase64String(readMedia(path)))); seen.add(path) }
        }
        input.put("images_base64",images)
        let computed = algorithmCompute(input)
        let analysis = (computed["analysis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let references = (analysis["reference_steps"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()
        let valid = (computed["reference_math_valid"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()
        if (valid.size != references.size || stripWhitespace(jsonText(analysis,"final_answer")).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(503,"分析结果不完整") }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let current = analysisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"])
            if (!jsonEquivalent(analysisFingerprint(current), expected)) { throw ApiError(409,"题目或作答已变化，请重新分析") }
            invalidateQuestionLearning(tx,id)
            let analysisId = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO problem_analyses (id,question_id,subject,problem_type,known_conditions,goal,knowledge_points,prerequisites,final_answer,verified,raw_result,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(analysisId),Some(id),Some(jsonText(analysis,"subject")),Some(jsonText(analysis,"problem_type")),Some(analysis["known_conditions"].toString()),Some(jsonText(analysis,"goal")),Some(analysis["knowledge_points"].toString()),Some(analysis["prerequisites"].toString()),Some(jsonText(analysis,"final_answer")),Some(if (analysis["answer_verified"].toString() == "true") { "1" } else { "0" }),Some(analysis.toString())])
            for (index in 0..references.size) {
                let text = (references[index] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                let equation = text.contains("=")
                tx.query("INSERT INTO reference_steps (id,analysis_id,step_index,text,expression,explanation,math_valid,uses_condition,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(newId()),Some(analysisId),Some(index.toString()),Some(text),if (equation) { Some(text) } else { None },Some(if (equation) { "由题目条件进行等价变形" } else { "推理步骤" }),Some(if (valid[index].toString() == "true") { "1" } else { "0" }),Some(if (index == 0) { "1" } else { "0" })])
            }
            syncKnowledgeLinks(tx,question,analysis)
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'analyzed',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)])
            let result = rowJson("problem_analyses",tx.query("SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE id = ?",[Some(analysisId)]).rows[0])
            result.put("reference_steps",analysis["reference_steps"])
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/auth.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.time.*
import stdx.encoding.base64.*
import stdx.encoding.hex.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

foreign {
    func zw_random(output: CPointer<UInt8>, length: Int32): Int32
    func zw_pbkdf2(password: CPointer<UInt8>, length: Int32, salt: CPointer<UInt8>, saltLength: Int32, output: CPointer<UInt8>): Int32
    func zw_hmac(key: CPointer<UInt8>, keyLength: Int32, data: CPointer<UInt8>, dataLength: UInt64, output: CPointer<UInt8>): Int32
    func zw_equal(left: CPointer<UInt8>, right: CPointer<UInt8>, length: UInt64): Int32
}

public func randomBytes(length: Int32): Array<UInt8> {
    let bytes = Array<UInt8>(Int64(length), repeat: 0)
    unsafe {
        let output = acquireArrayRawData(bytes)
        try {
            if (zw_random(output.pointer, length) != 1) { throw Exception("安全随机数生成失败") }
        } finally { releaseArrayRawData(output) }
    }
    return bytes
}

public func newId(): String {
    let bytes = randomBytes(16)
    bytes[6] = (bytes[6] & 0x0f) | 0x40
    bytes[8] = (bytes[8] & 0x3f) | 0x80
    let hex = toHexString(bytes).toAsciiLower()
    return "${hex[..8]}-${hex[8..12]}-${hex[12..16]}-${hex[16..20]}-${hex[20..]}"
}

public func constantEqual(left: Array<UInt8>, right: Array<UInt8>): Bool {
    if (left.size != right.size) { return false }
    unsafe {
        let a = acquireArrayRawData(left)
        let b = acquireArrayRawData(right)
        try { return zw_equal(a.pointer, b.pointer, UInt64(left.size)) == 1 }
        finally { releaseArrayRawData(b); releaseArrayRawData(a) }
    }
}

func hmac256(secret: String, text: String): Array<UInt8> {
    let key = secret.toArray()
    let input = text.toArray()
    let output = Array<UInt8>(32, repeat: 0)
    unsafe {
        let k = acquireArrayRawData(key)
        let p = acquireArrayRawData(input)
        let result = acquireArrayRawData(output)
        try {
            if (zw_hmac(k.pointer, Int32(key.size), p.pointer, UInt64(input.size), result.pointer) != 1) {
                throw Exception("会话签名失败")
            }
        } finally { releaseArrayRawData(result); releaseArrayRawData(p); releaseArrayRawData(k) }
    }
    return output
}

func urlBase64(bytes: Array<UInt8>): String {
    return toBase64String(bytes).replace("+", "-").replace("/", "_")
}

func unpaddedBase64(bytes: Array<UInt8>): String {
    var value = urlBase64(bytes)
    while (value.endsWith("=")) { value = value[..value.size - 1] }
    return value
}

func decodeBase64(value: String): Array<UInt8> {
    var padded = value.replace("-", "+").replace("_", "/")
    while (padded.size % 4 != 0) { padded += "=" }
    return fromBase64String(padded).getOrThrow()
}

public func hashPassword(password: String): String { hashPassword(password, randomBytes(16)) }
public func hashPassword(password: String, salt: Array<UInt8>): String {
    let input = password.toArray()
    let output = Array<UInt8>(32, repeat: 0)
    unsafe {
        let p = acquireArrayRawData(input)
        let s = acquireArrayRawData(salt)
        let result = acquireArrayRawData(output)
        try {
            if (zw_pbkdf2(p.pointer, Int32(input.size), s.pointer, Int32(salt.size), result.pointer) != 1) {
                throw Exception("密码派生失败")
            }
        } finally { releaseArrayRawData(result); releaseArrayRawData(s); releaseArrayRawData(p) }
    }
    return "pbkdf2_sha256\$${urlBase64(salt)}\$${urlBase64(output)}"
}

public func verifyPassword(password: String, encoded: String): Bool {
    try {
        let parts = encoded.split("$")
        if (parts.size != 3 || parts[0] != "pbkdf2_sha256") { return false }
        let salt = decodeBase64(parts[1])
        if (salt.size != 16) { return false }
        let actual = hashPassword(password, salt)
        return constantEqual(actual.toArray(), encoded.toArray())
    } catch (_: Exception) { return false }
}

public func credentialTag(credential: String, secret: String): String {
    return toHexString(hmac256(secret, "credential:v1:${credential}")).toAsciiLower()
}

public func unixNow(): Int64 { DateTime.now().toUnixTimeStamp().toSeconds() }

public func createToken(userId: String, secret: String, credential: String, expiresAt: Int64): String {
    let claims = JsonObject()
    claims.put("sub", JsonString(userId))
    claims.put("exp", JsonInt(expiresAt))
    claims.put("credential", JsonString(credentialTag(credential, secret)))
    let payload = unpaddedBase64(claims.toString().toArray())
    return payload + "." + unpaddedBase64(hmac256(secret, payload))
}

public func readToken(token: String, secret: String, credential: Option<String>): Option<String> {
    try {
        if (token.size > 8192) { return None }
        let parts = token.split(".")
        if (parts.size != 2) { return None }
        let expected = unpaddedBase64(hmac256(secret, parts[0]))
        if (!constantEqual(parts[1].toArray(), expected.toArray())) { return None }
        let claims = (JsonValue.fromStr(String.fromUtf8(decodeBase64(parts[0]))) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let expires = (claims["exp"] as JsonInt).getOrThrow().getValue()
        let userId = (claims["sub"] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        if (expires <= unixNow() || userId.isEmpty() || userId.size > 36) { return None }
        if (let Some(value) <- credential) {
            let tag = (claims["credential"] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
            if (!constantEqual(tag.toArray(), credentialTag(value, secret).toArray())) { return None }
        }
        return Some(userId)
    } catch (_: Exception) { return None }
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/cognition_graph.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

// Only these four node kinds can be built. Their display budgets and wire labels
// travel together, rather than being independently writable JSON strings.
enum CognitionKind {
    Knowledge | Question | Misconception | Strategy
    func label(): String {
        match (this) {
            case Knowledge => "knowledge"
            case Question => "question"
            case Misconception => "misconception"
            case Strategy => "strategy"
        }
    }
    func limit(): Int64 {
        match (this) { case Knowledge => 8; case Question => 4; case _ => 2 }
    }
}

class CognitionNode {
    let id: String
    let kind: CognitionKind
    let value: JsonObject
    init(id: String, name: String, kind: CognitionKind, score: Int64) {
        this.id = id; this.kind = kind; value = JsonObject()
        value.put("id",JsonString(id)); value.put("name",JsonString(name)); value.put("category",JsonString(kind.label())); value.put("value",JsonInt(score))
    }
    func text(key: String, text: String): Unit { value.put(key,JsonString(text)) }
}

class CognitionEdge {
    let source: String
    let target: String
    let relation: String
    init(source: String, target: String, relation: String) { this.source=source; this.target=target; this.relation=relation }
    func json(): JsonObject {
        let value=JsonObject(); value.put("source",JsonString(source)); value.put("target",JsonString(target)); value.put("relation",JsonString(relation)); return value
    }
}

class CognitionGraph {
    let nodes = HashMap<String,CognitionNode>()
    let edges = ArrayList<CognitionEdge>()
    func connect(source: String, target: String, relation: String): Unit {
        if (nodes.contains(source) && nodes.contains(target)) { edges.add(CognitionEdge(source,target,relation)) }
    }
    func visible(): (JsonArray,JsonArray) {
        let visible=HashSet<String>(); let result=JsonArray(); let links=JsonArray()
        for (kind in [CognitionKind.Knowledge,CognitionKind.Question,CognitionKind.Misconception,CognitionKind.Strategy]) {
            for (_ in 0..kind.limit()) {
                var best: Option<CognitionNode> = None
                for ((id,node) in nodes) {
                    if (node.kind.label()!=kind.label() || visible.contains(id)) { continue }
                    if (let Some(previous) <- best) {
                        let score=tutorNumber(node.value,"value"); let old=tutorNumber(previous.value,"value")
                        if (score<old || (score==old && jsonText(node.value,"name")>=jsonText(previous.value,"name"))) { continue }
                    }
                    best=Some(node)
                }
                if (let Some(node) <- best) { visible.add(node.id); result.getItems().add(node.value) } else { break }
            }
        }
        for (edge in edges) {
            if (visible.contains(edge.source) && visible.contains(edge.target)) { links.getItems().add(edge.json()) }
        }
        return (result,links)
    }
}

func cognitionPercent(value: Float64): Int64 {
    let scaled=value*100.0; let lower=Int64(scaled); let fraction=scaled-Float64(lower)
    return if (fraction>0.5 || (fraction==0.5 && lower%2!=0)) { lower+1 } else { lower }
}

func graphTopic(question: JsonObject): String {
    var raw=tutorText(question,"question_type")
    if (raw.isEmpty()) { raw="错题学习" }
    let points=tutorArray(question,"knowledge_points")
    if (!points.getItems().isEmpty()) { raw=stripWhitespace(tutorAnswerText(points.getItems()[0])) }
    match (raw) {
        case "fill_blank" => "填空题"
        case "choice" => "单选题"
        case "multiple_choice" => "多选题"
        case "calculation" => "计算题"
        case "proof" => "证明题"
        case "subjective" => "主观题"
        case "other" => "其他题型"
        case "" => "错题学习"
        case _ => raw
    }
}

func readCognitionGraph(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let form=insightQuery(context); let days=insightDays(form,90,3650); let subject=queryLast(form,"subject")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let allUpdates=tx.query("SELECT * FROM cognitive_updates WHERE user_id=? AND created_at>=TIMESTAMPADD(DAY,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)) AND entity_type<>'note_evidence' ORDER BY created_at,id",[user["id"],Some((-days).toString())]).rows
            let allMappings=tx.query("SELECT * FROM user_question_knowledge_links WHERE user_id=? AND created_at>=TIMESTAMPADD(DAY,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)) ORDER BY created_at,id",[user["id"],Some((-days).toString())]).rows
            let referenced=HashSet<String>()
            for (row in allUpdates) { if (let Some(id) <- row["question_id"]) { if (!id.isEmpty()) { referenced.add(id) } } }
            for (row in allMappings) { referenced.add(rowText(row,"question_id")) }
            let questions=HashMap<String,JsonObject>(); let seen=HashSet<String>()
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE user_id=? AND status<>'archived' ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let id=rowText(row,"id"); if (!referenced.contains(id)) { continue }
                let key=questionDisplayKey(rowText(row,"question_text"),id)
                if (seen.contains(key)) { continue }; seen.add(key)
                if (subject.isEmpty() || rowText(row,"subject")==subject) { questions[id]=rowJson("questions",row) }
            }
            let updates=ArrayList<JsonObject>(); let mappings=ArrayList<JsonObject>(); let mappedQuestions=HashSet<String>()
            for (row in allUpdates) {
                let id=row["question_id"] ?? ""
                if (id.isEmpty() || questions.contains(id)) { updates.add(rowJson("cognitive_updates",row)) }
            }
            for (row in allMappings) {
                let id=rowText(row,"question_id")
                if (subject.isEmpty() || questions.contains(id)) { mappings.add(rowJson("user_question_knowledge_links",row)); mappedQuestions.add(id) }
            }
            let cardsById=HashMap<String,JsonObject>(); let cardsByLabel=HashMap<String,JsonObject>()
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points ORDER BY id").rows) {
                let card=rowJson("knowledge_points",row); let cardSubject=jsonText(card,"subject")
                if (!subject.isEmpty() && cardSubject!=subject) { continue }
                cardsById[jsonText(card,"id")]=card
                cardsByLabel[insightPair(cardSubject,jsonText(card,"name"))]=card
                cardsByLabel[insightPair(cardSubject,jsonText(card,"code"))]=card
            }
            let topics=HashMap<String,String>()
            for (update in updates) {
                let id=tutorText(update,"question_id"); let key=tutorText(update,"entity_key")
                if (!id.isEmpty() && tutorText(update,"entity_type")=="mastery" && !key.isEmpty() && !topics.contains(id)) { topics[id]=key }
            }
            for (mapping in mappings) {
                if (let Some(card) <- cardsById.get(jsonText(mapping,"knowledge_point_id"))) {
                    let id=jsonText(mapping,"question_id")
                    if (jsonText(mapping,"role")=="primary" || !topics.contains(id)) { topics[id]=jsonText(card,"name") }
                }
            }
            let graph=CognitionGraph(); let mistakesByCode=HashMap<String,String>()
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY updated_at,id",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let item=rowJson("mastery_states",row); let rowSubject=jsonText(item,"subject"); let point=jsonText(item,"knowledge_point")
                if (!subject.isEmpty() && rowSubject!=subject) { continue }
                let card=cardsByLabel.get(insightPair(rowSubject,point))
                if (!subject.isEmpty() && card.isNone()) { continue }
                let key=if (let Some(found) <- card) { jsonText(found,"code") } else { point }
                let node=CognitionNode("knowledge:${rowSubject}:${key}",point,CognitionKind.Knowledge,cognitionPercent(editNumber(item["mastery_score"])))
                node.text("subject",rowSubject); node.text("detail",insightLearnerText(tutorText(item,"update_reason"))); node.value.put("confidence",item["confidence"]); graph.nodes[node.id]=node
            }
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM misconception_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY probability DESC,id",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let item=rowJson("misconception_states",row); let rowSubject=jsonText(item,"subject")
                if (!subject.isEmpty() && rowSubject!=subject) { continue }
                let label=canonicalMistakeLabel(jsonText(item,"label")); let id="misconception:${rowSubject}:${label}"
                mistakesByCode[insightPair(rowSubject,jsonText(item,"code"))]=id
                if (graph.nodes.contains(id)) { continue }
                let node=CognitionNode(id,label,CognitionKind.Misconception,cognitionPercent(editNumber(item["probability"])))
                node.text("subject",rowSubject); node.text("status",jsonText(item,"status"))
                node.text("detail","已参考 ${tutorNumber(item,'evidence_count')} 次学习记录；有效方法："+learningMethodLabel(tutorText(item,"successful_strategy")))
                graph.nodes[id]=node
                let strategies=HashSet<String>()
                for (field in ["successful_strategy","failed_strategy"]) {
                    let strategy=tutorText(item,field)
                    if (strategy.isEmpty() || strategies.contains(strategy)) { continue }; strategies.add(strategy)
                    let strategyNode=CognitionNode("strategy:${strategy}",learningMethodLabel(strategy),CognitionKind.Strategy,35)
                    strategyNode.text("detail","学习过程中使用过的方法"); graph.nodes[strategyNode.id]=strategyNode
                    graph.connect(id,strategyNode.id,if (strategy==tutorText(item,"successful_strategy")) { "有效策略" } else { "待改进策略" })
                }
            }
            for ((id,question) in questions) {
                let topic=topics.get(id) ?? graphTopic(question)
                let title=if (topic=="错题学习" || topic=="其他题型") { "相关错题" } else { topic+"相关错题" }
                let node=CognitionNode("question:${id}",title,CognitionKind.Question,28)
                node.text("subject",jsonText(question,"subject")); node.text("detail",academicSummary(jsonText(question,"question_text"),120)); node.text("question_id",id); graph.nodes[node.id]=node
            }
            for (mapping in mappings) {
                let questionNode="question:"+jsonText(mapping,"question_id")
                if (!graph.nodes.contains(questionNode)) { continue }
                if (let Some(card) <- cardsById.get(jsonText(mapping,"knowledge_point_id"))) {
                    let id="knowledge:"+jsonText(card,"subject")+":"+jsonText(card,"code")
                    if (!graph.nodes.contains(id)) {
                        let node=CognitionNode(id,jsonText(card,"name"),CognitionKind.Knowledge,45)
                        node.text("subject",jsonText(card,"subject")); node.text("detail","由你的错题映射到共享知识点；依据："+tutorText(mapping,"evidence_text")); node.value.put("confidence",mapping["confidence"]); graph.nodes[id]=node
                    }
                    graph.connect(questionNode,id,if (jsonText(mapping,"role")=="primary") { "涉及知识点" } else { "需要先修" })
                }
            }
            for (update in updates) {
                let questionId=tutorText(update,"question_id"); let questionNode="question:${questionId}"
                if (let Some(question) <- questions.get(questionId)) {
                    let rowSubject=jsonText(question,"subject"); let key=jsonText(update,"entity_key")
                    if (jsonText(update,"entity_type")=="mastery") {
                        for ((id,node) in graph.nodes) {
                            if (node.kind.label()=="knowledge" && tutorText(node.value,"subject")==rowSubject && jsonText(node.value,"name")==key) { graph.connect(questionNode,id,"提供掌握证据"); break }
                        }
                    } else if (jsonText(update,"entity_type")=="misconception") {
                        if (let Some(target) <- mistakesByCode.get(insightPair(rowSubject,key))) { graph.connect(questionNode,target,"暴露错因") }
                    }
                }
            }
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_relations ORDER BY id").rows) {
                if (let Some(source) <- cardsById.get(rowText(row,"source_id"))) {
                    if (let Some(target) <- cardsById.get(rowText(row,"target_id"))) {
                        graph.connect("knowledge:"+jsonText(source,"subject")+":"+jsonText(source,"code"),"knowledge:"+jsonText(target,"subject")+":"+jsonText(target,"code"),rowText(row,"relation_type"))
                    }
                }
            }
            let (nodes,links)=graph.visible(); let filters=JsonObject()
            filters.put("subject",if (form.getAll("subject").isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(subject) }); filters.put("days",JsonInt(days))
            let categories=JsonArray(); for (kind in [CognitionKind.Knowledge,CognitionKind.Misconception,CognitionKind.Question,CognitionKind.Strategy]) { categories.getItems().add(JsonString(kind.label())) }
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("nodes",nodes); result.put("links",links); result.put("categories",categories); result.put("filters",filters)
            result.put("evidence_count",JsonInt(updates.size)); result.put("personal_mapping_count",JsonInt(mappings.size)); result.put("mapped_question_count",JsonInt(mappedQuestions.size))
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/counterfactual_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

// Older cache entries labelled a reference-text match as a SymPy proof.
// Keep the stored history, but never expose that legacy claim as verified.
func safeCounterfactual(value: JsonValue, diagnosis!: Option<JsonObject> = None): JsonValue {
    if (let Some(result) <- (value as JsonObject)) {
        let method=tutorText(result,"verification_method")
        if ((method=="qwen_proposal_sympy_check" || method=="reference_repair_sympy_check") && tutorText(result,"validation_scope").isEmpty() && !result.get("preserved_prefix").isSome()) {
            result.put("verification_method",JsonString("legacy_reference_alignment"))
            result.put("legacy_review_required",JsonBool(true))
            if (tutorFlag(result,"root_cause_confirmed")) {
                result.put("root_cause_confirmed",JsonBool(false)); result.put("status",JsonString("partial"))
                result.put("confidence",JsonFloat(0.5))
                result.put("conclusion",JsonString("这是历史参考过程的修复建议，尚未经过当前规则核验，请结合原题核对。"))
            }
        }
        if (let Some(current) <- diagnosis) {
            if (tutorFlag(current,"user_confirmed") && tutorText(result,"status")!="not_needed" &&
                (!result.get("suspected_step").isSome() || !jsonEquivalent(result.get("suspected_step") ?? JsonNull(),current.get("first_error_step") ?? JsonNull()))) {
                result.put("root_cause_confirmed",JsonBool(false)); result.put("status",JsonString("partial"))
                result.put("stale_after_correction",JsonBool(true)); result.put("confidence",JsonFloat(0.5))
                result.put("verification_method",JsonString("previous_diagnosis_evidence"))
                result.put("conclusion",JsonString("你已调整出错步骤，原有修复建议需要重新核对。"))
            }
        }
    }
    return value
}

func cachedCounterfactual(db: Database, diagnosisId: String): Option<JsonValue> {
    let rows = db.query("SELECT * FROM diagnosis_evidence WHERE diagnosis_id = ? AND kind = 'counterfactual_validation' ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 1",[Some(diagnosisId)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { return None }
    let diagnoses=db.query("SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE id=?",[Some(diagnosisId)]).rows
    let current: Option<JsonObject> = if (diagnoses.isEmpty()) { None } else { Some(rowJson("diagnoses",diagnoses[0])) }
    return Some(safeCounterfactual(rowJson("diagnosis_evidence",rows[0])["metadata_json"],diagnosis:current))
}

public func counterfactualQuestion(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size - 3]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let initial = db.transaction<(JsonObject,Option<JsonValue>)>({ tx =>
            let snapshot = diagnosisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"])
            if (snapshot["analysis"] is JsonNull || snapshot["diagnosis"] is JsonNull) { throw ApiError(404,"题目诊断不存在") }
            let diagnosis = (snapshot["diagnosis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            return (snapshot,cachedCounterfactual(tx,jsonText(diagnosis,"id")))
        })
        if (let Some(cached) <- initial[1]) { return cached }
        let snapshot = initial[0]
        let input = diagnosisInput(snapshot,includeImages: false)
        input.put("operation",JsonString("counterfactual"))
        let computed = algorithmCompute(input)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let current = diagnosisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"])
            if (!jsonEquivalent(diagnosisFingerprint(current),diagnosisFingerprint(snapshot))) { throw ApiError(409,"题目或诊断已变化，请重新验证") }
            let diagnosis = (current["diagnosis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            let diagnosisId = jsonText(diagnosis,"id")
            if (let Some(cached) <- cachedCounterfactual(tx,diagnosisId)) { return cached }
            saveDiagnosticEvidence(tx,diagnosisId,current,computed,includeAlignment: false)
            syncKnowledgeLinks(tx,(current["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),(current["analysis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),diagnosis: Some(diagnosis))
            return safeCounterfactual(computed["counterfactual"],diagnosis:Some(diagnosis))
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/crop_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.base64.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func readCropBox(data: JsonObject): Array<Float64> {
    try {
        let items = (data["box"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()
        if (items.size != 4) { throw IllegalArgumentException("box") }
        let values = Array<Float64>(4, repeat: 0.0)
        for (index in 0..4) {
            let item = items[index]
            values[index] = Float64.parse(if (let Some(text) <- (item as JsonString)) { text.getValue() } else { item.toString() })
        }
        let x = values[0]; let y = values[1]; let w = values[2]; let h = values[3]
        if (!(x >= 0.0 && x < 1.0 && y >= 0.0 && y < 1.0 && w > 0.0 && w <= 1.0 && h > 0.0 && h <= 1.0 && x + w <= 1.000001 && y + h <= 1.000001)) { throw IllegalArgumentException("box") }
        return values
    } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422, "裁剪范围无效，请重新框选题目") }
}

func cropNumbers(values: Array<Float64>): JsonArray {
    let result = JsonArray()
    for (value in values) { result.getItems().add(JsonFloat(value)) }
    return result
}

public func cropQuestion(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    let files = ArrayList<String>()
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let box = readCropBox(readJson(context))
        let result = db.transaction<(JsonObject, Option<String>)>({ tx =>
            let questions = tx.query("SELECT status FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
            if (questions.isEmpty() || rowText(questions[0], "status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(404, "题目不存在或已归档") }
            let active = tx.query("SELECT id,status FROM processing_jobs WHERE question_id = ? AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') AND status IN ('pending','running') ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
            let old = tx.query("SELECT * FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND kind = 'crop' ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
            if (!active.isEmpty()) {
                if (old.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(409, "已有识别任务正在处理，请先查看结果再调整范围") }
                let asset = rowJson("question_assets", old[0])
                let list = JsonArray(); list.getItems().add(asset)
                let stored = assetCrop(list)
                if (!(stored is JsonArray)) { throw ApiError(409, "已有识别任务正在处理") }
                let values = (stored as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()
                for (index in 0..4) {
                    let difference = Float64.parse(values[index].toString()) - box[index]
                    if (difference > 0.000001 || difference < -0.000001) { throw ApiError(409, "已有识别任务正在处理，请先查看结果再调整范围") }
                }
                asset.put("url", JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/" + rowText(old[0], "path"))))
                asset.put("job_id", JsonString(rowText(active[0], "id")))
                asset.put("status", JsonString(rowText(active[0], "status")))
                asset.put("resumed", JsonBool(true)); asset.put("crop_box", stored)
                return (asset, None)
            }
            let originals = tx.query("SELECT path FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND kind = 'original' ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
            if (originals.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "原图不存在") }
            let input = JsonObject()
            input.put("operation", JsonString("crop")); input.put("box", cropNumbers(box))
            input.put("image_base64", JsonString(toBase64String(readMedia(rowText(originals[0], "path")))))
            let computed = algorithmCompute(input)
            if (jsonText(computed, "kind") != "crop") { throw ApiError(503, "裁剪结果无效") }
            let path = "uploads/${id}/crops/" + newId() + ".jpg"
            writeMedia(path, algorithmImage(computed)); files.add(path)
            let right = if (box[0] + box[2] > 1.0) { 1.0 } else { box[0] + box[2] }
            let bottom = if (box[1] + box[3] > 1.0) { 1.0 } else { box[1] + box[3] }
            let bbox = cropNumbers([box[0], box[1], right, bottom]).toString()
            let assetId = if (old.isEmpty()) { newId() } else { rowText(old[0], "id") }
            if (old.isEmpty()) {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO question_assets (id,question_id,kind,path,sha256,bbox,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'crop',?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(assetId), Some(id), Some(path), Some(jsonText(computed, "sha256")), Some(bbox)])
            } else {
                tx.query("UPDATE question_assets SET path = ?,sha256 = ?,bbox = ?,updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(path), Some(jsonText(computed, "sha256")), Some(bbox), Some(assetId)])
            }
            let jobId = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO processing_jobs (id,question_id,kind,status,progress,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'crop','pending',0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(jobId), Some(id)])
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'processing',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)])
            let asset = rowJson("question_assets", tx.query("SELECT * FROM question_assets WHERE id = ?", [Some(assetId)]).rows[0])
            asset.put("url", JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/" + path)))
            asset.put("geometry", computed["geometry"]); asset.put("original_preserved", JsonBool(true))
            asset.put("job_id", JsonString(jobId)); asset.put("status", JsonString("pending"))
            return (asset, Some(path))
        })
        files.clear()
        if (let Some(path) <- result[1]) {
            let jobId = jsonText(result[0], "job_id")
            spawn { runRecognition(jobId, id, path, requireCrop: true) }
        }
        return result[0]
    } finally { for (path in files) { try { removeMedia(path) } catch (_: Exception) {} } }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/dashboard.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func dashboardNextStep(stage: String): String {
    match (stage) {
        case "understanding" => "理解错误原因"
        case "teach_back" => "用自己的话讲清思路"
        case "teach_back_retry" => "补充说明关键理由"
        case "near_transfer" => "完成一道相似题"
        case "near_remediation" => "再巩固一次"
        case "far_transfer" => "完成综合应用"
        case "transfer_insufficient" => "回顾方法后再练习"
        case _ => "继续完成本次学习"
    }
}

func learningDashboard(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let questions=tx.query("SELECT id,question_text FROM questions WHERE user_id=? AND status<>'archived'",[user["id"]]).rows
            let uniqueQuestions=HashSet<String>()
            for (row in questions) { uniqueQuestions.add(questionDisplayKey(row["question_text"] ?? "",rowText(row,"id"))) }
            let reviewCount=Int64.parse(rowText(tx.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? AND status='pending' AND due_at<TIMESTAMP(DATE(UTC_TIMESTAMP()+INTERVAL 8 HOUR))+INTERVAL 16 HOUR",[user["id"]]).rows[0],"count"))
            let mastery=JsonArray(); let mistakes=JsonArray(); let discoveries=JsonArray()
            let seenPoints=HashSet<String>(); let seenMistakes=HashSet<String>(); var mastered: Int64=0
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 8",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let key=insightPair(rowText(row,"subject"),rowText(row,"knowledge_point"))
                if (seenPoints.contains(key)) { continue }; seenPoints.add(key)
                let item=rowJson("mastery_states",row); mastery.getItems().add(item)
                if (editNumber(item["mastery_score"])>=0.7) { mastered++ }
            }
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM misconception_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY probability DESC,id LIMIT 8",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let label=canonicalMistakeLabel(rowText(row,"label")); let key=insightPair(rowText(row,"subject"),label)
                if (seenMistakes.contains(key)) { continue }; seenMistakes.add(key)
                let item=rowJson("misconception_states",row); item.put("label",JsonString(label)); mistakes.getItems().add(item)
                if (discoveries.getItems().size<2) {
                    let discovery=JsonObject(); discovery.put("kind",JsonString("attention")); discovery.put("title",JsonString(label)); discovery.put("detail",JsonString("这是目前最需要优先巩固的错误原因")); discovery.put("value",JsonInt(cognitionPercent(editNumber(item["probability"])))); discoveries.getItems().add(discovery)
                }
            }
            for (index in 0..mastery.getItems().size) {
                if (index>=2) { break }
                let item=(mastery.getItems()[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let stable=editNumber(item["mastery_score"])>=0.75
                let discovery=JsonObject(); discovery.put("kind",JsonString(if (stable) { "stable" } else { "progress" })); discovery.put("title",item["knowledge_point"]); discovery.put("detail",JsonString(if (stable) { "掌握状态已经比较稳定" } else { "继续完成相似题会更牢固" })); discovery.put("value",JsonInt(cognitionPercent(editNumber(item["mastery_score"])))); discoveries.getItems().add(discovery)
            }
            let sessions=tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE user_id=? AND status='active' ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[user["id"]]).rows
            var continuation: JsonValue=JsonNull()
            if (!sessions.isEmpty()) {
                let session=rowJson("tutor_sessions",sessions[0])
                let active=tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status<>'archived'",[sessions[0]["question_id"],user["id"]]).rows
                if (!active.isEmpty() && !tutorFlag(tutorObject(session,"state"),"question_changed")) {
                    let item=JsonObject(); item.put("session_id",session["id"]); item.put("question_id",session["question_id"]); item.put("stage",session["stage"])
                    item.put("knowledge_point",JsonString(graphTopic(rowJson("questions",active[0])))); item.put("next_step",JsonString(dashboardNextStep(jsonText(session,"stage")))); continuation=item
                }
            }
            var streak: Int64=0; var expected: Int64 = -1
            let days=tx.query("SELECT DISTINCT DATEDIFF(DATE(UTC_TIMESTAMP()+INTERVAL 8 HOUR),DATE(created_at+INTERVAL 8 HOUR)) AS day_offset FROM questions WHERE user_id=? AND status<>'archived' AND created_at IS NOT NULL ORDER BY day_offset",[user["id"]]).rows
            for (row in days) {
                let day=Int64.parse(rowText(row,"day_offset"))
                if (streak==0) { if (day!=0 && day!=1) { break }; expected=day }
                if (day!=expected) { break }; streak++; expected++
            }
            let profiles=tx.query("SELECT grade FROM student_profiles WHERE user_id=? LIMIT 1",[user["id"]]).rows
            let grade: JsonValue=if (profiles.isEmpty()) { JsonString("初二") } else if (let Some(value) <- profiles[0]["grade"]) { JsonString(value) } else { JsonNull() }
            let hasData=!questions.isEmpty() || reviewCount>0 || !mastery.getItems().isEmpty() || !mistakes.getItems().isEmpty() || !(continuation is JsonNull)
            var minutes: Int64=0
            if (hasData) { minutes=reviewCount+if (continuation is JsonNull) { 2 } else { 5 }; if (minutes<3) { minutes=3 } }
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("today_reviews",JsonInt(reviewCount)); result.put("weekly_questions",JsonInt(uniqueQuestions.size)); result.put("mastered_points",JsonInt(mastered)); result.put("streak_days",JsonInt(streak)); result.put("grade",grade); result.put("estimated_minutes",JsonInt(minutes))
            result.put("continue_learning",continuation); result.put("discoveries",discoveries); result.put("mastery",mastery); result.put("misconceptions",mistakes)
            result.put("ai_suggestion",JsonString(if (hasData) { "先弄懂最常出错的一步，再完成一道相似题。" } else { "拍下第一道错题，我会从你的解题步骤开始分析。" }))
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```
