### backend-cangjie/src/database.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.env.*
import std.convert.*
import std.sync.*

foreign {
    func zw_mysql_open(host: CString, port: UInt32, user: CString, password: CString, database: CString): CPointer<UInt8>
    func zw_mysql_close(db: CPointer<UInt8>): Unit
    func zw_mysql_transaction(db: CPointer<UInt8>, operation: Int32): UInt32
    func zw_stmt_prepare(db: CPointer<UInt8>, sql: CString, length: UInt64): CPointer<UInt8>
    func zw_stmt_close(stmt: CPointer<UInt8>): Unit
    func zw_stmt_error(stmt: CPointer<UInt8>): UInt32
    func zw_stmt_param_count(stmt: CPointer<UInt8>): UInt64
    func zw_stmt_bind(stmt: CPointer<UInt8>, index: UInt64, value: CString, length: UInt64, isNull: Int32): Int32
    func zw_stmt_execute(stmt: CPointer<UInt8>): Int32
    func zw_stmt_next(stmt: CPointer<UInt8>): Int32
    func zw_stmt_columns(stmt: CPointer<UInt8>): UInt32
    func zw_stmt_column_name(stmt: CPointer<UInt8>, index: UInt32): CString
    func zw_stmt_value(stmt: CPointer<UInt8>, index: UInt32): CString
    func zw_stmt_length(stmt: CPointer<UInt8>, index: UInt32): UInt64
    func zw_stmt_copy(stmt: CPointer<UInt8>, index: UInt32, output: CPointer<UInt8>, capacity: UInt64): Int32
    func zw_stmt_affected(stmt: CPointer<UInt8>): UInt64
}

class NativeString <: Resource {
    let value: CString
    var closed = false
    init(value: String) { this.value = unsafe { LibC.mallocCString(value) } }
    public func isClosed(): Bool { closed }
    public func close(): Unit {
        if (!closed) { unsafe { LibC.free(value) }; closed = true }
    }
}

public class DatabaseException <: Exception {
    public let code: UInt32
    public init(code: UInt32) {
        super("MySQL 操作失败（${code}）")
        this.code = code
    }
}

public class QueryResult {
    public let rows: ArrayList<HashMap<String, Option<String>>>
    public let affected: UInt64
    init(rows: ArrayList<HashMap<String, Option<String>>>, affected: UInt64) {
        this.rows = rows
        this.affected = affected
    }
}

// A connection is scoped to one request/transaction. No SQL or parameter values
// are included in exceptions. Prepared statements bind every external value.
public class Database <: Resource {
    private var handle: CPointer<UInt8> = CPointer<UInt8>()
    private let mutex = Mutex()
    private var closed = false
    private var inTransaction = false

    public init(host: String, port: UInt32, user: String, password: String, name: String) {
        try (h = NativeString(host), u = NativeString(user), p = NativeString(password), n = NativeString(name)) {
            handle = unsafe { zw_mysql_open(h.value, port, u.value, p.value, n.value) }
        }
        if (handle.isNull()) { throw DatabaseException(2002) }
    }

    public static func fromEnvironment(): Database {
        let name = getVariable("CANGJIE_DB_NAME") ?? "zhiwei_cangjie"
        if (!name.startsWith("zhiwei_cangjie")) {
            throw IllegalArgumentException("仓颉版仅允许使用 zhiwei_cangjie 前缀的独立数据库")
        }
        return Database(getVariable("CANGJIE_DB_HOST") ?? "127.0.0.1",
            UInt32.parse(getVariable("CANGJIE_DB_PORT") ?? "3307"),
            getVariable("CANGJIE_DB_USER") ?? "zhiwei_cangjie",
            getVariable("CANGJIE_DB_PASSWORD") ?? "", name)
    }

    public func isClosed(): Bool { synchronized(mutex) { closed } }
    public func close(): Unit {
        synchronized(mutex) {
            if (!closed) { unsafe { zw_mysql_close(handle) }; closed = true }
        }
    }

    public func query(sql: String): QueryResult { query(sql, []) }

    public func query(sql: String, params: Array<Option<String>>): QueryResult {
        synchronized(mutex) {
            if (closed) { throw DatabaseException(2006) }
            try (text = NativeString(sql)) {
                let stmt = unsafe { zw_stmt_prepare(handle, text.value, UInt64(sql.toArray().size)) }
                try {
                    let error = unsafe { zw_stmt_error(stmt) }
                    if (error != 0) { throw DatabaseException(error) }
                    if (unsafe { zw_stmt_param_count(stmt) } != UInt64(params.size)) {
                        throw IllegalArgumentException("SQL 参数数量不匹配")
                    }
                    for (index in 0..params.size) {
                        let item = params[index] ?? ""
                        try (value = NativeString(item)) {
                            let result = unsafe { zw_stmt_bind(stmt, UInt64(index), value.value,
                                UInt64(item.toArray().size), if (params[index].isNone()) { 1 } else { 0 }) }
                            if (result != 0) { throw DatabaseException(unsafe { zw_stmt_error(stmt) }) }
                        }
                    }
                    if (unsafe { zw_stmt_execute(stmt) } != 0) {
                        throw DatabaseException(unsafe { zw_stmt_error(stmt) })
                    }
                    let rows = ArrayList<HashMap<String, Option<String>>>()
                    let columns = unsafe { zw_stmt_columns(stmt) }
                    while (true) {
                        let result = unsafe { zw_stmt_next(stmt) }
                        if (result == 0) { break }
                        if (result < 0) { throw DatabaseException(unsafe { zw_stmt_error(stmt) }) }
                        let row = HashMap<String, Option<String>>()
                        for (index in 0..columns) {
                            let key = unsafe { zw_stmt_column_name(stmt, index) }.toString()
                            if (unsafe { zw_stmt_value(stmt, index) }.isNull()) {
                                row[key] = None
                            } else {
                                let size = Int64(unsafe { zw_stmt_length(stmt, index) })
                                let bytes = Array<UInt8>(size, repeat: 0)
                                unsafe {
                                    let raw = acquireArrayRawData(bytes)
                                    try {
                                        if (zw_stmt_copy(stmt, index, raw.pointer, UInt64(size)) != 0) {
                                            throw DatabaseException(2032)
                                        }
                                    } finally { releaseArrayRawData(raw) }
                                }
                                row[key] = Some(String.fromUtf8(bytes))
                            }
                        }
                        rows.add(row)
                    }
                    return QueryResult(rows, unsafe { zw_stmt_affected(stmt) })
                } finally {
                    unsafe { zw_stmt_close(stmt) }
                }
            }
            throw DatabaseException(2000)
        }
    }

    private func controlTransaction(operation: Int32): Unit {
        if (closed) { throw DatabaseException(2006) }
        let code = unsafe { zw_mysql_transaction(handle, operation) }
        if (code != 0) { throw DatabaseException(code) }
    }

    public func transaction<T>(work: (Database) -> T): T {
        synchronized(mutex) {
            if (inTransaction) { throw IllegalArgumentException("不支持嵌套事务") }
            controlTransaction(0)
            inTransaction = true
            try {
                let result = work(this)
                controlTransaction(1)
                return result
            } catch (error: Exception) {
                try { controlTransaction(2) } catch (_: Exception) { close() }
                throw error
            } finally {
                inTransaction = false
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/dataset_images.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

func datasetImageMarkers(text: String): Int64 {
    if (text.isEmpty()) { return 0 }
    return text.split("<ImageHere>").size-1
}

func datasetDisplayOptions(question: JsonObject): JsonArray {
    let options=JsonArray()
    for (entry in tutorArray(question,"options").getItems()) {
        let option=if (let Some(object) <- (entry as JsonObject)) {
            (JsonValue.fromStr(object.toString()) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        } else { let object=JsonObject(); object.put("text",JsonString(tutorAnswerText(entry))); object }
        if (option.get("text").isNone()) { option.put("text",option.get("content") ?? option.get("value") ?? JsonString("")) }
        options.add(option)
    }
    return options
}

// CMM-Math stores figures in occurrence order: stem, options, answer, solution.
// Keep the original paths/indices immutable, including in historical snapshots.
func datasetImageLayout(question: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let paths=tutorArray(question,"image_paths"); let count=paths.getItems().size
    let stem=datasetImageMarkers(tutorText(question,"question_text"))
    let optionCounts=ArrayList<Int64>(); var publicCount=stem
    for (entry in datasetDisplayOptions(question).getItems()) {
        let option=(entry as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let amount=datasetImageMarkers(tutorText(option,"text"))
        optionCounts.add(amount); publicCount+=amount
    }
    let answer=datasetImageMarkers(tutorAnswerText(question.get("answer") ?? JsonNull()))
    let solution=datasetImageMarkers(tutorText(question,"solution"))
    let total=publicCount+answer+solution
    let metadata=tutorObject(question,"source_metadata")
    let importedMath=tutorFlag(metadata,"has_visual")
    let review=(total!=count && (total>0 || importedMath))
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("review",JsonBool(review))
    let stemIndices=JsonArray(); let options=JsonArray(); let answerIndices=JsonArray(); let solutionIndices=JsonArray()
    var index: Int64=0
    // Other datasets have ordinary attached question figures and no marker syntax.
    let stemCount=if (total==0 && !importedMath) { count } else { stem }
    for (_ in 0..stemCount) { if (index<count) { stemIndices.add(JsonInt(index)) }; index++ }
    for (amount in optionCounts) {
        let indices=JsonArray()
        for (_ in 0..amount) { if (index<count) { indices.add(JsonInt(index)) }; index++ }
        options.add(indices)
    }
    for (_ in 0..answer) { if (index<count) { answerIndices.add(JsonInt(index)) }; index++ }
    for (_ in 0..solution) { if (index<count) { solutionIndices.add(JsonInt(index)) }; index++ }
    result.put("stem",stemIndices); result.put("options",options)
    result.put("answer",answerIndices); result.put("solution",solutionIndices)
    return result
}

func datasetImageUrls(question: JsonObject, indices: JsonArray, accounts: Accounts): JsonArray {
    let urls=JsonArray()
    for (index in indices.getItems()) {
        urls.add(JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/api/v1/practice/questions/${jsonText(question,"id")}/images/${index.toString()}")))
    }
    return urls
}

func datasetQuestionImages(question: JsonObject, accounts: Accounts): JsonObject {
    let layout=datasetImageLayout(question); let result=JsonObject()
    let images=if (tutorFlag(layout,"review")) { JsonArray() } else { datasetImageUrls(question,tutorArray(layout,"stem"),accounts) }
    let previews=(JsonValue.fromStr(images.toString()) as JsonArray).getOrThrow()
    let options=datasetDisplayOptions(question)
    let optionIndices=tutorArray(layout,"options")
    for (i in 0..options.getItems().size) {
        let option=(options[i] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let urls=if (tutorFlag(layout,"review")) { JsonArray() } else { datasetImageUrls(question,(optionIndices[i] as JsonArray).getOrThrow(),accounts) }
        if (!urls.getItems().isEmpty()) { option.put("image_urls",urls) }
        for (url in urls.getItems()) { previews.add(url) }
    }
    result.put("image_urls",images); result.put("preview_image_urls",previews)
    result.put("options",options); result.put("image_layout_review",layout["review"])
    return result
}

func datasetSolutionImages(question: JsonObject, accounts: Accounts): JsonObject {
    let layout=datasetImageLayout(question); let result=JsonObject()
    result.put("answer_image_urls",datasetImageUrls(question,tutorArray(layout,"answer"),accounts))
    result.put("solution_image_urls",datasetImageUrls(question,tutorArray(layout,"solution"),accounts))
    return result
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/diagnosis_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.base64.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func diagnosisSnapshot(db: Database, id: String, owner: Option<String>): JsonObject {
    let result = analysisSnapshot(db,id,owner)
    result.put("references",queryArray(db,"reference_steps","SELECT r.* FROM reference_steps r JOIN problem_analyses a ON r.analysis_id = a.id WHERE a.question_id = ? ORDER BY r.step_index,r.id",id))
    result.put("diagnosis",firstJson(queryArray(db,"diagnoses","SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",id)))
    let question=tutorObject(result,"question")
    result.put("diagnostic_cards",dialogueKnowledge(db,tutorText(question,"question_text"),tutorText(question,"subject"),tutorText(question,"grade")))
    return result
}

func diagnosisFingerprint(snapshot: JsonObject): JsonArray {
    let value = analysisFingerprint(snapshot)
    value.getItems().add(snapshot["references"]); value.getItems().add(snapshot["diagnosis"])
    // Learning-task snapshots predate retrieval cards. Preserve their original
    // fingerprint representation so an upgrade does not invalidate saved work.
    if (let Some(cards) <- snapshot.get("diagnostic_cards")) { value.getItems().add(cards) }
    return value
}

func diagnosisInput(snapshot: JsonObject, includeImages!: Bool = true): JsonObject {
    let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    let input = JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("diagnose")); input.put("text",question["question_text"])
    input.put("diagnostic_cards",snapshot.get("diagnostic_cards") ?? JsonArray()); input.put("grade",JsonString(tutorText(question,"grade")))
    for (key in ["subject","question_type","student_answer","options"]) { input.put(key,question[key]) }
    for ((source,target) in [("references","reference_steps"),("steps","student_records")]) {
        let values = JsonArray()
        for (item in (snapshot[source] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) {
            let row = (item as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let record = JsonObject()
            record.put("id",row["id"]); record.put("text",row["text"]); values.getItems().add(record)
        }
        input.put(target,values)
    }
    let images = JsonArray(); let seen = HashSet<String>()
    if (includeImages) {
    for (item in (snapshot["assets"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) {
        let path = jsonText((item as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"path")
        if (!seen.contains(path)) { images.getItems().add(JsonString(toBase64String(readMedia(path)))); seen.add(path) }
    }
    }
    input.put("images_base64",images)
    return input
}

func sqlValue(value: JsonValue): Option<String> {
    if (value is JsonNull) { return None }
    if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) { return Some(text.getValue()) }
    if (let Some(flag) <- (value as JsonBool)) { return Some(if (flag.getValue()) { "1" } else { "0" }) }
    return Some(value.toString())
}

func insertDiagnosisEvidence(db: Database, diagnosisId: String, kind: String, content: String, entry: JsonObject, metadata: JsonObject): Unit {
    let values = ArrayList<Option<String>>([Some(newId()),Some(diagnosisId),Some(kind),Some(content)])
    for (key in ["reference_step_id","student_step_id","alignment","equivalent","uses_condition","is_first_error","propagated","math_valid"]) { values.add(sqlValue(entry[key])) }
    values.add(Some(metadata.toString()))
    db.query("INSERT INTO diagnosis_evidence (id,diagnosis_id,kind,content,reference_step_id,student_step_id,alignment,equivalent,uses_condition,is_first_error,propagated,math_valid,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",values.toArray())
}

func saveDiagnosticEvidence(db: Database, diagnosisId: String, snapshot: JsonObject, computed: JsonObject, includeAlignment!: Bool = true): Unit {
    let refs = HashSet<String>(); let steps = HashSet<String>()
    for (item in (snapshot["references"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) { refs.add(jsonText((item as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"id")) }
    for (item in (snapshot["steps"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) { steps.add(jsonText((item as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"id")) }
    let alignment = (computed["alignment"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow()
    let counter = (computed["counterfactual"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    let suspected = counter.get("suspected_step") ?? JsonNull()
    var first: Option<JsonObject> = None
    for (item in alignment.getItems()) {
        let entry = (item as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        for ((key,allowed) in [("reference_step_id",refs),("student_step_id",steps)]) {
            if (let Some(text) <- (entry[key] as JsonString)) { if (!allowed.contains(text.getValue())) { throw ApiError(503,"诊断证据不属于当前作答") } }
        }
        if (includeAlignment) { insertDiagnosisEvidence(db,diagnosisId,"step_alignment",jsonText(entry,"reference_text") + " ⇄ " + jsonText(entry,"student_text"),entry,entry) }
        if (!(suspected is JsonNull) && jsonEquivalent(entry["index"],suspected)) { first = Some(entry) }
    }
    let record = JsonObject()
    record.put("reference_step_id",if (let Some(entry) <- first) { entry["reference_step_id"] } else { JsonNull() })
    record.put("student_step_id",if (let Some(entry) <- first) { entry["student_step_id"] } else { JsonNull() })
    record.put("alignment",counter["status"]); record.put("equivalent",counter["root_cause_confirmed"])
    record.put("uses_condition",JsonBool(!(record["reference_step_id"] is JsonNull)))
    record.put("is_first_error",JsonBool(!(suspected is JsonNull)))
    record.put("propagated",JsonBool(editNumber(counter.get("recovered_steps") ?? JsonInt(0)) > 0.0))
    record.put("math_valid",counter["root_cause_confirmed"])
    insertDiagnosisEvidence(db,diagnosisId,"counterfactual_validation",jsonText(counter,"conclusion"),record,counter)
    let assessment=tutorObject(computed,"evidence_diagnosis")
    if (!tutorText(assessment,"algorithm").isEmpty()) {
        let source=JsonObject()
        for (key in ["reference_step_id","student_step_id"]) { source.put(key,JsonNull()) }
        source.put("alignment",JsonString("evidence_assessment"))
        for (key in ["equivalent","uses_condition","is_first_error","propagated","math_valid"]) { source.put(key,JsonBool(false)) }
        source.put("math_valid",JsonBool(tutorFlag(assessment,"verified")))
        insertDiagnosisEvidence(db,diagnosisId,"eced_assessment",tutorText(assessment,"reason"),source,assessment)
    }
}

public func diagnoseQuestion(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let initial = db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx => diagnosisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"]) })
        if (initial["analysis"] is JsonNull) { analyzeQuestion(context,accounts) }
        let snapshot = db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx => diagnosisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"]) })
        if (snapshot["analysis"] is JsonNull) { throw ApiError(409,"题目分析已变化，请重新诊断") }
        let computed = algorithmCompute(diagnosisInput(snapshot))
        let diagnosis = (computed["diagnosis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let current = diagnosisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"])
            if (!jsonEquivalent(diagnosisFingerprint(snapshot),diagnosisFingerprint(current))) { throw ApiError(409,"题目、作答或分析已变化，请重新诊断") }
            let diagnosisId = newId()
            let values = ArrayList<Option<String>>([Some(diagnosisId),Some(id)])
            for (key in ["first_error_step","first_error_text","error_type","root_misconception","propagated_errors","strategy_candidates","confidence","error_location","missing_prerequisites","recommended_strategy"]) { values.add(sqlValue(diagnosis[key])) }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO diagnoses (id,question_id,first_error_step,first_error_text,error_type,root_misconception,propagated_errors,strategy_candidates,confidence,error_location,missing_prerequisites,recommended_strategy,user_confirmed,correction,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,0,JSON_OBJECT(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",values.toArray())
            saveDiagnosticEvidence(tx,diagnosisId,snapshot,computed)
            syncKnowledgeLinks(tx,(snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),(snapshot["analysis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),diagnosis: Some(diagnosis))
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'diagnosed',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",[Some(id)])
            let result = rowJson("diagnoses",tx.query("SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE id = ?",[Some(diagnosisId)]).rows[0])
            result.put("evidence",computed["alignment"]); result.put("counterfactual",computed["counterfactual"])
            result.put("evidence_diagnosis",computed.get("evidence_diagnosis") ?? JsonNull())
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

public func getDiagnosis(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    let detail = (questionDetail(context,accounts,id) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    return detail["diagnosis"]
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/diagnosis_edit.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func diagnosisEdits(body: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let edits = JsonObject()
    for ((key,value) in body.getFields()) {
        match (key) {
            case "first_error_step" =>
                if (value is JsonNull) { edits.put(key,value) }
                else if (value is JsonInt) {
                    let index = Int64.parse(value.toString())
                    if (index < 0 || index > 2147483647) { throw ApiError(422,"错误步骤序号无效") }
                    edits.put(key,value)
                } else { throw ApiError(422,"错误步骤序号必须为非负整数或空值") }
            case "first_error_text" | "root_misconception" | "error_location" | "recommended_strategy" => edits.put(key,editText(value,12000))
            case "error_type" =>
                let code=(editText(value,60) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                if (!(["exact","equivalent","branch_solution","necessary_condition","missing_step","extra_step","sign_error","calculation_error","invalid_transformation","condition_omission","formula_error","formula_selection","concept_error","reasoning_gap","unknown","unit_error","model_error","direction_error","substance_error","equation_error","balance_error","condition_error","phenomenon_error","evidence_missing","context_error","theme_error","expression_error","language_error","grammar_error","vocabulary_error","reading_evidence_missing","writing_error","structure_function_error","process_error","variable_error","control_error","evidence_error","fact_error","chronology_error","causality_confusion","impact_error","location_error","map_reading_error","factor_error","spatial_relation_error","chart_error","material_mismatch","concept_confusion","rule_error","action_missing","logic_error","analysis_shallow"].contains(code))) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择支持的错误类型") }
                edits.put(key,value)
            case "confidence" =>
                let confidence = editNumber(value)
                if (!(confidence >= 0.0 && confidence <= 1.0)) { throw ApiError(422,"置信度超出范围") }
                edits.put(key,JsonFloat(confidence))
            case "missing_prerequisites" =>
                let values = manualArray(body,key,100)
                for (item in values.getItems()) { editText(item,12000) }
                edits.put(key,values)
            case _ => ()
        }
    }
    return edits
}

public func correctDiagnosis(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let body = readJson(context,maxBytes: 2 * 1024 * 1024)
        let edits = diagnosisEdits(body)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let questions = tx.query("SELECT status FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (questions.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"题目不存在或无权访问") }
            if (rowText(questions[0],"status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已归档，不能修正诊断") }
            let diagnoses = tx.query("SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE",[Some(id)]).rows
            if (diagnoses.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"诊断不存在") }
            if (edits.getFields().isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"请提供需要修正的诊断内容") }
            if (let Some(index) <- edits.get("first_error_step")) {
                if (index is JsonInt) {
                    let steps=tx.query("SELECT s.text FROM student_steps s WHERE s.work_id=(SELECT id FROM student_works WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1) ORDER BY s.step_index",[Some(id)]).rows
                    let number=Int64.parse(index.toString())
                    if (number>=steps.size) { throw ApiError(422,"错误步骤不在当前作答中，请重新核对") }
                    edits.put("first_error_text",JsonString(rowText(steps[number],"text")))
                } else { edits.put("first_error_text",JsonString("")) }
            }
            let current=rowJson("diagnoses",diagnoses[0])
            let effectiveType=(edits.get("error_type") ?? current["error_type"]).toString()
            if (effectiveType==JsonString("exact").toString() || effectiveType==JsonString("equivalent").toString()) {
                if (let Some(index) <- edits.get("first_error_step")) {
                    if (!(index is JsonNull)) { throw ApiError(422,"未发现错误时不能同时指定出错步骤") }
                }
                edits.put("first_error_step",JsonNull()); edits.put("first_error_text",JsonString(""))
            }
            let diagnosisId = rowText(diagnoses[0],"id")
            let sql = StringBuilder(); let params = ArrayList<Option<String>>()
            sql.append("UPDATE diagnoses SET user_confirmed = 1,correction = ?,updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)")
            params.add(Some(body.toString()))
            for ((key,value) in edits.getFields()) { sql.append(", `${key}` = ?"); params.add(sqlValue(value)) }
            sql.append(" WHERE id = ?"); params.add(Some(diagnosisId))
            tx.query(sql.toString(),params.toArray())
            return rowJson("diagnoses",tx.query("SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE id = ?",[Some(diagnosisId)]).rows[0])
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

public func diagnosisRoute(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    if (context.request.method == "GET") { return getDiagnosis(context,accounts) }
    if (context.request.method == "PATCH") { return correctDiagnosis(context,accounts) }
    context.responseBuilder.header("Allow","GET, PATCH")
    throw ApiError(405,"请求方法不支持")
}
```

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.base64.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func validateTutorHandwriting(body: JsonObject, owner: String, sessionId: String, kind!: String = "tutor"): JsonObject {
    let raw = (editText(body.get("handwriting_image_url") ?? JsonString(""),2048,trim: true) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
    let recognition = body.get("handwriting_recognition") ?? JsonNull(); let result = JsonObject()
    if (raw.isEmpty()) {
        if (!(recognition is JsonNull)) { throw ApiError(422,"手写识别结果缺少对应原图") }
        result.put("image_url",JsonString("")); result.put("recognition",JsonNull()); return result
    }
    let path = raw.split("?",2)[0]; let prefix = "/uploads/answers/${owner}/${kind}/${sessionId}/handwriting-"
    if (!path.startsWith(prefix) || !path.endsWith(".jpg")) { throw ApiError(422,"手写作答图片无效") }
    let suffix = path[prefix.size..path.size-4]
    if (suffix.size != 32) { throw ApiError(422,"手写作答图片无效") }
    for (byte in suffix.toArray()) { if (!((byte >= 48 && byte <= 57) || (byte >= 97 && byte <= 102))) { throw ApiError(422,"手写作答图片无效") } }
    try { readMedia(path[1..]) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"手写作答图片无效") }
    if (!(recognition is JsonObject) && !(recognition is JsonNull)) { throw ApiError(422,"手写作答识别结果无效") }
    let input = JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("validate_handwriting")); input.put("recognition",if (recognition is JsonNull) { JsonObject() } else { recognition })
    result.put("image_url",JsonString(path)); result.put("recognition",algorithmCompute(input)); return result
}

public func uploadHandwriting(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let upload = readImageUpload(context)
        let kind = upload.fields.get("context_type") ?? ""; let id = upload.fields.get("context_id") ?? ""; let activityId = upload.fields.get("activity_id") ?? ""
        if (kind != "tutor" && kind != "review" && kind != "practice") { throw ApiError(422,"不支持的答题场景") }
        let visible = db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx =>
            if (kind == "practice") { return practiceHandwritingContext(tx,rowText(user,"id"),upload.fields.get("mistake_id") ?? "",id,upload.fields.get("mode") ?? "reinforce") }
            if (kind == "review") {
                let plans = tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status='pending'",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
                if (plans.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"待复习题不存在") }
                let question = reviewQuestion(tx,rowJson("review_plans",plans[0]),rowText(user,"id"))
                if (!tutorArray(question,"options").getItems().isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"选择题请直接选择选项") }
                let result = JsonObject(); result.put("subject",question["subject"]); result.put("text",question["question_text"]); return result
            }
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"学习会话不存在") }
            let session = rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let snapshot = tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"])
            let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (tutorFlag(tutorObject(session,"state"),"question_changed") || jsonText(question,"status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let value = tutorActivityForSession(tx,session,snapshot)
            if (!(value is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(409,"当前练习已经变化，请刷新后重新上传") }
            let activity = (value as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (tutorText(activity,"id") != activityId) { throw ApiError(409,"当前练习已经变化，请刷新后重新上传") }
            let activityType = tutorText(activity,"type")
            if (activityType == "single_choice" || activityType == "multiple_choice" || activityType == "explanation" || activityType == "teach_back") { throw ApiError(422,"当前练习不需要上传手写过程") }
            let result = JsonObject(); result.put("subject",question["subject"]); result.put("text",activity["question"]); return result
        })
        let normalize = JsonObject(); normalize.put("operation",JsonString("normalize")); normalize.put("image_base64",JsonString(toBase64String(upload.bytes)))
        let image = algorithmImage(algorithmCompute(normalize)); let owner = rowText(user,"id")
        let imageId = newId().replace("-",""); let path = "uploads/answers/${owner}/${kind}/${id}/handwriting-${imageId}.jpg"
        writeMedia(path,image)
        let input = JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("recognize_handwriting")); input.put("image_base64",JsonString(toBase64String(image)))
        input.put("subject",visible["subject"]); input.put("text",visible["text"])
        var computed = JsonObject()
        try { computed = algorithmCompute(input) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(503,"照片已保留，但暂时无法识别，请稍后重试或使用文字输入") }
        let result = JsonObject(); result.put("image_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+path))); result.put("recognition",computed["recognition"]); return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/http_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.net.http.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

public class ApiError <: Exception {
    public let status: UInt16
    public init(status: UInt16, message: String) { super(message); this.status = status }
}

public func jsonResponse(context: HttpContext, value: JsonValue, status!: UInt16 = 200): Unit {
    context.responseBuilder.status(status).header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
        .header("Cache-Control", "no-store").body(value.toString())
}

public func envelope(data: JsonValue): JsonObject {
    let result = JsonObject()
    result.put("success", JsonBool(true))
    result.put("data", data)
    result.put("error", JsonNull())
    result.put("request_id", JsonString(newId()))
    return result
}

public func guarded(context: HttpContext, work: () -> JsonValue, wrap!: Bool = true, successStatus!: UInt16 = 200): Unit {
    try { let result=work(); jsonResponse(context, if (wrap) { envelope(result) } else { result }, status:successStatus) }
    catch (error: ApiError) {
        let result = JsonObject()
        result.put("detail", JsonString(error.message))
        jsonResponse(context, result, status: error.status)
    } catch (_: DatabaseException) {
        let result = JsonObject()
        result.put("detail", JsonString("数据库暂时不可用，请稍后重试"))
        jsonResponse(context, result, status: 503)
    } catch (_: Exception) {
        let result = JsonObject()
        result.put("detail", JsonString("请求未完成，请稍后重试"))
        jsonResponse(context, result, status: 500)
    }
}

public func requireMethod(context: HttpContext, expected: String): Unit {
    if (context.request.method != expected) {
        context.responseBuilder.header("Allow", expected)
        throw ApiError(405, "请求方法不支持")
    }
}

public func readJson(context: HttpContext, maxBytes!: Int64 = 65536, defaultEmpty!: Bool = false): JsonObject {
    let bytes = Array<UInt8>(maxBytes + 1, repeat: 0)
    var length: Int64 = 0
    while (true) {
        let count = context.request.body.read(bytes[length..])
        if (count == 0) { break }
        length += count
        if (length > maxBytes) { throw ApiError(413, "请求内容过大") }
    }
    if (length == 0 && defaultEmpty) { return JsonObject() }
    try {
        return (JsonValue.fromStr(String.fromUtf8(bytes[..length])) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422, "请输入有效的 JSON 对象") }
}

public func textField(data: JsonObject, key: String, limit: Int64, trim!: Bool = true,
    fallback!: Option<String> = None): String {
    var value = ""
    if (let Some(item) <- data.get(key)) {
        if (let Some(text) <- (item as JsonString)) { value = text.getValue() }
        else { throw ApiError(422, "${key} 必须是文字") }
    } else if (let Some(text) <- fallback) { value = text }
    else { throw ApiError(422, "缺少 ${key}") }
    if (trim) { value = stripWhitespace(value) }
    var count: Int64 = 0
    for (_ in value.runes()) { count++ }
    if (count == 0 || count > limit) { throw ApiError(422, "${key} 长度不正确") }
    return value
}

public func emailField(data: JsonObject): String {
    let email = textField(data, "email", 190).toAsciiLower()
    let parts = email.split("@")
    if (parts.size != 2 || parts[0].isEmpty() || parts[1].isEmpty() ||
        !parts[1].contains(".") || parts[1].startsWith(".") || parts[1].endsWith(".")) {
        throw ApiError(422, "请输入正确的邮箱")
    }
    for (space in [" ", "\t", "\r", "\n", "\u{b}", "\u{c}", "\u{85}", "\u{a0}", "\u{1680}",
        "\u{2000}", "\u{2001}", "\u{2002}", "\u{2003}", "\u{2004}", "\u{2005}", "\u{2006}",
        "\u{2007}", "\u{2008}", "\u{2009}", "\u{200a}", "\u{2028}", "\u{2029}", "\u{202f}", "\u{205f}", "\u{3000}"]) {
        if (email.contains(space)) { throw ApiError(422, "请输入正确的邮箱") }
    }
    return email
}

public func rowText(row: HashMap<String, Option<String>>, key: String): String {
    return row[key].getOrThrow()
}

public func userJson(row: HashMap<String, Option<String>>): JsonObject {
    let result = JsonObject()
    for (key in ["id", "email", "name", "role", "created_at", "updated_at"]) {
        var value = rowText(row, key)
        if (key == "created_at" || key == "updated_at") { value = value.replace(" ", "T") + "+00:00" }
        result.put(key, JsonString(value))
    }
    return result
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/ingest_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.base64.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.encoding.url.*
import stdx.net.http.*

// The validated upload is retained independently of the normalized JPEG used
// by existing crop/recognition workflows. Never derive this suffix from a name.
func uploadedImageSuffix(bytes: Array<UInt8>): String {
    if (bytesMatch(bytes,[0x89,0x50,0x4e,0x47,0x0d,0x0a,0x1a,0x0a],0)) { return "png" }
    if (bytesMatch(bytes,[0xff,0xd8,0xff],0)) { return "jpg" }
    if (bytesMatch(bytes,"RIFF".toArray(),0) && bytesMatch(bytes,"WEBP".toArray(),8)) { return "webp" }
    if (bytesMatch(bytes,"GIF87a".toArray(),0) || bytesMatch(bytes,"GIF89a".toArray(),0)) { return "gif" }
    if (bytesMatch(bytes,"BM".toArray(),0)) { return "bmp" }
    if (bytesMatch(bytes,[0x49,0x49,0x2a,0],0) || bytesMatch(bytes,[0x4d,0x4d,0,0x2a],0)) { return "tif" }
    return "bin"
}

public func ingestQuestion(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "POST")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let upload = readImageUpload(context)
        let form = Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? "")
        let subject = queryLast(form, "subject")
        let actualSubject = if (subject.isEmpty()) { "数学" } else { subject }
        if (runeCount(actualSubject) > 30) { throw ApiError(422, "学科名称过长") }
        let input = JsonObject()
        input.put("operation", JsonString("normalize"))
        input.put("image_base64", JsonString(toBase64String(upload.bytes)))
        let normalized = algorithmCompute(input)
        if (jsonText(normalized, "kind") != "original") { throw ApiError(503, "原图结果无效") }
        let bytes = algorithmImage(normalized)
        let id = newId()
        let jobId = newId()
        let path = "uploads/${id}/original.jpg"
        let uploadedPath = "uploads/${id}/uploaded." + uploadedImageSuffix(upload.bytes)
        let uploadedHash = sha256(upload.bytes)
        try {
            writeMedia(path, bytes)
            writeMedia(uploadedPath, upload.bytes)
            db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
                tx.query("INSERT INTO questions (id,user_id,subject,title,question_text,status,confirmed,favorite,tags,formulas,options,question_type,knowledge_points,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,'',?,0,0,JSON_ARRAY(),JSON_ARRAY(),JSON_ARRAY(),'综合题',JSON_ARRAY(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                    [Some(id), user["id"], Some(actualSubject), Some(upload.filename), Some(if (upload.deferred) { "awaiting_crop" } else { "processing" })])
                tx.query("INSERT INTO question_assets (id,question_id,kind,path,sha256,bbox,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'original',?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                    [Some(newId()), Some(id), Some(path), Some(jsonText(normalized, "sha256"))])
                tx.query("INSERT INTO question_assets (id,question_id,kind,path,sha256,bbox,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'uploaded_original',?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                    [Some(newId()), Some(id), Some(uploadedPath), Some(uploadedHash)])
                if (!upload.deferred) {
                    tx.query("INSERT INTO processing_jobs (id,question_id,kind,status,progress,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'ingest','pending',0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(jobId), Some(id)])
                }
                ()
            })
        } catch (error: Exception) {
            try { removeMedia(path) } catch (_: Exception) {}
            try { removeMedia(uploadedPath) } catch (_: Exception) {}
            throw error
        }
        if (!upload.deferred) { spawn { runRecognition(jobId, id, path) } }
        let result = JsonObject()
        result.put("question_id", JsonString(id))
        result.put("job_id", if (upload.deferred) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(jobId) })
        result.put("status", JsonString(if (upload.deferred) { "awaiting_crop" } else { "pending" }))
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/job_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.net.http.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

public func getJob(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "GET")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 1]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let rows = db.query("SELECT j.* FROM processing_jobs j JOIN questions q ON q.id = j.question_id WHERE j.id = ? AND q.user_id = ?", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
        if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "任务不存在或无权访问") }
        return rowJson("processing_jobs", rows[0])
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}

public func retryJob(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let owned = db.query("SELECT j.question_id FROM processing_jobs j JOIN questions q ON q.id = j.question_id WHERE j.id = ? AND q.user_id = ?", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
        if (owned.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "任务不存在或无权访问") }
        let questionId = rowText(owned[0], "question_id")
        let result = db.transaction<(JsonObject, Option<String>, Bool)>({ tx =>
            let questions = tx.query("SELECT status FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(questionId), user["id"]]).rows
            if (questions.isEmpty() || rowText(questions[0], "status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(404, "题目不存在或已归档") }
            let jobs = tx.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE id = ? AND question_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(id), Some(questionId)]).rows
            if (jobs.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "任务不存在") }
            let job = jobs[0]
            if (rowText(job, "status") != "failed") { throw ApiError(409, "任务仍在处理或已完成，请刷新后查看结果") }
            let kind = rowText(job, "kind")
            if (kind != "ingest" && kind != "crop" && kind != "ocr" && kind != "reprocess") { throw ApiError(409, "该任务不支持识别重试") }
            let active = tx.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE question_id = ? AND status IN ('pending','running') AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(questionId)]).rows
            if (!active.isEmpty()) {
                let response = rowJson("processing_jobs", active[0])
                response.put("resumed", JsonBool(true))
                return (response, None, false)
            }
            let assets = tx.query("SELECT path,kind FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND (kind = 'crop' OR (kind = 'original' AND ? = 'ingest')) ORDER BY (kind = 'crop') DESC,created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(questionId), Some(kind)]).rows
            if (assets.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422, "缺少识别图片，请重新框选题目") }
            tx.query("UPDATE processing_jobs SET status = 'pending',progress = 0,error = NULL,updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)])
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'processing',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(questionId)])
            let updated = tx.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)]).rows[0]
            return (rowJson("processing_jobs", updated), Some(rowText(assets[0], "path")), rowText(assets[0], "kind") == "crop")
        })
        if (let Some(path) <- result[1]) {
            let crop = result[2]
            spawn { runRecognition(id, questionId, path, requireCrop: crop) }
        }
        return result[0]
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/knowledge_links.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

func knowledgeLabel(text: String): String {
    let result = StringBuilder()
    for (rune in text.toAsciiLower().runes()) {
        if ((rune >= r'a' && rune <= r'z') || (rune >= r'0' && rune <= r'9') || (rune >= r'\u{4e00}' && rune <= r'\u{9fff}')) { result.append(rune) }
    }
    return result.toString()
}

func syncKnowledgeLinks(db: Database, question: JsonObject, analysis: JsonObject, diagnosis!: Option<JsonObject> = None): Unit {
    let cards = db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE subject = ? ORDER BY id", [Some(jsonText(question,"subject"))]).rows
    let selected = HashMap<String, JsonObject>()
    let sources = ArrayList<(JsonValue,String)>([(question["knowledge_points"], "primary"), (analysis["knowledge_points"], "primary"), (analysis["prerequisites"], "prerequisite")])
    if (let Some(item) <- diagnosis) { sources.add((item["missing_prerequisites"],"prerequisite")) }
    for ((values, role) in sources) {
        for (item in (values as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) {
            let phrase = (item as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
            let needle = knowledgeLabel(phrase)
            if (runeCount(needle) < 2) { continue }
            var bestId = ""
            var bestScore = 0.0
            var bestLength: Int64 = -1
            for (row in cards) {
                let card = rowJson("knowledge_points",row)
                let names = ArrayList<String>([jsonText(card,"name"),jsonText(card,"code")])
                if (let Some(content) <- (card["content"] as JsonObject)) {
                    if (let Some(aliases) <- content.get("related_knowledge")) {
                        if (let Some(array) <- (aliases as JsonArray)) {
                            for (alias in array.getItems()) {
                                names.add(if (let Some(text) <- (alias as JsonString)) { text.getValue() } else { alias.toString() })
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                var score = 0.0
                for (name in names) {
                    let normalized = knowledgeLabel(name)
                    if (normalized == needle) { score = 1.0; break }
                    if (runeCount(normalized) >= 2 && (normalized.contains(needle) || needle.contains(normalized))) { score = 0.86 }
                }
                let length = runeCount(knowledgeLabel(jsonText(card,"name")))
                if (score > 0.0 && (score > bestScore || (score == bestScore && length > bestLength))) {
                    bestId = jsonText(card,"id"); bestScore = score; bestLength = length
                }
            }
            if (bestId.isEmpty()) { continue }
            if (let Some(old) <- selected.get(bestId)) {
                let oldRole = jsonText(old,"role")
                if ((oldRole == "primary" && role == "prerequisite") || (oldRole == role && editNumber(old["confidence"]) >= bestScore)) { continue }
            }
            let link = JsonObject()
            link.put("role",JsonString(role)); link.put("confidence",JsonFloat(bestScore))
            link.put("method",JsonString(if (bestScore == 1.0) { "exact_label" } else { "contained_label" }))
            link.put("evidence",JsonString(shortTitle(phrase,255)))
            selected[bestId] = link
        }
    }
    for ((cardId,link) in selected) {
        db.query("INSERT INTO user_question_knowledge_links (id,user_id,question_id,knowledge_point_id,role,confidence,mapping_method,evidence_text,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP()) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE role=VALUES(role),confidence=VALUES(confidence),mapping_method=VALUES(mapping_method),evidence_text=VALUES(evidence_text),updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP()", [Some(newId()),Some(jsonText(question,"user_id")),Some(jsonText(question,"id")),Some(cardId),Some(jsonText(link,"role")),Some(link["confidence"].toString()),Some(jsonText(link,"method")),Some(jsonText(link,"evidence"))])
    }
}
```

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.fs.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

func seedPublicKnowledge(): Unit {
    let data=(JsonValue.fromStr(String.fromUtf8(File.readFrom("schema/knowledge_seed.json"))) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
            for (value in tutorArray(data,"cards").getItems()) {
                let card=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                tx.query("INSERT INTO knowledge_points (id,code,subject,name,content,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP()) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE subject=VALUES(subject),name=VALUES(name),content=VALUES(content),updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP()",[Some(newId()),sqlValue(card["code"]),sqlValue(card["subject"]),sqlValue(card["name"]),Some(card["content"].toString())])
            }
            for (value in tutorArray(data,"relations").getItems()) {
                let relation=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                tx.query("INSERT INTO knowledge_relations (id,source_id,target_id,relation_type,created_at,updated_at) SELECT ?,s.id,t.id,'prerequisite',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP() FROM knowledge_points s JOIN knowledge_points t ON t.code=? WHERE s.code=? AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM knowledge_relations r WHERE r.source_id=s.id AND r.target_id=t.id AND r.relation_type='prerequisite')",[Some(newId()),sqlValue(relation["target"]),sqlValue(relation["source"])])
            }
            ()
        })
    }
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/learning_evidence.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*

func learningKnowledgePoint(db: Database, question: JsonObject, preferred!: String = "", includeAnalysis!: Bool = true): String {
    var point=stripWhitespace(preferred)
    if (point.isEmpty()) { point=cognitionFirstLabel(question["knowledge_points"]) }
    if (point.isEmpty() && includeAnalysis) {
        let analyses=db.query("SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 1",[Some(jsonText(question,"id"))]).rows
        if (!analyses.isEmpty()) { point=cognitionFirstLabel(rowJson("problem_analyses",analyses[0])["knowledge_points"]) }
    }
    if (point.isEmpty()) { point=stripWhitespace(tutorText(question,"title")) }
    if (point.isEmpty()) { point=stripWhitespace(tutorText(question,"subject"))+"·"+tutorText(question,"question_type") }
    return point
}

// Python computes unchanged arithmetic; Cangjie alone reads and writes business data.
func applyLearningEvidence(db: Database, owner: String, question: JsonObject, evidence: JsonObject, preferred!: String = "", includeAnalysis!: Bool = true, computeTimeoutSeconds!: Int64 = 200): JsonObject {
    let questionId=jsonText(question,"id"); let subject=stripWhitespace(jsonText(question,"subject"))
    let point=learningKnowledgePoint(db,question,preferred: preferred,includeAnalysis: includeAnalysis)
    let diagnoses=db.query("SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(questionId)]).rows
    let diagnosis=if (diagnoses.isEmpty()) { JsonObject() } else { rowJson("diagnoses",diagnoses[0]) }
    let rawCode=stripWhitespace(tutorText(diagnosis,"error_type")); let code=if (rawCode.isEmpty() || ["exact","equivalent","branch_solution"].contains(rawCode)) { "unclassified" } else { rawCode }
    let rawLabel=stripWhitespace(tutorText(diagnosis,"root_misconception")); let label=if (rawLabel.isEmpty()) { code } else { rawLabel }
    let oldMastery=db.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? AND subject=? AND knowledge_point=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(subject),Some(point)]).rows
    let oldMistake=db.query("SELECT * FROM misconception_states WHERE user_id=? AND subject=? AND code=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(subject),Some(code)]).rows
    let beforeMastery: JsonValue=if (oldMastery.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { rowJson("mastery_states",oldMastery[0]) }
    let beforeMistake: JsonValue=if (oldMistake.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { rowJson("misconception_states",oldMistake[0]) }
    let mastery=JsonObject(); let mistake=JsonObject()
    for ((key,fallback) in [("mastery_score",0.0),("evidence_count",0.0),("confidence",0.0),("near_transfer_rate",0.0),("far_transfer_rate",0.0),("forgetting_risk",0.5)]) {
        mastery.put(key,if (beforeMastery is JsonObject) { (beforeMastery as JsonObject).getOrThrow()[key] } else { if (key=="evidence_count") { JsonInt(0) } else { JsonFloat(fallback) } })
    }
    mastery.put("update_reason",JsonString(""))
    for ((key,fallback) in [("probability",0.5),("evidence_count",1.0),("related_question_count",1.0)]) {
        mistake.put(key,if (beforeMistake is JsonObject) { (beforeMistake as JsonObject).getOrThrow()[key] } else { if (key=="probability") { JsonFloat(fallback) } else { JsonInt(1) } })
    }
    for (key in ["label","status","successful_strategy","failed_strategy"]) { mistake.put(key,if (beforeMistake is JsonObject) { (beforeMistake as JsonObject).getOrThrow()[key] } else { JsonString(if (key=="label") { label } else if (key=="status") { "active" } else { "" }) }) }
    let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("cognition_update")); input.put("mastery_values",mastery); input.put("misconception_values",mistake); input.put("learning_evidence",evidence); input.put("misconception_label",JsonString(label))
    let computed=algorithmCompute(input,timeoutSeconds:computeTimeoutSeconds); let nextMastery=tutorObject(computed,"mastery"); let nextMistake=tutorObject(computed,"misconception")
    let masteryId=if (oldMastery.isEmpty()) { newId() } else { rowText(oldMastery[0],"id") }
    let mistakeId=if (oldMistake.isEmpty()) { newId() } else { rowText(oldMistake[0],"id") }
    if (oldMastery.isEmpty()) { db.query("INSERT INTO mastery_states (id,user_id,subject,knowledge_point,mastery_score,confidence,evidence_count,near_transfer_rate,far_transfer_rate,forgetting_risk,update_reason,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,0,0,0,0,0,0.5,'',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(masteryId),Some(owner),Some(subject),Some(point)]) }
    if (oldMistake.isEmpty()) { db.query("INSERT INTO misconception_states (id,user_id,subject,code,label,probability,evidence_count,status,related_question_count,successful_strategy,failed_strategy,first_seen_at,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,0.5,1,'active',1,'','',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(mistakeId),Some(owner),Some(subject),Some(code),Some(label)]) }
    db.query("UPDATE mastery_states SET mastery_score=?,confidence=?,evidence_count=?,near_transfer_rate=?,far_transfer_rate=?,forgetting_risk=?,update_reason=?,last_evidence_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(),updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(nextMastery["mastery_score"]),sqlValue(nextMastery["confidence"]),sqlValue(nextMastery["evidence_count"]),sqlValue(nextMastery["near_transfer_rate"]),sqlValue(nextMastery["far_transfer_rate"]),sqlValue(nextMastery["forgetting_risk"]),sqlValue(nextMastery["update_reason"]),Some(masteryId)])
    db.query("UPDATE misconception_states SET probability=?,evidence_count=?,related_question_count=?,label=?,status=?,successful_strategy=?,failed_strategy=?,last_seen_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(),updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(nextMistake["probability"]),sqlValue(nextMistake["evidence_count"]),sqlValue(nextMistake["related_question_count"]),sqlValue(nextMistake["label"]),sqlValue(nextMistake["status"]),sqlValue(nextMistake["successful_strategy"]),sqlValue(nextMistake["failed_strategy"]),Some(mistakeId)])
    evidence.put("memory_source",memoryEvidenceSource(db,question,evidence,masteryId,mistakeId))
    for ((kind,key,oldValue,newValue) in [("mastery",point,mastery["mastery_score"],nextMastery["mastery_score"]),("misconception",code,mistake["probability"],nextMistake["probability"])]) { db.query("INSERT INTO cognitive_updates (id,user_id,question_id,entity_type,entity_key,old_value,new_value,evidence,reason,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(newId()),Some(owner),Some(questionId),Some(kind),Some(key),sqlValue(oldValue),sqlValue(newValue),Some(evidence.toString()),sqlValue(nextMastery["update_reason"])]) }
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("mastery_before",beforeMastery); result.put("misconception_before",beforeMistake); result.put("mastery_after",rowJson("mastery_states",db.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE id=?",[Some(masteryId)]).rows[0])); result.put("misconception_after",rowJson("misconception_states",db.query("SELECT * FROM misconception_states WHERE id=?",[Some(mistakeId)]).rows[0])); return result
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/learning_insights.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.encoding.url.*
import stdx.net.http.*

// JSON encoding makes compound keys unambiguous even when labels contain separators.
func insightPair(subject: String, label: String): String {
    let pair = JsonArray(); pair.getItems().add(JsonString(subject)); pair.getItems().add(JsonString(label)); return pair.toString()
}

func insightQuery(context: HttpContext): Form {
    try { return Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? "") }
    catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"查询参数格式不正确") }
}

func insightDays(form: Form, fallback: Int64, maximum: Int64): Int64 {
    let raw = queryLast(form,"days")
    if (form.getAll("days").isEmpty()) { return fallback }
    let value = try { Int64.parse(stripWhitespace(raw)) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"days 必须是整数") }
    if (value < 1 || value > maximum) { throw ApiError(422,"days 超出允许范围") }
    return value
}

func canonicalMistakeLabel(value: String): String {
    if (value.contains("移项") && (value.contains("符号") || value.contains("变号"))) { return "移项符号" }
    return value
}

func learningMethodLabel(value: String): String {
    match (value) {
        case "socratic" => "逐步提问"
        case "micro_activity" => "小步练习"
        case "assisted_progression" => "分步辅助练习"
        case "independent_dataset_practice" => "独立完成题库练习"
        case "incremental_hint" => "分层提示"
        case "understanding_check_prompt" => "理解检查"
        case "preserve_correct_part" => "保留正确部分再提示"
        case "visual_counterexample" => "图形演示和反例"
        case "prerequisite_example" => "补充基础例子"
        case "temporary_rediagnosis" => "重新分析题目"
        case "answer_with_explanation" => "结合答案讲解"
        case "targeted_remediation" => "针对错误讲解"
        case "guided" => "逐步引导"
        case "" => "尚未确定"
        case _ => value
    }
}

func insightLearnerText(value: String): String {
    let text = if (value.isEmpty()) { "根据最近的学习情况更新" } else { value }
    return text.replace("教回","讲解思路").replace("近迁移","相似题").replace("远迁移","综合题").replace("提示等级","使用提示")
}

func learningAnalytics(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let days = insightDays(insightQuery(context),14,365)
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let mastery = JsonArray(); let mistakes = JsonArray(); let trend = JsonArray()
            let seenPoints = HashSet<String>()
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let key = insightPair(rowText(row,"subject"),rowText(row,"knowledge_point"))
                if (seenPoints.contains(key)) { continue }; seenPoints.add(key)
                mastery.getItems().add(rowJson("mastery_states",row))
            }
            let seenMistakes = HashSet<String>()
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM misconception_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY probability DESC,id",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let label = canonicalMistakeLabel(rowText(row,"label")); let key = insightPair(rowText(row,"subject"),label)
                if (seenMistakes.contains(key)) { continue }; seenMistakes.add(key)
                let item = rowJson("misconception_states",row); item.put("label",JsonString(label))
                for (field in ["successful_strategy","failed_strategy"]) {
                    let raw = tutorText(item,field)
                    item.put(field,JsonString(if (raw.isEmpty()) { "" } else { learningMethodLabel(raw) }))
                }
                mistakes.getItems().add(item)
            }
            // Keep the original rolling UTC window and group by UTC date, not local midnight.
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT DATE(created_at) AS day,AVG(new_value) AS average,COUNT(*) AS count FROM cognitive_updates WHERE user_id=? AND entity_type='mastery' AND created_at>=TIMESTAMPADD(DAY,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)) GROUP BY DATE(created_at) ORDER BY day",[user["id"],Some((1-days).toString())]).rows
            var evidenceCount: Int64 = 0
            for (row in rows) {
                let count = Int64.parse(rowText(row,"count")); evidenceCount += count
                let average = Float64.parse(rowText(row,"average"))
                let item = JsonObject(); item.put("date",JsonString(rowText(row,"day")))
                item.put("score",JsonFloat(Float64(Int64(average*1000.0+0.5))/10.0)); item.put("evidence_count",JsonInt(count)); trend.getItems().add(item)
            }
            let summary = JsonObject(); summary.put("knowledge_count",JsonInt(mastery.getItems().size)); summary.put("misconception_count",JsonInt(mistakes.getItems().size)); summary.put("evidence_count",JsonInt(evidenceCount))
            let result = JsonObject(); result.put("mastery",mastery); result.put("misconceptions",mistakes); result.put("trend",trend); result.put("summary",summary)
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

func knowledgeCards(context: HttpContext): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let form = insightQuery(context); let subject = queryLast(form,"subject"); let grade = queryLast(form,"grade"); let misconception = queryLast(form,"misconception")
    let words = ArrayList<String>(); let word = StringBuilder()
    // Match Python Unicode split, including Chinese separators.
    for (rune in queryLast(form,"q").runes()) {
        let one = StringBuilder(); one.append(rune); let character = one.toString()
        if (pythonWhitespace.contains(character) || character == "，" || character == "、") {
            if (runeCount(word.toString()) >= 2 && words.size < 5) { words.add(word.toString()) }; word.reset()
        } else { word.append(rune) }
    }
    if (runeCount(word.toString()) >= 2 && words.size < 5) { words.add(word.toString()) }
    let sql = StringBuilder(); sql.append("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE 1=1")
    let params = ArrayList<Option<String>>()
    if (!subject.isEmpty()) { sql.append(" AND subject=?"); params.add(Some(subject)) }
    if (!words.isEmpty()) {
        sql.append(" AND (")
        for (index in 0..words.size) {
            if (index > 0) { sql.append(" OR ") }
            sql.append("(name LIKE ? OR code LIKE ? OR CAST(content AS CHAR) LIKE ?)")
            for (_ in 0..3) { params.add(Some("%"+words[index]+"%")) }
        }
        sql.append(")")
    }
    if (!grade.isEmpty()) { sql.append(" AND LOCATE(?,JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(content,'$.grade'))) > 0"); params.add(Some(grade)) }
    if (!misconception.isEmpty()) { sql.append(" AND LOCATE(?,JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(content,'$.common_misconceptions'))) > 0"); params.add(Some(misconception)) }
    sql.append(" ORDER BY subject,code LIMIT 50")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let result = JsonArray()
        for (row in db.query(sql.toString(),params.toArray()).rows) {
            let item = rowJson("knowledge_points",row); let content = tutorObject(item,"content")
            if (!grade.isEmpty() && !tutorAnswerText(content.get("grade") ?? JsonString("")).contains(grade)) { continue }
            if (!misconception.isEmpty()) {
                let labels = StringBuilder()
                if (let Some(array) <- ((content.get("common_misconceptions") ?? JsonArray()) as JsonArray)) {
                    for (label in array.getItems()) { if (!labels.toString().isEmpty()) { labels.append(" ") }; labels.append(tutorAnswerText(label)) }
                }
                if (!labels.toString().contains(misconception)) { continue }
            }
            result.getItems().add(item)
        }
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

func readKnowledgeCard(context: HttpContext): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let code = parts[parts.size-1]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE code=? OR id=? LIMIT 1",[Some(code),Some(code)]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"知识卡不存在") }
            let result = rowJson("knowledge_points",rows[0]); let id = rows[0]["id"]
            for ((key,sql) in [("prerequisites","SELECT k.* FROM knowledge_relations r JOIN knowledge_points k ON k.id=r.target_id WHERE r.source_id=?"),("next_knowledge","SELECT k.* FROM knowledge_relations r JOIN knowledge_points k ON k.id=r.source_id WHERE r.target_id=?")]) {
                let values = JsonArray()
                for (row in tx.query(sql,[id]).rows) { values.getItems().add(rowJson("knowledge_points",row)) }
                result.put(key,values)
            }
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

### backend-cangjie/src/learning_journey.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.time.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

// These are navigation decisions from persisted facts, not grading or mastery calculations.
enum JourneyAction {
    Crop | Processing | Confirm | Diagnose | Resume | Review | Learn | Practice
    func payload(question: String, target: String): JsonObject {
        let (label,path,reason)=match (this) {
            case Crop => ("继续框选题目","/pages/crop/crop?id="+question,"框选要学习的题目，再开始识别。")
            case Processing => ("查看识别进度","/pages/ocr/ocr?id="+question+"&job="+target,"识别仍在处理中，完成后核对题干和答案。")
            case Confirm => ("核对题目信息","/pages/ocr/ocr?id="+question+if (target.isEmpty()) { "" } else { "&job="+target },"确认题干和自己的作答，避免识别偏差影响诊断。")
            case Diagnose => ("开始错因诊断","/pages/diagnosis/diagnosis?id="+question,"根据确认后的题目与步骤寻找错误原因。")
            case Resume => ("继续上次辅导","/pages/tutor/tutor?id="+target,"从已保存的学习阶段继续。")
            case Review => ("完成到期复习","/pages/review/review?plan="+target,"先独立回忆，再根据判题结果安排下次复习。")
            case Learn => ("查看诊断并学习","/pages/diagnosis/diagnosis?id="+question,"理解首错原因，再练习验证。")
            case Practice => ("练一道相似题","/pages/practice/practice?id="+question,"用独立作答检验理解。")
        }
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("label",JsonString(label)); result.put("url",JsonString(path)); result.put("reason",JsonString(reason)); return result
    }
}

func journeyTimeKey(time: String): String {
    let utc=time.replace("+00:00","").replace("Z","").replace(" ","T")
    if (!utc.contains(".")) { return utc+".000000" }
    var result=utc; while (result.size<26) { result+="0" }; return result
}

func journeyTime(time: String): String {
    let key=journeyTimeKey(time)
    return (if (key.endsWith(".000000")) { key[..19] } else { key })+"+00:00"
}

func journeyTrusted(item: JsonObject): Bool {
    let details=tutorObject(item,"grading_details")
    return tutorFlag(details,"can_update_learning") && !tutorFlag(details,"question_changed")
}

func journeyStep(key: String,label: String,done: Bool,detail: String): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("key",JsonString(key)); result.put("label",JsonString(label)); result.put("completed",JsonBool(done)); result.put("detail",JsonString(detail)); return result
}

func journeyEvent(events: ArrayList<JsonObject>,id: String,time: String,title: String,detail: String): Unit {
    let item=JsonObject(); item.put("id",JsonString(id)); item.put("time",JsonString(journeyTime(time))); item.put("title",JsonString(title)); item.put("detail",JsonString(detail)); events.add(item)
}

func buildLearningJourney(data: JsonObject,now: String): JsonObject {
    let question=tutorObject(data,"question"); let qid=jsonText(question,"id")
    let diagnosis=tutorObject(data,"diagnosis"); let analysis=tutorObject(data,"analysis"); let job=tutorObject(data,"job"); let ocr=tutorObject(data,"ocr")
    var latest=""; var active=""; var activeTime=""; var understanding=false; var independent=false
    let events=ArrayList<JsonObject>()
    journeyEvent(events,"question:"+qid,jsonText(question,"created_at"),"记录错题",jsonText(question,"subject")+" · "+jsonText(question,"question_type"))
    if (!ocr.getFields().isEmpty()) { journeyEvent(events,"ocr:"+jsonText(ocr,"id"),jsonText(ocr,"created_at"),"图片识别","识别完成，请以人工核对后的内容为准。") }
    if (!diagnosis.getFields().isEmpty()) { let root=tutorText(diagnosis,"root_misconception"); journeyEvent(events,"diagnosis:"+jsonText(diagnosis,"id"),jsonText(diagnosis,"created_at"),"错因诊断",if (root.isEmpty()) { "可在诊断页查看具体证据。" } else { root }) }
    for (raw in tutorArray(data,"sessions").getItems()) {
        let session=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let flags=tutorObject(session,"state"); let time=journeyTimeKey(jsonText(session,"created_at"))
        if (!tutorFlag(flags,"question_changed")) {
            if (latest.isEmpty() || time>latest) { latest=time }
            if (jsonText(session,"status")=="active" && (activeTime.isEmpty() || time>activeTime)) { active=jsonText(session,"id"); activeTime=time }
            understanding=understanding || (tutorFlag(flags,"understanding_passed") && tutorFlag(flags,"teach_back_passed"))
            independent=independent || (tutorFlag(flags,"near_transfer") && tutorFlag(flags,"far_transfer") && !tutorFlag(flags,"assisted") && !tutorFlag(flags,"transfer_insufficient"))
        }
        var detail=if (jsonText(session,"status")=="completed") { "本轮已结束，后续仍需独立练习和复习。" } else { "学习进度已保存，可继续。" }
        if (tutorFlag(flags,"question_changed")) { detail="题目信息已修改，本次记录保留为历史，不计入当前进度。" }
        else if (tutorFlag(flags,"assisted") || tutorFlag(flags,"transfer_insufficient")) { detail="本轮使用了辅助或迁移尚不稳定，仍需巩固。" }
        journeyEvent(events,"session:"+jsonText(session,"id"),jsonText(session,"updated_at"),"辅导记录",detail)
    }
    var attempts: Int64=0; var verified: Int64=0; var correct: Int64=0; var waiting: Int64=0
    for (raw in tutorArray(data,"practices").getItems()) {
        let item=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); attempts++; let trusted=journeyTrusted(item); let changed=tutorFlag(tutorObject(item,"grading_details"),"question_changed")
        if (trusted) { verified++; if (tutorFlag(item,"correct")) { correct++ } }
        if (!tutorFlag(tutorObject(item,"grading_details"),"can_update_learning") && !changed) { waiting++ }
        var detail=if (trusted) { if (tutorFlag(item,"correct")) { "独立练习作答正确" } else { "本次作答仍需巩固" } } else { "本次结果待核对，未作为掌握证据" }
        if (changed) { detail="题目信息已修改，此次练习作为历史记录保留。" }
        journeyEvent(events,"practice:"+jsonText(item,"id"),jsonText(item,"created_at"),"题库练习",detail)
    }
    var completed: Int64=0; var reviewCorrect=false
    for (raw in tutorArray(data,"reviews").getItems()) {
        let item=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let trusted=journeyTrusted(item) && jsonText(item,"grade")!="pending"
        if (trusted) { completed++; reviewCorrect=reviewCorrect || tutorFlag(item,"correct") }
        var detail=if (trusted) { if (tutorFlag(item,"correct")) { "复习作答正确" } else { "复习仍需巩固" } } else { "等待完成判定和难度反馈" }
        if (tutorFlag(tutorObject(item,"grading_details"),"question_changed")) { detail="题目信息已修改，此次复习作为历史记录保留。" }
        journeyEvent(events,"review:"+jsonText(item,"id"),jsonText(item,"created_at"),"复习作答",detail)
    }
    // Chinese local-day boundary matches the original student review page.
    let seconds=DateTime.parse(now).toUnixTimeStamp().toSeconds(); let endDay=((seconds+8*3600)/86400+1)*86400-8*3600
    var due: Int64=0; var nextTime=""; var nextId=""
    for (raw in tutorArray(data,"plans").getItems()) {
        let plan=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); if (jsonText(plan,"status")!="pending") { continue }
        let time=jsonText(plan,"due_at"); let key=journeyTimeKey(time)
        if (nextTime.isEmpty() || key<journeyTimeKey(nextTime)) { nextTime=time; nextId=jsonText(plan,"id") }
        if (DateTime.parse(time).toUnixTimeStamp().toSeconds()<endDay) { due++ }
    }
    var action=JourneyAction.Practice; var target=""
    if (jsonText(question,"status")=="awaiting_crop") { action=JourneyAction.Crop }
    else if (tutorText(job,"status")=="pending" || tutorText(job,"status")=="running") { action=JourneyAction.Processing; target=jsonText(job,"id") }
    else if (!tutorFlag(question,"confirmed")) { action=JourneyAction.Confirm; if (tutorText(job,"status")=="failed") { target=jsonText(job,"id") } }
    else if (diagnosis.getFields().isEmpty()) { action=JourneyAction.Diagnose }
    else if (!active.isEmpty()) { action=JourneyAction.Resume; target=active }
    else if (due>0) { action=JourneyAction.Review; target=nextId }
    else if (latest.isEmpty()) { action=JourneyAction.Learn }
    let stages=JsonArray()
    stages.add(journeyStep("confirmed","核对题目",tutorFlag(question,"confirmed"),if (tutorFlag(question,"confirmed")) { "题干与作答已确认" } else { "等待核对识别内容" }))
    stages.add(journeyStep("diagnosed","找到错因",!diagnosis.getFields().isEmpty(),if (diagnosis.getFields().isEmpty()) { "等待完成诊断" } else { "已有诊断证据，可查看和纠正" }))
    stages.add(journeyStep("understanding","理解并讲清",understanding,if (understanding) { "理解检查与教回均通过" } else { "以实际检查与教回结果为准" }))
    stages.add(journeyStep("transfer","独立迁移",independent,if (independent) { "相似题和综合应用均独立通过" } else { "尚无完整的独立迁移证据" }))
    stages.add(journeyStep("review","间隔复习",reviewCorrect,if (completed>0) { "完成 ${completed} 次有效复习" } else { "等待复习验证长期保持" }))
    // At most 203 events, return newest 30; equal-time events retain source order.
    let recent=JsonArray(); let used=HashSet<Int64>()
    for (_ in 0..30) {
        var best: Int64 = -1
        for (index in 0..events.size) { if (!used.contains(index) && (best<0 || journeyTimeKey(jsonText(events[index],"time"))>journeyTimeKey(jsonText(events[best],"time")))) { best=index } }
        if (best<0) { break }; used.add(best); recent.add(events[best])
    }
    let result=JsonObject(); for (key in ["title","question_text","subject"]) { result.put(key,question[key]) }
    result.put("question_id",JsonString(qid)); result.put("generated_at",JsonString(journeyTime(now))); result.put("stages",stages); result.put("next_action",action.payload(qid,target)); result.put("summary",JsonString(if (independent) { "曾有独立迁移证据，继续用复习检验保持。" } else { "按记录逐步巩固，完成辅导不等于已经掌握。" }))
    let verification=JsonObject(); verification.put("label",JsonString(if (tutorFlag(analysis,"verified")) { "已完成规则校验" } else { "尚未通过规则校验" })); verification.put("verified",JsonBool(tutorFlag(analysis,"verified"))); verification.put("diagnosis_confirmed",JsonBool(tutorFlag(diagnosis,"user_confirmed"))); result.put("verification",verification)
    let practice=JsonObject(); practice.put("attempts",JsonInt(attempts)); practice.put("verified_attempts",JsonInt(verified)); practice.put("correct",JsonInt(correct)); practice.put("pending",JsonInt(waiting)); result.put("practice",practice)
    let review=JsonObject(); review.put("completed",JsonInt(completed)); review.put("due",JsonInt(due)); review.put("next_due_at",if (nextTime.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(journeyTime(nextTime)) }); result.put("review",review); result.put("events",recent); result.put("history_limit",JsonInt(50)); return result
}

func learningJourney(context: HttpContext,accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status<>'archived'",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"题目不存在") }
            let data=JsonObject(); data.put("question",rowJson("questions",rows[0]))
            for ((key,table) in [("analysis","problem_analyses"),("diagnosis","diagnoses"),("job","processing_jobs"),("ocr","ocr_runs")]) { data.put(key,firstJson(queryArray(tx,table,"SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",id))) }
            for ((key,table,column) in [("sessions","tutor_sessions","question_id"),("practices","dataset_practice_attempts","mistake_id"),("plans","review_plans","question_id")]) {
                let values=JsonArray(); let filter=if (key=="plans") { " AND status='pending' ORDER BY due_at,id" } else { " ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC" }
                for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE ${column}=? AND user_id=?"+filter+" LIMIT 50",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows) { values.add(rowJson(table,row)) }; data.put(key,values)
            }
            let reviews=JsonArray(); for (row in tx.query("SELECT r.* FROM review_records r JOIN review_plans p ON p.id=r.plan_id WHERE p.question_id=? AND p.user_id=? ORDER BY r.created_at DESC,r.id DESC LIMIT 50",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows) { reviews.add(rowJson("review_records",row)) }; data.put("reviews",reviews)
            let now=rowText(tx.query("SELECT UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) AS now").rows[0],"now").replace(" ","T")+"+00:00"
            return addTaskJourney(tx,buildLearningJourney(data,now),rowText(user,"id"),id)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```
