### backend-cangjie/src/learning_memory.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.time.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

func memoryTime(value: String): Int64 { return DateTime.parse(value).toUnixTimeStamp().toSeconds() }

func memoryEntry(kind: String, row: JsonObject, questionId: String, classification: String, summary: String, days: Int64, sourceVersion: String, sources: JsonArray, independence!: String="not_applicable", outcome!: String="unknown"): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject(); let observed=memoryTime(tutorText(row,"updated_at")); let ref=kind+":"+jsonText(row,"id")
    result.put("ref",JsonString(ref)); result.put("kind",JsonString(kind)); result.put("classification",JsonString(classification)); result.put("question_id",JsonString(questionId)); result.put("source_refs",sources)
    result.put("observed_at",JsonInt(observed)); result.put("expires_at",JsonInt(observed+days*86400)); result.put("summary",JsonString(shortTitle(summary,400)))
    let version=JsonArray(); version.add(row); version.add(JsonString(sourceVersion)); result.put("version",JsonString(sha256(version.toString().toArray())))
    result.put("independence",JsonString(independence)); result.put("outcome",JsonString(outcome)); return result
}

func memoryRefs(values: Array<String>): JsonArray { let result=JsonArray(); for (v in values) { result.add(JsonString(v)) }; return result }

func memoryEstimateCurrent(db: Database, row: JsonObject, kind: String, owner: String, subject: String): Bool {
    let key=if (kind=="mastery") { "knowledge_point" } else { "code" }
    let sourceKey=if (kind=="mastery") { "mastery_id" } else { "misconception_id" }
    let rows=db.query("SELECT u.* FROM cognitive_updates u WHERE u.user_id=? AND u.entity_type=? AND u.entity_key=? AND (JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(u.evidence,'$.memory_source.${sourceKey}'))=? OR JSON_EXTRACT(u.evidence,'$.memory_source') IS NULL) ORDER BY u.created_at DESC,u.id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(kind),sqlValue(row[key]),sqlValue(row["id"])]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { return true } // Unattributed estimates stay explicitly uncertain.
    let update=rowJson("cognitive_updates",rows[0]); let source=tutorObject(tutorObject(update,"evidence"),"memory_source")
    if (source.getFields().isEmpty()) { return true }
    return memoryUpdateValid(db,update,owner,subject)
}

// Rebuilt from source rows per execution; no immortal generated summary cache.
func learningMemory(db: Database, owner: String, question: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let subject=jsonText(question,"subject"); let questionId=jsonText(question,"id"); let entries=JsonArray(); let legacy=JsonObject()
    for (key in ["mastery","misconceptions","updates","reviews"]) { legacy.put(key,JsonArray()) }
    let mastery=tutorArray(legacy,"mastery"); let mistakes=tutorArray(legacy,"misconceptions"); let updates=tutorArray(legacy,"updates"); let plans=tutorArray(legacy,"reviews")
    for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? AND subject=? AND updated_at<=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) AND updated_at>UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)-INTERVAL 30 DAY ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 12",[Some(owner),Some(subject)]).rows) {
        if (mastery.getItems().size>=3) { break }
        let record=rowJson("mastery_states",row)
        if (!memoryEstimateCurrent(db,record,"mastery",owner,subject)) { continue }
        mastery.add(record); let ref="mastery:"+jsonText(record,"id")
        entries.add(memoryEntry("mastery",record,"","estimate",tutorText(record,"knowledge_point")+"：历史掌握估计 "+record["mastery_score"].toString()+"，不是掌握证明",30,"",memoryRefs([ref])))
    }
    for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM misconception_states WHERE user_id=? AND subject=? AND status='active' AND updated_at<=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) AND updated_at>UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)-INTERVAL 30 DAY ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 12",[Some(owner),Some(subject)]).rows) {
        if (mistakes.getItems().size>=3) { break }
        let record=rowJson("misconception_states",row)
        if (!memoryEstimateCurrent(db,record,"misconception",owner,subject)) { continue }
        mistakes.add(record); let ref="misconception:"+jsonText(record,"id")
        entries.add(memoryEntry("misconception",record,"","candidate",tutorText(record,"label")+"：待核对误区候选，不能对学生定性",30,"",memoryRefs([ref])))
    }
    for (row in db.query("SELECT u.* FROM cognitive_updates u JOIN questions q ON q.id=u.question_id WHERE u.user_id=? AND q.subject=? AND u.entity_type='mastery' AND u.updated_at<=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) AND u.updated_at>UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)-INTERVAL 90 DAY ORDER BY u.updated_at DESC,u.id DESC LIMIT 30",[Some(owner),Some(subject)]).rows) {
        if (updates.getItems().size>=4) { break }
        let record=rowJson("cognitive_updates",row)
        if (!memoryUpdateValid(db,record,owner,subject)) { continue }
        let evidence=tutorObject(record,"evidence"); let strategy=tutorText(evidence,"strategy")
        let aemt=tutorObject(evidence,"aemt_event"); let aemtVerdict=tutorText(aemt,"verdict")
        let correct=tutorFlag(evidence,"near_transfer") || tutorFlag(evidence,"far_transfer") || (strategy=="aemt_assistance_aware" && aemtVerdict=="confirmed_correct")
        let outcome=if (correct) { "correct" } else if (strategy=="independent_dataset_practice" || (strategy=="aemt_assistance_aware" && aemtVerdict=="confirmed_incorrect")) { "incorrect" } else { "unknown" }
        let independence=if (strategy=="assisted_progression" || tutorNumber(evidence,"hint_level")>0) { "assisted" } else { "independent" }
        let ref="cognitive:"+jsonText(record,"id"); let sources=memoryRefs([ref,"question:"+tutorText(record,"question_id")])
        for ((key,prefix) in [("learning_task_id","task:"),("practice_attempt_id","practice:"),("tutor_session_id","tutor:")]) { let id=tutorText(evidence,key); if (!id.isEmpty()) { sources.add(JsonString(prefix+id)) } }
        let scope=if (strategy=="aemt_assistance_aware" && tutorText(aemt,"scope")=="original_question") { "本次原题作答" } else { "本次迁移作答" }
        let summary=tutorText(record,"entity_key")+"：历史学习事件，"+if (outcome=="correct") { if (strategy=="aemt_assistance_aware") { scope+"正确" } else { "本次迁移判定正确" } } else if (outcome=="incorrect") { if (strategy=="aemt_assistance_aware") { scope+"有误" } else { "本次迁移作答有误" } } else { "结果尚不充分" }
        entries.add(memoryEntry("cognitive",record,tutorText(record,"question_id"),"verified_event",summary+"；不代表整个知识点掌握",90,tutorText(tutorObject(evidence,"memory_source"),"question_version"),sources,independence:independence,outcome:outcome)); updates.add(record)
    }
    var reviewed: Int64=0
    for (row in db.query("SELECT r.* FROM review_records r JOIN review_plans p ON p.id=r.plan_id JOIN questions q ON q.id=p.question_id WHERE p.user_id=? AND q.user_id=? AND q.subject=? AND q.status<>'archived' AND q.confirmed=1 AND r.grade<>'pending' AND r.updated_at<=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) AND r.updated_at>UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)-INTERVAL 90 DAY ORDER BY r.updated_at DESC,r.id DESC LIMIT 20",[Some(owner),Some(owner),Some(subject)]).rows) {
        if (reviewed>=3) { break }
        let record=rowJson("review_records",row); let details=tutorObject(record,"grading_details")
        if (!tutorFlag(details,"can_update_learning") || !["confirmed_correct","confirmed_incorrect"].contains(tutorText(details,"verdict"))) { continue }
        let planRows=db.query("SELECT question_id FROM review_plans WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[sqlValue(record["plan_id"]),Some(owner)]).rows
        if (planRows.isEmpty()) { continue }
        let id=rowText(planRows[0],"question_id"); let sourceQuestion=memoryQuestion(db,id,subject); let version=memoryQuestionVersion(db,sourceQuestion)
        if (tutorText(details,"memory_question_version")!=version) { continue }
        let outcome=if (tutorFlag(record,"correct")) { "correct" } else { "incorrect" }
        entries.add(memoryEntry("review_record",record,id,"verified_event","本次复习系统判定"+if (outcome=="correct") { "正确；难度自评不等于掌握" } else { "有误；不以学生的难度自评改为正确" },90,version,memoryRefs(["review_record:"+jsonText(record,"id"),"review:"+tutorText(record,"plan_id")]),independence:"unknown",outcome:outcome)); reviewed++
    }
    var notes: Int64=0
    for (row in db.query("SELECT n.* FROM learning_self_checks n JOIN learning_tasks t ON t.id=n.task_id JOIN questions q ON q.id=n.question_id WHERE n.user_id=? AND q.user_id=? AND q.subject=? AND q.status<>'archived' AND q.confirmed=1 AND n.deleted=0 AND t.status='completed' AND n.updated_at<=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) AND n.updated_at>UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)-INTERVAL 90 DAY ORDER BY n.updated_at DESC,n.id DESC LIMIT 20",[Some(owner),Some(owner),Some(subject)]).rows) {
        if (notes>=3) { break }
        let record=rowJson("learning_self_checks",row); let id=tutorText(record,"question_id")
        let version=taskFingerprint(taskSnapshot(db,id,owner,lock:false))
        if (version!=tutorText(record,"input_fingerprint")) { continue }
        entries.add(memoryEntry("self_check",record,id,"student_self_report","学生确认的自述（未证明掌握）："+tutorText(record,"text"),90,version,memoryRefs(["self_check:"+jsonText(record,"id"),"task:"+tutorText(record,"task_id")]))); notes++
    }
    for (row in db.query("SELECT p.* FROM review_plans p JOIN questions q ON q.id=p.question_id WHERE p.user_id=? AND q.user_id=? AND q.id=? AND q.subject=? AND q.status<>'archived' AND q.confirmed=1 AND p.status='pending' AND p.updated_at<=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) AND p.updated_at>UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)-INTERVAL 90 DAY ORDER BY p.due_at,p.id LIMIT 2",[Some(owner),Some(owner),Some(questionId),Some(subject)]).rows) {
        let record=rowJson("review_plans",row); let version=memoryQuestionVersion(db,question); plans.add(record)
        entries.add(memoryEntry("review",record,questionId,"reminder","当前题有待复习安排，到期时间："+tutorText(record,"due_at")+"；安排不证明学会",90,version,memoryRefs(["review:"+jsonText(record,"id"),"question:"+questionId])))
    }
    let snapshot=JsonObject(); snapshot.put("rule_version",JsonString("learning-memory-v1")); snapshot.put("entries",entries)
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("snapshot",snapshot); result.put("legacy_rows",legacy); return result
}

func applyTaskMemoryContext(context: JsonObject, memory: JsonObject): Unit {
    let snapshot=tutorObject(memory,"snapshot"); let refs=JsonArray(); let versions=JsonObject(); let summary=StringBuilder()
    for (value in tutorArray(snapshot,"entries").getItems()) {
        let entry=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); refs.add(entry["ref"]); versions.put(jsonText(entry,"ref"),entry["version"])
        let text=tutorText(entry,"summary")+"；"
        if (runeCount(summary.toString())+runeCount(text)<=1800) { summary.append(text) }
    }
    context.put("memory_snapshot",snapshot); context.put("memory_refs",refs); context.put("memory_versions",versions); context.put("memory_summary",JsonString(shortTitle(summary.toString(),1800)))
}

func memoryAuditManifest(snapshot: JsonObject): JsonArray {
    let result=JsonArray()
    for (raw in tutorArray(snapshot,"entries").getItems()) {
        let entry=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let item=JsonObject()
        for (key in ["ref","version","classification","observed_at","expires_at","question_id","source_refs"]) { item.put(key,entry[key]) }
        result.add(item)
    }
    return result
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/learning_memory_sources.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*

// Educational source stamp. Display metadata and progress status are excluded;
// archive/confirmation are checked separately when a memory is read.
func memoryQuestionVersion(db: Database, question: JsonObject): String {
    let data=JsonArray()
    for (key in ["id","user_id","subject","grade","question_type","question_text","student_answer","options","knowledge_points"]) { data.add(question[key]) }
    let id=jsonText(question,"id")
    data.add(firstJson(queryArray(db,"student_works","SELECT * FROM student_works WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",id)))
    data.add(queryArray(db,"student_steps","SELECT * FROM student_steps WHERE work_id=(SELECT id FROM student_works WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1) ORDER BY step_index,id",id))
    data.add(queryArray(db,"question_assets","SELECT * FROM question_assets WHERE question_id=? AND kind IN ('crop','question_figure') ORDER BY created_at,id",id))
    data.add(firstJson(queryArray(db,"problem_analyses","SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 1",id)))
    data.add(queryArray(db,"reference_steps","SELECT r.* FROM reference_steps r JOIN problem_analyses a ON a.id=r.analysis_id WHERE a.question_id=? ORDER BY r.step_index,r.id",id))
    // Keep the v1 source-stamp shape for existing personal learning records.
    data.add(JsonArray())
    return sha256(data.toString().toArray())
}

func memoryDatasetVersion(db: Database, id: String): String {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=? AND usable_for_auto_grade=1",[Some(id)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { return "" }
    return sha256(rowJson("dataset_questions",rows[0]).toString().toArray())
}

func memoryEvidenceSource(db: Database, question: JsonObject, evidence: JsonObject, masteryId: String, mistakeId: String): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("rule_version",JsonString("learning-memory-v1")); result.put("question_version",JsonString(memoryQuestionVersion(db,question)))
    result.put("mastery_id",JsonString(masteryId)); result.put("misconception_id",JsonString(mistakeId))
    let dataset=tutorText(evidence,"dataset_question_id")
    result.put("dataset_version",JsonString(if (dataset.isEmpty()) { "" } else { memoryDatasetVersion(db,dataset) }))
    return result
}

func memoryQuestion(db: Database, id: String, subject: String): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND subject=? AND status<>'archived' AND confirmed=1",[Some(id),Some(subject)]).rows
    return if (rows.isEmpty()) { JsonObject() } else { rowJson("questions",rows[0]) }
}

func memoryUpdateValid(db: Database, update: JsonObject, owner: String, subject: String): Bool {
    if (tutorText(update,"user_id")!=owner) { return false }
    let evidence=tutorObject(update,"evidence"); let source=tutorObject(evidence,"memory_source")
    if (tutorText(source,"rule_version")!="learning-memory-v1") { return false }
    let question=memoryQuestion(db,tutorText(update,"question_id"),subject)
    if (question.getFields().isEmpty() || tutorText(source,"question_version")!=memoryQuestionVersion(db,question)) { return false }
    let strategy=tutorText(evidence,"strategy")
    if (tutorText(question,"user_id")!=owner) { return false }
    if (strategy=="aemt_assistance_aware") {
        let rows=db.query("SELECT a.result,t.input_fingerprint FROM learning_task_actions a JOIN learning_tasks t ON t.id=a.task_id WHERE t.id=? AND t.user_id=? AND t.question_id=? AND a.request_id=? AND a.status='applied'",[Some(tutorText(evidence,"learning_task_id")),Some(owner),sqlValue(question["id"]),Some(tutorText(evidence,"action_request_id"))]).rows
        if (rows.isEmpty() || rowText(rows[0],"input_fingerprint")!=taskFingerprint(taskSnapshot(db,jsonText(question,"id"),owner,lock:false))) { return false }
        let result=(JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(rows[0],"result")) as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let attempt=tutorObject(result,"attempt"); let event=tutorObject(evidence,"aemt_event")
        let verdict=tutorText(attempt,"verdict")
        return ["confirmed_correct","confirmed_incorrect"].contains(verdict) && tutorText(event,"verdict")==verdict && tutorFlag(tutorObject(attempt,"grading"),"can_update_learning") && !tutorFlag(event,"solution_seen") && ["original_question","contrast_question"].contains(tutorText(event,"scope"))
    }
    if (strategy=="verified_condition_contrast") {
        let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM learning_tasks WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND question_id=? AND status='completed' AND learning_applied=1",[Some(tutorText(evidence,"learning_task_id")),Some(owner),sqlValue(question["id"])]).rows
        if (rows.isEmpty()) { return false }
        return rowText(rows[0],"input_fingerprint")==taskFingerprint(taskSnapshot(db,jsonText(question,"id"),owner,lock:false))
    }
    if (strategy=="independent_dataset_practice") {
        let dataset=tutorText(evidence,"dataset_question_id"); let version=memoryDatasetVersion(db,dataset)
        if (version.isEmpty() || version!=tutorText(source,"dataset_version")) { return false }
        let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_practice_attempts WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND mistake_id=? AND dataset_question_id=?",[Some(tutorText(evidence,"practice_attempt_id")),Some(owner),sqlValue(question["id"]),Some(dataset)]).rows
        if (rows.isEmpty()) { return false }
        let attempt=rowJson("dataset_practice_attempts",rows[0]); let details=tutorObject(attempt,"grading_details")
        let preparation=JsonObject(); preparation.put("target_knowledge_id",attempt["knowledge_id"])
        let trace=JsonObject(); trace.put("alignment_proof",tutorObject(details,"alignment_proof"))
        let candidates=db.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=?",[Some(dataset)]).rows
        return !candidates.isEmpty() && tutorFlag(details,"can_update_learning") && practiceProofValid(db,question,preparation,rowJson("dataset_questions",candidates[0]),trace)
    }
    if (["micro_activity","assisted_progression"].contains(strategy)) {
        return !db.query("SELECT id FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND question_id=? AND status='completed'",[Some(tutorText(evidence,"tutor_session_id")),Some(owner),sqlValue(question["id"])]).rows.isEmpty()
    }
    return false
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/learning_task_budget.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.time.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

class TaskBudgetExceeded <: Exception {
    let stage: String
    init(stage: String) { super("本次执行已到时限，结果未提交"); this.stage=stage }
}

public class TaskBudget {
    let started: MonoTime
    let limit: Duration
    init(started!: MonoTime=MonoTime.now(), limit!: Duration=Duration.second*45) {
        this.started=started; this.limit=limit
    }
    func elapsedMs(): Int64 { return (MonoTime.now()-started).toNanoseconds()/1000000 }
    func remaining(): Duration { return limit-(MonoTime.now()-started) }
    func check(stage: String): Unit { if (remaining()<=Duration.Zero) { throw TaskBudgetExceeded(stage) } }
    func timeoutSeconds(cap: Int64, reserveSeconds!: Int64=0): Int64 {
        let seconds=remaining().toSeconds()-reserveSeconds
        // A zero network timeout can mean unlimited; never pass it onward.
        if (seconds<1) { throw TaskBudgetExceeded("remaining_budget") }
        return if (seconds<cap) { seconds } else { cap }
    }
}

func taskAtLimit(task: JsonObject): Bool {
    return tutorNumber(task,"run_count")>=72 || tutorNumber(tutorObject(task,"checkpoint"),"rounds")>=24
}

func taskLimitView(): JsonObject {
    let view=taskStoppedView("已到本次学习上限。结束后可从原题重开，记录会保留。")
    view.put("title",JsonString("本次先停在这里")); return view
}

func stopTaskAtLimit(db: Database, task: JsonObject, reason: String): Unit {
    let recoverable=reason=="prepare_budget"
    let view=if (recoverable) { taskStoppedView("准备耗时较长，内容已保留，可以稍后继续") } else { taskLimitView() }
    if (recoverable) {
        let actions=JsonArray(); actions.add(JsonString("resume")); view.put("secondary_actions",actions)
        let photo=tutorObject(tutorObject(task,"student_view"),"handwriting")
        if (!photo.getFields().isEmpty()) { view.put("handwriting",photo) }
    }
    db.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='failed',version=version+1,student_view=?,exit_reason=?,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(view.toString()),Some(reason),sqlValue(task["id"])])
    db.query("UPDATE learning_task_actions SET status='failed',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE task_id=? AND status='pending'",[sqlValue(task["id"])])
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/learning_task_images.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.base64.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func taskImage(db: Database, task: JsonObject, id: String): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM learning_task_images WHERE id=? AND task_id=? AND input_fingerprint=?",[Some(id),sqlValue(task["id"]),sqlValue(task["input_fingerprint"])]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"这张作答照片不属于当前任务版本") }
    return rowJson("learning_task_images",rows[0])
}

func attachTaskImage(db: Database, task: JsonObject, body: JsonObject, event: JsonObject): Unit {
    let id=optionalText(body,"handwriting_id","",36)
    if (id.isEmpty()) {
        if (body.get("handwriting_confirmed").isSome()) { throw ApiError(422,"核对标记缺少对应照片") }
        return
    }
    if (tutorText(event,"kind")!="answer") { throw ApiError(422,"照片只能关联本人确认后的作答") }
    let flag=body.get("handwriting_confirmed") ?? JsonNull()
    if (!(flag is JsonBool) || !tutorFlag(body,"handwriting_confirmed")) { throw ApiError(422,"请先核对照片识别文字，再确认提交") }
    let image=taskImage(db,task,id)
    if (tutorText(image,"confirmation_step_id")!=jsonText(task,"step_id") || !(image["recognition"] is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(409,"照片对应的步骤已更新，请重新核对当前作答") }
    event.put("handwriting_id",JsonString(id)); event.put("handwriting_confirmed",JsonBool(true)); event.put("handwriting_recognition",image["recognition"])
}

func taskImageComputeAction(task: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let event=(JsonValue.fromStr(task["pending_action"].toString()) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    if (tutorText(event,"kind")!="recognize_handwriting") { return event }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let image=taskImage(db,task,jsonText(event,"handwriting_id")); let raw=readMedia(jsonText(image,"original_path"))
        if (sha256(raw)!=jsonText(image,"sha256")) { throw ApiError(503,"作答原图校验失败，请重新上传") }
        event.put("image_base64",JsonString(toBase64String(raw))); return event
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"作答照片暂时不可用")
}

func saveTaskImageResult(db: Database, task: JsonObject, view: JsonObject, nextStep: String): Unit {
    let event=tutorObject(task,"pending_action")
    if (tutorText(event,"kind")!="recognize_handwriting") { return }
    let draft=tutorObject(view,"handwriting"); let image=taskImage(db,task,jsonText(event,"handwriting_id"))
    if (jsonText(draft,"id")!=jsonText(image,"id") || !((draft.get("recognition") ?? JsonNull()) is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(503,"手写转写结果格式不正确") }
    db.query("UPDATE learning_task_images SET recognition=?,confirmation_step_id=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(draft["recognition"].toString()),Some(nextStep),sqlValue(image["id"])])
    draft.put("image_url",JsonString("/"+jsonText(image,"original_path")))
    view.put("handwriting",draft)
}

func retainTaskImageForSupport(db: Database, task: JsonObject, result: JsonObject, view: JsonObject, nextStep: String): Unit {
    let kind=tutorText(tutorObject(task,"pending_action"),"kind")
    if (!["visualize","observe_visualization","close_visualization","hint","reveal","resume"].contains(kind) || tutorText(tutorObject(task,"checkpoint"),"phase")!=tutorText(tutorObject(result,"checkpoint"),"phase") || tutorText(view,"input_kind")!="text") { return }
    let draft=tutorObject(tutorObject(task,"student_view"),"handwriting")
    if (draft.getFields().isEmpty() || !tutorObject(view,"handwriting").getFields().isEmpty()) { return }
    let image=taskImage(db,task,jsonText(draft,"id"))
    if (tutorText(image,"confirmation_step_id")!=jsonText(task,"step_id")) { return }
    db.query("UPDATE learning_task_images SET confirmation_step_id=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(nextStep),sqlValue(image["id"])])
    view.put("handwriting",draft)
}

public func uploadTaskHandwriting(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    let written=JsonObject()
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts); let initial=taskRow(db,id,owner); let upload=readImageUpload(context)
        let body=JsonObject()
        for ((key,value) in upload.fields) {
            if (!["request_id","version","step_id","input_fingerprint"].contains(key)) { throw ApiError(422,"上传包含不支持的字段") }
            body.put(key,JsonString(value))
        }
        let requestId=textField(body,"request_id",80); let step=textField(body,"step_id",36); let fingerprint=textField(body,"input_fingerprint",64)
        if (requestId=="__initial__") { throw ApiError(422,"请求编号不可用") }
        let version=try { Int64.parse(textField(body,"version",12)) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"步骤版本无效") }
        let bytes=upload.bytes
        let extension=if (bytesMatch(bytes,[0x89,0x50,0x4e,0x47,0x0d,0x0a,0x1a,0x0a],0)) { "png" } else if (bytesMatch(bytes,[0xff,0xd8,0xff],0)) { "jpg" } else if (bytesMatch(bytes,"RIFF".toArray(),0) && bytesMatch(bytes,"WEBP".toArray(),8)) { "webp" } else { throw ApiError(422,"请上传JPEG、PNG或WEBP作答照片") }
        let checksum=sha256(bytes); let values=JsonArray(); for (value in [version.toString(),step,fingerprint,checksum]) { values.add(JsonString(value)) }; let digest=sha256(values.toString().toArray())
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,jsonText(initial,"question_id"),owner); let task=taskRow(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            let prior=tx.query("SELECT body_hash FROM learning_task_actions WHERE task_id=? AND request_id=?",[Some(id),Some(requestId)]).rows
            if (!prior.isEmpty()) {
                if (rowText(prior[0],"body_hash")!=digest) { throw ApiError(409,"同一上传编号不能更换照片或步骤") }
                taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot); return taskPublic(task,accounts)
            }
            if (!taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot)) { return taskPublic(task,accounts) }
            if (!["waiting_student","needs_review"].contains(tutorText(task,"status")) || tutorText(tutorObject(task,"student_view"),"input_kind")!="text") { throw ApiError(409,"请先回到当前可作答步骤再上传") }
            if (version!=tutorNumber(task,"version") || step!=jsonText(task,"step_id") || fingerprint!=jsonText(task,"input_fingerprint")) { throw ApiError(409,"学习步骤已更新，请刷新后重新上传") }
            if (taskAtLimit(task)) { throw ApiError(409,"这次学习已到上限，请结束后回原题重开") }
            let imageId=newId(); let path="uploads/answers/${owner}/learning_task/${id}/${imageId}.${extension}"
            written.put("path",JsonString(path)); writeMedia(path,bytes)
            tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_task_images (id,task_id,request_id,input_fingerprint,upload_step_id,original_path,sha256,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(imageId),Some(id),Some(requestId),Some(fingerprint),Some(step),Some(path),Some(checksum)])
            let event=JsonObject(); event.put("kind",JsonString("recognize_handwriting")); event.put("text",JsonString("")); event.put("handwriting_id",JsonString(imageId))
            tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_task_actions (id,task_id,request_id,body_hash,kind,input_version,input_step_id,body,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,'recognize_handwriting',?,?,?,'pending',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(requestId),Some(digest),Some(version.toString()),Some(step),Some(event.toString())])
            tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='queued',version=version+1,pending_action=?,active_request=?,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,exit_reason='',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(event.toString()),Some(requestId),Some(id)])
            return taskPublic(taskRow(tx,id,owner),accounts)
        })
    } catch (error: Exception) { if (!tutorText(written,"path").isEmpty()) { removeMedia(tutorText(written,"path")) }; throw error }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/learning_task_mastery.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

// Reuse the locked task transaction and existing cognitive_updates ledger.
// Python never selects the owner, source question or the authoritative prior.
func applyTaskAemt(db: Database, task: JsonObject, result: JsonObject, budget: TaskBudget, transferEligible: Bool): Unit {
    let state=tutorObject(result,"checkpoint")
    if (tutorText(state,"workflow_version")!="langgraph-learning-v1") { return }
    let attempt=tutorObject(result,"attempt"); let grading=tutorObject(attempt,"grading")
    let phase=tutorText(attempt,"phase"); let verdict=tutorText(attempt,"verdict")
    if (!["independent","contrast"].contains(phase) || !["confirmed_correct","confirmed_incorrect"].contains(verdict) || !tutorFlag(grading,"can_update_learning")) { return }
    if (phase=="contrast" && verdict=="confirmed_correct" && !transferEligible && !tutorFlag(attempt,"assisted")) { return }
    let action=tutorObject(task,"pending_action")
    if (tutorText(action,"kind")!="answer" || tutorText(attempt,"answer")!=tutorText(action,"text")) { throw ApiError(503,"作答证据与提交不一致") }
    if (tutorText(tutorObject(task,"checkpoint"),"phase")!=phase || tutorText(grading,"verdict")!=verdict || tutorFlag(grading,"correct")!=(verdict=="confirmed_correct")) { throw ApiError(503,"作答核验与当前步骤不一致") }
    let owner=tutorText(task,"user_id"); let questionId=tutorText(task,"question_id"); let requestId=tutorText(task,"active_request"); let taskId=tutorText(task,"id")
    if (!db.query("SELECT id FROM cognitive_updates WHERE user_id=? AND question_id=? AND entity_type='mastery' AND JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(evidence,'$.learning_task_id'))=? AND JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(evidence,'$.action_request_id'))=? LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(questionId),Some(taskId),Some(requestId)]).rows.isEmpty()) { return }
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(questionId),Some(owner)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(409,"题目来源已变化") }
    let question=rowJson("questions",rows[0]); let event=JsonObject()
    event.put("event_id",JsonString(taskId+":"+requestId)); event.put("verdict",JsonString(verdict)); event.put("scope",JsonString(if (phase=="contrast") { "contrast_question" } else { "original_question" }))
    var revealed=false
    for (value in tutorArray(state,"revealed_phases").getItems()) { if ((value as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()==phase) { revealed=true } }
    let hints=tutorArray(tutorObject(state,"hints"),phase).getItems(); let assisted=tutorFlag(attempt,"assisted")
    // Exposure remains in task/action history. It must not refresh the last
    // effective mastery timestamp or create an objective mastery observation.
    if (revealed || (assisted && hints.isEmpty())) { return }
    event.put("hint_used",JsonBool(assisted && !hints.isEmpty())); event.put("solution_seen",JsonBool(revealed || (assisted && hints.isEmpty())))
    event.put("student_confirmed",JsonBool(true)); event.put("source_current",JsonBool(true)); event.put("grading_reliable",JsonBool(true)); event.put("option_count",JsonInt(tutorArray(question,"options").getItems().size))
    let elapsed=db.query("SELECT GREATEST(0,COALESCE(TIMESTAMPDIFF(SECOND,last_evidence_at,UTC_TIMESTAMP()),0)) AS seconds FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? AND subject=? AND knowledge_point=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),sqlValue(question["subject"]),Some(learningKnowledgePoint(db,question))]).rows
    let days=if (elapsed.isEmpty()) { 0.0 } else { Float64.parse(rowText(elapsed[0],"seconds"))/86400.0 }
    event.put("days_since_last",JsonFloat(days)); event.put("delayed_similar",JsonBool(phase=="contrast" && !assisted && days>=1.0))
    let clock=db.query("SELECT DATE_FORMAT(UTC_TIMESTAMP(),'%Y-%m-%dT%H:%i:%sZ') AS instant").rows
    event.put("occurred_at",JsonString(rowText(clock[0],"instant")))
    let evidence=JsonObject(); evidence.put("aemt_event",event); evidence.put("learning_task_id",JsonString(taskId)); evidence.put("action_request_id",JsonString(requestId)); evidence.put("strategy",JsonString("aemt_assistance_aware")); evidence.put("hint_level",JsonInt(if (assisted) { 1 } else { 0 }))
    if (phase=="contrast" && !assisted) { evidence.put("near_transfer",JsonBool(verdict=="confirmed_correct")) }
    budget.check("before_aemt")
    applyLearningEvidence(db,owner,question,evidence,computeTimeoutSeconds:budget.timeoutSeconds(5,reserveSeconds:1))
    if (db.query("SELECT id FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? AND question_id=? AND status='pending' LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(questionId)]).rows.isEmpty()) {
        let interval=if (assisted || verdict=="confirmed_incorrect") { "1" } else { "3" }
        db.query("INSERT INTO review_plans (id,user_id,question_id,due_at,interval_days,reason,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,TIMESTAMPADD(DAY,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP()),?,'根据本次作答安排复习','pending',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(newId()),Some(owner),Some(questionId),Some(interval),Some(interval)])
    }
    budget.check("after_aemt")
}

```

### backend-cangjie/src/learning_task_planning.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.convert.*
import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

func taskBankRow(row: HashMap<String,Option<String>>): JsonObject {
    let base=HashMap<String,Option<String>>()
    for ((key,value) in row) {
        if (key!="recent_n" && key!="recent_pass") { base[key]=value }
    }
    return rowJson("dataset_questions",base)
}

// Fixed ordering keeps the optimistic source comparison deterministic.
// Candidate IDs come only from the caller's due notebook or eligible bank.
func taskPlanCandidates(db: Database, owner: String, question: JsonObject, points: Array<String>): JsonArray {
    let result=JsonArray(); let questionId=tutorText(question,"id")
    for (row in db.query("SELECT q.* FROM questions q WHERE q.user_id=? AND q.subject=? AND q.id<>? AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM review_plans r WHERE r.question_id=q.id AND r.user_id=? AND r.status='pending' AND r.due_at<=UTC_TIMESTAMP()) ORDER BY q.id LIMIT 30",[Some(owner),sqlValue(question["subject"]),Some(questionId),Some(owner)]).rows) {
        let source=rowJson("questions",row); let item=JsonObject()
        item.put("id",source["id"]); item.put("subject",source["subject"]); item.put("source",JsonString("notebook")); item.put("due",JsonBool(true)); item.put("estimated_minutes",JsonInt(3))
        item.put("question_type",source["question_type"]); result.add(item)
    }
    for (row in db.query("SELECT q.*,(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM study_session_items i JOIN study_sessions s ON s.id=i.session_id WHERE s.user_id=? AND s.created_at>=UTC_TIMESTAMP()-INTERVAL 7 DAY AND JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(i.snapshot,'$.id'))=q.id) AS recent_n,EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM study_session_items i JOIN study_sessions s ON s.id=i.session_id WHERE s.user_id=? AND s.status='submitted' AND s.created_at>=UTC_TIMESTAMP()-INTERVAL 1 DAY AND JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(i.snapshot,'$.id'))=q.id AND JSON_EXTRACT(i.grading,'$.correct')=true AND JSON_EXTRACT(i.grading,'$.can_update_learning')=true) AS recent_pass FROM dataset_questions q WHERE q.subject=? AND q.usable_for_practice=1 AND q.usable_for_auto_grade=1 AND q.has_answer=1 ORDER BY q.id LIMIT 200",[Some(owner),Some(owner),sqlValue(question["subject"])]).rows) {
        if (result.getItems().size>=200) { break }
        let source=taskBankRow(row)
        if (!usableRetrievalCandidate(question,source,"practice")) { continue }
        var related=false
        for (raw in tutorArray(source,"knowledge_points").getItems()) {
            let label=if (raw is JsonString) { (raw as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue() } else { tutorText((raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"name") }
            for (point in points) { if (label==point && !label.isEmpty()) { related=true } }
        }
        // Without concept tags, keep only the current question type as an
        // explicit coarse fallback; do not suggest arbitrary same-subject work.
        if (!related && tutorText(source,"question_type")!=tutorText(question,"question_type")) { continue }
        let item=JsonObject()
        for (key in ["id","subject","question_type","grade_band","knowledge_points","options"]) { item.put(key,source[key]) }
        item.put("source",JsonString("dataset")); item.put("error_relevance",JsonFloat(if (related) { 1.0 } else { 0.2 })); item.put("estimated_minutes",JsonInt(3))
        let difficulty=tutorText(source,"difficulty")
        item.put("difficulty",JsonFloat(if (["easy","简单","容易"].contains(difficulty)) { 0.25 } else if (["hard","困难","较难"].contains(difficulty)) { 0.8 } else { 0.5 }))
        item.put("recent_exposures",JsonInt(Int64.parse(rowText(row,"recent_n"))))
        item.put("just_mastered",JsonBool(rowText(row,"recent_pass")=="1")); result.add(item)
    }
    return result
}

func validateTaskRecommendation(result: JsonObject, domain: JsonObject): Unit {
    let view=tutorObject(result,"student_view"); let plan=tutorObject(view,"recommendation"); let id=tutorText(plan,"question_id")
    if (id.isEmpty()) { return }
    var valid=false
    for (raw in tutorArray(domain,"practice_candidates").getItems()) {
        let candidate=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        if (tutorText(candidate,"id")==id && tutorText(candidate,"source")==tutorText(plan,"question_source") && !tutorFlag(candidate,"just_mastered")) { valid=true }
    }
    if (!valid) { throw ApiError(503,"推荐题目不在本次可用范围内") }
    for (raw in tutorArray(plan,"alternatives").getItems()) {
        let alternate=(raw as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue(); var found=false
        for (entry in tutorArray(domain,"practice_candidates").getItems()) {
            let candidate=(entry as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (tutorText(candidate,"id")==alternate && !tutorFlag(candidate,"just_mastered")) { found=true }
        }
        if (!found || alternate==id) { throw ApiError(503,"备选题目不在本次可用范围内") }
    }
}
```

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func selfCheckRow(db: Database, task: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM learning_self_checks WHERE user_id=? AND task_id=?",[sqlValue(task["user_id"]),sqlValue(task["id"])]).rows
    return if (rows.isEmpty()) { JsonObject() } else { rowJson("learning_self_checks",rows[0]) }
}

func selfCheckPublic(note: JsonObject, current: Bool, canSave: Bool): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject(); let present=!note.getFields().isEmpty()
    result.put("version",JsonInt(if (present) { tutorNumber(note,"version") } else { 0 }))
    result.put("source_current",JsonBool(current)); result.put("can_save",JsonBool(canSave))
    if (!present || tutorFlag(note,"deleted")) { result.put("note",JsonNull()); return result }
    let item=JsonObject()
    for (key in ["id","task_id","question_id","text","version","updated_at"]) { item.put(key,note[key]) }
    item.put("kind",JsonString("student_self_report")); result.put("note",item); return result
}

func auditSelfCheck(db: Database, task: JsonObject, note: JsonObject, operation: String): Unit {
    let runId=newId(); let evidence=JsonObject()
    for (key in ["task_id","question_id","version","input_fingerprint"]) { evidence.put(key,note[key]) }
    evidence.put("note_id",note["id"]); evidence.put("operation",JsonString(operation)); evidence.put("scope",JsonString("student_self_report")); evidence.put("student_confirmed",JsonBool(true))
    db.query("INSERT INTO agent_runs (id,session_id,agent_name,status,result,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'student_self_check','completed',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(runId),sqlValue(task["session_id"]),Some(evidence.toString())])
    db.query("INSERT INTO agent_tool_calls (id,agent_run_id,tool_name,input_json,output_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'save_self_check',?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(runId),Some(evidence.toString()),Some(evidence.toString())])
}

public func learningSelfCheck(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let method=context.request.method
    if (!["GET","PATCH","DELETE"].contains(method)) { throw ApiError(405,"此接口不支持该方法") }
    let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts); let initial=taskRow(db,id,owner)
        let body=if (method=="GET") { JsonObject() } else { readJson(context) }
        if (method!="GET") { taskStrict(body,if (method=="DELETE") { ["version"] } else { ["text","version","confirmed","input_fingerprint"] }) }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,jsonText(initial,"question_id"),owner); let task=taskRow(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            let current=taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot); let canSave=current && tutorText(task,"status")=="completed"
            let note=selfCheckRow(tx,task); let version=if (note.getFields().isEmpty()) { 0 } else { tutorNumber(note,"version") }
            if (method=="GET") { return selfCheckPublic(note,current,canSave) }
            let supplied=body.get("version") ?? JsonNull()
            if (!(supplied is JsonInt) || (supplied as JsonInt).getOrThrow().getValue()<0) { throw ApiError(422,"请提供要点版本") }
            if (method=="DELETE" && (note.getFields().isEmpty() || tutorFlag(note,"deleted"))) { return selfCheckPublic(note,current,canSave) }
            var text=""
            if (method=="PATCH") {
                if (!canSave) { throw ApiError(409,"请完成当前题目版本的学习，再保存自检要点") }
                let confirmation=body.get("confirmed") ?? JsonNull()
                if (!(confirmation is JsonBool) || !tutorFlag(body,"confirmed")) { throw ApiError(422,"请先确认这是你要保存的自检要点") }
                if (textField(body,"input_fingerprint",64)!=jsonText(task,"input_fingerprint")) { throw ApiError(409,"原题版本已变化，请重新核对") }
                text=textField(body,"text",300)
                if (stripWhitespace(text).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"请写一句自检要点") }
                // Retrying an acknowledged-but-lost response does not create a new revision/audit.
                if (!note.getFields().isEmpty() && !tutorFlag(note,"deleted") && jsonText(note,"text")==text) { return selfCheckPublic(note,current,canSave) }
            }
            if (!jsonEquivalent(supplied,JsonInt(version))) { throw ApiError(409,"自检要点已在别处更新，请重新读取后再保存") }
            if (note.getFields().isEmpty()) {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_self_checks (id,user_id,question_id,task_id,input_fingerprint,text,version,deleted,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,1,0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(owner),sqlValue(task["question_id"]),Some(id),sqlValue(task["input_fingerprint"]),Some(text)])
            } else {
                tx.query("UPDATE learning_self_checks SET text=?,deleted=?,version=version+1,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(text),Some(if (method=="DELETE") { "1" } else { "0" }),sqlValue(note["id"])])
            }
            let saved=selfCheckRow(tx,task); auditSelfCheck(tx,task,saved,if (method=="DELETE") { "delete" } else { if (version==0) { "create" } else { "edit" } })
            return selfCheckPublic(saved,current,canSave)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

public func learningTaskHistory(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts); taskRow(db,id,owner)
        let items=JsonArray(); let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM learning_task_actions WHERE task_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 51",[Some(id)]).rows
        for (index in 0..rows.size) {
            if (index>=50) { break }
            let action=rowJson("learning_task_actions",rows[index]); let result=tutorObject(action,"result"); let item=JsonObject()
            for (key in ["id","kind","status","created_at"]) { item.put(key,action[key]) }
            item.put("text",JsonString(tutorText(tutorObject(action,"body"),"text")))
            // Only public feedback and the verdict/scope of this submitted attempt leave the server.
            item.put("feedback",JsonString(tutorText(tutorObject(result,"student_view"),"feedback")))
            let attempt=tutorObject(result,"attempt")
            item.put("phase",JsonString(tutorText(attempt,"phase"))); item.put("verdict",JsonString(tutorText(attempt,"verdict"))); item.put("assisted",JsonBool(tutorFlag(attempt,"assisted")))
            item.put("source",attempt.get("source") ?? JsonNull())
            let kind=tutorText(action,"kind"); let visual=tutorObject(tutorObject(result,"checkpoint"),"visualization")
            item.put("visualization",JsonNull())
            if (["visualize","observe_visualization","close_visualization"].contains(kind) && !visual.getFields().isEmpty()) {
                let summary=JsonObject(); summary.put("title",tutorObject(visual,"spec")["title"]); summary.put("relation_tex",visual["current_equation_tex"])
                let controls=StringBuilder()
                for (rawVariable in tutorArray(tutorObject(visual,"spec"),"interactive_variables").getItems()) {
                    let variable=(rawVariable as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let name=tutorText(variable,"name")
                    controls.append(tutorText(variable,"label")+"："+tutorObject(visual,"values")[name].toString()+"；")
                }
                summary.put("controls",JsonString(controls.toString())); item.put("visualization",summary); item.put("phase",visual["source_phase"]); item.put("assisted",JsonBool(true))
            }
            let imageId=tutorText(tutorObject(action,"body"),"handwriting_id")
            item.put("handwriting_image_url",JsonString(if (imageId.isEmpty()) { "" } else { accounts.mediaUrl("/"+jsonText(taskImage(db,taskRow(db,id,owner),imageId),"original_path")) }))
            items.add(item)
        }
        let output=JsonObject(); output.put("items",items); output.put("has_more",JsonBool(rows.size>50)); output.put("source_question",taskSourcePublic(db,id)); return output
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

func addTaskJourney(db: Database, result: JsonObject, owner: String, questionId: String): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM learning_tasks WHERE user_id=? AND question_id=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 50",[Some(owner),Some(questionId)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { return result }
    let snapshot=taskSnapshot(db,questionId,owner); let items=JsonArray(); let events=ArrayList<JsonObject>()
    for (raw in tutorArray(result,"events").getItems()) { events.add((raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow()) }
    var active=""; var repair=false; var original=false; var near=false; var finished=false
    for (row in rows) {
        let task=rowJson("learning_tasks",row); let current=taskValidateVersion(db,task,snapshot); let status=tutorText(task,"status"); let flags=tutorObject(task,"checkpoint"); let assistance=tutorObject(flags,"assistance")
        if (current) {
            repair=repair || tutorFlag(flags,"repair_passed")
            original=original || (tutorFlag(flags,"independent_passed") && !tutorFlag(assistance,"independent"))
            near=near || tutorFlag(task,"learning_applied")
            finished=finished || status=="completed"
            if (active.isEmpty() && ["queued","running","waiting_student","needs_review","failed"].contains(status)) { active=jsonText(task,"id") }
        }
        let item=JsonObject(); for (key in ["id","status","updated_at","learning_applied"]) { item.put(key,task[key]) }; item.put("source_current",JsonBool(current)); items.add(item)
        let detail=if (!current) { "原题已更新，本次过程保留为历史。" } else { if (status=="completed") { if (tutorFlag(task,"learning_applied")) { "本次对照已独立完成，仍需间隔复习检验保持。" } else { "本轮学习已结束，未新增独立迁移证据。" } } else { if (status=="cancelled") { "本次已结束，回答与订正记录保留。" } else { "已保存当前步骤，可回任务继续查看。" } } }
        journeyEvent(events,"task:"+jsonText(task,"id"),jsonText(task,"updated_at"),"订正学习",detail)
    }
    let progress=JsonObject(); progress.put("items",items); progress.put("repair_verified",JsonBool(repair)); progress.put("original_independent",JsonBool(original)); progress.put("near_transfer",JsonBool(near)); result.put("learning_tasks",progress)
    let currentAction=tutorText(tutorObject(result,"next_action"),"url")
    let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question")
    if (!active.isEmpty() && tutorFlag(question,"confirmed") && tutorText(question,"status")!="awaiting_crop" && !currentAction.startsWith("/pages/ocr/")) {
        let action=JsonObject(); action.put("label",JsonString("继续订正学习")); action.put("url",JsonString("/pages/task/task?id="+active)); action.put("reason",JsonString("从已保存的当前步骤继续，不必重新开始。")); result.put("next_action",action)
    } else if (finished && currentAction.startsWith("/pages/diagnosis/")) { result.put("next_action",JourneyAction.Practice.payload(questionId,"")) }
    if (near) { result.put("summary",JsonString("已有本次单条件对照的独立证据，继续用间隔复习检验保持。")) }
    let notes=JsonArray()
    for (row in db.query("SELECT n.* FROM learning_self_checks n WHERE n.user_id=? AND n.question_id=? AND n.deleted=0 ORDER BY n.updated_at DESC,n.id DESC LIMIT 50",[Some(owner),Some(questionId)]).rows) {
        let note=rowJson("learning_self_checks",row); let task=taskRow(db,jsonText(note,"task_id"),owner)
        let current=jsonText(note,"input_fingerprint")==taskFingerprint(snapshot) && tutorText(task,"status")!="stale" && tutorText(question,"status")!="archived"
        let view=tutorObject(selfCheckPublic(note,current,false),"note"); view.put("source_current",JsonBool(current)); notes.add(view)
        journeyEvent(events,"selfcheck:"+jsonText(note,"id"),jsonText(note,"updated_at"),"保存自检要点",if (current) { "学生确认的提醒，不作为掌握证明。" } else { "原题已更新，这条要点保留为历史提醒。" })
    }
    result.put("self_checks",notes)
    let recent=JsonArray(); let used=HashSet<Int64>()
    for (_ in 0..30) {
        var best: Int64 = -1
        for (index in 0..events.size) { if (!used.contains(index) && (best<0 || journeyTimeKey(jsonText(events[index],"time"))>journeyTimeKey(jsonText(events[best],"time")))) { best=index } }
        if (best<0) { break }; used.add(best); recent.add(events[best])
    }
    result.put("events",recent); return result
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/learning_task_sources.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

class TaskSourceError <: Exception {
    let reason: String
    init(reason: String) { super("任务来源校验未通过"); this.reason=reason }
}

// Insert once. Legacy tasks may only acquire a snapshot after the live source
// is revalidated; never reconstruct a stale task from today's question.
func taskSource(db: Database, task: JsonObject, current: JsonObject, origin!: String="legacy_revalidated"): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM learning_task_sources WHERE task_id=?",[sqlValue(task["id"])]).rows
    if (!rows.isEmpty()) {
        let row=try { rowJson("learning_task_sources",rows[0]) } catch (_: Exception) { throw TaskSourceError("source_parse") }
        let source=tutorObject(row,"source")
        if (tutorText(row,"input_fingerprint")!=tutorText(task,"input_fingerprint")) { throw TaskSourceError("source_fingerprint") }
        let old=diagnosisFingerprint(source); let fresh=diagnosisFingerprint(current)
        for (index in 0..old.getItems().size) {
            if (!taskSourceEqual(old[index],fresh[index])) { throw TaskSourceError("source_field_"+index.toString()) }
        }
        return source
    }
    if (tutorText(task,"input_fingerprint")!=taskFingerprint(current)) { throw TaskSourceError("source_legacy_stale") }
    db.query("INSERT INTO learning_task_sources (task_id,schema_version,input_fingerprint,origin,source,created_at) VALUES (?,'1.0',?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[sqlValue(task["id"]),sqlValue(task["input_fingerprint"]),Some(origin),Some(current.toString())])
    return current
}

func taskStepIds(snapshot: JsonObject, key: String): JsonArray {
    let ids=JsonArray()
    for (item in tutorArray(snapshot,key).getItems()) { ids.add((item as JsonObject).getOrThrow()["id"]) }
    return ids
}

// Public history is built from the immutable source, never from client IDs.
func taskAttemptSource(db: Database, task: JsonObject, phase: String): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("snapshot_id",task["id"]); result.put("student_step_id",JsonNull()); result.put("step_index",JsonNull()); result.put("student_step_text",JsonString(""))
    let rows=db.query("SELECT source FROM learning_task_sources WHERE task_id=?",[sqlValue(task["id"])]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { return JsonObject() }
    let source=(JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(rows[0],"source")) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    result.put("student_work_id",JsonString(tutorText(tutorObject(source,"work"),"id")))
    if (phase=="repair") {
        let steps=tutorArray(source,"steps").getItems()
        if (!steps.isEmpty()) {
            let assessment=tutorObject(tutorObject(task,"checkpoint"),"evidence_diagnosis")
            let first=if (tutorFlag(assessment,"verified") && assessment.get("first_error_step").isSome() && !(assessment["first_error_step"] is JsonNull)) { tutorNumber(assessment,"first_error_step") } else { tutorNumber(tutorObject(source,"diagnosis"),"first_error_step") }
            let index=if (first<0 || first>99) { 0 } else if (first>=steps.size) { steps.size-1 } else { first }
            let step=(steps[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            result.put("student_step_id",step["id"]); result.put("step_index",JsonInt(index)); result.put("student_step_text",step["text"])
        }
    }
    return result
}

func taskSourcePublic(db: Database, taskId: String): JsonValue {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM learning_task_sources WHERE task_id=?",[Some(taskId)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { return JsonNull() }
    let record=rowJson("learning_task_sources",rows[0]); let question=tutorObject(tutorObject(record,"source"),"question"); let result=JsonObject()
    result.put("snapshot_id",JsonString(taskId)); result.put("origin",record["origin"]); result.put("captured_at",record["created_at"])
    for (key in ["subject","grade","question_text","options"]) { result.put(key,question[key]) }
    return result
}

// MySQL JSON may store 1.0 as 1. Numeric transport representation is not a
// source change; bools/strings, object keys and array order remain distinct.
func taskSourceEqual(left: JsonValue, right: JsonValue): Bool {
    if ((left is JsonInt || left is JsonFloat) && (right is JsonInt || right is JsonFloat)) {
        if (left is JsonInt && right is JsonInt) { return left.toString()==right.toString() }
        return Float64.parse(left.toString())==Float64.parse(right.toString())
    }
    if (let Some(a) <- (left as JsonObject)) {
        if (let Some(b) <- (right as JsonObject)) {
            if (a.getFields().size!=b.getFields().size) { return false }
            for ((key,value) in a.getFields()) {
                if (let Some(other) <- b.get(key)) { if (!taskSourceEqual(value,other)) { return false } }
                else { return false }
            }
            return true
        }
        return false
    }
    if (let Some(a) <- (left as JsonArray)) {
        if (let Some(b) <- (right as JsonArray)) {
            if (a.getItems().size!=b.getItems().size) { return false }
            for (i in 0..a.getItems().size) { if (!taskSourceEqual(a[i],b[i])) { return false } }
            return true
        }
        return false
    }
    return left.toString()==right.toString()
}
```

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

func taskVisualValues(body: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let raw=body.get("visual_values") ?? JsonObject()
    if (!(raw is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(422,"图示参数格式不正确") }
    let values=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let clean=JsonObject()
    if (values.getFields().size>3) { throw ApiError(422,"图示参数过多") }
    for ((key,value) in values.getFields()) {
        if (!["delta","k","b","a","h"].contains(key) || !(value is JsonInt || value is JsonFloat)) { throw ApiError(422,"包含不支持的图示参数") }
        let number=Float64.parse(value.toString())
        if (!(number>= -100000000.0 && number<=100000000.0)) { throw ApiError(422,"图示参数超出范围") }
        clean.put(key,JsonFloat(number))
    }
    return clean
}

func validateTaskVisualAction(task: JsonObject, body: JsonObject, kind: String, values: JsonObject): Unit {
    if (kind!="observe_visualization" && body.get("visual_values").isSome()) { throw ApiError(422,"当前动作不接受图示参数") }
    if (kind=="visualize" && tutorText(tutorObject(task,"student_view"),"input_kind")!="text") { throw ApiError(409,"请先核对原题再查看图示") }
    if (!["observe_visualization","close_visualization"].contains(kind)) { return }
    let state=tutorObject(task,"checkpoint"); let visual=tutorObject(state,"visualization")
    if (!tutorFlag(state,"visual_open") || visual.getFields().isEmpty() || tutorText(visual,"source_phase")!=tutorText(state,"phase")) { throw ApiError(409,"当前没有可操作的图示，请先打开") }
    if (kind!="observe_visualization") { return }
    let variables=tutorArray(tutorObject(visual,"spec"),"interactive_variables")
    if (values.getFields().size!=variables.getItems().size) { throw ApiError(422,"图示参数与当前图示不匹配") }
    for (item in variables.getItems()) {
        let variable=(item as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let name=jsonText(variable,"name")
        if (values.get(name).isNone()) { throw ApiError(422,"图示参数与当前图示不匹配") }
        let number=Float64.parse(values[name].toString()); let low=Float64.parse(variable["min"].toString()); let high=Float64.parse(variable["max"].toString()); let step=Float64.parse(variable["step"].toString())
        if (step<=0.0 || number<low || number>high) { throw ApiError(422,"图示参数超出范围") }
        let grid=(number-low)/step; let difference=grid-Float64(Int64(grid+0.5))
        if (difference< -0.0000001 || difference>0.0000001) { throw ApiError(422,"图示参数不符合当前步长") }
    }
}
```

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*

func taskLeaseValid(task: JsonObject, lease: String): Bool {
    return tutorText(task,"status")=="running" && tutorText(task,"lease_token")==lease && !tutorFlag(task,"cancel_requested")
}

func taskPreparationFailure(db: Database, task: JsonObject, reason: String): Unit {
    let view=taskStoppedView("暂时未能准备题目，内容已保留，可以重试")
    let actions=JsonArray(); actions.add(JsonString("resume")); view.put("secondary_actions",actions)
    let photo=tutorObject(tutorObject(task,"student_view"),"handwriting")
    if (!photo.getFields().isEmpty()) { view.put("handwriting",photo) }
    db.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='failed',version=version+1,student_view=?,exit_reason=?,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(view.toString()),Some(reason),sqlValue(task["id"])])
    db.query("UPDATE learning_task_actions SET status='failed',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE task_id=? AND status='pending'",[sqlValue(task["id"])])
}

func claimLearningTask(id: String, budget: TaskBudget): Option<JsonObject> {
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let rows=db.query("SELECT user_id,question_id FROM learning_tasks WHERE id=?",[Some(id)]).rows
        if (rows.isEmpty()) { return None }
        let owner=rowText(rows[0],"user_id"); let questionId=rowText(rows[0],"question_id")
        return db.transaction<Option<JsonObject>>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,questionId,owner); let task=taskRow(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            let claimable=tx.query("SELECT id FROM learning_tasks WHERE id=? AND cancel_requested=0 AND (status='queued' OR (status='running' AND lease_until<UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)))",[Some(id)]).rows
            if (claimable.isEmpty() || !taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot)) { return None }
            if (taskAtLimit(task)) { stopTaskAtLimit(tx,task,if (tutorNumber(task,"run_count")>=72) { "retry_budget" } else { "round_budget" }); return None }
            tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET run_count=run_count+1 WHERE id=?",[Some(id)])
            let lease=newId()
            var source=JsonObject()
            try { source=taskSource(tx,task,snapshot) }
            catch (error: TaskSourceError) {
                tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='failed',version=version+1,student_view=?,exit_reason=?,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(taskStoppedView("任务来源无法核对，请结束本次学习后回原题重开；旧记录仍保留").toString()),Some(error.reason),Some(id)])
                tx.query("UPDATE learning_task_actions SET status='failed',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE task_id=? AND status='pending'",[Some(id)])
                return None
            }
            var domain=JsonObject()
            try {
                domain=taskContext(tx,source,owner); domain.put("source_snapshot_id",task["id"])
                domain.put("action_id",JsonString(id+":"+tutorText(task,"active_request")))
            } catch (error: DatabaseException) {
                taskPreparationFailure(tx,task,"prepare_database_"+error.code.toString()); return None
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                taskPreparationFailure(tx,task,"prepare_context_invalid"); return None
            }
            try { budget.check("prepare") } catch (_: TaskBudgetExceeded) {
                stopTaskAtLimit(tx,task,"prepare_budget"); return None
            }
            tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='running',lease_token=?,lease_until=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)+INTERVAL 90 SECOND,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(lease),Some(id)])
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("task",task); result.put("context",domain); result.put("lease",JsonString(lease)); return Some(result)
        })
    }
    return None
}

func taskFailure(id: String, owner: String, questionId: String, lease: String, reason!: String="computation_unavailable"): Unit {
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,questionId,owner); let task=taskRow(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            if (!taskLeaseValid(task,lease) || !taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot)) { return }
            let view=taskStoppedView("这次没能核对完成，内容已保留，可以稍后继续")
            let photo=tutorObject(tutorObject(task,"student_view"),"handwriting")
            if (!photo.getFields().isEmpty()) { view.put("handwriting",photo) }
            let actions=JsonArray(); actions.add(JsonString("resume")); if (tutorText(tutorObject(task,"pending_action"),"kind")=="recognize_handwriting") { actions.add(JsonString("use_text")) }; view.put("secondary_actions",actions)
            tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='failed',version=version+1,student_view=?,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,exit_reason=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(view.toString()),Some(reason),Some(id)])
            tx.query("UPDATE learning_task_actions SET status='failed',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE task_id=? AND request_id=? AND status='pending'",[Some(id),sqlValue(task["active_request"])])
            ()
        })
    } catch (_: Exception) { /* No upstream or database credentials enter logs. Lease expiry permits recovery. */ }
}

func saveLearningTaskResult(db: Database, task: JsonObject, result: JsonObject, budget: TaskBudget, memory: JsonObject, domain: JsonObject): Unit {
    let id=jsonText(task,"id"); let owner=jsonText(task,"user_id"); let requestId=jsonText(task,"active_request"); let traces=tutorArray(result,"traces").getItems()
    if (traces.size>3 || !["waiting_student","needs_review","completed"].contains(jsonText(result,"status"))) { throw ApiError(503,"学习任务结果格式不正确") }
    validateTaskRecommendation(result,domain)
    let manifest=JsonArray(); let validRefs=JsonArray()
    for (raw in tutorArray(memory,"entries").getItems()) {
        let entry=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let item=JsonObject()
        for (key in ["ref","version","classification","observed_at","expires_at","question_id","source_refs"]) { item.put(key,entry[key]) }
        manifest.add(item); validRefs.add(entry["ref"])
    }
    for (raw in traces) {
        let trace=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        for (ref in tutorArray(trace,"memory_refs").getItems()) {
            var found=false
            for (valid in validRefs.getItems()) { if (jsonEquivalent(ref,valid)) { found=true } }
            if (!found) { throw ApiError(503,"学习规划引用了无效记忆") }
        }
    }
    let boundAttempt=result.get("attempt") ?? JsonNull()
    if (boundAttempt is JsonObject) {
        let answer=(boundAttempt as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let source=taskAttemptSource(db,task,tutorText(answer,"phase"))
        answer.put("source",source)
        for (raw in traces) {
            let trace=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (["check_step","grade_answer"].contains(tutorText(trace,"tool_name"))) { let evidence=tutorObject(trace,"evidence"); evidence.put("source",source); trace.put("evidence",evidence) }
        }
    }
    for (index in 0..traces.size) {
        let trace=(traces[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let tool=jsonText(trace,"tool_name"); let validation=jsonText(trace,"validation")
        if (!["check_step","grade_answer","knowledge_search","step_alignment","compare_variant","recognize_handwriting","inspect_figure","visualization"].contains(tool) || !["verified","rejected","needs_review","not_applicable"].contains(validation)) { throw ApiError(503,"学习任务工具结果不正确") }
        let runId=newId(); let run=JsonObject(); run.put("task_id",JsonString(id)); run.put("request_id",JsonString(requestId)); run.put("trace",trace)
        run.put("native_elapsed_ms",JsonInt(budget.elapsedMs())); run.put("elapsed_ms",result["elapsed_ms"]); run.put("usage",result["usage"]); run.put("memory_rule",JsonString("learning-memory-v1")); run.put("memory_manifest",manifest)
        db.query("INSERT INTO agent_runs (id,session_id,agent_name,status,result,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'learning_task','completed',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(runId),sqlValue(task["session_id"]),Some(run.toString())])
        let input=JsonObject(); input.put("task_id",JsonString(id)); input.put("request_id",JsonString(requestId)); input.put("student_action",task["pending_action"])
        db.query("INSERT INTO agent_tool_calls (id,agent_run_id,tool_name,input_json,output_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(runId),Some(tool),Some(input.toString()),Some(trace.toString())])
        db.query("INSERT INTO learning_task_steps (id,task_id,request_id,step_no,plan_version,tool_name,validation,agent_run_id,trace,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(requestId),Some(index.toString()),Some((tutorNumber(task,"version")*3+index).toString()),Some(tool),Some(validation),Some(runId),Some(trace.toString())])
    }
    let attempt=result.get("attempt") ?? JsonNull(); let state=tutorObject(result,"checkpoint")
    var eligible=tutorFlag(result,"learning_eligible") && !tutorFlag(task,"learning_applied") && jsonText(result,"status")=="completed"
    let contrast=tutorText(tutorObject(state,"contrast"),"question")
    if (!contrast.isEmpty() && (tutorFlag(state,"contrast_passed") || tutorFlag(tutorObject(state,"assistance"),"contrast"))) {
        let subject=db.query("SELECT subject FROM questions WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(task["question_id"])]).rows
        let contrastHash=sha256((rowText(subject[0],"subject")+"|"+contrast).toArray())
        // A unique claim also serializes equal contrasts in different notebook entries.
        // It commits with the result/evidence; a rollback must release the claim too.
        db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO learning_task_exposures (user_id,contrast_hash,task_id,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(owner),Some(contrastHash),Some(id)])
        let exposure=db.query("SELECT task_id FROM learning_task_exposures WHERE user_id=? AND contrast_hash=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(owner),Some(contrastHash)]).rows
        if (exposure.isEmpty() || rowText(exposure[0],"task_id")!=id) { eligible=false }
    }
    if (eligible && !db.query("SELECT t.id FROM learning_tasks t JOIN questions q ON q.id=t.question_id WHERE t.user_id=? AND q.subject=(SELECT subject FROM questions WHERE id=?) AND JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(t.checkpoint,'$.contrast.question'))=? AND (JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(t.checkpoint,'$.contrast_passed'))='true' OR JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(t.checkpoint,'$.assistance.contrast'))='true') LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),sqlValue(task["question_id"]),sqlValue(tutorObject(state,"contrast")["question"])]).rows.isEmpty()) { eligible=false }
    if (eligible) {
        if (tutorText(tutorObject(task,"pending_action"),"kind")!="answer") { throw ApiError(503,"缺少学生确认的实际作答") }
        if (!(attempt is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(503,"缺少学习证据") }
        let answer=(attempt as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let grading=tutorObject(answer,"grading")
        if (jsonText(answer,"phase")!="contrast" || jsonText(answer,"verdict")!="confirmed_correct" || tutorFlag(answer,"assisted") || !tutorFlag(grading,"can_update_learning") || !tutorFlag(state,"contrast_passed")) { throw ApiError(503,"学习证据未达到更新条件") }
        let question=rowJson("questions",db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[sqlValue(task["question_id"]),Some(owner)]).rows[0])
        let evidence=JsonObject(); evidence.put("near_transfer",JsonBool(true)); evidence.put("hint_level",JsonInt(0)); evidence.put("strategy",JsonString("verified_condition_contrast")); evidence.put("learning_task_id",JsonString(id)); evidence.put("action_request_id",JsonString(requestId))
        if (tutorText(state,"workflow_version")!="langgraph-learning-v1") {
            budget.check("before_learning"); applyLearningEvidence(db,owner,question,evidence,computeTimeoutSeconds:budget.timeoutSeconds(5,reserveSeconds:1)); budget.check("after_learning")
        }
        // A single contrast is near-transfer evidence, never a claim of full mastery.
        if (db.query("SELECT id FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? AND question_id=? AND status='pending' LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),sqlValue(task["question_id"])]).rows.isEmpty()) {
            db.query("INSERT INTO review_plans (id,user_id,question_id,due_at,interval_days,reason,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP()+INTERVAL 3 DAY,3,'单条件对照已独立完成，稍后再确认稳定性','pending',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(newId()),Some(owner),sqlValue(task["question_id"])])
        }
    }
    applyTaskAemt(db,task,result,budget,eligible)
    if (requestId!="__initial__") {
        db.query("UPDATE learning_task_actions SET result=?,status='applied',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE task_id=? AND request_id=? AND status='pending'",[Some(result.toString()),Some(id),Some(requestId)])
    }
    budget.check("before_checkpoint")
    let nextStep=newId(); let view=tutorObject(result,"student_view"); saveTaskImageResult(db,task,view,nextStep); retainTaskImageForSupport(db,task,result,view,nextStep)
    db.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status=?,version=version+1,step_id=?,checkpoint=?,student_view=?,learning_applied=?,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,exit_reason=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(result["status"]),Some(nextStep),Some(state.toString()),Some(view.toString()),Some(if (eligible || tutorFlag(task,"learning_applied")) { "1" } else { "0" }),sqlValue(result["exit_reason"]),Some(id)])
    budget.check("after_checkpoint")
    db.query("UPDATE agent_runs r JOIN learning_task_steps s ON s.agent_run_id=r.id SET r.result=JSON_SET(r.result,'$.native_elapsed_ms',CAST(? AS UNSIGNED)) WHERE s.task_id=? AND s.request_id=?",[Some(budget.elapsedMs().toString()),Some(id),Some(requestId)])
    budget.check("after_audit")
}

func runLearningTask(id: String): Unit {
    var owner=""; var questionId=""; var lease=""; var stage="claim"
    let budget=TaskBudget()
    try {
        let claimed=claimLearningTask(id,budget)
        if (claimed.isNone()) { return }
        let prepared=claimed.getOrThrow(); let before=tutorObject(prepared,"task"); owner=jsonText(before,"user_id"); questionId=jsonText(before,"question_id"); lease=jsonText(prepared,"lease")
        budget.check("claimed"); stage="image_input"
        let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("learning_task")); input.put("task_context",prepared["context"]); input.put("task_checkpoint",before["checkpoint"]); input.put("task_action",taskImageComputeAction(before))
        stage="compute"
        let computeSeconds=budget.timeoutSeconds(40,reserveSeconds:5)
        if (computeSeconds<2) { throw TaskBudgetExceeded("compute_reserve") }
        input.put("task_budget_ms",JsonInt(if (computeSeconds>38) { 38000 } else { (computeSeconds-1)*1000 }))
        let result=algorithmCompute(input,timeoutSeconds:computeSeconds,taskBudget:Some(budget))
        budget.check("computed")
        stage="commit"
        let stableOwner=owner; let stableQuestion=questionId; let stableLease=lease
        try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
                let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,stableQuestion,stableOwner); let current=taskRow(tx,id,stableOwner,lock:true)
                if (!taskLeaseValid(current,stableLease) || !taskValidateVersion(tx,current,snapshot)) { return }
                let domain=taskContext(tx,taskSource(tx,current,snapshot),stableOwner); domain.put("source_snapshot_id",current["id"])
                domain.put("action_id",JsonString(id+":"+tutorText(current,"active_request")))
                if (!jsonEquivalent(prepared["context"],domain)) { throw ApiError(409,"学习依据已更新，需要重新核对") }
                budget.check("validated")
                saveLearningTaskResult(tx,current,result,budget,tutorObject(domain,"memory_snapshot"),domain); budget.check("before_commit"); ()
            })
        }
    } catch (error: TaskBudgetExceeded) { if (!lease.isEmpty()) { taskFailure(id,owner,questionId,lease,reason:"budget_"+error.stage) } }
    catch (error: DatabaseException) { if (!lease.isEmpty()) { taskFailure(id,owner,questionId,lease,reason:stage+"_database_"+error.code.toString()) } }
    catch (error: ApiError) { if (!lease.isEmpty()) { taskFailure(id,owner,questionId,lease,reason:stage+"_api_"+error.status.toString()) } }
    catch (_: Exception) { if (!lease.isEmpty()) { taskFailure(id,owner,questionId,lease,reason:stage+"_runtime") } }
}

public func learningTaskWorker(): Unit {
    while (true) {
        try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            let rows=db.query("SELECT id FROM learning_tasks WHERE cancel_requested=0 AND (status='queued' OR (status='running' AND lease_until<UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))) ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 1").rows
            if (!rows.isEmpty()) { runLearningTask(rowText(rows[0],"id")) }
            else { sleep(Duration.second) }
        } catch (_: Exception) { sleep(Duration.second * 2) }
    }
}
```
