### backend-cangjie/src/learning_tasks.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.encoding.url.*
import stdx.net.http.*


func taskStrict(body: JsonObject, allowed: Array<String>): Unit {
    for ((key,_) in body.getFields()) { if (!allowed.contains(key)) { throw ApiError(422,"请求包含不支持的字段") } }
}

func taskOwner(context: HttpContext, db: Database, accounts: Accounts): String {
    let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
    if (rowText(user,"role")!="student") { throw ApiError(403,"请使用学生账号学习") }
    return rowText(user,"id")
}

func taskRow(db: Database, id: String, owner: String, lock!: Bool=false): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM learning_tasks WHERE id=? AND user_id=?"+if (lock) { " FOR UPDATE" } else { "" },[Some(id),Some(owner)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"学习任务不存在或无权访问") }
    return rowJson("learning_tasks",rows[0])
}

func taskSnapshot(db: Database, id: String, owner: String, lock!: Bool=true): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=?"+if (lock) { " FOR UPDATE" } else { "" },[Some(id),Some(owner)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"题目不存在或无权访问") }
    let snapshot=tutorDataSnapshot(db,id,Some(owner))
    snapshot.put("work",firstJson(queryArray(db,"student_works","SELECT * FROM student_works WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",id)))
    snapshot.put("steps",queryArray(db,"student_steps","SELECT * FROM student_steps WHERE work_id=(SELECT id FROM student_works WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1) ORDER BY step_index,id",id))
    snapshot.put("assets",queryArray(db,"question_assets","SELECT * FROM question_assets WHERE question_id=? AND kind IN ('crop','question_figure') ORDER BY created_at,id",id))
    snapshot.put("diagnosis",firstJson(queryArray(db,"diagnoses","SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",id)))
    return snapshot
}

func taskFingerprint(snapshot: JsonObject): String { return sha256(diagnosisFingerprint(snapshot).toString().toArray()) }

func taskPublic(task: JsonObject, accounts: Accounts): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject()
    for (key in ["id","question_id","session_id","status","version","step_id","input_fingerprint","student_view","created_at","updated_at","learning_applied"]) { result.put(key,task[key]) }
    let view=(JsonValue.fromStr(task["student_view"].toString()) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    for (raw in tutorArray(view,"prompt_blocks").getItems()) {
        let block=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let images=JsonArray()
        for (path in tutorArray(block,"images").getItems()) { let raw=tutorAnswerText(path); images.add(JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl(if (raw.startsWith("/")) { raw } else { "/"+raw }))) }
        if (!images.getItems().isEmpty()) { block.put("images",images) }
    }
    let draft=tutorObject(view,"handwriting")
    if (!tutorText(draft,"image_url").isEmpty()) { draft.put("image_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl(tutorText(draft,"image_url")))); view.put("handwriting",draft) }
    let limited=taskAtLimit(task) && ["waiting_student","needs_review","failed"].contains(tutorText(task,"status"))
    result.put("restart_required",JsonBool(limited))
    result.put("student_view",if (limited) { taskLimitView() } else { view })
    result.put("last_request_id",task["active_request"])
    result.put("current_action",JsonString(tutorText(tutorObject(task,"pending_action"),"kind")))
    result.put("exit_reason",JsonString(tutorText(task,"exit_reason")))
    return result
}

func taskStoppedView(message: String): JsonObject {
    let view=JsonObject(); view.put("title",JsonString(message)); view.put("prompt_blocks",JsonArray()); view.put("input_kind",JsonString("none")); view.put("primary_action",JsonString("")); view.put("secondary_actions",JsonArray()); view.put("feedback",JsonString(message)); return view
}

func taskValidateVersion(db: Database, task: JsonObject, snapshot: JsonObject): Bool {
    let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question")
    if (tutorText(task,"input_fingerprint")==taskFingerprint(snapshot) && tutorText(question,"status")!="archived" && tutorFlag(question,"confirmed")) {
        // Older persisted views contain only the stem. Restore options only for
        // the unchanged original question, never for a generated contrast.
        let view=tutorObject(task,"student_view")
        for (raw in tutorArray(view,"prompt_blocks").getItems()) {
            let block=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (tutorText(block,"kind")=="question" && tutorText(block,"text")==tutorText(question,"question_text") && tutorText(tutorObject(task,"checkpoint"),"phase")!="contrast") {
                block.put("options",tutorArray(question,"options")); block.put("question_type",question["question_type"])
                let figures=JsonArray()
                for (asset in tutorArray(snapshot,"assets").getItems()) {
                    let figure=(asset as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                    if (tutorText(figure,"kind")=="question_figure") { figures.add(figure["path"]) }
                }
                block.put("images",figures)
            }
        }
        task.put("student_view",view)
        return true
    }
    if (tutorText(task,"status")!="cancelled" && tutorText(task,"status")!="stale") {
        let view=taskStoppedView("题目或学习依据已更新，请回原题重新开始")
        db.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='stale',version=version+1,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,student_view=?,exit_reason='input_changed',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(view.toString()),sqlValue(task["id"])])
        db.query("UPDATE learning_task_actions SET status='stale',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE task_id=? AND status='pending'",[sqlValue(task["id"])])
        task.put("status",JsonString("stale")); task.put("student_view",view); task.put("version",JsonInt(tutorNumber(task,"version")+1))
    }
    return false
}

func taskContext(db: Database, snapshot: JsonObject, owner: String): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject(); let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question"); let analysis=tutorObject(snapshot,"analysis"); let diagnosis=tutorObject(snapshot,"diagnosis")
    result.put("question_id",question["id"])
    for (key in ["subject","grade","question_type","question_text","options"]) { result.put(key,question.get(key) ?? JsonString("")) }
    if (result["grade"] is JsonNull) { result.put("grade",JsonString("")) }
    result.put("student_work_id",JsonString(tutorText(tutorObject(snapshot,"work"),"id")))
    result.put("student_step_ids",taskStepIds(snapshot,"steps")); result.put("reference_step_ids",taskStepIds(snapshot,"references"))
    result.put("expected",JsonString(tutorText(analysis,"final_answer"))); result.put("reference_steps",tutorStringArray(tutorRowTexts(snapshot["references"]))); result.put("student_steps",tutorStringArray(tutorRowTexts(snapshot["steps"])))
    let step=if (diagnosis.get("first_error_step").isSome() && !(diagnosis["first_error_step"] is JsonNull)) { tutorNumber(diagnosis,"first_error_step") } else { 0 }
    result.put("first_error_step",JsonInt(if (step<0 || step>99) { 0 } else { step }))
    result.put("diagnosed_correct",JsonBool(((diagnosis.get("first_error_step") ?? JsonNull()) is JsonNull) && ["exact","equivalent"].contains(tutorText(diagnosis,"error_type"))))
    result.put("diagnosis_confirmed",JsonBool(tutorFlag(diagnosis,"user_confirmed")))
    result.put("diagnosis_hypothesis",JsonString(tutorText(diagnosis,"root_misconception")))
    let points=tutorKnowledge(snapshot); result.put("knowledge_points",tutorStringArray(points)); let cards=JsonArray(); let seen=HashSet<String>()
    for (point in points) {
        if (cards.getItems().size>=3) { break }
        for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE subject=? AND (name=? OR code=?) ORDER BY code LIMIT 3",[sqlValue(question["subject"]),Some(point),Some(point)]).rows) {
            let card=rowJson("knowledge_points",row); let code=jsonText(card,"code")
            if (!seen.contains(code) && cards.getItems().size<3) { cards.add(card); seen.add(code) }
        }
    }
    result.put("cards",cards)
    let prerequisiteCards=JsonArray()
    for (raw in tutorArray(diagnosis,"missing_prerequisites").getItems()) {
        if (prerequisiteCards.getItems().size>=6) { break }
        let label=(raw as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        for (row in db.query("SELECT k.id FROM knowledge_points k JOIN mastery_states m ON m.subject=k.subject AND (m.knowledge_point=k.name OR m.knowledge_point=k.code) WHERE m.user_id=? AND k.subject=? AND (k.name=? OR k.code=?) AND m.evidence_count>0 AND m.mastery_score<0.3 ORDER BY k.id LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),sqlValue(question["subject"]),Some(label),Some(label)]).rows) { prerequisiteCards.add(JsonString(rowText(row,"id"))) }
    }
    result.put("prerequisite_card_ids",prerequisiteCards)
    result.put("practice_candidates",taskPlanCandidates(db,owner,question,points))
    let seenContrasts=JsonArray()
    for (row in db.query("SELECT DISTINCT JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(t.checkpoint,'$.contrast.question')) AS prompt FROM learning_tasks t JOIN questions q ON q.id=t.question_id WHERE t.user_id=? AND q.subject=? AND (JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(t.checkpoint,'$.contrast_passed'))='true' OR JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(t.checkpoint,'$.assistance.contrast'))='true') AND JSON_EXTRACT(t.checkpoint,'$.contrast.question') IS NOT NULL ORDER BY prompt LIMIT 50",[Some(owner),sqlValue(question["subject"])]).rows) { seenContrasts.add(JsonString(rowText(row,"prompt"))) }
    result.put("seen_contrasts",seenContrasts)
    let masteryRows=db.query("SELECT mastery_score,evidence_count,last_evidence_at FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? AND subject=? AND knowledge_point=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),sqlValue(question["subject"]),Some(learningKnowledgePoint(db,question))]).rows
    if (!masteryRows.isEmpty()) {
        let state=JsonObject(); let count=Int64.parse(rowText(masteryRows[0],"evidence_count")); state.put("mastery",JsonFloat(if (count>0) { Float64.parse(rowText(masteryRows[0],"mastery_score")) } else { 0.35 })); state.put("evidence_count",JsonInt(count))
        state.put("last_effective_at",JsonString(masteryRows[0]["last_evidence_at"] ?? ""))
        let counts=db.query("SELECT COUNT(CASE WHEN JSON_EXTRACT(u.evidence,'$.aemt_event.hint_used')=false THEN 1 END) AS independent_count,COUNT(CASE WHEN JSON_EXTRACT(u.evidence,'$.aemt_event.hint_used')=false AND JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(u.evidence,'$.aemt_event.verdict'))='confirmed_correct' THEN 1 END) AS independent_correct,COUNT(CASE WHEN JSON_EXTRACT(u.evidence,'$.aemt_event.hint_used')=true THEN 1 END) AS hinted_count FROM cognitive_updates u JOIN questions q ON q.id=u.question_id WHERE u.user_id=? AND q.subject=? AND u.entity_type='mastery' AND u.entity_key=? AND JSON_EXTRACT(u.evidence,'$.aemt_event.solution_seen')=false",[Some(owner),sqlValue(question["subject"]),Some(learningKnowledgePoint(db,question))]).rows[0]
        for (key in ["independent_count","independent_correct","hinted_count"]) { state.put(key,JsonInt(Int64.parse(rowText(counts,key)))) }
        result.put("mastery_state",state)
    }
    let profiles=db.query("SELECT preferences FROM student_profiles WHERE user_id=?",[Some(owner)]).rows
    if (!profiles.isEmpty()) {
        let preferences=(JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(profiles[0],"preferences")) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let minutes=tutorNumber(preferences,"daily_minutes")
        if (minutes>0 && minutes<=120) {
            let goal=if (minutes<=5) { 3 } else if (minutes<=10) { 5 } else { 10 }
            let completed=db.query("SELECT (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT question_id) FROM learning_tasks WHERE user_id=? AND status='completed' AND updated_at>=UTC_DATE()) AS tasks,(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM study_session_items i JOIN study_sessions s ON s.id=i.session_id WHERE s.user_id=? AND s.status='submitted' AND s.submitted_at>=UTC_DATE() AND JSON_EXTRACT(i.grading,'$.can_update_learning')=true) AS practice",[Some(owner),Some(owner)]).rows[0]
            let tasks=Int64.parse(rowText(completed,"tasks")); let practice=Int64.parse(rowText(completed,"practice")); let count=tasks+practice; let estimated=tasks*3+practice
            // A transparent estimate, not a fabricated measurement of screen
            // time. A correction reserves three minutes, a bank item one; the active task
            // and actual next-item estimate are accounted for by the planner.
            result.put("available_minutes",JsonInt(if (estimated>=minutes) { 0 } else { minutes-estimated }))
            result.put("goal_remaining",JsonInt(if (count>=goal) { 0 } else { goal-count }))
        }
    }
    applyTaskMemoryContext(result,learningMemory(db,owner,question))
    return result
}

public func learningTasks(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts)
        if (context.request.method=="GET") {
            let result=JsonArray()
            let form=try { Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? "") } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"筛选条件不正确") }
            let questionId=queryLast(form,"question_id")
            for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM learning_tasks WHERE user_id=? AND (?='' OR question_id=?) ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 50",[Some(owner),Some(questionId),Some(questionId)]).rows) {
                let item=taskPublic(rowJson("learning_tasks",row),accounts)
                let titles=db.query("SELECT question_text FROM questions WHERE id=?",[row["question_id"]]).rows
                item.put("question_title",JsonString(if (titles.isEmpty()) { "本题学习" } else { academicSummary(rowText(titles[0],"question_text"),60) }))
                result.add(item)
            }
            return result
        }
        requireMethod(context,"POST"); let body=readJson(context); taskStrict(body,["question_id","session_id","request_id"])
        let questionId=textField(body,"question_id",36); let requestId=textField(body,"request_id",80)
        let sessionId=optionalText(body,"session_id","",36)
        let digest=sha256((questionId+"|"+sessionId).toArray())
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,questionId,owner); let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question")
            let previous=tx.query("SELECT task_id,body_hash FROM learning_task_requests WHERE user_id=? AND request_id=?",[Some(owner),Some(requestId)]).rows
            if (!previous.isEmpty()) {
                if (rowText(previous[0],"body_hash")!=digest) { throw ApiError(409,"同一请求编号不能用于不同任务") }
                let task=taskRow(tx,rowText(previous[0],"task_id"),owner,lock:true); taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot); return taskPublic(task,accounts)
            }
            if (tutorText(question,"status")=="archived" || !tutorFlag(question,"confirmed") || !(snapshot["analysis"] is JsonObject) || stripWhitespace(tutorText(tutorObject(snapshot,"analysis"),"final_answer")).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(409,"请先核对题目并完成分析，再开始订正学习") }
            if (!sessionId.isEmpty() && tx.query("SELECT id FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND question_id=?",[Some(sessionId),Some(owner),Some(questionId)]).rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"关联学习会话不存在") }
            var id=""; var sourceOrigin="legacy_revalidated"
            let active=tx.query("SELECT * FROM learning_tasks WHERE user_id=? AND question_id=? AND status IN ('queued','running','waiting_student','needs_review','failed') ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE",[Some(owner),Some(questionId)]).rows
            if (!active.isEmpty()) {
                let task=rowJson("learning_tasks",active[0]); if (taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot)) { id=jsonText(task,"id") }
            }
            if (id.isEmpty()) {
                id=newId(); sourceOrigin="created"; let event=JsonObject(); event.put("kind",JsonString("start")); event.put("text",JsonString(""))
                tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_tasks (id,user_id,question_id,session_id,input_fingerprint,status,step_id,checkpoint,student_view,pending_action,active_request,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,'queued',?,JSON_OBJECT(),?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(id),Some(owner),Some(questionId),if (sessionId.isEmpty()) { None } else { Some(sessionId) },Some(taskFingerprint(snapshot)),Some(newId()),Some(taskStoppedView("正在准备这一步").toString()),Some(event.toString()),Some("__initial__")])
            }
            taskSource(tx,taskRow(tx,id,owner),snapshot,origin:sourceOrigin)
            tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_task_requests (user_id,request_id,task_id,body_hash,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(owner),Some(requestId),Some(id),Some(digest)])
            return taskPublic(taskRow(tx,id,owner),accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

public func readLearningTask(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-1]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts); let initial=taskRow(db,id,owner)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,jsonText(initial,"question_id"),owner); let task=taskRow(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot); return taskPublic(task,accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

public func learningTaskAction(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, cancel!: Bool=false): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts); let initial=taskRow(db,id,owner); let body=readJson(context,defaultEmpty:cancel)
        if (!cancel) { taskStrict(body,["kind","text","version","step_id","input_fingerprint","request_id","handwriting_id","handwriting_confirmed","visual_values"]) }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,jsonText(initial,"question_id"),owner); let task=taskRow(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            if (cancel) {
                if (!["completed","cancelled","stale"].contains(jsonText(task,"status"))) {
                    tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='cancelled',cancel_requested=1,version=version+1,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,student_view=?,exit_reason='student_cancelled',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(taskStoppedView("已结束这次学习，之前的记录仍在").toString()),Some(id)])
                    tx.query("UPDATE learning_task_actions SET status='cancelled',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE task_id=? AND status='pending'",[Some(id)])
                }
                return taskPublic(taskRow(tx,id,owner),accounts)
            }
            let requestId=textField(body,"request_id",80); let visualValues=taskVisualValues(body); let values=JsonArray(); for (key in ["kind","text","version","step_id","input_fingerprint","handwriting_id","handwriting_confirmed"]) { values.add(body.get(key) ?? if (key=="text") { JsonString("") } else { JsonNull() }) }; let visualHash=JsonArray(); for (key in ["delta","k","b","a","h"]) { visualHash.add(visualValues.get(key) ?? JsonNull()) }; if (body.get("visual_values").isSome()) { values.add(visualHash) }; let digest=sha256(values.toString().toArray())
            if (requestId=="__initial__") { throw ApiError(422,"请求编号不可用") }
            let prior=tx.query("SELECT body_hash FROM learning_task_actions WHERE task_id=? AND request_id=?",[Some(id),Some(requestId)]).rows
            if (!prior.isEmpty()) {
                if (rowText(prior[0],"body_hash")!=digest) { throw ApiError(409,"同一请求编号不能修改提交内容") }
                taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot); return taskPublic(task,accounts)
            }
            if (!taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot)) { return taskPublic(task,accounts) }
            let status=jsonText(task,"status")
            if (!["waiting_student","needs_review","failed"].contains(status)) { throw ApiError(409,"任务仍在处理或已结束，请先刷新状态") }
            let version=body.get("version") ?? JsonNull()
            if (!(version is JsonInt) || !jsonEquivalent(version,task["version"]) || textField(body,"step_id",36)!=jsonText(task,"step_id") || textField(body,"input_fingerprint",64)!=jsonText(task,"input_fingerprint")) { throw ApiError(409,"这一步已更新，请保留内容并刷新后再提交") }
            let kind=textField(body,"kind",30)
            if (!["answer","hint","reveal","resume","use_text","visualize","observe_visualization","close_visualization"].contains(kind)) { throw ApiError(422,"不支持的学习动作") }
            validateTaskVisualAction(task,body,kind,visualValues)
            let text=if (kind=="observe_visualization") { textField(body,"text",1200) } else { optionalText(body,"text","",12000) }
            if (["answer","observe_visualization"].contains(kind) && stripWhitespace(text).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"请先写下你的回答") }
            if (taskAtLimit(task)) { throw ApiError(409,"这次学习已到上限，请结束后回原题重开") }
            let event=JsonObject(); event.put("kind",JsonString(kind)); event.put("text",JsonString(text))
            if (kind=="observe_visualization") { event.put("visual_values",visualValues) }
            attachTaskImage(tx,task,body,event)
            if (kind=="resume" && status=="failed" && tutorText(tutorObject(task,"pending_action"),"kind")=="recognize_handwriting") {
                event.put("kind",JsonString("recognize_handwriting")); event.put("handwriting_id",tutorObject(task,"pending_action")["handwriting_id"])
            }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_task_actions (id,task_id,request_id,body_hash,kind,input_version,input_step_id,body,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'pending',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(requestId),Some(digest),Some(kind),sqlValue(version),sqlValue(task["step_id"]),Some(event.toString())])
            tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='queued',version=version+1,pending_action=?,active_request=?,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,exit_reason='',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(event.toString()),Some(requestId),Some(id)])
            return taskPublic(taskRow(tx,id,owner),accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/local_users.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.env.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

// Local deployment command only. Never registered as an HTTP handler.
func createLocalUser(body: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let email=emailField(body)
    let role=textField(body,"role",30)
    if (role!="admin") { throw ApiError(422,"本地维护命令只创建管理员账号；学生请使用注册页面") }
    let name=textField(body,"name",100)
    let flag=body.get("check_only").getOrDefault({ => JsonBool(false) })
    if (!(flag is JsonBool)) { throw ApiError(422,"check_only 必须是布尔值") }
    let checkOnly=(flag as JsonBool).getOrThrow().getValue()
    let hash=if (checkOnly) { "" } else {
        let password=textField(body,"password",256,trim:false)
        var length: Int64=0; for (_ in password.runes()) { length++ }
        if (length<12 || password.contains("\n") || password.contains("\r")) { throw ApiError(422,"角色账号密码至少12个字符") }
        hashPassword(password)
    }
    let database=getVariable("CANGJIE_DB_NAME") ?? ""
    if (database.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"请显式设置 CANGJIE_DB_NAME，命令不会选择默认数据库") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version='009_task_sources'").rows.size!=1) { throw ApiError(409,"请先完成仓颉数据库升级，命令不会自动初始化或升级") }
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("database",JsonString(database)); result.put("email",JsonString(email)); result.put("role",JsonString(role))
        if (checkOnly) {
            let rows=db.query("SELECT role FROM users WHERE email=?",[Some(email)]).rows
            result.put("available",JsonBool(rows.isEmpty()))
            if (!rows.isEmpty()) { result.put("existing_role",JsonString(rowText(rows[0],"role"))) }
            result.put("created",JsonBool(false)); return result
        }
        let id=newId()
        try {
            db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
                tx.query("INSERT INTO users (id,email,name,role,password_hash,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(id),Some(email),Some(name),Some(role),Some(hash)])
                ()
            })
        } catch (error: DatabaseException) {
            if (error.code==1062) { throw ApiError(409,"邮箱已存在；未修改身份、密码或学习记录，请使用专用的新邮箱") }
            throw error
        }
        result.put("id",JsonString(id)); result.put("created",JsonBool(true)); return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

func localUserCommand(): Int64 {
    try {
        let raw=getStdIn().readln() ?? ""
        if (raw.size>8192 || raw.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"请输入一行有效的账号配置") }
        let body: JsonObject
        try { body=(JsonValue.fromStr(raw) as JsonObject).getOrThrow() }
        catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"请输入有效的账号配置JSON对象") }
        println(createLocalUser(body).toString()); return 0
    } catch (error: ApiError) {
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("code",JsonInt(Int64(error.status))); result.put("message",JsonString(error.message)); println(result.toString()); return 1
    } catch (_: Exception) {
        // Parser/DB exceptions may contain user input or operational detail.
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("code",JsonInt(503)); result.put("message",JsonString("账号未创建，请检查仓颉运行环境、独立数据库和当前版本")); println(result.toString()); return 1
    }
}
```

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.env.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.net.http.*
import stdx.log.*

main(args: Array<String>): Int64 {
    if (args.size == 1 && args[0] == "--question-bank") { return questionBankCommand() }
    if (args.size == 1 && args[0] == "--create-local-user") { return localUserCommand() }
    if (args.size == 1 && args[0] == "--init-db") {
        initializeDatabase()
        println("仓颉独立数据库初始化完成")
        return 0
    }
    if (args.size == 1 && args[0] == "--upgrade-db") { upgradeDatabase(); println("仓颉数据库升级完成"); return 0 }
    if (args.size == 1 && args[0] == "--seed-knowledge") { seedPublicKnowledge(); println("公开知识卡初始化完成"); return 0 }
    if (!args.isEmpty()) { throw IllegalArgumentException("未知启动参数") }
    let port = UInt16.parse(getVariable("CANGJIE_BACKEND_PORT") ?? "8002")
    let server = ServerBuilder().addr("127.0.0.1").port(port).maxRequestBodySize(16 * 1024 * 1024).distributor(ApiDistributor()).build()
    server.logger.level = LogLevel.OFF
    server.afterBind({ => recoverRecognitionJobs(); spawn { learningTaskWorker() }; spawn { learningTaskWorker() }; () })
    let accounts = Accounts(getVariable("CANGJIE_AUTH_SECRET") ?? "")
    accounts.routes(server)
    server.distributor.register("/api/v1/learning-tasks", { ctx => guarded(ctx, { => learningTasks(ctx,accounts) }, successStatus:if (ctx.request.method=="POST") { 202 } else { 200 }) })
    server.distributor.register("/api/v1/learning-tasks/{task_id}", { ctx => guarded(ctx, { => readLearningTask(ctx,accounts) }) })

    server.distributor.register("/api/v1/questions/{question_id}", { ctx => guarded(ctx, { => questionRoute(ctx, accounts) }) })
    // Liveness deliberately makes no claim about database or AI readiness.
    server.distributor.register("/livez", { context =>
        if (context.request.method != "GET") {
            context.responseBuilder.status(405).header("Allow", "GET").body("")
        } else {
            context.responseBuilder.header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
                .body(##"{"service":"zhiwei-cangjie","status":"alive","version":"0.1.0"}"##)
        }
        ()
    })
    server.distributor.register("/readyz", { context =>
        context.responseBuilder.header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
        if (context.request.method != "GET") {
            context.responseBuilder.status(405).header("Allow", "GET").body("")
        } else {
            try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
                db.query("SELECT id FROM users LIMIT 1")
                let version = db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = ?", [Some("010_study_sessions")])
                if (version.rows.size != 1) { throw DatabaseException(2000) }
                context.responseBuilder.body(##"{"database":"mysql","schema":"010_study_sessions","status":"database_ready","ai":"not_connected"}"##)
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                context.responseBuilder.status(503).body(##"{"status":"database_unavailable"}"##)
            }
        }
        ()
    })
    println("错题帮仓颉独立后端 http://127.0.0.1:${port}")
    server.serve()
    return 0
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/manual_questions.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.net.http.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

func runeCount(value: String): Int64 {
    var count: Int64 = 0
    for (_ in value.runes()) { count++ }
    return count
}

func optionalAnswer(body: JsonObject): String {
    if (let Some(value) <- body.get("student_answer")) {
        if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) {
            if (runeCount(text.getValue()) <= 6000) { return stripWhitespace(text.getValue()) }
        }
        throw ApiError(422, "作答必须为不超过 6000 字的文字")
    }
    return ""
}

func manualArray(body: JsonObject, key: String, limit: Int64): JsonArray {
    if (let Some(value) <- body.get(key)) {
        if (let Some(array) <- (value as JsonArray)) {
            if (array.getItems().size <= limit) { return array }
        }
        throw ApiError(422, "${key} 格式或数量不正确")
    }
    return JsonArray()
}

public func createManualQuestion(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "POST")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        if (rowText(user, "role") != "student") { throw ApiError(403, "请使用学生账号记录错题") }
        let body = readJson(context, maxBytes: 8 * 1024 * 1024)
        let source = studyMistakeSource(db,rowText(user,"id"),body)
        let text = textField(body, "question_text", 12000)
        let subject = textField(body, "subject", 30, fallback: Some("数学"))
        let kind = textField(body, "question_type", 50, fallback: Some("综合题"))
        if (kind != "综合题" && kind != "单选题" && kind != "多选题") { throw ApiError(422, "题目类型不正确") }
        var grade: Option<String> = None
        if (let Some(value) <- body.get("grade")) {
            if (let Some(item) <- (value as JsonString)) {
                if (runeCount(item.getValue()) > 30) { throw ApiError(422, "年级过长") }
                grade = Some(item.getValue())
            } else if (!(value is JsonNull)) { throw ApiError(422, "年级格式不正确") }
        }
        let answer = optionalAnswer(body)
        let options = manualArray(body, "options", 8)
        if ((kind == "综合题" && !options.getItems().isEmpty()) ||
            (kind != "综合题" && options.getItems().size < 2)) {
            throw ApiError(422, "题目类型和选项数量不匹配")
        }
        let labels = HashSet<String>()
        let validatedOptions = JsonArray()
        for (item in options.getItems()) {
            if (let Some(option) <- (item as JsonObject)) {
                let label = textField(option, "label", 1, trim: false)
                if (!"ABCDEFGH".contains(label) || labels.contains(label)) { throw ApiError(422, "选项标记无效或重复") }
                labels.add(label)
                let clean = JsonObject()
                clean.put("label", JsonString(label))
                clean.put("text", JsonString(textField(option, "text", 12000)))
                validatedOptions.getItems().add(clean)
            } else { throw ApiError(422, "选项格式不正确") }
        }
        let steps = ArrayList<String>()
        for (item in manualArray(body, "student_steps", 30).getItems()) {
            if (let Some(value) <- (item as JsonString)) {
                let clean = stripWhitespace(value.getValue())
                if (clean.isEmpty() || runeCount(clean) > 12000) { throw ApiError(422, "步骤文字长度不正确") }
                steps.add(clean)
            } else { throw ApiError(422, "步骤必须为文字") }
        }
        let title = StringBuilder()
        var count: Int64 = 0
        for (rune in text.runes()) {
            if (count >= 80) { break }
            title.append(rune)
            count++
        }
        let id = newId()
        let workId = newId()
        let studentGrade = grade
        let copied=ArrayList<String>()
        let savedId=try { db.transaction<String>({ tx =>
            let itemId=optionalText(body,"study_item_id","",36)
            if (!source.getFields().isEmpty()) {
                ownedStudy(tx,optionalText(body,"study_session_id","",36),rowText(user,"id"),lock:true)
                let previous=tx.query("SELECT id FROM questions WHERE user_id=? AND status<>'archived' AND JSON_CONTAINS(tags,JSON_QUOTE(?)) LIMIT 1",[user["id"],Some("study-item:"+itemId)]).rows
                if (!previous.isEmpty()) { return rowText(previous[0],"id") }
            }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO questions (id,user_id,subject,grade,title,question_text,student_answer,status,confirmed,favorite,tags,formulas,options,question_type,knowledge_points,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'confirmed',1,0,JSON_ARRAY(),JSON_ARRAY(),?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                [Some(id), user["id"], Some(subject), studentGrade, Some(title.toString()), Some(text),
                 if (answer.isEmpty()) { None } else { Some(answer) }, Some(validatedOptions.toString()), Some(kind)])
            tx.query("INSERT INTO student_works (id,question_id,user_id,answer,explanation,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,'',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",
                [Some(workId), Some(id), user["id"], Some(answer)])
            for (index in 0..steps.size) {
                let step = steps[index]
                tx.query("INSERT INTO student_steps (id,work_id,step_index,text,bbox,raw_text,normalized_text,formula,confidence,source,user_confirmed,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),?,?,?,1,'user',1,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                    [Some(newId()), Some(workId), Some(index.toString()), Some(step), Some(step), Some(step),
                     Some(if (step.contains("=")) { step } else { "" })])
            }
            if (!source.getFields().isEmpty()) {
                let tags=JsonArray(); tags.add(JsonString("study-item:"+itemId))
                tx.query("UPDATE questions SET tags=?,knowledge_points=? WHERE id=?",[Some(tags.toString()),Some(tutorArray(source,"knowledge_points").toString()),Some(id)])
                copyStudyFigures(tx,id,source,copied)
            }
            return id
        }) } catch (error: Exception) {
            for (path in copied) { try { removeMedia(path) } catch (_: Exception) {} }
            throw error
        }
        let result = JsonObject()
        result.put("id", JsonString(savedId))
        result.put("status", JsonString("confirmed"))
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/media.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.convert.*
import std.env.*
import std.io.*
import stdx.encoding.hex.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

foreign {
    func zw_media_open(root: CString, path: CString): Int32
    func zw_media_stat(fd: Int32, field: Int32): Int64
    func zw_media_read(fd: Int32, output: CPointer<UInt8>, capacity: UInt64, offset: Int64): Int64
    func zw_media_close(fd: Int32): Unit
}

// SDK 1.0.5 checks a known empty body's size against Content-Length even for HEAD.
// An unknown-length empty stream lets HEAD advertise the actual resource length.
class HeadOnlyBody <: InputStream {
    public func read(_: Array<UInt8>): Int64 { 0 }
}

class MediaFile <: Resource {
    var descriptor: Int32 = -1
    public let length: Int64
    public let etag: String
    init(root: String, path: String) {
        if (root.contains("\u{0}") || path.contains("\u{0}") || path.contains("\\")) { throw ApiError(404, "资源不存在") }
        try (nativeRoot = NativeString(root), nativePath = NativeString(path)) {
            descriptor = unsafe { zw_media_open(nativeRoot.value, nativePath.value) }
        }
        if (descriptor < 0) { throw ApiError(404, "资源不存在") }
        length = unsafe { zw_media_stat(descriptor, 0) }
        let modified = unsafe { zw_media_stat(descriptor, 1) }
        let inode = unsafe { zw_media_stat(descriptor, 2) }
        if (length < 0) { unsafe { zw_media_close(descriptor) }; descriptor = -1; throw ApiError(404, "资源不存在") }
        etag = try { "\"" + toHexString(hmac256("media-etag-v1", "${path}:${length}:${modified}:${inode}")).toAsciiLower() + "\"" }
            catch (error: Exception) { unsafe { zw_media_close(descriptor) }; descriptor = -1; throw error }
    }
    public func isClosed(): Bool { descriptor < 0 }
    public func close(): Unit {
        if (descriptor >= 0) { unsafe { zw_media_close(descriptor) }; descriptor = -1 }
    }
    public func readAt(buffer: Array<UInt8>, offset: Int64): Int64 {
        if (isClosed()) { throw IllegalStateException("文件已关闭") }
        unsafe {
            let raw = acquireArrayRawData(buffer)
            try { return zw_media_read(descriptor, raw.pointer, UInt64(buffer.size), offset) }
            finally { releaseArrayRawData(raw) }
        }
    }
}

func mediaContentType(path: String): String {
    let name = path.toAsciiLower()
    for ((suffix, kind) in [(".png", "image/png"), (".jpg", "image/jpeg"), (".jpeg", "image/jpeg"),
        (".webp", "image/webp"), (".bmp", "image/bmp"), (".tif", "image/tiff"), (".gif", "image/gif"), (".svg", "image/svg+xml"),
        (".mp3", "audio/mpeg"), (".wav", "audio/wav"), (".webm", "audio/webm"), (".m4a", "audio/mp4"), (".ogg", "audio/ogg"), (".mp4", "video/mp4"),
        (".pdf", "application/pdf"), (".docx", "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document"),
        (".zip", "application/zip"), (".json", "application/json"), (".txt", "text/plain; charset=utf-8")]) {
        if (name.endsWith(suffix)) { return kind }
    }
    return "application/octet-stream"
}

func mediaRange(header: String, length: Int64): (Int64, Int64) {
    try {
        if (!header.startsWith("bytes=")) { throw ApiError(416, "范围无效") }
        let parts = header[6..].split("-", 2)
        if (parts.size != 2 || length == 0) { throw ApiError(416, "范围无效") }
        if (parts[0].isEmpty()) {
            let suffix = Int64.parse(parts[1])
            if (suffix <= 0) { throw ApiError(416, "范围无效") }
            let count = if (suffix > length) { length } else { suffix }
            return (length - count, count)
        }
        let start = Int64.parse(parts[0])
        let requestedEnd = if (parts[1].isEmpty()) { length - 1 } else { Int64.parse(parts[1]) }
        if (start < 0 || start >= length || requestedEnd < start) { throw ApiError(416, "范围无效") }
        let end = if (requestedEnd >= length) { length - 1 } else { requestedEnd }
        return (start, end - start + 1)
    } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(416, "范围无效") }
}

public func serveMedia(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, storageRoot!: Option<String> = None, storagePath!: Option<String> = None): Unit {
    var started = false
    try {
        let method = context.request.method
        if (method != "GET" && method != "HEAD") {
            context.responseBuilder.header("Allow", "GET, HEAD")
            throw ApiError(405, "请求方法不支持")
        }
        if (!accounts.validMedia(context)) { throw ApiError(403, "资源链接已失效，请返回页面刷新") }
        let path = storagePath ?? context.request.url.path[1..]
        let root = storageRoot ?? (getVariable("CANGJIE_MEDIA_ROOT") ?? "media")
        try (file = MediaFile(root, path)) {
            context.responseBuilder.header("ETag", file.etag).header("Accept-Ranges", "bytes")
                .header("Cache-Control", "private, max-age=0, must-revalidate")
                .header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
                .header("Content-Type", mediaContentType(path))
            for (tag in (context.request.headers.getFirst("If-None-Match") ?? "").split(",")) {
                let clean = tag.trimAscii()
                if (clean == "*" || clean == file.etag || clean == "W/" + file.etag) {
                    context.responseBuilder.status(304).body("")
                    return
                }
            }
            if (method == "HEAD") {
                context.responseBuilder.header("Content-Length", file.length.toString()).body(HeadOnlyBody())
                return
            }
            var offset: Int64 = 0
            var remaining = file.length
            let range = context.request.headers.getFirst("Range") ?? ""
            let ifRange = context.request.headers.getFirst("If-Range") ?? file.etag
            // A server may ignore multipart ranges; respond with the full resource.
            if (!range.isEmpty() && !range.contains(",") && ifRange == file.etag) {
                try {
                    let (start, count) = mediaRange(range, file.length)
                    offset = start
                    remaining = count
                    context.responseBuilder.status(206).header("Content-Range", "bytes ${start}-${start + count - 1}/${file.length}")
                } catch (error: ApiError) {
                    context.responseBuilder.header("Content-Range", "bytes */${file.length}")
                    throw error
                }
            }
            context.responseBuilder.header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
            let writer = HttpResponseWriter(context)
            while (remaining > 0) {
                let capacity = if (remaining > 65536) { 65536 } else { remaining }
                let buffer = Array<UInt8>(capacity, repeat: 0)
                let count = file.readAt(buffer, offset)
                if (count <= 0) { throw IllegalStateException("文件读取中断") }
                started = true
                writer.write(buffer[..count])
                offset += count
                remaining -= count
            }
        }
    } catch (error: ApiError) {
        if (started) { throw error }
        let result = JsonObject(); result.put("detail", JsonString(error.message))
        jsonResponse(context, result, status: error.status)
    } catch (error: Exception) {
        if (started) { throw error }
        let result = JsonObject(); result.put("detail", JsonString("资源暂时不可用"))
        jsonResponse(context, result, status: 500)
    }
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/media_storage.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.env.*
import stdx.encoding.hex.*
import stdx.encoding.base64.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

foreign {
    func zw_sha256(data: CPointer<UInt8>, size: UInt64, output: CPointer<UInt8>): Int32
    func zw_media_write(root: CString, path: CString, data: CPointer<UInt8>, size: UInt64): Int32
    func zw_media_remove(root: CString, path: CString): Int32
}

func mediaRoot(): String { getVariable("CANGJIE_MEDIA_ROOT") ?? "media" }

public func sha256(data: Array<UInt8>): String {
    let output = Array<UInt8>(32, repeat: 0)
    unsafe {
        let source = acquireArrayRawData(data)
        let target = acquireArrayRawData(output)
        try {
            if (zw_sha256(source.pointer, UInt64(data.size), target.pointer) != 1) { throw IllegalStateException("摘要计算失败") }
        } finally { releaseArrayRawData(source); releaseArrayRawData(target) }
    }
    return toHexString(output).toAsciiLower()
}

func validStoragePath(path: String): Unit {
    if (!(path.startsWith("uploads/") || path.startsWith("reports/")) || path.contains("\u{0}") || path.contains("\\") || mediaRoot().contains("\u{0}")) {
        throw ApiError(422, "资源路径无效")
    }
    for (part in path.split("/")) { if (part.isEmpty() || part == "." || part == "..") { throw ApiError(422, "资源路径无效") } }
}

public func writeMedia(path: String, data: Array<UInt8>): Unit {
    validStoragePath(path)
    try (root = NativeString(mediaRoot()), name = NativeString(path)) {
        unsafe {
            let source = acquireArrayRawData(data)
            try {
                if (zw_media_write(root.value, name.value, source.pointer, UInt64(data.size)) != 0) { throw ApiError(503, "资源保存失败") }
            } finally { releaseArrayRawData(source) }
        }
    }
}

public func removeMedia(path: String): Unit {
    validStoragePath(path)
    try (root = NativeString(mediaRoot()), name = NativeString(path)) { unsafe { zw_media_remove(root.value, name.value) } }
}

public func readMedia(path: String): Array<UInt8> {
    validStoragePath(path)
    try (file = MediaFile(mediaRoot(), path)) {
        if (file.length <= 0 || file.length > 64 * 1024 * 1024) { throw ApiError(422, "图片文件大小无效") }
        let result = Array<UInt8>(file.length, repeat: 0)
        var offset: Int64 = 0
        while (offset < result.size) {
            let count = file.readAt(result[offset..], offset)
            if (count <= 0) { throw ApiError(503, "图片读取中断") }
            offset += count
        }
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "图片暂时不可用")
}

func algorithmImage(result: JsonObject): Array<UInt8> {
    let data = fromBase64String(jsonText(result, "image_base64")).getOrThrow()
    if (data.isEmpty() || data.size > 64 * 1024 * 1024 || sha256(data) != jsonText(result, "sha256")) {
        throw ApiError(503, "算法图片校验失败")
    }
    return data
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/multipart.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func headerParameters(value: String): HashMap<String, String> {
    let result = HashMap<String, String>()
    let parts = ArrayList<String>()
    var current = ArrayList<UInt8>()
    var quoted = false
    var escaped = false
    for (byte in value.toArray()) {
        if (escaped) { current.add(byte); escaped = false }
        else if (quoted && byte == 92) { escaped = true }
        else if (byte == 34) { quoted = !quoted }
        else if (!quoted && byte == 59) { parts.add(String.fromUtf8(current.toArray()).trimAscii()); current = ArrayList<UInt8>() }
        else { current.add(byte) }
    }
    if (quoted || escaped) { throw ApiError(422, "上传头格式不正确") }
    parts.add(String.fromUtf8(current.toArray()).trimAscii())
    result.add("", parts[0].toAsciiLower())
    for (index in 1..parts.size) {
        let pair = parts[index].split("=", 2)
        if (pair.size == 2) { result.add(pair[0].trimAscii().toAsciiLower(), pair[1].trimAscii()) }
    }
    return result
}

func bytesMatch(data: Array<UInt8>, needle: Array<UInt8>, start: Int64): Bool {
    if (start < 0 || start + needle.size > data.size) { return false }
    for (i in 0..needle.size) { if (data[start + i] != needle[i]) { return false } }
    return true
}

// KMP keeps adversarial, repeated boundary prefixes linear in the upload size.
func findBytes(data: Array<UInt8>, needle: Array<UInt8>, start: Int64): Int64 {
    let prefix = Array<Int64>(needle.size, repeat: 0)
    var matched: Int64 = 0
    for (i in 1..needle.size) {
        while (matched > 0 && needle[i] != needle[matched]) { matched = prefix[matched - 1] }
        if (needle[i] == needle[matched]) { matched++ }
        prefix[i] = matched
    }
    matched = 0
    for (i in start..data.size) {
        while (matched > 0 && data[i] != needle[matched]) { matched = prefix[matched - 1] }
        if (data[i] == needle[matched]) { matched++ }
        if (matched == needle.size) { return i - needle.size + 1 }
    }
    return -1
}

class ImageUpload {
    let bytes: Array<UInt8>
    let filename: String
    let fields: HashMap<String, String>
    let deferred: Bool
    init(bytes: Array<UInt8>, filename: String, deferred: Bool, fields: HashMap<String, String>) { this.fields = fields; this.bytes = bytes; this.filename = filename; this.deferred = deferred }
}

func readImageUpload(context: HttpContext, audio!: Bool=false): ImageUpload {
    let contentType = headerParameters(context.request.headers.getFirst("Content-Type") ?? "")
    if (contentType[""] != "multipart/form-data") { throw ApiError(422, "请选择上传图片") }
    let boundary = contentType.get("boundary") ?? ""
    if (boundary.isEmpty() || boundary.size > 70 || boundary.contains("\r") || boundary.contains("\n")) { throw ApiError(422, "上传分隔符无效") }
    let maxBytes: Int64 = 15 * 1024 * 1024 + 65536
    let buffer = Array<UInt8>(maxBytes + 1, repeat: 0)
    var length: Int64 = 0
    while (true) {
        let count = context.request.body.read(buffer[length..])
        if (count == 0) { break }
        length += count
        if (length > maxBytes) { throw ApiError(413, "图片不能超过 15 MB") }
    }
    let data = buffer[..length]
    let initial = ("--" + boundary + "\r\n").toArray()
    let delimiter = ("\r\n--" + boundary).toArray()
    let newline = "\r\n".toArray()
    let closing = "--".toArray()
    if (!bytesMatch(data, initial, 0)) { throw ApiError(422, "上传内容格式不正确") }
    var offset = initial.size
    var file: Option<Array<UInt8>> = None
    var filename = "上传错题"
    let fields = HashMap<String, String>()
    var deferred = false
    var ended = false
    var partCount: Int64 = 0
    while (!ended) {
        partCount++
        if (partCount > 32) { throw ApiError(422, "上传字段过多") }
        let endHeaders = findBytes(data, "\r\n\r\n".toArray(), offset)
        if (endHeaders < offset || endHeaders - offset > 8192) { throw ApiError(422, "上传头无效") }
        let headers = HashMap<String, String>()
        try {
            for (line in String.fromUtf8(data[offset..endHeaders]).split("\r\n")) {
                let pair = line.split(":", 2)
                if (pair.size != 2) { throw ApiError(422, "上传头无效") }
                headers.add(pair[0].trimAscii().toAsciiLower(), pair[1].trimAscii())
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422, "上传头无效") }
        let disposition = headerParameters(headers.get("content-disposition") ?? "")
        if (disposition[""] != "form-data") { throw ApiError(422, "上传字段无效") }
        let start = endHeaders + 4
        var end = findBytes(data, delimiter, start)
        while (end >= 0 && !bytesMatch(data, newline, end + delimiter.size) && !bytesMatch(data, closing, end + delimiter.size)) {
            end = findBytes(data, delimiter, end + 1)
        }
        if (end < 0) { throw ApiError(422, "上传内容不完整") }
        let value = data[start..end]
        let name = disposition.get("name") ?? ""
        if (name == "file") {
            if (file.isSome() || !disposition.contains("filename")) { throw ApiError(422, "请上传一张图片") }
            if (value.isEmpty()) { if (audio) { throw ApiError(400,"音频文件为空") }; throw ApiError(422, "图片为空，请重新选择图片") }
            if (value.size > 15 * 1024 * 1024) { throw ApiError(413, "图片不能超过 15 MB") }
            file = Some(value)
            filename = disposition["filename"]
            if (filename.isEmpty()) { filename = "上传错题" }
            if (runeCount(filename) > 255) { throw ApiError(422, "文件名称过长") }
        } else {
            if (value.size > 4096) { throw ApiError(422, "上传字段过长") }
            try { fields.add(name, String.fromUtf8(value)) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"上传字段无效") }
            if (name == "defer_processing") {
                try {
                    let text = String.fromUtf8(value).toAsciiLower()
                    if (text == "true" || text == "1" || text == "on" || text == "yes" || text == "t" || text == "y") { deferred = true }
                    else if (text == "false" || text == "0" || text == "off" || text == "no" || text == "f" || text == "n" || text.isEmpty()) { deferred = false }
                    else { throw ApiError(422, "处理方式无效") }
                } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422, "处理方式无效") }
            }
        }
        offset = end + delimiter.size
        if (bytesMatch(data, closing, offset)) { ended = true }
        else { offset += 2 }
    }
    if (let Some(bytes) <- file) { return ImageUpload(bytes, filename, deferred, fields) }
    throw ApiError(422, "缺少图片")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/ocr_edits.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func stepObject(item: JsonValue, teacher: Bool): JsonObject {
    if (let Some(object) <- (item as JsonObject)) { return object }
    let result = JsonObject()
    result.put(if (teacher) { "comment" } else { "text" }, item)
    return result
}

func editedBbox(object: JsonObject): JsonArray {
    let result = JsonArray()
    let source = (object.get("bbox") ?? JsonArray()) as JsonArray
    for (value in source.getOrThrow().getItems()) { result.getItems().add(JsonFloat(editNumber(value))) }
    return result
}

func stepText(item: JsonValue): String {
    let object = stepObject(item, false)
    return ((object.get("text") ?? object.get("raw_text") ?? JsonString("")) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
}

func choiceStepAnswer(steps: JsonArray, options: JsonValue): Option<String> {
    let texts = ArrayList<String>()
    for (item in steps.getItems()) {
        let text = stripWhitespace(stepText(item))
        if (!text.isEmpty()) { texts.add(text.toAsciiUpper()) }
    }
    if (texts.size != 1) { return None }
    let known = HashSet<String>()
    if (let Some(array) <- (options as JsonArray)) {
        for (item in array.getItems()) {
            if (let Some(object) <- (item as JsonObject)) {
                let value = object.get("label") ?? object.get("key") ?? JsonString("")
                if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) { known.add(text.getValue().toAsciiUpper()) }
            }
        }
    }
    let labels = ArrayList<String>()
    var expectLabel = true
    for (rune in texts[0].runes()) {
        let builder = StringBuilder(); builder.append(rune); let one = builder.toString()
        if (pythonWhitespace.contains(one)) { continue }
        if (expectLabel) {
            if (!"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ".contains(one) || (!known.isEmpty() && !known.contains(one))) { return None }
            if (!labels.contains(one)) { labels.add(one) }
        } else if (!(one == "、" || one == "," || one == "，")) { return None }
        expectLabel = !expectLabel
    }
    if (expectLabel || labels.isEmpty()) { return None }
    return Some(String.join(labels.toArray(), delimiter: "、"))
}

public func saveOcrEdits(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, confirm: Bool): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let body = readJson(context, maxBytes: 8 * 1024 * 1024, defaultEmpty: true)
        let validated = validateQuestionEdit(body)
        db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "题目不存在或无权访问") }
            if (rowText(rows[0], "status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409, "题目已归档，不能修改") }
            let existing = rowJson("questions", rows[0])
            let edits = JsonObject()
            for (key in ["question_text","subject","formulas","options","question_type","knowledge_points"]) {
                if (let Some(value) <- validated.get(key)) { edits.put(key, value) }
            }
            let steps = manualArray(body, "student_steps", 100)
            var answer = ""
            if (let Some(value) <- validated.get("student_answer")) {
                if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) { answer = stripWhitespace(text.getValue()) }
            }
            let typeName = ((edits.get("question_type") ?? existing["question_type"]) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
            var normalizedSteps = steps
            if (answer.isEmpty() && (typeName.contains("选择") || typeName.toAsciiLower().contains("choice") || typeName.toAsciiLower().contains("multiple"))) {
                if (let Some(inferred) <- choiceStepAnswer(steps, edits.get("options") ?? existing["options"])) {
                    answer = inferred; normalizedSteps = JsonArray()
                }
            }
            if (validated.get("student_answer").isSome()) { edits.put("student_answer", if (answer.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(answer) }) }
            if (let Some(value) <- validated.get("grade")) {
                edits.put("grade", if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) { if (text.getValue().isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(stripWhitespace(text.getValue())) } } else { JsonNull() })
            }
            var changed = false
            for ((key,value) in edits.getFields()) { if (!jsonEquivalent(existing[key],value)) { changed = true } }
            if (body.get("student_steps").isSome()) {
                let previous = tx.query("SELECT s.text FROM student_steps s WHERE s.work_id = (SELECT id FROM student_works WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1) ORDER BY s.step_index", [Some(id)]).rows
                if (previous.size != normalizedSteps.getItems().size) { changed = true }
                else { for (index in 0..previous.size) { if (rowText(previous[index],"text") != stepText(normalizedSteps[index])) { changed = true } } }
                let work = newId()
                let currentAnswer = edits.get("student_answer") ?? existing["student_answer"]
                tx.query("INSERT INTO student_works (id,question_id,user_id,answer,explanation,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,'',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))", [Some(work),Some(id),user["id"],Some(if (let Some(text) <- (currentAnswer as JsonString)) { text.getValue() } else { "" })])
                for (index in 0..normalizedSteps.getItems().size) {
                    let object = stepObject(normalizedSteps[index],false)
                    let text = stepText(object)
                    let raw = ((object.get("raw_text") ?? JsonString(text)) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                    let bbox = editedBbox(object)
                    let confidence = editNumber(object.get("confidence") ?? JsonInt(1))
                    let source = ((object.get("source") ?? JsonString("user")) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                    tx.query("INSERT INTO student_steps (id,work_id,step_index,text,bbox,raw_text,normalized_text,formula,confidence,source,user_confirmed,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))", [Some(newId()),Some(work),Some(index.toString()),Some(text),Some(bbox.toString()),Some(raw),Some(collapseWhitespace(text)),Some(if (text.contains("=")) { text } else { "" }),Some(confidence.toString()),Some(source)])
                }
            }
            if (body.get("teacher_marks").isSome()) {
                tx.query("DELETE FROM teacher_marks WHERE question_id = ?", [Some(id)])
                for (item in manualArray(body,"teacher_marks",100).getItems()) {
                    let object = stepObject(item,true)
                    tx.query("INSERT INTO teacher_marks (id,question_id,mark_type,comment,bbox,confidence,source,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,'user',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(((object.get("mark_type") ?? JsonString("comment")) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()),Some(((object.get("comment") ?? JsonString("")) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()),Some(editedBbox(object).toString()),Some(editNumber(object.get("confidence") ?? JsonInt(1)).toString())])
                }
            }
            let text = ((edits.get("question_text") ?? existing["question_text"]) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
            if (confirm && stripWhitespace(text).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422, "请先补充题目文字再确认") }
            tx.query("UPDATE processing_jobs SET status = 'cancelled',error = '题目已人工核对，旧识别结果未应用',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE question_id = ? AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') AND status IN ('pending','running')", [Some(id)])
            if (changed) { invalidateQuestionLearning(tx,id) }
            let sql = StringBuilder(); let params = ArrayList<Option<String>>()
            sql.append("UPDATE questions SET updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP(),status = ?")
            params.add(Some(if (confirm) { "confirmed" } else { "ocr_draft" }))
            if (confirm) { sql.append(",confirmed = 1") }
            for ((key,value) in edits.getFields()) {
                sql.append(", `${key}` = ?")
                params.add(if (value is JsonNull) { None } else if (let Some(textValue) <- (value as JsonString)) { Some(textValue.getValue()) } else { Some(value.toString()) })
            }
            sql.append(" WHERE id = ?"); params.add(Some(id)); tx.query(sql.toString(),params.toArray())
            ()
        })
        return questionDetail(context,accounts,id)
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/paper_candidates.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.encoding.url.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func paperCandidatesData(db: Database, owner: String, filters: JsonObject): JsonArray {
    let result=JsonArray(); let seen=HashSet<String>(); let params=ArrayList<Option<String>>([Some(owner)])
    var sql="SELECT * FROM questions WHERE user_id=? AND status<>'archived'"
    let subject=tutorText(filters,"subject")
    if (!subject.isEmpty()) { sql+=" AND subject=?"; params.add(Some(subject)) }
    sql+=" ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC"
    for (row in db.query(sql,params.toArray()).rows) {
        let question=rowJson("questions",row); let id=jsonText(question,"id"); let key=questionDisplayKey(jsonText(question,"question_text"),id)
        if (seen.contains(key)) { continue }; seen.add(key)
        let analyses=db.query("SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 1",[Some(id)]).rows
        let knowledge=if (!tutorArray(question,"knowledge_points").getItems().isEmpty()) { tutorArray(question,"knowledge_points") } else if (analyses.isEmpty()) { JsonArray() } else { tutorArray(rowJson("problem_analyses",analyses[0]),"knowledge_points") }
        let diagnoses=db.query("SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(id)]).rows
        let diagnosis=if (diagnoses.isEmpty()) { JsonObject() } else { rowJson("diagnoses",diagnoses[0]) }
        let knowledgeFilter=tutorText(filters,"knowledge_point"); var matched=knowledgeFilter.isEmpty()
        for (point in knowledge.getItems()) { if (tutorAnswerText(point).contains(knowledgeFilter)) { matched=true } }
        if (!matched) { continue }
        let errorFilter=tutorText(filters,"error_type")
        if (!errorFilter.isEmpty() && !tutorText(diagnosis,"error_type").contains(errorFilter)) { continue }
        var score=0.0
        for (masteryRow in db.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? AND subject=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC",[Some(owner),row["subject"]]).rows) {
            let point=rowText(masteryRow,"knowledge_point"); var matchPoint=false
            for (value in knowledge.getItems()) { if (tutorAnswerText(value)==point) { matchPoint=true } }
            if (matchPoint) { score=editNumber(rowJson("mastery_states",masteryRow)["mastery_score"]); break }
        }
        if (score<editNumber(filters.get("mastery_min") ?? JsonInt(0)) || score>editNumber(filters.get("mastery_max") ?? JsonInt(1))) { continue }
        let reviews=db.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? AND question_id=? ORDER BY due_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(id)]).rows
        if (tutorFlag(filters,"due_review") && (reviews.isEmpty() || rowText(reviews[0],"status")!="pending")) { continue }
        question.put("knowledge_points",knowledge); question.put("mastery_score",JsonFloat(score))
        question.put("error_type",JsonString(if (diagnoses.isEmpty()) { "未诊断" } else { tutorText(diagnosis,"error_type") })); question.put("root_misconception",JsonString(tutorText(diagnosis,"root_misconception")))
        question.put("review_due_at",if (reviews.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { rowJson("review_plans",reviews[0])["due_at"] }); result.add(question)
    }
    return result
}

func paperCandidates(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let form=Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? ""); let raw=JsonObject()
    for (key in ["subject","knowledge_point","error_type"]) { let value=queryLast(form,key); if (!value.isEmpty()) { raw.put(key,JsonString(value)) } }
    for (key in ["mastery_min","mastery_max"]) {
        let value=queryLast(form,key)
        if (!value.isEmpty()) { try { raw.put(key,JsonValue.fromStr(value)) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"掌握度范围无效") } }
    }
    let due=queryLast(form,"due_review"); if (!due.isEmpty()) { raw.put("due_review",JsonString(due)) }
    let filters=paperFilters(raw)
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) { let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); return paperCandidatesData(db,rowText(user,"id"),filters) }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/paper_exports.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.base64.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.encoding.url.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func paperAnalysisReady(db: Database, questionId: String): Bool {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT id,final_answer FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(questionId)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty() || stripWhitespace(rowText(rows[0],"final_answer")).isEmpty()) { return false }
    for (step in db.query("SELECT text FROM reference_steps WHERE analysis_id=? ORDER BY step_index,id",[Some(rowText(rows[0],"id"))]).rows) {
        if (!stripWhitespace(rowText(step,"text")).isEmpty()) { return true }
    }
    return false
}

func exportQuestions(db: Database, paper: JsonObject, includeImages: Bool): JsonArray {
    let result=JsonArray()
    for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM paper_items WHERE paper_id=? ORDER BY position,id",[sqlValue(paper["id"])]).rows) {
        let question=paperQuestion(db,rowText(row,"question_id"),jsonText(paper,"user_id")); let id=jsonText(question,"id")
        let analyses=db.query("SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(id)]).rows
        let diagnoses=db.query("SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(id)]).rows
        let analysis=if (analyses.isEmpty()) { JsonObject() } else { rowJson("problem_analyses",analyses[0]) }
        let item=JsonObject(); item.put("question_text",question["question_text"]); item.put("answer",JsonString(tutorText(analysis,"final_answer")))
        let explanation=StringBuilder()
        if (!analyses.isEmpty()) {
            for (text in tutorRowTexts(queryArray(db,"reference_steps","SELECT * FROM reference_steps WHERE analysis_id=? ORDER BY step_index",jsonText(analysis,"id")))) { if (!explanation.toString().isEmpty()) { explanation.append("\n") }; explanation.append(text) }
        }
        item.put("explanation",JsonString(explanation.toString())); item.put("subject",question["subject"]); item.put("score",rowJson("paper_items",row)["score"])
        let formula=StringBuilder(); for (value in tutorArray(question,"formulas").getItems()) { if (!formula.toString().isEmpty()) { formula.append("；") }; formula.append(tutorAnswerText(value)) }
        item.put("formula",JsonString(formula.toString())); item.put("options",question["options"]); item.put("misconception",JsonString(if (diagnoses.isEmpty()) { "" } else { rowText(diagnoses[0],"root_misconception") }))
        let images=JsonArray()
        if (includeImages) {
            for (figure in db.query("SELECT path FROM question_assets WHERE question_id=? AND kind='question_figure' ORDER BY created_at,id",[Some(id)]).rows) {
                try { images.add(JsonString(toBase64String(readMedia(rowText(figure,"path"))))) }
                catch (error: ApiError) { if (error.status!=404) { throw error } }
            }
        }
        item.put("figure_images",images); result.add(item)
    }
    return result
}

func exportPaper(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=readJson(context,defaultEmpty:true); let kind=if (body.get("kind").isNone()) { "question" } else { tutorText(body,"kind") }
        if (kind!="question" && kind!="answer" && kind!="excel") { throw ApiError(422,"不支持的导出格式") }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let paper=ownedPaper(tx,id,rowText(user,"id")); let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("render_paper")); input.put("paper_title",paper["title"]); input.put("paper_kind",JsonString(kind)); input.put("paper_questions",exportQuestions(tx,paper,kind!="excel"))
            if (kind=="answer") {
                var index: Int64=0
                for (value in tutorArray(input,"paper_questions").getItems()) {
                    index++; let item=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                    if (stripWhitespace(tutorText(item,"answer")).isEmpty() || stripWhitespace(tutorText(item,"explanation")).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(409,"第${index}题缺少答案或解析，请先补齐分析后再导出答案 PDF") }
                }
            }
            let rendered=algorithmCompute(input); let bytes=fromBase64String(jsonText(rendered,"file_base64")).getOrThrow()
            if (bytes.isEmpty() || bytes.size>64*1024*1024 || sha256(bytes)!=jsonText(rendered,"sha256")) { throw ApiError(503,"导出文件校验失败") }
            let jobId=newId(); let suffix=if (kind=="excel") { "xlsx" } else { "pdf" }; let path="reports/${id}-${kind}-${jobId}.${suffix}"
            writeMedia(path,bytes)
            try {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO export_jobs (id,paper_id,kind,status,path,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,'completed',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(jobId),Some(id),Some(kind),Some(path)])
                let result=rowJson("export_jobs",tx.query("SELECT * FROM export_jobs WHERE id=?",[Some(jobId)]).rows[0]); result.put("url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+path))); return result
            } catch (error: Exception) { try { removeMedia(path) } catch (_: Exception) {}; throw error }
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

func downloadPaper(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): Unit {
    var started=false
    try {
        requireMethod(context,"GET"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
        let form=Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? ""); let requested=queryLast(form,"kind"); let kind=if (requested.isEmpty()) { "question" } else { requested }
        try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); ownedPaper(db,id,rowText(user,"id"))
            let jobs=db.query("SELECT * FROM export_jobs WHERE paper_id=? AND kind=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(id),Some(kind)]).rows
            if (jobs.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"请先生成导出文件") }
            let path=rowText(jobs[0],"path"); let data=readMedia(path); let suffix=if (kind=="excel") { "xlsx" } else { "pdf" }
            context.responseBuilder.header("Content-Type",if (kind=="excel") { "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet" } else { "application/pdf" })
                .header("Content-Disposition","attachment; filename=\"${id}-${kind}.${suffix}\"").header("Content-Length",data.size.toString())
            started=true; HttpResponseWriter(context).write(data)
        }
    } catch (error: ApiError) {
        if (started) { throw error }; let body=JsonObject(); body.put("detail",JsonString(error.message)); jsonResponse(context,body,status:error.status)
    } catch (error: Exception) {
        if (started) { throw error }; let body=JsonObject(); body.put("detail",JsonString("导出文件暂时不可用")); jsonResponse(context,body,status:500)
    }
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/paper_validation.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*

func paperInteger(value: JsonValue, minimum: Int64, maximum: Int64): Int64 {
    let number=editNumber(value)
    if (number<Float64(minimum) || number>Float64(maximum)) { throw ApiError(422,"整数超出允许范围") }
    let integer=Int64(number)
    if (number!=Float64(integer) || integer<minimum || integer>maximum) { throw ApiError(422,"整数超出允许范围") }
    return integer
}

func paperFilters(value: JsonValue): JsonObject {
    if (!(value is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(422,"筛选条件格式不正确") }
    let source=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let result=JsonObject()
    for ((name,limit) in [("subject",30),("knowledge_point",100),("error_type",100)]) {
        if (let Some(item) <- source.get(name)) { result.put(name,editText(item,limit)) }
    }
    let low=editNumber(source.get("mastery_min") ?? JsonInt(0)); let high=editNumber(source.get("mastery_max") ?? JsonInt(1))
    if (low<0.0 || high>1.0 || low>high) { throw ApiError(422,"掌握度范围无效") }
    if (let Some(item) <- source.get("mastery_min")) { result.put("mastery_min",JsonFloat(low)) }
    if (let Some(item) <- source.get("mastery_max")) { result.put("mastery_max",JsonFloat(high)) }
    if (let Some(item) <- source.get("due_review")) { result.put("due_review",editBool(item)) }
    return result
}

func paperItems(value: JsonValue): JsonArray {
    if (!(value is JsonArray)) { throw ApiError(422,"试卷题目格式不正确") }
    let result=JsonArray(); let values=(value as JsonArray).getOrThrow()
    if (values.getItems().size>200) { throw ApiError(422,"试卷不能超过 200 题") }
    for (raw in values.getItems()) {
        if (!(raw is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(422,"试卷题目格式不正确") }
        let item=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let clean=JsonObject()
        clean.put("question_id",editText(item.get("question_id") ?? JsonNull(),36,minimum:1,trim:true))
        clean.put("score",JsonInt(paperInteger(item.get("score") ?? JsonInt(10),1,100))); result.add(clean)
    }
    return result
}

func paperPayload(body: JsonObject, creating: Bool): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject()
    if (let Some(title) <- body.get("title")) { result.put("title",editText(title,255,minimum:1,trim:true)) }
    if (let Some(filters) <- body.get("filters")) { result.put("filters",paperFilters(filters)) }
    if (let Some(items) <- body.get("items")) { result.put("items",paperItems(items)) }
    if (creating) {
        result.put("limit",JsonInt(paperInteger(body.get("limit") ?? JsonInt(10),1,200)))
        result.put("default_score",JsonInt(paperInteger(body.get("default_score") ?? JsonInt(10),1,100)))
        let ids=body.get("question_ids") ?? JsonArray()
        if (!(ids is JsonArray)) { throw ApiError(422,"题目编号格式不正确") }
        let clean=JsonArray(); let values=(ids as JsonArray).getOrThrow()
        if (values.getItems().size>200) { throw ApiError(422,"试卷不能超过 200 题") }
        for (value in values.getItems()) { clean.add(editText(value,36,minimum:1,trim:true)) }; result.put("question_ids",clean)
    }
    return result
}
```
