```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func ownedPaper(db: Database, id: String, owner: String): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM papers WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(id),Some(owner)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"试卷不存在或无权访问") }
    return rowJson("papers",rows[0])
}

func paperQuestion(db: Database, id: String, owner: String): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(id),Some(owner)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"题目不存在或无权访问") }
    return rowJson("questions",rows[0])
}

func paperDetail(db: Database, accounts: Accounts, paper: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let id=jsonText(paper,"id"); let items=queryArray(db,"paper_items","SELECT * FROM paper_items WHERE paper_id=? ORDER BY position,id",id)
    for (value in items.getItems()) {
        let item=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); item.put("question",paperQuestion(db,jsonText(item,"question_id"),jsonText(paper,"user_id")))
        item.put("analysis_ready",JsonBool(paperAnalysisReady(db,jsonText(item,"question_id"))))
    }
    let exports=queryArray(db,"export_jobs","SELECT * FROM export_jobs WHERE paper_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC",id)
    for (value in exports.getItems()) { let item=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); item.put("url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+jsonText(item,"path")))) }
    paper.put("items",items); paper.put("exports",exports); return paper
}

func insertPaperItems(db: Database, id: String, owner: String, items: JsonArray): Unit {
    var position: Int64=0
    for (value in items.getItems()) {
        let item=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let questionId=jsonText(item,"question_id"); paperQuestion(db,questionId,owner)
        db.query("INSERT INTO paper_items (id,paper_id,question_id,position,score,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(questionId),Some(position.toString()),sqlValue(item["score"])]); position++
    }
}

func papersApi(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let creating=context.request.method=="POST"; if (!creating) { requireMethod(context,"GET") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        if (!creating) { return queryArray(db,"papers","SELECT * FROM papers WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY created_at,id",rowText(user,"id")) }
        let body=paperPayload(readJson(context),true)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let id=newId(); let filters=tutorObject(body,"filters"); var ids=tutorArray(body,"question_ids")
            if (ids.getItems().isEmpty()) {
                let candidates=paperCandidatesData(tx,rowText(user,"id"),filters); let chosen=JsonArray(); var count: Int64=0
                for (candidate in candidates.getItems()) {
                    if (count>=tutorNumber(body,"limit")) { break }; chosen.add((candidate as JsonObject).getOrThrow()["id"]); count++
                }
                ids=chosen
            }
            let items=JsonArray()
            for (questionId in ids.getItems()) {
                let item=JsonObject(); item.put("question_id",questionId); var score: JsonValue=body["default_score"]
                for (scored in tutorArray(body,"items").getItems()) { let entry=(scored as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); if (entry["question_id"].toString()==questionId.toString()) { score=entry["score"] } }
                item.put("score",score); items.add(item)
            }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO papers (id,user_id,title,filters,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(id),user["id"],Some(if (body.get("title").isSome()) { jsonText(body,"title") } else { "我的错题练习" }),Some(filters.toString())])
            insertPaperItems(tx,id,rowText(user,"id"),items); return ownedPaper(tx,id,rowText(user,"id"))
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

func paperApi(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let method=context.request.method
    if (method!="GET" && method!="PATCH" && method!="DELETE") { throw ApiError(405,"请求方法不支持") }
    let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-1]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=if (method=="PATCH") { paperPayload(readJson(context),false) } else { JsonObject() }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            if (method=="DELETE") {
                let rows=tx.query("SELECT id FROM papers WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
                let result=JsonObject(); result.put("deleted",JsonBool(!rows.isEmpty()))
                if (rows.isEmpty()) { return result }
                tx.query("DELETE FROM export_jobs WHERE paper_id=?",[Some(id)]); tx.query("DELETE FROM paper_items WHERE paper_id=?",[Some(id)]); tx.query("DELETE FROM papers WHERE id=?",[Some(id)]); return result
            }
            ownedPaper(tx,id,rowText(user,"id"))
            if (method=="PATCH") {
                if (let Some(title) <- body.get("title")) { tx.query("UPDATE papers SET title=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(title),Some(id)]) }
                if (let Some(filters) <- body.get("filters")) { tx.query("UPDATE papers SET filters=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(filters.toString()),Some(id)]) }
                if (body.get("items").isSome()) { tx.query("DELETE FROM paper_items WHERE paper_id=?",[Some(id)]); insertPaperItems(tx,id,rowText(user,"id"),tutorArray(body,"items")) }
            }
            return paperDetail(tx,accounts,ownedPaper(tx,id,rowText(user,"id")))
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/personal_learning.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

// Search projections never include reference answers or another learner's records.
func searchReviewQuestions(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let form=insightQuery(context); let query=stripWhitespace(queryLast(form,"q")); let subject=queryLast(form,"subject")
    let scope=if (queryLast(form,"scope").isEmpty()) { "pending" } else { queryLast(form,"scope") }
    if (query.size>300 || subject.size>100 || !["pending","today","completed"].contains(scope)) { throw ApiError(422,"复习筛选条件无效") }
    let page=if (queryLast(form,"page").isEmpty()) { 1 } else { try { Int64.parse(queryLast(form,"page")) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"页码无效") } }
    if (page<1 || page>10000) { throw ApiError(422,"页码无效") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let criteria=StringBuilder(); criteria.append(" FROM review_plans p JOIN questions q ON q.id=p.question_id AND q.user_id=p.user_id WHERE p.user_id=? AND q.status<>'archived'")
        let params=ArrayList<Option<String>>(); params.add(user["id"])
        criteria.append(if (scope=="completed") { " AND p.status='completed'" } else { " AND p.status='pending'" })
        if (scope=="today") { criteria.append(" AND p.due_at<TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,16,DATE(TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,8,UTC_TIMESTAMP())))") }
        if (!subject.isEmpty()) { criteria.append(" AND q.subject=?"); params.add(Some(subject)) }
        if (!query.isEmpty()) {
            criteria.append(" AND (LOCATE(?,q.question_text)>0 OR LOCATE(?,CAST(q.knowledge_points AS CHAR))>0 OR LOCATE(?,p.reason)>0 OR LOCATE(?,q.title)>0)")
            for (_ in 0..4) { params.add(Some(query)) }
        }
        let total=db.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total"+criteria.toString(),params.toArray()).rows[0]
        let items=JsonArray()
        for (row in db.query("SELECT p.id,p.question_id,p.due_at,p.reason,p.status,q.subject,q.grade,q.title,q.question_text,q.knowledge_points,(p.due_at<TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,16,DATE(TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,8,UTC_TIMESTAMP())))) AS is_due"+criteria.toString()+" ORDER BY p.due_at "+(if (scope=="completed") { "DESC" } else { "ASC" })+",p.id LIMIT 20 OFFSET "+((page-1)*20).toString(),params.toArray()).rows) {
            let item=JsonObject()
            for (key in ["id","question_id","due_at","reason","status","subject","grade","title","question_text"]) { item.put(key,JsonString(rowText(row,key))) }
            item.put("due_at",JsonString(rowText(row,"due_at").replace(" ","T")+"+00:00"))
            item.put("knowledge_points",JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(row,"knowledge_points"))); item.put("is_due",JsonBool(rowText(row,"is_due")=="1")); items.add(item)
        }
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("items",items); result.put("total",JsonInt(Int64.parse(rowText(total,"total")))); result.put("page",JsonInt(page)); result.put("page_size",JsonInt(20)); return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"复习题目暂时无法读取")
}

func personalKnowledgeFramework(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let nodes=JsonArray(); let edges=JsonArray(); let ids=HashMap<String,String>()
        let counts=HashMap<String,Int64>()
        for (row in db.query("SELECT l.knowledge_point_id,COUNT(DISTINCT l.question_id) AS total FROM user_question_knowledge_links l JOIN questions q ON q.id=l.question_id AND q.user_id=l.user_id WHERE l.user_id=? AND q.status<>'archived' GROUP BY l.knowledge_point_id",[user["id"]]).rows) { counts[rowText(row,"knowledge_point_id")]=Int64.parse(rowText(row,"total")) }
        let mastery=HashMap<String,JsonObject>()
        for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=?",[user["id"]]).rows) {
            let value=rowJson("mastery_states",row); mastery[insightPair(jsonText(value,"subject"),jsonText(value,"knowledge_point"))]=value
        }
        for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points ORDER BY subject,code").rows) {
            let card=rowJson("knowledge_points",row); let content=tutorObject(card,"content"); let node=JsonObject(); let id=jsonText(card,"id"); let code=jsonText(card,"code")
            ids[id]=code
            for (key in ["code","subject","name"]) { node.put(key,card[key]) }
            node.put("grade",JsonString(tutorText(content,"grade"))); node.put("chapter",JsonString(tutorText(content,"chapter")))
            node.put("question_count",JsonInt(counts.get(id) ?? 0))
            node.put("mastery",if (let Some(state) <- mastery.get(insightPair(jsonText(card,"subject"),jsonText(card,"name")))) { state } else { JsonNull() })
            nodes.add(node)
        }
        for (row in db.query("SELECT source_id,target_id FROM knowledge_relations WHERE relation_type='prerequisite' ORDER BY source_id,target_id").rows) {
            if (let Some(source) <- ids.get(rowText(row,"source_id"))) {
                if (let Some(target) <- ids.get(rowText(row,"target_id"))) { let edge=JsonObject(); edge.put("code",JsonString(source)); edge.put("prerequisite_code",JsonString(target)); edges.add(edge) }
            }
        }
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("nodes",nodes); result.put("relations",edges)
        let profiles=db.query("SELECT grade,preferences FROM student_profiles WHERE user_id=?",[user["id"]]).rows
        var defaultSubject="数学"; var defaultGrade=""
        if (!profiles.isEmpty()) {
            defaultGrade=rowText(profiles[0],"grade")
            let raw=rowText(profiles[0],"preferences")
            if (!raw.isEmpty()) {
                if (let Some(preferences) <- (JsonValue.fromStr(raw) as JsonObject)) {
                    let focus=tutorText(preferences,"focus_subject"); if (!focus.isEmpty()) { defaultSubject=focus }
                }
            }
        }
        result.put("default_subject",JsonString(defaultSubject)); result.put("default_grade",JsonString(defaultGrade)); return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"知识框架暂时无法读取")
}

func lessonRow(row: HashMap<String,Option<String>>): JsonObject {
    let item=JsonObject()
    for (key in ["id","knowledge_code","prompt","created_at"]) { item.put(key,JsonString(rowText(row,key))) }
    item.put("result",JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(row,"result"))); return item
}

func lessonCards(db: Database, code: String): JsonArray {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE code=?",[Some(code)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"知识卡不存在") }
    let result=JsonArray(); let anchor=rowJson("knowledge_points",rows[0]); result.add(anchor)
    for (row in db.query("SELECT k.* FROM knowledge_relations r JOIN knowledge_points k ON k.id=r.target_id WHERE r.source_id=? AND r.relation_type='prerequisite' AND k.subject=? ORDER BY k.code LIMIT 3",[rows[0]["id"],rows[0]["subject"]]).rows) {
        let card=rowJson("knowledge_points",row); card.put("retrieval_role",JsonString("prerequisite")); card.put("anchor_code",JsonString(code)); result.add(card)
    }
    return result
}

// Import timestamps and internal row IDs do not change the teaching material.
// Keep the content, subject and dependency roles in the version boundary.
func lessonSourceVersion(cards: JsonArray): String {
    let source=JsonArray()
    for (value in cards.getItems()) {
        let card=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let fields=JsonArray()
        for (key in ["code","name","subject","content","retrieval_role","anchor_code"]) { fields.add(card.get(key) ?? JsonNull()) }
        source.add(fields)
    }
    return sha256(source.toString().toArray())
}

func lessonVersionMatches(record: JsonObject, cards: JsonArray, prompt: String): Bool {
    let suffix=":"+sha256(prompt.toArray()); let fingerprint=tutorText(record,"input_fingerprint")
    // Old records remain readable and replayable when their original source is
    // still provable; never silently upgrade an already-stale record.
    return fingerprint==lessonSourceVersion(cards)+suffix || fingerprint==sha256(cards.toString().toArray())+suffix
}

func knowledgeLessons(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let code=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        if (context.request.method=="GET") {
            let cards=lessonCards(db,code); let result=JsonArray()
            for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_lessons WHERE user_id=? AND knowledge_code=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 10",[user["id"],Some(code)]).rows) {
                let item=lessonRow(row); let record=JsonObject(); record.put("input_fingerprint",JsonString(rowText(row,"input_fingerprint")))
                item.put("source_current",JsonBool(lessonVersionMatches(record,cards,rowText(row,"prompt")))); result.add(item)
            }
            return result
        }
        requireMethod(context,"POST")
        let body=readJson(context); let prompt=stripWhitespace(optionalText(body,"prompt","",800)); let requestId=stripWhitespace(optionalText(body,"request_id","",80))
        if (requestId.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"缺少请求编号") }
        let cards=lessonCards(db,code); let sourceHash=lessonSourceVersion(cards); let fingerprint=sourceHash+":"+sha256(prompt.toArray())
        let previous=db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_lessons WHERE user_id=? AND request_id=?",[user["id"],Some(requestId)]).rows
        if (!previous.isEmpty()) {
            let record=JsonObject(); record.put("input_fingerprint",JsonString(rowText(previous[0],"input_fingerprint")))
            if (rowText(previous[0],"knowledge_code")!=code || !lessonVersionMatches(record,cards,prompt)) { throw ApiError(409,"这次提问或知识内容已变化，请重新提问") }
            return lessonRow(previous[0])
        }
        let anchor=(cards.getItems()[0] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("knowledge_lesson")); input.put("subject",anchor["subject"]); input.put("grade",JsonString(tutorText(tutorObject(anchor,"content"),"grade"))); input.put("text",JsonString(prompt)); input.put("dialogue_cards",cards)
        let history=JsonArray(); let recent=db.query("SELECT prompt,result FROM knowledge_lessons WHERE user_id=? AND knowledge_code=? AND (input_fingerprint LIKE ? OR input_fingerprint LIKE ?) ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 3",[user["id"],Some(code),Some(sourceHash+":%"),Some(sha256(cards.toString().toArray())+":%")]).rows
        for (index in 0..recent.size) {
            let row=recent[recent.size-1-index]; let result=(JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(row,"result")) as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let turn=JsonObject(); turn.put("question",JsonString(rowText(row,"prompt"))); turn.put("explanation",JsonString(tutorText(result,"explanation"))); turn.put("check_question",JsonString(tutorText(result,"check_question"))); history.add(turn)
        }
        input.put("knowledge_history",history)
        let result=try { algorithmCompute(input,timeoutSeconds:30) } catch (_: ApiError) { throw ApiError(503,"讲解暂时没有完成，请稍后重试。可以先查看知识卡中的公式和示例。") }
        if ((result.get("can_update_learning") ?? JsonNull()).toString()!="false") { throw ApiError(503,"讲解结果未通过检查") }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let latest=lessonCards(tx,code)
            if (lessonSourceVersion(latest)!=sourceHash) { throw ApiError(409,"知识内容刚刚更新，请重新提问") }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO knowledge_lessons (id,user_id,knowledge_code,request_id,input_fingerprint,prompt,result,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE id=id",[Some(newId()),user["id"],Some(code),Some(requestId),Some(fingerprint),Some(prompt),Some(result.toString())])
            let saved=tx.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_lessons WHERE user_id=? AND request_id=?",[user["id"],Some(requestId)]).rows[0]
            let savedRecord=JsonObject(); savedRecord.put("input_fingerprint",JsonString(rowText(saved,"input_fingerprint")))
            if (!lessonVersionMatches(savedRecord,latest,prompt) || rowText(saved,"knowledge_code")!=code) { throw ApiError(409,"请求编号已用于其他提问") }
            return lessonRow(saved)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"知识讲解暂时不可用")
}

func savedReviewFilters(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, deleting: Bool): JsonValue {
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        if (deleting) {
            requireMethod(context,"DELETE"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-1]
            if (db.query("SELECT id FROM saved_review_filters WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"复习范围不存在") }
            db.query("DELETE FROM saved_review_filters WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(id),user["id"]]); let result=JsonObject(); result.put("deleted",JsonBool(true)); return result
        }
        if (context.request.method=="POST") {
            let body=readJson(context); let name=stripWhitespace(optionalText(body,"name","",80)); let query=stripWhitespace(optionalText(body,"q","",300)); let subject=optionalText(body,"subject","",30); let scope=optionalText(body,"scope","pending",20)
            if (name.isEmpty() || !["pending","today","completed"].contains(scope)) { throw ApiError(422,"请填写名称并选择有效的复习范围") }
            db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
                tx.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE",[user["id"]])
                let existing=tx.query("SELECT id FROM saved_review_filters WHERE user_id=? AND name=? AND query_text=? AND subject=? AND scope=?",[user["id"],Some(name),Some(query),Some(subject),Some(scope)]).rows
                if (existing.isEmpty()) {
                    if (Int64.parse(rowText(tx.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM saved_review_filters WHERE user_id=?",[user["id"]]).rows[0],"total"))>=20) { throw ApiError(409,"最多保存20个复习范围，请先移除不再使用的范围") }
                    tx.query("INSERT INTO saved_review_filters (id,user_id,name,query_text,subject,scope,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),user["id"],Some(name),Some(query),Some(subject),Some(scope)])
                }
                ()
            })
        } else { requireMethod(context,"GET") }
        let result=JsonArray()
        for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM saved_review_filters WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC",[user["id"]]).rows) {
            let item=JsonObject(); for (key in ["id","name","subject","scope"]) { item.put(key,JsonString(rowText(row,key))) }; item.put("q",JsonString(rowText(row,"query_text"))); result.add(item)
        }
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"复习范围暂时无法保存")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/practice_alignment.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*

// The same admission check is used for new, cached and submitted practice.
func practiceAligned(db: Database, source: JsonObject, preparation: JsonObject, candidates: JsonArray): JsonArray {
    let target=tutorText(preparation,"target_knowledge_id")
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE id=? AND subject=?",[Some(target),sqlValue(source["subject"])]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { return JsonArray() }
    let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("practice_alignment")); input.put("retrieval_source",source)
    input.put("retrieval_target",rowJson("knowledge_points",rows[0])); input.put("retrieval_candidates",candidates)
    return tutorArray(algorithmCompute(input),"checks")
}

func practiceCandidate(question: JsonObject): JsonArray {
    let row=JsonObject(); row.put("question",question); let rows=JsonArray(); rows.add(row); return rows
}

func practiceAdmitted(checks: JsonArray, id: String): Bool {
    for (entry in checks.getItems()) {
        let check=(entry as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        if (tutorText(check,"id")==id && tutorFlag(check,"aligned")) { return true }
    }
    return false
}

func practiceAlignmentMethod(checks: JsonArray, id: String): String {
    for (entry in checks.getItems()) {
        let check=(entry as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        if (tutorText(check,"id")==id && tutorFlag(check,"aligned")) { return tutorText(check,"method") }
    }
    return ""
}

func practiceSourceVersion(db: Database, question: JsonObject): String {
    let value=JsonObject(); let id=jsonText(question,"id")
    value.put("question",JsonString(learningSourceFingerprint(db,question)))
    value.put("analysis",firstJson(queryArray(db,"problem_analyses","SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",id)))
    value.put("diagnosis",firstJson(queryArray(db,"diagnoses","SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",id)))
    return sha256(learningFingerprintJson(value).toArray())
}

func practiceProofContext(db: Database, source: JsonObject, preparation: JsonObject, candidate: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let value=JsonObject(); value.put("policy",JsonInt(1)); value.put("source",JsonString(practiceSourceVersion(db,source)))
    value.put("candidate",JsonString(sha256(learningFingerprintJson(candidate).toArray())))
    let cards=db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE id=? AND subject=?",[sqlValue(preparation["target_knowledge_id"]),sqlValue(source["subject"])]).rows
    value.put("target",JsonString(if (cards.isEmpty()) { "" } else { sha256(learningFingerprintJson(rowJson("knowledge_points",cards[0])).toArray()) }))
    return value
}

func practiceProofValid(db: Database, source: JsonObject, preparation: JsonObject, candidate: JsonObject, trace: JsonObject): Bool {
    let proof=tutorObject(trace,"alignment_proof"); let method=tutorText(proof,"method")
    return (method=="symbolic_linear" || method=="qwen_grounded_concept") && jsonEquivalent(proof.get("context") ?? JsonNull(),practiceProofContext(db,source,preparation,candidate))
}

func savePracticeProof(db: Database, source: JsonObject, preparation: JsonObject, candidate: JsonObject, trace: JsonObject, method: String): Unit {
    let proof=JsonObject(); proof.put("method",JsonString(method)); proof.put("context",practiceProofContext(db,source,preparation,candidate))
    trace.put("alignment_proof",proof); trace.put("alignment_method",JsonString(method))
}

func currentPracticePreparation(db: Database, question: JsonObject, mode: String): JsonObject {
    let analyses=queryArray(db,"problem_analyses","SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",jsonText(question,"id"))
    return retrievalPreparation(db,question,firstJson(analyses),"practice",mode)
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/practice_attempt.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func practiceMode(body: JsonObject): String {
    let mode=optionalText(body,"mode","",30)
    if (mode!="reinforce" && mode!="variation") { throw ApiError(422,"请选择有效的练习模式") }
    return mode
}

func attemptPractice(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=readJson(context)
        let owner=rowText(user,"id"); let mistakeId=optionalText(body,"mistake_id","",100); let id=optionalText(body,"question_id","",100); let mode=practiceMode(body)
        let answer=body.get("student_answer") ?? { => throw ApiError(422,"请提交作答") }()
        let duration=paperInteger(body.get("duration_seconds") ?? JsonInt(0),0,7200)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let mistakes=tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status<>'archived' FOR UPDATE",[Some(mistakeId),Some(owner)]).rows
            if (mistakes.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"没有找到这道错题") }
            let mistake=rowJson("questions",mistakes[0])
            let generated=tx.query("SELECT v.* FROM variant_questions v JOIN tutor_sessions s ON s.id=v.session_id WHERE v.id=? AND v.question_id=? AND s.user_id=? AND s.stage='standalone_practice' FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),Some(mistakeId),Some(owner)]).rows
            if (!generated.isEmpty()) { validateTutorHandwriting(body,owner,id,kind: "practice"); return attemptGeneratedPractice(tx,owner,mistake,rowJson("variant_questions",generated[0]),mode,answer,duration) }
            let questions=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id)]).rows
            if (questions.isEmpty() || rowText(questions[0],"usable_for_auto_grade")!="1") { throw ApiError(422,"这道题不能自动判分") }
            let question=rowJson("dataset_questions",questions[0])
            let existing=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_practice_attempts WHERE user_id=? AND mistake_id=? AND dataset_question_id=? AND mode=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE",[Some(owner),Some(mistakeId),Some(id),Some(mode)]).rows
            if (!existing.isEmpty()) {
                practiceServed(tx,owner,mistake,id,mode)
                let record=rowJson("dataset_practice_attempts",existing[0]); let details=tutorObject(record,"grading_details"); let receipt=tutorObject(details,"practice_receipt")
                let context=practiceProofContext(tx,mistake,currentPracticePreparation(tx,mistake,mode),question)
                if (jsonEquivalent(tutorObject(tutorObject(details,"alignment_proof"),"context"),context) && jsonEquivalent(record["student_answer"],answer) && !receipt.getFields().isEmpty()) { return refreshedPracticeReceipt(accounts,question,receipt) }
                throw ApiError(409,"这道练习已经完成，请换一道题继续练习")
            }
            let handwriting=validateTutorHandwriting(body,owner,id,kind: "practice")
            let served=practiceServed(tx,owner,mistake,id,mode); let trace=tutorObject(served,"retrieval_trace"); let answerSeen=tutorFlag(trace,"answer_revealed")
            let preparation=currentPracticePreparation(tx,mistake,mode)
            if (!usableRetrievalCandidate(mistake,question,"practice") || tutorText(trace,"target_knowledge_id").isEmpty() || tutorText(trace,"target_knowledge_id")!=tutorText(preparation,"target_knowledge_id")) { throw ApiError(409,"学习目标或题目资格已变化，请重新获取练习") }
            if (!practiceProofValid(tx,mistake,preparation,question,trace)) {
                let checks=practiceAligned(tx,mistake,preparation,practiceCandidate(question))
                if (!practiceAdmitted(checks,id)) { throw ApiError(409,"这道练习与当前学习目标不匹配，请重新获取练习；本次未更新学习记录") }
                savePracticeProof(tx,mistake,preparation,question,trace,practiceAlignmentMethod(checks,id))
            }
            let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("grade_answer")); input.put("text",question["question_text"]); input.put("answer",answer); input.put("expected",question["answer"])
            input.put("subject",question["subject"]); input.put("question_type",question["question_type"]); input.put("options",question["options"])
            let solution=JsonArray(); for (line in jsonText(question,"solution").split("\n")) { let clean=stripWhitespace(line); if (!clean.isEmpty()) { solution.add(JsonString(clean)) } }; input.put("reference_solution",solution)
            let grading=algorithmCompute(input); let decisive=tutorFlag(grading,"can_update_learning")
            tx.query("UPDATE practice_recommendations SET retrieval_trace=JSON_SET(retrieval_trace,'$.answer_revealed',true),updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(served["id"])])
            let target=tutorText(trace,"target_knowledge_id")
            let knowledgeRows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE id=? AND subject=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(target),sqlValue(mistake["subject"])]).rows
            if (knowledgeRows.isEmpty() || rowText(knowledgeRows[0],"name")!=tutorText(preparation,"target_knowledge_name")) { throw ApiError(409,"练习知识点已变化，请重新获取练习") }
            let knowledge=if (knowledgeRows.isEmpty()) { JsonObject() } else { rowJson("knowledge_points",knowledgeRows[0]) }
            let preferred=tutorText(knowledge,"name"); let point=learningKnowledgePoint(tx,mistake,preferred: preferred,includeAnalysis: false)
            let old=tx.query("SELECT mastery_score FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? AND subject=? AND knowledge_point=? LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(stripWhitespace(jsonText(mistake,"subject"))),Some(point)]).rows
            let before: JsonValue=if (old.isEmpty()) { JsonFloat(0.0) } else { rowJson("mastery_states",old[0])["mastery_score"] }
            var mastery: JsonValue=JsonNull(); var practiceAttemptId=""
            if (decisive) {
                let sources=tx.query("SELECT `key` FROM dataset_sources WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(question["dataset_source_id"])]).rows
                let source=if (sources.isEmpty()) { "unknown" } else { rowText(sources[0],"key") }
                let details=(JsonValue.fromStr(grading.toString()) as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); details.put("independent_answer",JsonBool(!answerSeen)); details.put("handwriting_image_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl(jsonText(handwriting,"image_url")))); details.put("handwriting_recognition",handwriting["recognition"])
                details.put("alignment_method",tutorObject(trace,"alignment_proof")["method"]); details.put("target_knowledge_name",preparation["target_knowledge_name"])
                details.put("alignment_proof",trace["alignment_proof"])
                practiceAttemptId=newId()
                tx.query("INSERT INTO dataset_practice_attempts (id,user_id,mistake_id,dataset_question_id,source_dataset,knowledge_id,mode,student_answer,correct,duration_seconds,grading_details,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(practiceAttemptId),Some(owner),Some(mistakeId),Some(id),Some(source),if (knowledgeRows.isEmpty()) { None } else { sqlValue(knowledge["id"]) },Some(mode),Some(answer.toString()),sqlValue(grading["correct"]),Some(duration.toString()),Some(details.toString())])
                let evidence=JsonObject(); evidence.put(if (mode=="reinforce") { "near_transfer" } else { "far_transfer" },grading["correct"]); evidence.put("hint_level",JsonInt(0)); evidence.put("strategy",JsonString("independent_dataset_practice")); evidence.put("dataset_question_id",JsonString(id)); evidence.put("practice_attempt_id",JsonString(practiceAttemptId))
                if (!answerSeen) { let learning=applyLearningEvidence(tx,owner,mistake,evidence,preferred: preferred,includeAnalysis: false); mastery=tutorObject(learning,"mastery_after")["mastery_score"] }
            }
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("correct",if (decisive) { grading["correct"] } else { JsonNull() })
            for (key in ["verdict","can_update_learning","confidence","evidence","feedback"]) { result.put(key,grading[key]) }
            if (answerSeen) { result.put("learning_note",JsonString("已看过参考答案，这次练习不重复计入掌握情况。")) }
            result.put("answer",question["answer"]); result.put("solution",question["solution"])
            for ((key,value) in datasetSolutionImages(question,accounts).getFields()) { result.put(key,value) }
            result.put("mastery_before",before); result.put("mastery",mastery); result.put("grading_method",grading["method"])
            if (!practiceAttemptId.isEmpty()) { tx.query("UPDATE dataset_practice_attempts SET grading_details=JSON_SET(grading_details,'$.practice_receipt',JSON_EXTRACT(?,'$')) WHERE id=?",[Some(result.toString()),Some(practiceAttemptId)]) }
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/practice_context.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.env.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

// Keep Python's sorted JSON separators so recommendations survive switching backends.
func learningFingerprintJson(value: JsonValue): String {
    let output=StringBuilder()
    if (let Some(object) <- (value as JsonObject)) {
        let keys=ArrayList<String>(); for ((key,_) in object.getFields()) { keys.add(key) }
        let ordered=keys.toArray()
        for (i in 0..ordered.size) { for (j in i+1..ordered.size) { if (ordered[j]<ordered[i]) { let swap=ordered[i]; ordered[i]=ordered[j]; ordered[j]=swap } } }
        output.append("{"); var first=true
        for (key in ordered) { if (!first) { output.append(", ") }; first=false; output.append(JsonString(key).toString()); output.append(": "); output.append(learningFingerprintJson(object[key])) }
        output.append("}"); return output.toString()
    }
    if (let Some(array) <- (value as JsonArray)) {
        output.append("["); var first=true
        for (item in array.getItems()) { if (!first) { output.append(", ") }; first=false; output.append(learningFingerprintJson(item)) }
        output.append("]"); return output.toString()
    }
    return value.toString()
}

func learningSourceFingerprint(db: Database, question: JsonObject): String {
    let source=JsonObject()
    for (key in ["question_text","student_answer","subject","grade","title","formulas","options","question_type","knowledge_points"]) { source.put(key,question[key]) }
    let works=JsonArray()
    for (row in db.query("SELECT id FROM student_works WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY id",[Some(jsonText(question,"id"))]).rows) { works.add(JsonString(rowText(row,"id"))) }
    source.put("student_work_revisions",works)
    return sha256(learningFingerprintJson(source).toArray())
}

func practiceServed(db: Database, owner: String, mistake: JsonObject, questionId: String, mode: String): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM practice_recommendations WHERE user_id=? AND mistake_id=? AND dataset_question_id=? AND mode=? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(jsonText(mistake,"id")),Some(questionId),Some(mode)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(409,"请先获取推荐题再作答") }
    let served=rowJson("practice_recommendations",rows[0])
    if (tutorText(tutorObject(served,"retrieval_trace"),"source_question_fingerprint")!=learningSourceFingerprint(db,mistake)) { throw ApiError(409,"题目或推荐已更新，请重新获取推荐题再提交") }
    return served
}

func practiceHandwritingContext(db: Database, owner: String, mistakeId: String, questionId: String, mode: String): JsonObject {
    let mistakes=db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(mistakeId),Some(owner)]).rows
    let generated=db.query("SELECT v.* FROM variant_questions v JOIN tutor_sessions s ON s.id=v.session_id WHERE v.id=? AND v.question_id=? AND s.user_id=? AND s.stage='standalone_practice'",[Some(questionId),Some(mistakeId),Some(owner)]).rows
    if (!mistakes.isEmpty() && !generated.isEmpty()) {
        let variant=rowJson("variant_questions",generated[0]); let meta=tutorObject(variant,"generation_metadata")
        let mistake=rowJson("questions",mistakes[0])
        if (tutorText(meta,"mode")!=mode || tutorText(meta,"source_version")!=practiceSourceVersion(db,mistake)) { throw ApiError(409,"原题已更新，请重新获取练习") }
        if (!tutorArray(meta,"options").getItems().isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"选择题请直接选择选项") }
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("subject",mistake["subject"]); result.put("text",variant["question_text"]); return result
    }
    let questions=db.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=?",[Some(questionId)]).rows
    if (mistakes.isEmpty() || questions.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"没有找到这道练习题") }
    let question=rowJson("dataset_questions",questions[0]); practiceServed(db,owner,rowJson("questions",mistakes[0]),questionId,mode)
    if (!tutorArray(question,"options").getItems().isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"选择题请直接选择选项") }
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("subject",question["subject"]); result.put("text",question["question_text"]); return result
}

func servePracticeImage(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): Unit {
    try {
        if (context.request.method!="GET" && context.request.method!="HEAD") { throw ApiError(405,"请求方法不支持") }
        if (!accounts.validMedia(context)) { throw ApiError(403,"资源链接已失效，请返回页面刷新") }
        let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-3]
        let index=try { Int64.parse(parts[parts.size-1]) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"图片序号无效") }
        var stored=""
        try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=?",[Some(id)]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"图片不存在") }
            let paths=tutorArray(rowJson("dataset_questions",rows[0]),"image_paths")
            if (index<0 || index>=paths.getItems().size) { throw ApiError(404,"图片不存在") }
            stored=(paths[index] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        }
        let working=getWorkingDirectory().toString()
        let project=if (working.endsWith("/backend-cangjie")) { working[..working.size-16] } else { working }
        let configured=getVariable("CANGJIE_QUESTION_BANK_ROOT") ?? "data/question_bank_assets"
        let root=if (configured.startsWith("/")) { configured } else { project+"/"+configured }
        let relative=if (stored.startsWith("/")) { stored } else { project+"/"+stored }
        if (!relative.startsWith(root+"/")) { throw ApiError(404,"图片不存在") }
        serveMedia(context,accounts,storageRoot: Some(root),storagePath: Some(relative[root.size+1..]))
    } catch (error: ApiError) { let body=JsonObject(); body.put("detail",JsonString(error.message)); jsonResponse(context,body,status: error.status) }
    catch (_: Exception) { let body=JsonObject(); body.put("detail",JsonString("图片暂时不可用")); jsonResponse(context,body,status: 503) }
}

// Persist the result, but renew expiring image links whenever it is read.
func refreshedPracticeReceipt(accounts: Accounts, question: JsonObject, receipt: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    for ((key,value) in datasetSolutionImages(question,accounts).getFields()) { receipt.put(key,value) }
    return receipt
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/practice_generated.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*

func generatedPracticePayload(question: JsonObject, variant: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let meta=tutorObject(variant,"generation_metadata"); let result=JsonObject()
    result.put("question_id",variant["id"]); result.put("stem",variant["question_text"])
    result.put("subject",question["subject"]); result.put("grade",question["grade"])
    result.put("question_type",meta["question_type"]); result.put("options",tutorArray(meta,"options"))
    result.put("images",JsonArray()); result.put("mode",meta["mode"]); result.put("generated",JsonBool(true))
    result.put("recommendation",JsonString("根据这道错题练习相同的方法"))
    return result
}

func generatedPractice(db: Database, owner: String, question: JsonObject, mode: String, force: Bool): JsonObject {
    let version=practiceSourceVersion(db,question); let questionId=jsonText(question,"id")
    let rows=db.query("SELECT v.* FROM variant_questions v JOIN tutor_sessions s ON s.id=v.session_id WHERE s.user_id=? AND v.question_id=? AND s.stage='standalone_practice' ORDER BY v.created_at DESC LIMIT 20",[Some(owner),Some(questionId)]).rows
    var previous: Option<JsonObject>=None
    for (row in rows) {
        let item=rowJson("variant_questions",row); let meta=tutorObject(item,"generation_metadata")
        if (tutorText(meta,"source_version")==version && tutorText(meta,"mode")==mode && tutorFlag(item,"verified")) {
            if (previous.isNone()) { previous=Some(item) }
            if (!force) {
                let result=generatedPracticePayload(question,item)
                let sessions=db.query("SELECT state FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(item["session_id"])]).rows
                if (!sessions.isEmpty()) {
                    let state=(JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(sessions[0],"state")) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                    if (let Some(receipt) <- state.get("receipt")) { result.put("previous_result",receipt); result.put("previous_answer",state["answer"]) }
                }
                return result
            }
        }
    }
    let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("generate_practice"))
    let original=StringBuilder(); original.append(tutorText(question,"question_text")); original.append("\n选项："+tutorArray(question,"options").toString()); original.append("\n年级："+tutorText(question,"grade"))
    if (let Some(old) <- previous) { original.append("\n请勿重复此前练习："+tutorText(old,"question_text")) }
    input.put("text",JsonString(original.toString())); input.put("subject",question["subject"])
    input.put("question_type",question["question_type"]); input.put("transfer_type",JsonString(if (mode=="variation") { "far" } else { "near" }))
    let snapshot=tutorDataSnapshot(db,questionId,Some(owner)); input.put("knowledge_points",tutorStringArray(tutorKnowledge(snapshot)))
    var computed=JsonObject()
    try { computed=algorithmCompute(input) } catch (_: ApiError) { () }
    if (!tutorFlag(computed,"verified")) {
        if (let Some(old) <- previous) {
            let result=generatedPracticePayload(question,old); result.put("recommendation",JsonString("再练一次，巩固这道题的方法")); return result
        }
        throw ApiError(503,"类似题暂未通过核对，请稍后重试。原错题和学习记录已保留。")
    }
    let generated=tutorObject(computed,"variant"); let meta=tutorObject(computed,"generation")
    meta.put("options",tutorArray(generated,"options")); meta.put("mode",JsonString(mode)); meta.put("source_version",JsonString(version))
    meta.put("question_type",JsonString(if (tutorArray(generated,"options").getItems().isEmpty()) { "综合题" } else { tutorText(question,"question_type") }))
    return db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx =>
        let current=tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status<>'archived' FOR UPDATE",[Some(questionId),Some(owner)]).rows
        if (current.isEmpty() || practiceSourceVersion(tx,rowJson("questions",current[0]))!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"原题已更新，请重新获取练习") }
        let sid=newId(); let vid=newId()
        tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_sessions (id,user_id,question_id,stage,hint_level,turn_count,mastery_score,state,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,'standalone_practice',0,0,0,JSON_OBJECT(),'standalone',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(sid),Some(owner),Some(questionId)])
        tx.query("INSERT INTO variant_questions (id,question_id,session_id,transfer_type,question_text,answer,explanation,reference_solution,verified,generation_metadata,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(vid),Some(questionId),Some(sid),Some(if (mode=="variation") { "far" } else { "near" }),sqlValue(generated["question_text"]),sqlValue(generated["answer"]),sqlValue(generated["explanation"]),Some(generated["reference_solution"].toString()),Some(meta.toString())])
        return generatedPracticePayload(question,rowJson("variant_questions",tx.query("SELECT * FROM variant_questions WHERE id=?",[Some(vid)]).rows[0]))
    })
}

func attemptGeneratedPractice(db: Database, owner: String, mistake: JsonObject, variant: JsonObject, mode: String, answer: JsonValue, duration: Int64): JsonObject {
    let meta=tutorObject(variant,"generation_metadata"); let id=jsonText(variant,"id")
    if (tutorText(meta,"mode")!=mode || tutorText(meta,"source_version")!=practiceSourceVersion(db,mistake)) { throw ApiError(409,"原题或练习目标已更新，请重新获取练习") }
    let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("grade_answer")); input.put("text",variant["question_text"]); input.put("answer",answer); input.put("expected",variant["answer"])
    input.put("subject",mistake["subject"]); input.put("question_type",meta["question_type"]); input.put("options",tutorArray(meta,"options")); input.put("reference_solution",variant["reference_solution"])
    let states=db.query("SELECT state FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE",[sqlValue(variant["session_id"])]).rows
    let previousState=if (states.isEmpty()) { JsonObject() } else { (JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(states[0],"state")) as JsonObject).getOrThrow() }
    if (jsonEquivalent(previousState.get("answer") ?? JsonNull(),answer) && tutorFlag(tutorObject(previousState,"receipt"),"can_update_learning")) { return tutorObject(previousState,"receipt") }
    let grading=algorithmCompute(input); let decisive=tutorFlag(grading,"can_update_learning") && tutorFlag(variant,"verified")
    let answerSeen=tutorFlag(previousState,"answer_revealed")
    let prior=db.query("SELECT id FROM variant_attempts WHERE variant_id=? AND user_id=? LIMIT 1",[Some(id),Some(owner)]).rows
    let point=learningKnowledgePoint(db,mistake)
    let old=db.query("SELECT mastery_score FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? AND subject=? AND knowledge_point=? LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),sqlValue(mistake["subject"]),Some(point)]).rows
    let before: JsonValue=if (old.isEmpty()) { JsonFloat(0.0) } else { rowJson("mastery_states",old[0])["mastery_score"] }
    let saved=JsonObject(); saved.put("answer",answer); saved.put("grading",grading); saved.put("duration_seconds",JsonInt(duration)); saved.put("answer_revealed",JsonBool(true))
    db.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET state=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(saved.toString()),sqlValue(variant["session_id"])])
    var mastery: JsonValue=JsonNull()
    if (decisive) {
        let aid=newId()
        db.query("INSERT INTO variant_attempts (id,variant_id,user_id,answer,correct,elapsed_seconds,hint_level,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(aid),Some(id),Some(owner),Some(tutorAnswerText(answer)),sqlValue(grading["correct"]),Some(duration.toString())])
        // Repeated cached exercises remain usable but do not inflate mastery.
        if (prior.isEmpty() && !answerSeen) {
            let evidence=JsonObject(); evidence.put(if (mode=="reinforce") { "near_transfer" } else { "far_transfer" },grading["correct"]); evidence.put("hint_level",JsonInt(0)); evidence.put("strategy",JsonString("generated_practice")); evidence.put("variant_id",JsonString(id)); evidence.put("practice_attempt_id",JsonString(aid))
            mastery=tutorObject(applyLearningEvidence(db,owner,mistake,evidence),"mastery_after").get("mastery_score") ?? JsonNull()
        }
    }
    let result=JsonObject()
    for (key in ["verdict","confidence","evidence","feedback"]) { result.put(key,grading[key]) }
    result.put("correct",if (decisive) { grading["correct"] } else { JsonNull() }); result.put("can_update_learning",JsonBool(decisive))
    if (answerSeen) { result.put("learning_note",JsonString("已看过参考答案，这次练习不重复计入掌握情况。")) }
    result.put("answer",variant["answer"]); result.put("solution",variant["explanation"]); result.put("mastery_before",before); result.put("mastery",mastery); result.put("grading_method",grading["method"])
    saved.put("receipt",result)
    db.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET state=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(saved.toString()),sqlValue(variant["session_id"])])
    return result
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/practice_recommend.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func practicePayload(accounts: Accounts, question: JsonObject, mode: String, trace: JsonObject, points: JsonArray): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("question_id",question["id"])
    for (key in ["subject","grade","question_type"]) { result.put(key,question[key]) }
    result.put("stem",question["question_text"])
    let pictures=datasetQuestionImages(question,accounts)
    result.put("images",pictures["image_urls"])
    let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("normalize_options")); input.put("raw_options",question["options"])
    let options=tutorArray(algorithmCompute(input),"options"); let pictured=tutorArray(pictures,"options")
    for (i in 0..options.getItems().size) {
        if (i<pictured.getItems().size) {
            let urls=tutorArray((pictured[i] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"image_urls")
            if (!urls.getItems().isEmpty()) { (options[i] as JsonObject).getOrThrow().put("image_urls",urls) }
        }
    }
    result.put("options",options); result.put("knowledge_points",points)
    let names=tutorArray(trace,"knowledge_match"); let name=if (names.getItems().isEmpty()) { "关键方法" } else { tutorAnswerText(names[0]) }
    result.put("recommendation",JsonString("这道题会继续检查你需要加强的${name}。")); result.put("mode",JsonString(mode)); return result
}

func recommendPractice(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let owner=rowText(user,"id"); let body=readJson(context)
        let id=optionalText(body,"mistake_id","",100,nonempty: true); let mode=practiceMode(body)
        let debug=editBool(body.get("debug") ?? JsonBool(false)).getValue(); let force=editBool(body.get("force_new") ?? JsonBool(false)).getValue()
        let setup=db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx =>
            let rows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status<>'archived'",[Some(id),Some(owner)]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"没有找到这道错题") }
            let question=rowJson("questions",rows[0]); let fingerprint=learningSourceFingerprint(tx,question)
            let setup=JsonObject(); setup.put("question",question); setup.put("fingerprint",JsonString(fingerprint))
            let generatedRows=tx.query("SELECT v.* FROM variant_questions v JOIN tutor_sessions s ON s.id=v.session_id WHERE s.user_id=? AND v.question_id=? AND s.stage='standalone_practice' AND v.verified=1 ORDER BY v.created_at DESC LIMIT 20",[Some(owner),Some(id)]).rows
            if (!generatedRows.isEmpty()) {
                let version=practiceSourceVersion(tx,question)
                for (row in generatedRows) {
                    let item=rowJson("variant_questions",row); let meta=tutorObject(item,"generation_metadata")
                    if (tutorText(meta,"mode")==mode && tutorText(meta,"source_version")==version) {
                        setup.put("generate",JsonBool(true)); return setup
                    }
                }
            }
            var preparation=JsonObject()
            try { preparation=currentPracticePreparation(tx,question,mode) } catch (_: ApiError) { () }

            setup.put("preparation",preparation)
            setup.put("source_version",JsonString(practiceSourceVersion(tx,question)))
            if (!force) {
                let previous=tx.query("SELECT * FROM practice_recommendations WHERE user_id=? AND mistake_id=? AND mode=? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(id),Some(mode)]).rows
                if (!previous.isEmpty()) {
                    let saved=rowJson("practice_recommendations",previous[0]); let trace=tutorObject(saved,"retrieval_trace"); let qid=jsonText(saved,"dataset_question_id")
                    let attempted=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_practice_attempts WHERE user_id=? AND mistake_id=? AND dataset_question_id=? AND mode=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(id),Some(qid),Some(mode)]).rows
                    let questions=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=?",[Some(qid)]).rows
                    if (tutorText(trace,"source_question_fingerprint")==fingerprint && tutorText(trace,"target_knowledge_id")==tutorText(preparation,"target_knowledge_id") && !questions.isEmpty() && usableRetrievalCandidate(question,rowJson("dataset_questions",questions[0]),"practice")) {
                        let selected=rowJson("dataset_questions",questions[0])
                        var valid=practiceProofValid(tx,question,preparation,selected,trace)
                        if (!valid) {
                            let checks=practiceAligned(tx,question,preparation,practiceCandidate(selected)); valid=practiceAdmitted(checks,qid)
                            if (valid) { savePracticeProof(tx,question,preparation,selected,trace,practiceAlignmentMethod(checks,qid)); tx.query("UPDATE practice_recommendations SET retrieval_trace=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(trace.toString()),sqlValue(saved["id"])]) }
                        }
                        if (valid && !attempted.isEmpty()) {
                            let details=tutorObject(rowJson("dataset_practice_attempts",attempted[0]),"grading_details")
                            valid=!tutorObject(details,"practice_receipt").getFields().isEmpty() && jsonEquivalent(tutorObject(tutorObject(details,"alignment_proof"),"context"),practiceProofContext(tx,question,preparation,selected))
                        }
                        if (valid) {
                            let result=practicePayload(accounts,selected,mode,trace,tutorArray(selected,"knowledge_points"))
                            if (!attempted.isEmpty()) {
                                let record=rowJson("dataset_practice_attempts",attempted[0]); let receipt=tutorObject(tutorObject(record,"grading_details"),"practice_receipt")
                                if (!receipt.getFields().isEmpty()) { result.put("previous_result",refreshedPracticeReceipt(accounts,selected,receipt)); result.put("previous_answer",record["student_answer"]) }
                            }
                            if (debug) { result.put("debug",trace) }; setup.put("cached",result); return setup
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            return setup
        })
        if (let Some(cached) <- setup.get("cached")) { return cached }
        if (tutorFlag(setup,"generate")) { return generatedPractice(db,owner,tutorObject(setup,"question"),mode,force) }
        let question=tutorObject(setup,"question"); let fingerprint=jsonText(setup,"fingerprint")
        let preparation=tutorObject(setup,"preparation")
        let excluded=JsonArray(); let seen=HashSet<String>()
        for (table in ["dataset_practice_attempts","practice_recommendations"]) {
            for (row in db.query("SELECT dataset_question_id FROM ${table} WHERE user_id=? AND mistake_id=?",[Some(owner),Some(id)]).rows) {
                let qid=rowText(row,"dataset_question_id"); if (!seen.contains(qid)) { seen.add(qid); excluded.add(JsonString(qid)) }
            }
        }
        if (tutorText(preparation,"target_knowledge_id").isEmpty()) { return generatedPractice(db,owner,question,mode,force) }
        var ranked=JsonArray(); var checked=JsonArray()
        try {
            ranked=tutorArray(retrievalCandidates(db,question,preparation,excluded,"practice",mode),"ranked")
            checked=practiceAligned(db,question,preparation,ranked)
        } catch (_: ApiError) { return generatedPractice(db,owner,question,mode,force) }
        let checks=checked
        let admitted=JsonArray()
        for (value in ranked.getItems()) { let row=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); if (practiceAdmitted(checks,jsonText(tutorObject(row,"question"),"id"))) { admitted.add(row) } }
        if (admitted.getItems().isEmpty()) { return generatedPractice(db,owner,question,mode,force) }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let current=tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status<>'archived' FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),Some(owner)]).rows
            if (current.isEmpty() || learningSourceFingerprint(tx,rowJson("questions",current[0]))!=fingerprint) { throw ApiError(409,"题目已修改，请重新获取推荐题") }
            if (practiceSourceVersion(tx,rowJson("questions",current[0]))!=jsonText(setup,"source_version")) { throw ApiError(409,"题目分析或诊断已更新，请重新获取练习") }
            let candidate=(admitted[0] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let selected=tutorObject(candidate,"question"); let trace=JsonObject()
            if (tutorText(currentPracticePreparation(tx,rowJson("questions",current[0]),mode),"target_knowledge_id")!=tutorText(preparation,"target_knowledge_id")) { throw ApiError(409,"学习目标已更新，请重新获取练习") }
            let fresh=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=? AND subject=? AND usable_for_practice=1",[sqlValue(selected["id"]),sqlValue(question["subject"])]).rows
            if (fresh.isEmpty() || !usableRetrievalCandidate(question,rowJson("dataset_questions",fresh[0]),"practice") || !jsonEquivalent(rowJson("dataset_questions",fresh[0]),selected)) { throw ApiError(409,"推荐题已更新，请重新获取") }
            for (table in ["practice_recommendations","dataset_practice_attempts"]) {
                if (!tx.query("SELECT id FROM ${table} WHERE user_id=? AND mistake_id=? AND dataset_question_id=? LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(id),sqlValue(selected["id"])]).rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(409,"推荐已变化，请重新获取练习题") }
            }
            trace.put("source_question_fingerprint",JsonString(fingerprint))
            trace.put("alignment_method",JsonString(practiceAlignmentMethod(checks,jsonText(selected,"id"))))
            for (key in ["knowledge_match","target_knowledge_id","target_knowledge_name","target_skill","error_match"]) { trace.put(key,preparation[key]) }
            let difficulty=tutorText(selected,"difficulty"); trace.put("difficulty_match",JsonString(if (difficulty.isEmpty()) { "同年级" } else { difficulty }))
            trace.put("question_type_difference",JsonBool(jsonText(selected,"question_type")!=jsonText(question,"question_type")))
            for (key in ["dense_rank","sparse_rank","fusion_rank"]) { trace.put(key,candidate[key]) }
            for (key in ["structure_similarity","reranker_score","mode_score"]) { trace.put(key,tutorObject(candidate,"score_trace")[key]) }
            savePracticeProof(tx,question,preparation,selected,trace,practiceAlignmentMethod(checks,jsonText(selected,"id")))
            tx.query("INSERT INTO practice_recommendations (id,user_id,mistake_id,dataset_question_id,mode,retrieval_rank,retrieval_trace,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(owner),Some(id),sqlValue(selected["id"]),Some(mode),sqlValue(candidate["fusion_rank"]),Some(trace.toString())])
            let result=practicePayload(accounts,selected,mode,trace,tutorArray(candidate,"knowledge_points")); if (debug) { result.put("debug",trace) }; return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/question_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.net.http.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

public func questionRoute(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 1]
    if (context.request.method == "GET") { return questionDetail(context, accounts, id) }
    if (context.request.method == "PATCH") { return questionEdit(context, accounts, id) }
    if (context.request.method != "DELETE") {
        context.responseBuilder.header("Allow", "GET, PATCH, DELETE")
        throw ApiError(405, "请求方法不支持")
    }
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
            let owned = tx.query("SELECT id FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE",
                [Some(id), user["id"]])
            if (owned.rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "内容不存在或无权访问") }
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'archived', updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",
                [Some(id)])
            tx.query("UPDATE review_plans SET status = 'cancelled', updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE question_id = ? AND status = 'pending'",
                [Some(id)])
            ()
        })
        let result = JsonObject()
        result.put("deleted", JsonBool(true))
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/question_bank_cli.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.env.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

// Deployment-only snapshots. No public HTTP route accepts dataset answers.
func questionBankImport(db: Database, body: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let source=(body["source"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    let key=optionalText(source,"key","",80,nonempty:true)
    if (key!="cmmu" && key!="cmm-math" && key!="gaokao" && key!="race" && key!="tal") { throw ApiError(422,"仅支持已配置的公开题源") }
    let rows=(body["questions"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow()
    if (rows.getItems().isEmpty() || rows.getItems().size>500) { throw ApiError(422,"每批需要1至500道题") }
    let seen=HashSet<String>()
    let strings=[("source_item_id",190),("split",30),("subject",30),("grade",30),("grade_band",30),("title",255),("question_text",200000),("question_type",60),("solution",200000),("difficulty",30),("content_hash",64),("quality_status",30)]
    for (value in rows.getItems()) {
        let item=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        for ((field,limit) in strings) { optionalText(item,field,"",limit,nonempty:field=="source_item_id" || field=="subject" || field=="question_text" || field=="content_hash") }
        let id=jsonText(item,"source_item_id")
        if (seen.contains(id)) { throw ApiError(422,"批次包含重复题目") }; seen.add(id)
        let hash=jsonText(item,"content_hash")
        if (hash.size!=64) { throw ApiError(422,"内容指纹无效") }
        for (byte in hash.toArray()) { if (!(byte>=48 && byte<=57 || byte>=97 && byte<=102)) { throw ApiError(422,"内容指纹无效") } }
        for (field in ["options","knowledge_points","image_paths","links"]) { if (!(item[field] is JsonArray)) { throw ApiError(422,"题目集合字段无效") } }
        if (!(item["source_metadata"] is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(422,"题目来源无效") }
        for (field in ["has_answer","has_solution","answer_verified","usable_for_practice","usable_for_auto_grade"]) { editBool(item[field]) }
        if (tutorFlag(item,"usable_for_practice") && (!tutorFlag(item,"has_answer") || tutorText(item,"quality_status")!="usable")) { throw ApiError(422,"未通过质量检查的题目不能用于练习") }
        if (tutorFlag(item,"usable_for_auto_grade") && !tutorFlag(item,"usable_for_practice")) { throw ApiError(422,"判题资格无效") }
        for (path in tutorArray(item,"image_paths").getItems()) { if (!(path is JsonString) || (path as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue().contains("..")) { throw ApiError(422,"题目图片路径无效") } }
    }
    let name=optionalText(source,"name","",160,nonempty:true); let version=optionalText(source,"version","",100,nonempty:true)
    let homepage=optionalText(source,"homepage","",500); let license=optionalText(source,"license","",100); let localPath=optionalText(source,"local_path","",500)
    return db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx =>
        tx.query("INSERT INTO dataset_sources (id,`key`,name,version,homepage,license,local_path,total_items,imported_items,source_metadata,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,0,0,JSON_OBJECT(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE name=VALUES(name),version=VALUES(version),homepage=VALUES(homepage),license=VALUES(license),local_path=VALUES(local_path)", [Some(newId()),Some(key),Some(name),Some(version),Some(homepage),Some(license),Some(localPath)])
        let sourceId=rowText(tx.query("SELECT id FROM dataset_sources WHERE `key`=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(key)]).rows[0],"id")
        for (value in rows.getItems()) {
            let item=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            let previous=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE dataset_source_id=? AND source_item_id=?",[Some(sourceId),sqlValue(item["source_item_id"])]).rows
            if (!previous.isEmpty()) {
                let saved=rowJson("dataset_questions",previous[0])
                // Re-importing the same source cannot silently re-enable a
                // quarantined question or discard its review evidence.
                let savedMetadata=tutorObject(saved,"source_metadata"); let review=tutorObject(savedMetadata,"format_review")
                let history=tutorArray(savedMetadata,"format_review_history")
                if (jsonText(saved,"content_hash")!=jsonText(item,"content_hash") && !tutorArray(review,"fields").getItems().isEmpty()) {
                    var recorded=false
                    for (entry in history.getItems()) { if (jsonText((entry as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"content_hash")==jsonText(review,"content_hash")) { recorded=true } }
                    if (!recorded) { history.add(review) }
                }
                if (!history.getItems().isEmpty()) { let metadata=tutorObject(item,"source_metadata"); metadata.put("format_review_history",history); item.put("source_metadata",metadata) }
                if (jsonText(saved,"content_hash")==jsonText(item,"content_hash") && !tutorArray(review,"fields").getItems().isEmpty()) {
                    let metadata=tutorObject(item,"source_metadata"); metadata.put("format_review",review); item.put("source_metadata",metadata)
                    item.put("quality_status",JsonString("format_review")); item.put("usable_for_practice",JsonBool(false)); item.put("usable_for_auto_grade",JsonBool(false))
                }
            }
            let fields="source_item_id,split,subject,grade,grade_band,title,question_text,question_type,options,answer,solution,knowledge_points,image_paths,difficulty,content_hash,source_metadata,has_answer,has_solution,answer_verified,quality_status,usable_for_practice,usable_for_auto_grade"
            let names=fields.split(","); let params=ArrayList<Option<String>>(); params.add(Some(newId())); params.add(Some(sourceId))
            let marks=StringBuilder(); let updates=StringBuilder(); var first=true
            for (field in names) {
                params.add(if (field=="answer") { Some(item[field].toString()) } else { sqlValue(item[field]) }); if (!first) { marks.append(","); updates.append(",") }; first=false
                marks.append("?"); updates.append("${field}=VALUES(${field})")
            }
            var unchanged=!previous.isEmpty()
            if (unchanged) {
                let saved=rowJson("dataset_questions",previous[0])
                for (field in names) { if (!jsonEquivalent(saved[field],item[field])) { unchanged=false; break } }
            }
            // Dataset memory is versioned by the stored row, including updated_at.
            if (!unchanged) { tx.query("INSERT INTO dataset_questions (id,dataset_source_id,${fields},created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,${marks.toString()},UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE ${updates.toString()},updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)",params.toArray()) }
            let id=rowText(tx.query("SELECT id FROM dataset_questions WHERE dataset_source_id=? AND source_item_id=?",[Some(sourceId),sqlValue(item["source_item_id"])]).rows[0],"id")
            tx.query("DELETE FROM question_knowledge_links WHERE dataset_question_id=?",[Some(id)])
            for (entry in tutorArray(item,"links").getItems()) {
                let link=(entry as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let code=optionalText(link,"code","",100,nonempty:true)
                let cards=tx.query("SELECT id FROM knowledge_points WHERE code=? AND subject=?",[Some(code),sqlValue(item["subject"])]).rows
                if (cards.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"知识卡不存在或学科不匹配") }
                let role=optionalText(link,"role","",30,nonempty:true)
                if (role!="primary" && role!="secondary") { throw ApiError(422,"知识映射角色无效") }
                let confidence=Float64.parse(link["confidence"].toString()); let method=optionalText(link,"method","",50,nonempty:true)
                if (!(confidence>=0.0 && confidence<=1.0) || method!="offline_rules" && method!="subject_grade_fallback") { throw ApiError(422,"知识映射依据无效") }
                tx.query("INSERT INTO question_knowledge_links (id,dataset_question_id,knowledge_point_id,role,confidence,mapping_method,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(rowText(cards[0],"id")),Some(role),Some(confidence.toString()),Some(method)])
            }
        }
        let count=rowText(tx.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM dataset_questions WHERE dataset_source_id=?",[Some(sourceId)]).rows[0],"count")
        tx.query("UPDATE dataset_sources SET total_items=?,imported_items=?,last_imported_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(count),Some(count),Some(sourceId)])
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("stored",JsonString(count)); result.put("source",JsonString(key)); return result
    })
}

// Deployment-only format reviews use the exported content hash as a CAS guard.
// Source text, answers, assets and student work are never rewritten here.
func questionBankFormatReview(db: Database, body: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let rows=tutorArray(body,"questions")
    if (rows.getItems().isEmpty() || rows.getItems().size>100) { throw ApiError(422,"每批需要1至100道题") }
    let seen=HashSet<String>()
    for (raw in rows.getItems()) {
        if (!(raw is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(422,"核验记录无效") }
        let item=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let id=optionalText(item,"id","",36,nonempty:true)
        if (seen.contains(id)) { throw ApiError(422,"核验记录重复") }; seen.add(id)
        let hash=optionalText(item,"content_hash","",64,nonempty:true)
        if (hash.size!=64) { throw ApiError(422,"内容指纹无效") }
        for (byte in hash.toArray()) { if (!(byte>=48 && byte<=57 || byte>=97 && byte<=102)) { throw ApiError(422,"内容指纹无效") } }
        if (!(item["fields"] is JsonArray)) { throw ApiError(422,"核验字段无效") }
        let fields=tutorArray(item,"fields"); if (fields.getItems().isEmpty() || fields.getItems().size>20) { throw ApiError(422,"核验字段无效") }
        for (entry in fields.getItems()) {
            if (!(entry is JsonString)) { throw ApiError(422,"核验字段无效") }
            let field=(entry as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
            if (field!="question" && field!="solution" && !(field.startsWith("option.") && field.size==8 && field.toArray()[7]>=65 && field.toArray()[7]<=72)) { throw ApiError(422,"核验字段无效") }
        }
    }
    return db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx =>
        var changed: Int64=0
        for (raw in rows.getItems()) {
            let item=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let id=jsonText(item,"id")
            let saved=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id)]).rows
            if (saved.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"题目不存在") }
            let value=rowJson("dataset_questions",saved[0])
            if (jsonText(value,"content_hash")!=jsonText(item,"content_hash")) { throw ApiError(409,"题目已更新，请重新核验") }
            let metadata=tutorObject(value,"source_metadata"); let review=JsonObject()
            review.put("content_hash",item["content_hash"]); review.put("fields",item["fields"])
            if (!jsonEquivalent(metadata.get("format_review") ?? JsonNull(),review) || jsonText(value,"quality_status")!="format_review" || tutorFlag(value,"usable_for_practice") || tutorFlag(value,"usable_for_auto_grade")) {
                metadata.put("format_review",review)
                tx.query("UPDATE dataset_questions SET source_metadata=?,quality_status='format_review',usable_for_practice=0,usable_for_auto_grade=0,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(metadata.toString()),Some(id)]); changed++
            }
        }
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("changed",JsonInt(changed)); return result
    })
}

func questionBankCommand(): Int64 {
    try {
        let raw=getStdIn().readln() ?? ""
        if (raw.isEmpty() || raw.size>16000000) { throw ApiError(422,"题库命令输入无效或过大") }
        let body=(JsonValue.fromStr(raw) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        if ((getVariable("CANGJIE_DB_NAME") ?? "").isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"必须显式配置独立仓颉数据库") }
        try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version='009_task_sources'").rows.size!=1) { throw ApiError(409,"请先初始化或升级仓颉数据库") }
            let operation=optionalText(body,"operation","",30,nonempty:true)
            if (operation=="import") { println(questionBankImport(db,body).toString()); return 0 }
            if (operation=="review-format") { println(questionBankFormatReview(db,body).toString()); return 0 }
            if (operation=="audit-export") {
                let after=optionalText(body,"after","",36); let output=JsonArray()
                for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id>? ORDER BY id LIMIT 100",[Some(after)]).rows) { output.add(rowJson("dataset_questions",row)) }
                println(output.toString()); return 0
            }
            if (operation=="cards") {
                let output=JsonArray(); for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points ORDER BY code").rows) { output.add(rowJson("knowledge_points",row)) }
                println(output.toString()); return 0
            }
            if (operation=="export") {
                let source=optionalText(body,"source","",80,nonempty:true); let after=optionalText(body,"after","",36)
                let rows=db.query("SELECT q.* FROM dataset_questions q JOIN dataset_sources s ON s.id=q.dataset_source_id WHERE s.`key`=? AND q.id>? AND q.usable_for_practice=1 ORDER BY q.id LIMIT 100",[Some(source),Some(after)]).rows
                let output=JsonArray(); for (row in rows) { output.add(rowJson("dataset_questions",row)) }
                println(output.toString()); return 0
            }
            throw ApiError(422,"未知题库操作")
        }
    } catch (error: ApiError) {
        let output=JsonObject(); output.put("code",JsonInt(Int64(error.status))); output.put("message",JsonString(error.message)); println(output.toString()); return 1
    } catch (_: Exception) { println(##"{"code":503,"message":"题库操作未完成，请检查输入及独立仓颉环境；当前批次未提交"}"##); return 1 }
    return 1
}
```
