### backend-cangjie/src/question_detail.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func queryArray(db: Database, table: String, sql: String, id: String): JsonArray {
    let result = JsonArray()
    for (row in db.query(sql, [Some(id)]).rows) { result.getItems().add(rowJson(table, row)) }
    return result
}

func firstJson(array: JsonArray): JsonValue {
    if (array.getItems().isEmpty()) { return JsonNull() }
    return array.getItems()[0]
}

func jsonText(object: JsonObject, key: String): String {
    return (object[key] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
}

func assetCrop(assets: JsonArray): JsonValue {
    for (item in assets.getItems()) {
        let asset = (item as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        if (jsonText(asset, "kind") != "crop") { continue }
        try {
            let bbox = (asset["bbox"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()
            if (bbox.size != 4) { return JsonNull() }
            let x = Float64.parse(bbox[0].toString())
            let y = Float64.parse(bbox[1].toString())
            let right = Float64.parse(bbox[2].toString())
            let bottom = Float64.parse(bbox[3].toString())
            if (!(x >= 0.0 && y >= 0.0 && right > x && bottom > y && right <= 1.0 && bottom <= 1.0)) { return JsonNull() }
            let result = JsonArray()
            for (value in [x, y, right - x, bottom - y]) { result.getItems().add(JsonFloat(value)) }
            return result
        } catch (_: Exception) { return JsonNull() }
    }
    return JsonNull()
}

public func questionDetail(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, id: String): JsonValue {
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        // All dependent rows belong to one database snapshot.
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "内容不存在或无权访问") }
            let result = rowJson("questions", rows[0])
            let assets = queryArray(tx, "question_assets", "SELECT * FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ?", id)
            let figures = JsonArray()
            let working = JsonArray()
            let annotations = JsonArray()
            for (item in assets.getItems()) {
                let asset = (item as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                asset.put("url", JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/" + jsonText(asset, "path"))))
                match (jsonText(asset, "kind")) {
                    case "question_figure" => figures.getItems().add(asset)
                    case "student_working_figure" => working.getItems().add(asset)
                    case "teacher_annotation_region" => annotations.getItems().add(asset)
                    case _ => ()
                }
            }
            result.put("assets", assets)
            result.put("question_figures", figures)
            result.put("student_working_figures", working)
            result.put("teacher_annotation_regions", annotations)
            result.put("crop_box", assetCrop(assets))
            let ocr = queryArray(tx, "ocr_runs", "SELECT * FROM ocr_runs WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC", id)
            var selected = firstJson(ocr)
            for (item in ocr.getItems()) {
                if (jsonText((item as JsonObject).getOrThrow(), "stream") == "qwen_crop") { selected = item; break }
            }
            result.put("ocr", selected)
            result.put("ocr_runs", ocr)
            let analyses = queryArray(tx, "problem_analyses", "SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id = ? LIMIT 1", id)
            let analysis = firstJson(analyses)
            result.put("analysis", analysis)
            result.put("reference_steps", if (let Some(object) <- (analysis as JsonObject)) {
                queryArray(tx, "reference_steps", "SELECT * FROM reference_steps WHERE analysis_id = ? ORDER BY step_index", jsonText(object, "id"))
            } else { JsonArray() })
            let diagnoses = queryArray(tx, "diagnoses", "SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", id)
            let diagnosis = firstJson(diagnoses)
            if (let Some(object) <- (diagnosis as JsonObject)) {
                let evidence = queryArray(tx, "diagnosis_evidence", "SELECT * FROM diagnosis_evidence WHERE diagnosis_id = ? ORDER BY COALESCE(CAST(JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(metadata_json,'$.index')) AS UNSIGNED),10000),created_at,id", jsonText(object, "id"))
                let steps = JsonArray()
                var counterfactual: JsonValue = JsonNull()
                var found = false
                for (item in evidence.getItems()) {
                    let entry = (item as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                    let kind = jsonText(entry, "kind")
                    if (kind == "step_alignment") { steps.getItems().add(entry) }
                    if (kind == "counterfactual_validation" && !found) { counterfactual = entry["metadata_json"]; found = true }
                    if (kind == "eced_assessment") { object.put("evidence_diagnosis",entry["metadata_json"]) }
                }
                object.put("evidence", steps)
                object.put("counterfactual", safeCounterfactual(counterfactual,diagnosis:Some(object)))
            }
            result.put("diagnosis", diagnosis)
            result.put("student_steps", queryArray(tx, "student_steps", "SELECT s.* FROM student_steps s WHERE s.work_id = (SELECT id FROM student_works WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1) ORDER BY s.step_index", id))
            result.put("teacher_marks", queryArray(tx, "teacher_marks", "SELECT * FROM teacher_marks WHERE question_id = ?", id))
            result.put("processing_job", firstJson(queryArray(tx, "processing_jobs", "SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE question_id = ? AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') AND status IN ('pending','running') ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1", id)))
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/question_edit.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func invalidateQuestionLearning(db: Database, id: String): Unit {
    for (sql in [
        "DELETE e FROM diagnosis_evidence e JOIN diagnoses d ON e.diagnosis_id = d.id WHERE d.question_id = ?",
        "DELETE FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id = ?",
        "DELETE s FROM reference_steps s JOIN problem_analyses a ON s.analysis_id = a.id WHERE a.question_id = ?",
        "DELETE FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id = ?",
        "DELETE FROM user_question_knowledge_links WHERE question_id = ?",
        "DELETE FROM practice_recommendations WHERE mistake_id = ?",
        "UPDATE tutor_sessions SET state = JSON_SET(IF(JSON_TYPE(state) = 'OBJECT', state, JSON_OBJECT()), '$.question_changed', CAST('true' AS JSON)), updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE question_id = ?",
        "UPDATE review_plans SET status = 'cancelled', updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE question_id = ? AND status = 'pending'",
        "UPDATE dataset_practice_attempts SET grading_details = JSON_SET(IF(JSON_TYPE(grading_details) = 'OBJECT', grading_details, JSON_OBJECT()), '$.question_changed', CAST('true' AS JSON)), updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE mistake_id = ?",
        "UPDATE review_records r JOIN review_plans p ON r.plan_id = p.id SET r.grading_details = JSON_SET(IF(JSON_TYPE(r.grading_details) = 'OBJECT', r.grading_details, JSON_OBJECT()), '$.question_changed', CAST('true' AS JSON)), r.updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE p.question_id = ?"
    ]) { db.query(sql, [Some(id)]) }
}

public func questionEdit(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, id: String): JsonValue {
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let body = readJson(context, maxBytes: 8 * 1024 * 1024)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "内容不存在或无权访问") }
            if (rowText(rows[0], "status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409, "题目已归档，不能修改") }
            let edits = validateQuestionEdit(body)
            let existing = rowJson("questions", rows[0])
            if (edits.get("options").isSome() || edits.get("question_type").isSome()) {
                let kind=((edits.get("question_type") ?? existing["question_type"]) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                let options=((edits.get("options") ?? existing["options"]) as JsonArray).getOrThrow()
                if ((kind=="单选题" || kind=="多选题") && options.getItems().size<2) { throw ApiError(422,"选择题至少需要两个不同选项") }
            }
            var changed = false
            var anyChanged = false
            for ((key, value) in edits.getFields()) {
                if (!jsonEquivalent(existing[key], value)) { anyChanged = true }
            }
            for (key in ["question_text", "student_answer", "subject", "grade", "formulas", "options", "question_type", "knowledge_points"]) {
                if (let Some(value) <- edits.get(key)) {
                    if (!jsonEquivalent(existing[key], value)) { changed = true }
                }
            }
            if (changed) { invalidateQuestionLearning(tx, id) }
            let sql = StringBuilder()
            let params = ArrayList<Option<String>>()
            sql.append("UPDATE questions SET updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP()")
            // Column names come only from validateQuestionEdit's fixed whitelist.
            for ((key, value) in edits.getFields()) {
                sql.append(", `${key}` = ?")
                if (value is JsonNull) { params.add(None) }
                else if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) { params.add(Some(text.getValue())) }
                else if (let Some(flag) <- (value as JsonBool)) { params.add(Some(if (flag.getValue()) { "1" } else { "0" })) }
                else { params.add(Some(value.toString())) }
            }
            if (changed) { sql.append(", status = IF(confirmed, 'confirmed', 'needs_confirmation')") }
            sql.append(" WHERE id = ?")
            params.add(Some(id))
            if (anyChanged) { tx.query(sql.toString(), params.toArray()) }
            return rowJson("questions", tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)]).rows[0])
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/question_edit_validation.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

func stripWhitespace(value: String): String {
    let characters = ArrayList<String>()
    for (rune in value.runes()) {
        let one = StringBuilder(); one.append(rune); characters.add(one.toString())
    }
    var start: Int64 = 0
    var end = characters.size
    while (start < end && pythonWhitespace.contains(characters[start])) { start++ }
    while (end > start && pythonWhitespace.contains(characters[end - 1])) { end-- }
    let result = StringBuilder()
    for (index in start..end) { result.append(characters[index]) }
    return result.toString()
}

func editText(value: JsonValue, limit: Int64, minimum!: Int64 = 0, trim!: Bool = false, nullable!: Bool = false): JsonValue {
    if (nullable && value is JsonNull) { return value }
    if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) {
        let raw = if (trim) { stripWhitespace(text.getValue()) } else { text.getValue() }
        let count = runeCount(raw)
        if (count >= minimum && count <= limit) { return JsonString(raw) }
    }
    throw ApiError(422, "输入文字格式或长度不正确")
}

func editNumber(value: JsonValue): Float64 {
    try {
        let number = if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) { Float64.parse(text.getValue()) }
            else if (let Some(flag) <- (value as JsonBool)) { if (flag.getValue()) { 1.0 } else { 0.0 } }
            else if (value is JsonInt || value is JsonFloat) { Float64.parse(value.toString()) }
            else { throw ApiError(422, "数字格式不正确") }
        if (number >= -1.7976931348623157e308 && number <= 1.7976931348623157e308) { return number }
    } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422, "数字格式不正确") }
    throw ApiError(422, "数字必须有限")
}

func editBool(value: JsonValue): JsonBool {
    if (let Some(flag) <- (value as JsonBool)) { return flag }
    if (value is JsonInt || value is JsonFloat) {
        let number = editNumber(value)
        if (number == 0.0 || number == 1.0) { return JsonBool(number == 1.0) }
    }
    if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) {
        let word = text.getValue().toAsciiLower()
        for (yes in ["1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"]) { if (word == yes) { return JsonBool(true) } }
        for (no in ["0", "false", "f", "no", "n", "off"]) { if (word == no) { return JsonBool(false) } }
    }
    throw ApiError(422, "收藏状态格式不正确")
}

func validateEditSteps(array: JsonArray, teacher: Bool): Unit {
    for (item in array.getItems()) {
        if (item is JsonString) { editText(item, 12000); continue }
        if (let Some(object) <- (item as JsonObject)) {
            let texts = if (teacher) { ["comment"] } else { ["text", "raw_text", "normalized_text", "formula"] }
            for (key in texts) { if (let Some(value) <- object.get(key)) { editText(value, 12000) } }
            let small = if (teacher) { "mark_type" } else { "source" }
            if (let Some(value) <- object.get(small)) { editText(value, 50) }
            if (let Some(value) <- object.get("confidence")) {
                let number = editNumber(value)
                if (!(number >= 0.0 && number <= 1.0)) { throw ApiError(422, "置信度超出范围") }
            }
            if (let Some(value) <- object.get("bbox")) {
                if (let Some(box) <- (value as JsonArray)) {
                    if (box.getItems().size > 4) { throw ApiError(422, "坐标数量不正确") }
                    for (number in box.getItems()) { editNumber(number) }
                } else { throw ApiError(422, "坐标格式不正确") }
            }
        } else { throw ApiError(422, "步骤格式不正确") }
    }
}

public func validateQuestionEdit(body: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let result = JsonObject()
    for ((key, value) in body.getFields()) {
        match (key) {
            case "question_text" => result.put(key, editText(value, 12000, minimum: 1, trim: true))
            case "student_answer" => result.put(key, editText(value, 12000, nullable: true))
            case "grade" => result.put(key, editText(value, 30, nullable: true))
            case "subject" => result.put(key, editText(value, 30, minimum: 1, trim: true))
            case "question_type" => result.put(key, editText(value, 50, minimum: 1))
            case "title" => result.put(key, editText(value, 255))
            case "favorite" => result.put(key, editBool(value))
            case "tags" | "formulas" | "knowledge_points" | "options" | "student_steps" | "teacher_marks" =>
                let max = if (key == "options") { 26 } else if (key == "tags" || key == "knowledge_points") { 50 } else { 100 }
                let array = manualArray(body, key, max)
                let clean = JsonArray()
                let optionLabels=HashSet<String>()
                if (key == "student_steps" || key == "teacher_marks") {
                    // The original PATCH validates these but changes work only via dedicated endpoints.
                    validateEditSteps(array, key == "teacher_marks")
                } else {
                    for (item in array.getItems()) {
                        if (key == "options") {
                            if (let Some(option) <- (item as JsonObject)) {
                                let entry = JsonObject()
                                let label = option.get("label") ?? JsonNull()
                                let text = option.get("text") ?? JsonNull()
                                entry.put("label", editText(label, 8, minimum: 1, trim: true))
                                let optionLabel=(entry["label"] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                                if (optionLabels.contains(optionLabel)) { throw ApiError(422,"选项标记不能重复") }
                                optionLabels.add(optionLabel)
                                entry.put("text", editText(text, 12000))
                                clean.getItems().add(entry)
                            } else { throw ApiError(422, "选项格式不正确") }
                        } else if (key == "formulas") { clean.getItems().add(editText(item, 12000)) }
                        else { clean.getItems().add(editText(item, 30, minimum: 1, trim: true)) }
                    }
                    result.put(key, clean)
                }
            case _ => ()
        }
    }
    return result
}

func jsonEquivalent(left: JsonValue, right: JsonValue): Bool {
    if (let Some(a) <- (left as JsonObject)) {
        if (let Some(b) <- (right as JsonObject)) {
            if (a.getFields().size != b.getFields().size) { return false }
            for ((key, value) in a.getFields()) {
                if (let Some(other) <- b.get(key)) { if (!jsonEquivalent(value, other)) { return false } }
                else { return false }
            }
            return true
        }
        return false
    }
    if (let Some(a) <- (left as JsonArray)) {
        if (let Some(b) <- (right as JsonArray)) {
            if (a.getItems().size != b.getItems().size) { return false }
            for (i in 0..a.getItems().size) { if (!jsonEquivalent(a[i], b[i])) { return false } }
            return true
        }
        return false
    }
    return left.toString() == right.toString()
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/question_list.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import std.fs.*
import std.unicode.UnicodeStringExtension
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.encoding.url.*
import stdx.net.http.*

let errorTaxonomy = (JsonValue.fromStr(String.fromUtf8(File.readFrom("schema/error_taxonomy.json"))) as JsonArray).getOrThrow()
let pythonWhitespace = " \t\n\r\u{b}\u{c}\u{1c}\u{1d}\u{1e}\u{1f}\u{85}\u{a0}\u{1680}\u{2000}\u{2001}\u{2002}\u{2003}\u{2004}\u{2005}\u{2006}\u{2007}\u{2008}\u{2009}\u{200a}\u{2028}\u{2029}\u{202f}\u{205f}\u{3000}"

func questionDisplayKey(text: String, id: String): String {
    let clean = StringBuilder()
    var hasContent = false
    for (rune in text.runes()) {
        let one = StringBuilder()
        one.append(rune)
        let character = one.toString()
        if (pythonWhitespace.contains(character)) { continue }
        hasContent = true
        if (!"，。？?：:".contains(character)) { clean.append(rune) }
    }
    return if (hasContent) { clean.toString().toLower() } else { "pending:${id}" }
}

func queryLast(form: Form, key: String): String {
    let values = form.getAll(key)
    return if (values.isEmpty()) { "" } else { values[values.size - 1] }
}

public func questionList(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "GET")
    let form = try { Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? "") }
        catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422, "查询参数格式不正确") }
    let subject = queryLast(form, "subject")
    let search = queryLast(form, "search")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let sql = StringBuilder()
        let params = ArrayList<Option<String>>([user["id"]])
        sql.append("SELECT q.* FROM questions q WHERE q.user_id = ? AND q.status <> 'archived'")
        if (!subject.isEmpty()) { sql.append(" AND q.subject = ?"); params.add(Some(subject)) }
        if (!search.isEmpty()) {
            sql.append(" AND (q.question_text LIKE ? OR q.title LIKE ? OR CAST(q.knowledge_points AS CHAR) LIKE ? OR CAST(q.tags AS CHAR) LIKE ? OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM diagnoses d WHERE d.question_id = q.id AND (d.root_misconception LIKE ? OR d.error_type LIKE ? OR d.first_error_text LIKE ?")
            for (_ in 0..7) { params.add(Some("%${search}%")) }
            let codes = HashSet<String>()
            for (item in errorTaxonomy.getItems()) {
                let pair = (item as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()
                let code = (pair[0] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                let label = (pair[1] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                if ((search.contains(label) || label.contains(search)) && !codes.contains(code)) {
                    codes.add(code)
                    sql.append(" OR d.error_type = ?")
                    params.add(Some(code))
                }
            }
            sql.append(")))")
        }
        sql.append(" ORDER BY q.created_at DESC")
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let result = JsonArray()
            let seen = HashSet<String>()
            for (row in tx.query(sql.toString(), params.toArray()).rows) {
                let id = rowText(row, "id")
                let key = questionDisplayKey(row["question_text"] ?? "", id)
                if (seen.contains(key)) { continue }
                seen.add(key)
                let item = rowJson("questions", row)
                let diagnosis = tx.query("SELECT root_misconception FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
                item.put("diagnosis_summary", JsonString(if (diagnosis.isEmpty()) { "等待完成错因诊断" } else { rowText(diagnosis[0], "root_misconception") }))
                let review = tx.query("SELECT due_at FROM review_plans WHERE question_id = ? AND user_id = ? AND status = 'pending' ORDER BY due_at LIMIT 1", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
                item.put("review_due_at", if (review.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { rowJson("review_plans", review[0])["due_at"] })
                var knowledge = row["knowledge_points"] ?? "[]"
                if (knowledge == "null" || (JsonValue.fromStr(knowledge) as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems().isEmpty()) { knowledge = "[\"__none__\"]" }
                let mastery = tx.query("SELECT mastery_score FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id = ? AND subject = ? AND JSON_CONTAINS(?, JSON_QUOTE(knowledge_point)) ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 1", [user["id"], row["subject"], Some(knowledge)]).rows
                let score = if (mastery.isEmpty()) { 0.0 } else { Float64.parse(rowText(mastery[0], "mastery_score")) }
                item.put("mastery_score", JsonFloat(score))
                item.put("mastery_status", JsonString(if (score >= 0.75) { "基本稳定" } else if (score >= 0.5) { "继续巩固" } else { "需要巩固" }))
                let asset = tx.query("SELECT path FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND kind IN ('crop','original') ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
                item.put("thumbnail_url", if (asset.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/" + rowText(asset[0], "path"))) })
                let job = tx.query("SELECT id,status FROM processing_jobs WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
                item.put("latest_job_id", if (job.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(rowText(job[0], "id")) })
                item.put("latest_job_status", if (job.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(rowText(job[0], "status")) })
                result.getItems().add(item)
            }
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/recognition_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.net.http.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

public func startRecognition(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, synchronous: Bool): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let started = db.transaction<(JsonObject, Option<String>)>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT status FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty() || rowText(rows[0], "status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(404, "题目不存在或已归档") }
            let active = tx.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE question_id = ? AND status IN ('pending','running') AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
            if (!active.isEmpty()) { return (rowJson("processing_jobs", active[0]), None) }
            let assets = tx.query("SELECT path FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND kind = 'crop' ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
            if (assets.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422, "请先框选题目，生成裁剪图片后再识别") }
            let jobId = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO processing_jobs (id,question_id,kind,status,progress,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,'pending',0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(jobId), Some(id), Some(if (synchronous) { "ocr" } else { "reprocess" })])
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'processing',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)])
            return (rowJson("processing_jobs", tx.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE id = ?", [Some(jobId)]).rows[0]), Some(rowText(assets[0], "path")))
        })
        let jobId = jsonText(started[0], "id")
        if (let Some(path) <- started[1]) {
            if (synchronous) { runRecognition(jobId, id, path, requireCrop: true) }
            else { spawn { runRecognition(jobId, id, path, requireCrop: true) } }
        }
        let result = JsonObject()
        if (synchronous) {
            result.put("job", rowJson("processing_jobs", db.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE id = ?", [Some(jobId)]).rows[0]))
            result.put("question", questionDetail(context, accounts, id))
        } else { result.put("job_id", JsonString(jobId)) }
        if (started[1].isNone()) { result.put("resumed", JsonBool(true)) }
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/recognition_worker.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.base64.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

func recognitionFingerprint(row: HashMap<String, Option<String>>): String {
    let value = JsonArray()
    for (key in ["question_text", "student_answer", "subject", "grade", "formulas", "options", "question_type", "knowledge_points", "confirmed"]) {
        value.getItems().add(if (let Some(text) <- row[key]) { JsonString(text) } else { JsonNull() })
    }
    return sha256(value.toString().toArray())
}

func nullableJsonText(object: JsonObject, key: String): Option<String> {
    if (let Some(value) <- object.get(key)) {
        if (value is JsonNull) { return None }
        return Some((value as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue())
    }
    return None
}

func collapseWhitespace(value: String): String {
    let result = StringBuilder()
    var space = false
    var hasText = false
    for (rune in value.runes()) {
        let one = StringBuilder(); one.append(rune)
        if (pythonWhitespace.contains(one.toString())) { space = hasText }
        else {
            if (space) { result.append(" ") }
            result.append(rune); space = false; hasText = true
        }
    }
    return result.toString()
}

// Display summaries must never cut a TeX delimiter or a mathematical step.
func academicSummary(value: String, limit: Int64): String {
    return if (value.contains("$") || value.contains("\\")) { value } else { shortTitle(value, limit) }
}

func shortTitle(value: String, limit: Int64): String {
    let builder = StringBuilder()
    var count: Int64 = 0
    var mathSeen = false
    var prefix = ""
    var truncated = false
    for (rune in value.runes()) {
        if (count >= limit) { truncated = true; break }
        let one = StringBuilder(); one.append(rune)
        if (!mathSeen && (one.toString() == "$" || one.toString() == "\\")) {
            mathSeen = true; prefix = builder.toString()
        }
        builder.append(rune); count++
    }
    if (truncated && mathSeen) { return if (stripWhitespace(prefix).isEmpty()) { "题目详情" } else { prefix + "…" } }
    return if (count == 0) { "待确认错题" } else { builder.toString() }
}

func finishRecognitionFailure(jobId: String, questionId: String, fingerprint: String, recovered!: Bool = false): Unit {
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
            let questions = tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(questionId)]).rows
            let jobs = tx.query("SELECT status FROM processing_jobs WHERE id = ? AND question_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(jobId), Some(questionId)]).rows
            if (questions.isEmpty() || jobs.isEmpty()) { return }
            let status = rowText(jobs[0], "status")
            if (status != "pending" && status != "running") { return }
            let cancelled = rowText(questions[0], "status") == "archived" || (!fingerprint.isEmpty() && recognitionFingerprint(questions[0]) != fingerprint)
            tx.query("UPDATE processing_jobs SET status = ?, error = ?, updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",
                [Some(if (cancelled) { "cancelled" } else { "failed" }), Some(if (cancelled) { "题目已修改或归档，识别结果未应用" } else if (recovered) { "识别任务因服务重启中断，请重试" } else { "识别暂时未完成，请检查图片后重试" }), Some(jobId)])
            let questionStatus = rowText(questions[0], "status")
            if (!cancelled && (!recovered || questionStatus == "processing" || questionStatus == "pending" || questionStatus == "awaiting_crop")) {
                tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'partial', updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(questionId)])
            }
            ()
        })
    } catch (_: Exception) { /* Startup recovery handles unavailable database failures. */ }
}

public func runRecognition(jobId: String, questionId: String, imagePath: String, requireCrop!: Bool = false): Unit {
    let files = ArrayList<String>()
    var committed = false
    var fingerprint = ""
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let started = db.transaction<Option<String>>({ tx =>
            let questions = tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(questionId)]).rows
            let jobs = tx.query("SELECT status FROM processing_jobs WHERE id = ? AND question_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(jobId), Some(questionId)]).rows
            if (questions.isEmpty() || jobs.isEmpty() || rowText(jobs[0], "status") != "pending") { return None }
            if (rowText(questions[0], "status") == "archived") {
                tx.query("UPDATE processing_jobs SET status = 'cancelled', error = '题目已归档', updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(jobId)])
                return None
            }
            let sources = tx.query("SELECT id FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND path = ? AND kind = ?", [Some(questionId), Some(imagePath), Some(if (requireCrop) { "crop" } else { "original" })]).rows
            if (sources.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422, "识别图片不存在") }
            tx.query("UPDATE processing_jobs SET status = 'running', progress = 15, error = NULL, updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(jobId)])
            return Some(recognitionFingerprint(questions[0]))
        })
        if (started.isNone()) { return }
        let expected = started.getOrThrow()
        fingerprint = expected
        let input = JsonObject()
        input.put("operation", JsonString("preprocess"))
        input.put("image_base64", JsonString(toBase64String(readMedia(imagePath))))
        let prepared = algorithmCompute(input)
        db.query("UPDATE processing_jobs SET progress = 45, updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ? AND status = 'running'", [Some(jobId)])
        input.put("operation", JsonString("recognize"))
        let computed = algorithmCompute(input)
        let recognition = (computed["recognition"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let assets = ArrayList<JsonObject>()
        for (collection in [(prepared["assets"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow(), (computed["assets"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow()]) {
            for (item in collection.getItems()) {
                let asset = (item as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                let kind = jsonText(asset, "kind")
                if (!(kind == "clean" || kind == "red_mask" || kind == "overlay" || kind == "question_figure" || kind == "student_working_figure" || kind == "teacher_annotation_region")) { throw ApiError(503, "图片结果类型不正确") }
                let path = "uploads/${questionId}/derived/" + newId() + if (kind == "red_mask") { ".png" } else { ".jpg" }
                writeMedia(path, algorithmImage(asset))
                files.add(path)
                asset.put("path", JsonString(path))
                assets.add(asset)
            }
        }
        committed = db.transaction<Bool>({ tx =>
            let questions = tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(questionId)]).rows
            let jobs = tx.query("SELECT status FROM processing_jobs WHERE id = ? AND question_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(jobId), Some(questionId)]).rows
            if (questions.isEmpty() || jobs.isEmpty() || rowText(jobs[0], "status") != "running") { return false }
            if (rowText(questions[0], "status") == "archived" || recognitionFingerprint(questions[0]) != expected) {
                tx.query("UPDATE processing_jobs SET status = 'cancelled', error = '题目已修改或归档，识别结果未应用', updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(jobId)])
                return false
            }
            invalidateQuestionLearning(tx, questionId)
            tx.query("DELETE FROM teacher_marks WHERE question_id = ?", [Some(questionId)])
            tx.query("DELETE FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND kind IN ('question_figure','student_working_figure','teacher_annotation_region')", [Some(questionId)])
            for (asset in assets) {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO question_assets (id,question_id,kind,path,sha256,role,bbox,description,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                    [Some(newId()), Some(questionId), Some(jsonText(asset, "kind")), Some(jsonText(asset, "path")), Some(jsonText(asset, "sha256")), nullableJsonText(asset, "role"), Some((asset.get("bbox") ?? JsonArray()).toString()), nullableJsonText(asset, "description")])
            }
            let confidence = recognition["recognition_confidence"].toString()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO ocr_runs (id,question_id,provider,raw_result,confidence,stream,status,model_name,duration_ms,error,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'qwen-pool',?,?,'qwen_crop','completed','Qwen',?,NULL,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                [Some(newId()), Some(questionId), Some(recognition.toString()), Some(confidence), Some(computed["duration_ms"].toString())])
            let text = jsonText(recognition, "question_text")
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET confirmed = 0, question_text = ?, student_answer = ?, subject = ?, grade = ?, question_type = ?, formulas = JSON_ARRAY(), options = ?, title = ?, status = 'needs_confirmation', updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",
                [Some(text), nullableJsonText(recognition, "student_answer"), Some(jsonText(recognition, "subject")), nullableJsonText(recognition, "grade"), Some(jsonText(recognition, "question_type")), Some(recognition["options"].toString()), Some(shortTitle(text, 60)), Some(questionId)])
            let work = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO student_works (id,question_id,user_id,answer,explanation,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,'',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))", [Some(work), Some(questionId), questions[0]["user_id"], Some(nullableJsonText(recognition, "student_answer") ?? "")])
            for (item in (recognition["student_steps"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) {
                let step = (item as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                let stepText = jsonText(step, "text")
                let index = Int64.parse(step["index"].toString()) - 1
                tx.query("INSERT INTO student_steps (id,work_id,step_index,text,bbox,raw_text,normalized_text,formula,confidence,source,user_confirmed,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),?,?,?,?,'qwen_crop',0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                    [Some(newId()), Some(work), Some(index.toString()), Some(stepText), Some(stepText), Some(collapseWhitespace(stepText)), Some(if (stepText.contains("=")) { stepText } else { "" }), Some(confidence)])
            }
            for (item in (recognition["teacher_annotations"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) {
                let mark = (item as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                tx.query("INSERT INTO teacher_marks (id,question_id,mark_type,comment,bbox,confidence,source,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),?,'qwen_crop',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                    [Some(newId()), Some(questionId), Some(jsonText(mark, "type")), Some(jsonText(mark, "text")), Some(confidence)])
            }
            tx.query("UPDATE processing_jobs SET status = 'completed', progress = 100, error = NULL, updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(jobId)])
            return true
        })
    } catch (_: Exception) { finishRecognitionFailure(jobId, questionId, fingerprint) }
    finally { if (!committed) { for (path in files) { try { removeMedia(path) } catch (_: Exception) {} } } }
}

public func recoverRecognitionJobs(): Unit {
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let jobs = db.query("SELECT id,question_id FROM processing_jobs WHERE status IN ('pending','running') AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') ORDER BY question_id,id").rows
        for (job in jobs) { finishRecognitionFailure(rowText(job, "id"), rowText(job, "question_id"), "", recovered: true) }
    } catch (_: Exception) { /* Database readiness is reported separately. */ }
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/request_fields.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

func queryCount(db: Database, sql: String, params: Array<Option<String>>): Int64 {
    return Int64.parse(rowText(db.query(sql,params).rows[0],"count"))
}

func optionalText(body: JsonObject, key: String, fallback: String, maximum: Int64, nonempty!: Bool=false): String {
    var text=fallback
    if (let Some(value) <- body.get(key)) {
        if (let Some(raw) <- (value as JsonString)) { text=raw.getValue() }
        else { throw ApiError(422,"${key} 必须是文字") }
    }
    if (runeCount(text)>maximum || (nonempty && stripWhitespace(text).isEmpty())) { throw ApiError(422,"${key} 长度不正确") }
    return text
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/retrieval.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*

func retrievalGrade(grade: String, band: String): String {
    let clean=stripWhitespace(grade)
    for ((prefix,label) in [("七年级","初一"),("八年级","初二"),("九年级","初三"),("初中","初中"),("高中","高中")]) {
        if (clean.startsWith(prefix)) { return label }
    }
    return if (clean.isEmpty()) { band } else { clean }
}

func usableRetrievalCandidate(source: JsonObject, candidate: JsonObject, kind: String): Bool {
    if (tutorFlag(datasetImageLayout(candidate),"review")) { return false }
    if (tutorText(source,"subject")!=tutorText(candidate,"subject") || !tutorFlag(candidate,"usable_for_practice") || !tutorFlag(candidate,"has_answer") || stripWhitespace(tutorAnswerText(candidate["answer"])).isEmpty()) { return false }
    if (kind=="practice" && !tutorFlag(candidate,"usable_for_auto_grade")) { return false }
    let raw=tutorText(source,"grade"); let band=if (raw.startsWith("高")) { "高中" } else { "初中" }
    let grade=retrievalGrade(tutorText(candidate,"grade"),tutorText(candidate,"grade_band"))
    return grade==retrievalGrade(raw,band) || grade==band
}

func retrievalPreparationInput(db: Database, question: JsonObject, analysis: JsonValue, kind: String, mode: String): JsonObject {
    let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("retrieval_prepare")); input.put("retrieval_source",question); input.put("retrieval_analysis",analysis)
    input.put("retrieval_kind",JsonString(kind)); input.put("retrieval_mode",JsonString(mode))
    input.put("retrieval_diagnosis",firstJson(queryArray(db,"diagnoses","SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",jsonText(question,"id"))))
    // Missing diagnoses are represented by an empty snapshot, never an ORM query in Python.
    if (input["retrieval_diagnosis"] is JsonNull) { input.put("retrieval_diagnosis",JsonObject()) }
    input.put("retrieval_cards",queryArray(db,"knowledge_points","SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE subject=?",jsonText(question,"subject")))
    return input
}

func retrievalPreparation(db: Database, question: JsonObject, analysis: JsonValue, kind: String, mode: String): JsonObject {
    return algorithmCompute(retrievalPreparationInput(db,question,analysis,kind,mode))
}

func retrievalCandidates(db: Database, question: JsonObject, preparation: JsonObject, excluded: JsonArray, kind: String, mode: String): JsonObject {
    let search=JsonObject(); search.put("operation",JsonString("retrieval_search")); search.put("retrieval_query",preparation["query"]); search.put("subject",question["subject"])
    let grade=tutorText(question,"grade"); search.put("grade",JsonString(grade)); search.put("grade_band",JsonString(if (grade.startsWith("高")) { "高中" } else { "初中" }))
    let hits=tutorArray(algorithmCompute(search),"hits"); let candidates=JsonArray()
    for (value in hits.getItems()) {
        let hit=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let id=jsonText(hit,"id")
        let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=? AND subject=? AND usable_for_practice=1",[Some(id),sqlValue(question["subject"])]).rows
        if (rows.isEmpty()) { continue }
        let item=rowJson("dataset_questions",rows[0]); if (!usableRetrievalCandidate(question,item,kind)) { continue }; let candidate=JsonObject()
        for (key in ["dense_rank","sparse_rank","fusion_rank","fusion_score"]) { candidate.put(key,hit[key]) }
        candidate.put("question",item)
        let sources=db.query("SELECT `key` FROM dataset_sources WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(item["dataset_source_id"])]).rows
        candidate.put("source_dataset",JsonString(if (sources.isEmpty()) { "unknown" } else { rowText(sources[0],"key") }))
        let links=JsonArray()
        for (row in db.query("SELECT k.id,k.name,l.role FROM question_knowledge_links l JOIN knowledge_points k ON k.id=l.knowledge_point_id WHERE l.dataset_question_id=?",[Some(id)]).rows) {
            let link=JsonObject(); for (key in ["id","name","role"]) { link.put(key,JsonString(rowText(row,key))) }; links.add(link)
        }
        candidate.put("knowledge_points",links); candidates.add(candidate)
    }
    let rank=JsonObject(); rank.put("operation",JsonString("retrieval_rank")); rank.put("retrieval_query",preparation["query"]); rank.put("text",question["question_text"]); rank.put("question_type",question["question_type"])
    rank.put("retrieval_candidates",candidates); rank.put("retrieval_excluded_ids",excluded); rank.put("retrieval_kind",JsonString(kind)); rank.put("retrieval_mode",JsonString(mode))
    return algorithmCompute(rank)
}

```

### backend-cangjie/src/reviews.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func reviewQuestion(db: Database, plan: JsonObject, owner: String): JsonObject {
    let rows = db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[sqlValue(plan["question_id"]),Some(owner)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"题目不存在") }
    return rowJson("questions",rows[0])
}

func reviewAttemptReceipt(record: JsonObject, accounts: Accounts): JsonObject {
    let details=tutorObject(record,"grading_details"); let result=JsonObject(); let grading=JsonObject()
    result.put("attempt_id",record["id"]); result.put("request_id",JsonString(tutorText(details,"review_request_id")))
    result.put("correct",if (tutorFlag(details,"can_update_learning")) { record["correct"] } else { JsonNull() })
    for (name in ["score","feedback","matched_points","can_update_learning"]) { result.put(name,details[name]) }
    for (name in ["verdict","correct","score","feedback","matched_points","can_update_learning"]) { grading.put(name,details[name]) }
    let raw=tutorText(details,"handwriting_image_url"); let path=if (raw.isEmpty()) { "" } else { raw.split("?",2)[0] }
    result.put("grading",grading); result.put("expected_answer",record["expected_answer"])
    result.put("student_answer",record["student_answer"])
    result.put("handwriting_image_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl(path))); result.put("handwriting_recognition",details["handwriting_recognition"])
    return result
}

public func listReviews(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, history: Bool): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let result = JsonArray()
        if (history) {
            for (row in db.query("SELECT r.* FROM review_records r JOIN review_plans p ON p.id=r.plan_id WHERE p.user_id=? AND r.grade<>'pending' ORDER BY r.created_at DESC,r.id DESC",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let record = rowJson("review_records",row); let plan = rowJson("review_plans",db.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE id=?",[row["plan_id"]]).rows[0])
                let questions = db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[sqlValue(plan["question_id"]),user["id"]]).rows
                let details = tutorObject(record,"grading_details"); let rawPath = tutorText(details,"handwriting_image_url"); let path = if (rawPath.isEmpty()) { "" } else { rawPath.split("?",2)[0] }
                if (!path.isEmpty()) { details.put("handwriting_image_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl(path))) }
                record.put("plan",plan); record.put("question",if (questions.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { rowJson("questions",questions[0]) }); result.add(record)
            }
        } else {
            let seen = HashSet<String>()
            for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? AND status='pending' AND due_at<TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,16,DATE(TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,8,UTC_TIMESTAMP()))) ORDER BY due_at,id",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let plan = rowJson("review_plans",row); let question = reviewQuestion(db,plan,rowText(user,"id"))
                if (jsonText(question,"status") == "archived") { continue }
                let key = questionDisplayKey(jsonText(question,"question_text"),jsonText(question,"id")); if (seen.contains(key)) { continue }; seen.add(key)
                let visible = JsonObject(); for ((name,value) in question.getFields()) { if (name != "student_answer") { visible.put(name,value) } }
                let figures = queryArray(db,"question_assets","SELECT * FROM question_assets WHERE question_id=? AND kind='question_figure' ORDER BY created_at,id",jsonText(question,"id"))
                for (value in figures.getItems()) { let figure = (value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); figure.put("url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+jsonText(figure,"path")))) }
                visible.put("question_figures",figures); plan.put("question",visible)
                let version=memoryQuestionVersion(db,question); plan.put("source_version",JsonString(version)); plan.put("pending_attempt",JsonNull())
                let pending=db.query("SELECT * FROM review_records WHERE plan_id=? AND grade='pending' AND COALESCE(JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(grading_details,'$.review_record_state')),'current')<>'superseded' ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[row["id"]]).rows
                if (!pending.isEmpty()) {
                    let record=rowJson("review_records",pending[0])
                    if (tutorText(tutorObject(record,"grading_details"),"memory_question_version")==version) { plan.put("pending_attempt",reviewAttemptReceipt(record,accounts)) }
                }
                if (tutorText(plan,"reason").toAsciiLower().contains("again")) { plan.put("reason",JsonString("本次作答需要重做，安排 1 天后再次复习")) }
                result.add(plan)
            }
        }
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

public func attemptReview(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body = readJson(context); let answer = editText(body.get("answer") ?? JsonString(""),12000,trim: true)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status='pending' FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"待复习题不存在") }
            let plan = rowJson("review_plans",rows[0]); let question = reviewQuestion(tx,plan,rowText(user,"id"))
            if (jsonText(question,"status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已归档") }
            let version=memoryQuestionVersion(tx,question); let suppliedVersion=tutorText(body,"source_version")
            if (!suppliedVersion.isEmpty() && suppliedVersion!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"原题或参考已更新，请刷新后核对答案") }
            let requestId=if (body.get("request_id").isSome()) { textField(body,"request_id",80) } else { "" }
            let payload=JsonArray(); for (key in ["answer","handwriting_image_url","handwriting_recognition"]) { payload.add(body.get(key) ?? JsonNull()) }; let digest=sha256(payload.toString().toArray())
            if (!requestId.isEmpty()) {
                let previous=tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_records WHERE plan_id=? AND JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(grading_details,'$.review_request_id'))=? LIMIT 1",[Some(id),Some(requestId)]).rows
                if (!previous.isEmpty()) {
                    let record=rowJson("review_records",previous[0]); let details=tutorObject(record,"grading_details")
                    if (tutorText(details,"review_record_state")=="superseded") { throw ApiError(409,"已有更新的复习作答，请刷新查看当前判题") }
                    if (tutorText(details,"review_request_digest")!=digest || tutorText(details,"memory_question_version")!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"提交编号对应的作答或原题已变化，请核对后重新提交") }
                    return reviewAttemptReceipt(record,accounts)
                }
            }
            let analyses = tx.query("SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 1",[sqlValue(plan["question_id"])]).rows
            if (analyses.isEmpty() || rowText(analyses[0],"final_answer").isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"这道题还没有标准答案，请先在题目详情中完成分析") }
            let analysis = rowJson("problem_analyses",analyses[0]); let handwriting = validateTutorHandwriting(body,rowText(user,"id"),id,kind: "review")
            let input = JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("grade_answer")); input.put("text",question["question_text"]); input.put("answer",answer); input.put("expected",analysis["final_answer"])
            input.put("subject",question["subject"]); input.put("question_type",question["question_type"]); input.put("options",question["options"])
            input.put("reference_solution",tutorStringArray(tutorRowTexts(queryArray(tx,"reference_steps","SELECT * FROM reference_steps WHERE analysis_id=? ORDER BY step_index",jsonText(analysis,"id")))))
            let grading = algorithmCompute(input); let details = (JsonValue.fromStr(grading.toString()) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            details.put("memory_question_version",JsonString(version)); details.put("review_request_id",JsonString(requestId)); details.put("review_request_digest",JsonString(digest))
            details.put("handwriting_image_url",handwriting["image_url"]); details.put("handwriting_recognition",handwriting["recognition"])
            tx.query("UPDATE review_records SET grading_details=JSON_SET(grading_details,'$.review_record_state','superseded') WHERE plan_id=? AND grade='pending'",[Some(id)])
            let recordId = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO review_records (id,plan_id,grade,correct,student_answer,expected_answer,feedback,score,grading_details,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'pending',?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(recordId),Some(id),sqlValue(grading["correct"]),sqlValue(answer),sqlValue(analysis["final_answer"]),sqlValue(grading["feedback"]),sqlValue(grading["score"]),Some(details.toString())])
            return reviewAttemptReceipt(rowJson("review_records",tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_records WHERE id=?",[Some(recordId)]).rows[0]),accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

public func gradeReview(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body = readJson(context)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE id=? AND user_id=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"复习计划不存在") }
            let plan = rowJson("review_plans",rows[0]); if (jsonText(plan,"status") != "pending") { throw ApiError(409,"这次复习已完成，请刷新复习列表") }
            let records = tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_records WHERE id=? AND plan_id=? AND grade='pending'",[Some(tutorText(body,"attempt_id")),Some(id)]).rows
            if (records.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"请先提交答案并完成系统判定") }
            let record = rowJson("review_records",records[0]); let details = tutorObject(record,"grading_details")
            if (tutorText(details,"review_record_state")=="superseded") { throw ApiError(409,"已有更新的复习作答，请刷新查看当前判题") }
            if ((details.get("can_update_learning") ?? JsonBool(true)).toString() != "true") { throw ApiError(409,"这次判题仍需核对，不能据此更新复习计划") }
            let currentQuestion=reviewQuestion(tx,plan,rowText(user,"id")); let sourceVersion=tutorText(details,"memory_question_version")
            if (jsonText(currentQuestion,"status")=="archived" || (!sourceVersion.isEmpty() && sourceVersion!=memoryQuestionVersion(tx,currentQuestion))) { throw ApiError(409,"原题或参考已更新，请重新提交作答再评价复习") }
            let grade = tutorText(body,"grade"); let actualGrade = if (body.get("grade").isNone()) { "good" } else { grade }
            let correct = tutorFlag(record,"correct"); var days: Int64 = 1; var label = "还没想起来"
            match (actualGrade) {
                case "again" => ()
                case "hard" => days = if (correct) { 2 } else { 1 }; label = "有点困难"
                case "good" => days = if (correct) { 7 } else { 2 }; label = "已经掌握"
                case "easy" => days = if (correct) { 15 } else { 4 }; label = "很轻松"
                case _ => throw ApiError(422,"请选择有效的复习反馈")
            }
            tx.query("UPDATE review_plans SET status='completed',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(id)])
            tx.query("UPDATE review_records SET grade=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(actualGrade),sqlValue(record["id"])])
            let nextId = newId(); let reason = "上次作答${if (correct) { "正确" } else { "有误" }}，随后选择了“${label}”"
            tx.query("INSERT INTO review_plans (id,user_id,question_id,due_at,interval_days,reason,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,TIMESTAMPADD(DAY,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP()),?,?,'pending',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(nextId),user["id"],sqlValue(plan["question_id"]),Some(days.toString()),Some(days.toString()),Some(reason)])
            let saved = rowJson("review_records",tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_records WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(record["id"])]).rows[0])
            let savedDetails = tutorObject(saved,"grading_details"); let rawImagePath = tutorText(savedDetails,"handwriting_image_url"); let imagePath = if (rawImagePath.isEmpty()) { "" } else { rawImagePath.split("?",2)[0] }
            if (!imagePath.isEmpty()) { savedDetails.put("handwriting_image_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl(imagePath))) }
            let result = JsonObject(); result.put("record",saved); result.put("next_plan",rowJson("review_plans",tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE id=?",[Some(nextId)]).rows[0])); return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/router.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.net.http.*

// Registration finishes before serve(); request threads only read these maps.
public class ApiDistributor <: HttpRequestDistributor {
    let exact = HashMap<String, HttpRequestHandler>()
    let patterns = ArrayList<(Array<String>, HttpRequestHandler)>()
    let prefixes = ArrayList<(String, HttpRequestHandler)>()

    public func register(path: String, handler: HttpRequestHandler): Unit {
        if (path.endsWith("*")) {
            prefixes.add((path[..path.size - 1], handler))
        } else if (path.contains("{")) {
            patterns.add((path.split("/"), handler))
        } else {
            if (exact.contains(path)) { throw IllegalArgumentException("重复路由") }
            exact.add(path, handler)
        }
    }

    public func distribute(path: String): HttpRequestHandler {
        if (let Some(handler) <- exact.get(path)) { return handler }
        for ((prefix, handler) in prefixes) { if (path.startsWith(prefix)) { return handler } }
        let parts = path.split("/")
        for ((pattern, handler) in patterns) {
            if (parts.size != pattern.size) { continue }
            var matches = true
            for (index in 0..parts.size) {
                let segment = pattern[index]
                if (segment.startsWith("{") && segment.endsWith("}")) {
                    if (parts[index].isEmpty()) { matches = false; break }
                } else if (segment != parts[index]) { matches = false; break }
            }
            if (matches) { return handler }
        }
        return NotFoundHandler()
    }
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/row_json.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import std.fs.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

// Static contract snapshot, generated from the original schema during migration.
// No Python process or original database is involved in request serialization.
let columnTypes = (JsonValue.fromStr(String.fromUtf8(File.readFrom("schema/column_types.json"))) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
let taskColumnTypes = (JsonValue.fromStr(String.fromUtf8(File.readFrom("schema/task_column_types.json"))) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()

public func rowJson(table: String, row: HashMap<String, Option<String>>): JsonObject {
    let types = ((columnTypes.get(table) ?? taskColumnTypes[table]) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    let result = JsonObject()
    for ((key, value) in row) {
        let kind = (types[key] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        if (let Some(raw) <- value) {
            let parsed: JsonValue = match (kind) {
                case "json" => JsonValue.fromStr(raw)
                case "bool" => JsonBool(raw != "0")
                case "int" => JsonInt(Int64.parse(raw))
                case "float" => JsonFloat(Float64.parse(raw))
                case "datetime" => JsonString(raw.replace(" ", "T") + "+00:00")
                case _ => JsonString(raw)
            }
            result.put(key, parsed)
        } else { result.put(key, JsonNull()) }
    }
    if (table == "questions" && row.contains("title") && row.contains("question_text")) {
        let title = rowText(row, "title"); let text = rowText(row, "question_text")
        // Historical auto-titles may be a truncated prefix inside a TeX formula.
        // Repair the response from the original question without changing stored data.
        if (!title.isEmpty() && text != title && text.startsWith(title) &&
            (title.contains("$") || title.contains("\\"))) {
            result.put("title", JsonString(text))
        }
    }
    return result
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/schema.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.fs.*

public func initializeDatabase(): Unit {
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let existing = db.query("SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = DATABASE()")
        if (!existing.rows.isEmpty()) {
            // Never reset or silently merge an existing application database.
            throw IllegalArgumentException("初始化只允许空数据库；已有数据库请使用后续版本迁移")
        }
        let schema = String.fromUtf8(File.readFrom("schema/001_initial.sql"))
        for (statement in schema.split(";")) {
            if (!statement.trimAscii().isEmpty()) { db.query(statement) }
        }
        db.query("CREATE TABLE cangjie_schema_versions (version VARCHAR(100) PRIMARY KEY, applied_at DATETIME NOT NULL) ENGINE=InnoDB")
        db.query("INSERT INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES (?, UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some("001_initial")])
    }
    upgradeDatabase()
}


public func upgradeDatabase(): Unit {
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let initial = db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '001_initial'").rows
        if (initial.size != 1) { throw IllegalArgumentException("缺少仓颉初始版本，不能升级") }
        let applied = db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '002_work_precision'").rows
        if (applied.isEmpty()) {
        // MySQL DDL commits implicitly. Repeating this ALTER after interruption is safe.
        db.query("ALTER TABLE student_works MODIFY created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL, MODIFY updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL")
        db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES ('002_work_precision', UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        }
        if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '003_diagnosis_precision'").rows.isEmpty()) {
            db.query("ALTER TABLE diagnoses MODIFY created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL, MODIFY updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL")
            db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES ('003_diagnosis_precision', UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        }
        if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '004_tutor_precision'").rows.isEmpty()) {
            db.query("ALTER TABLE tutor_turns MODIFY created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL, MODIFY updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL")
            db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES ('004_tutor_precision', UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        }
        if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '005_practice_precision'").rows.isEmpty()) {
            for (table in ["practice_recommendations","dataset_practice_attempts"]) {
                db.query("ALTER TABLE ${table} MODIFY created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL, MODIFY updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL")
            }
            db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES ('005_practice_precision', UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        }
        if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '006_learning_tasks'").rows.isEmpty()) {
            for (statement in String.fromUtf8(File.readFrom("schema/006_learning_tasks.sql")).split(";")) {
                if (!statement.trimAscii().isEmpty()) { db.query(statement) }
            }
            db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES ('006_learning_tasks', UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        }
        if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '007_self_checks'").rows.isEmpty()) {
            for (statement in String.fromUtf8(File.readFrom("schema/007_self_checks.sql")).split(";")) {
                if (!statement.trimAscii().isEmpty()) { db.query(statement) }
            }
            db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES ('007_self_checks', UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        }
        if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '008_task_handwriting'").rows.isEmpty()) {
            for (statement in String.fromUtf8(File.readFrom("schema/008_task_handwriting.sql")).split(";")) {
                if (!statement.trimAscii().isEmpty()) { db.query(statement) }
            }
            db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES ('008_task_handwriting', UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        }
        if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '009_task_sources'").rows.isEmpty()) {
            for (statement in String.fromUtf8(File.readFrom("schema/009_task_sources.sql")).split(";")) {
                if (!statement.trimAscii().isEmpty()) { db.query(statement) }
            }
            db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES ('009_task_sources', UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        }
        if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '010_study_sessions'").rows.isEmpty()) {
            for (statement in String.fromUtf8(File.readFrom("schema/010_study_sessions.sql")).split(";")) {
                if (!statement.trimAscii().isEmpty()) { db.query(statement) }
            }
            db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES ('010_study_sessions', UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        }
        if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '012_personal_learning'").rows.isEmpty()) {
            for (statement in String.fromUtf8(File.readFrom("schema/012_personal_learning.sql")).split(";")) {
                if (!statement.trimAscii().isEmpty()) { db.query(statement) }
            }
            db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES ('012_personal_learning', UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        }
        if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '011_bank_browse_index'").rows.isEmpty()) {
            // Cover eligibility, ordered browsing and facets without reading large question bodies.
            if (db.query("SELECT INDEX_NAME FROM information_schema.STATISTICS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND TABLE_NAME='dataset_questions' AND INDEX_NAME='ix_bank_browse'").rows.isEmpty()) {
                db.query("ALTER TABLE dataset_questions ADD INDEX ix_bank_browse (usable_for_practice,usable_for_auto_grade,has_answer,subject,id,grade_band,dataset_source_id), ALGORITHM=INPLACE, LOCK=NONE")
            }
            db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES ('011_bank_browse_index', UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        }
        if (db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = '013_bank_grade_index'").rows.isEmpty()) {
            if (db.query("SELECT INDEX_NAME FROM information_schema.STATISTICS WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND TABLE_NAME='dataset_questions' AND INDEX_NAME='ix_bank_grade'").rows.isEmpty()) {
                db.query("ALTER TABLE dataset_questions ADD INDEX ix_bank_grade (usable_for_practice,usable_for_auto_grade,has_answer,grade,subject,id), ALGORITHM=INPLACE, LOCK=NONE")
            }
            db.query("INSERT IGNORE INTO cangjie_schema_versions VALUES ('013_bank_grade_index', UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        }
    }
}
```

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

// A read-only projection of current diagnoses. No new mastery or predicted marks.
func studentErrorSources(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let form=insightQuery(context); let days=insightDays(form,90,365); let subject=queryLast(form,"subject")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows=tx.query("SELECT q.* FROM questions q JOIN diagnoses d ON d.id=(SELECT d2.id FROM diagnoses d2 WHERE d2.question_id=q.id ORDER BY d2.created_at DESC,d2.id DESC LIMIT 1) WHERE q.user_id=? AND q.status<>'archived' AND q.confirmed=1 AND (?='' OR q.subject=?) AND d.created_at>=TIMESTAMPADD(DAY,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)) ORDER BY q.updated_at DESC,q.id DESC LIMIT 201",[user["id"],Some(subject),Some(subject),Some((-days).toString())]).rows
            let sources=JsonArray(); let seen=HashSet<String>(); var scanned: Int64=0
            for (row in rows) {
                if (scanned>=200) { break }; scanned++
                let key=questionDisplayKey(rowText(row,"question_text"),rowText(row,"id"))
                if (seen.contains(key)) { continue }; seen.add(key)
                let question=rowJson("questions",row)
                let diagnosis=firstJson(queryArray(tx,"diagnoses","SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",rowText(row,"id")))
                let item=JsonObject()
                for (field in ["id","subject","grade","question_text","knowledge_points","updated_at"]) { item.put(field,question[field]) }
                item.put("diagnosis",diagnosis)
                let plans=tx.query("SELECT id FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? AND question_id=? AND status='pending' AND due_at<TIMESTAMP(DATE(UTC_TIMESTAMP()+INTERVAL 8 HOUR))+INTERVAL 16 HOUR ORDER BY due_at,id LIMIT 1",[user["id"],row["id"]]).rows
                item.put("due_review_id",if (plans.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(rowText(plans[0],"id")) })
                sources.add(item)
            }
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("sources",sources); result.put("days",JsonInt(days)); result.put("limit",JsonInt(200)); result.put("truncated",JsonBool(rows.size>200)); return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

func studentLearningPreferences(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let method=context.request.method
    if (method!="GET" && method!="PATCH") { throw ApiError(405,"请求方法不支持") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        if (rowText(user,"role")!="student") { throw ApiError(403,"仅学生可以修改本人的学习方向") }
        let body=if (method=="PATCH") { readJson(context) } else { JsonObject() }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM student_profiles WHERE user_id=? FOR UPDATE",[user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"学生档案不存在") }
            let profile=rowJson("student_profiles",rows[0]); let preferences=tutorObject(profile,"preferences")
            let version=tutorNumber(preferences,"experience_version")
            if (method=="PATCH") {
                taskStrict(body,["grade","subject","minutes","goal","version"])
                if (!((body.get("version") ?? JsonNull()) is JsonInt) || tutorNumber(body,"version")!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"学习方向已变化，请重新读取后保存") }
                let grade=if (body.get("grade").isSome()) { textField(body,"grade",30) } else { tutorText(profile,"grade") }
                if (body.get("grade").isSome() && !(["初一","初二","初三","高一","高二","高三"].contains(grade))) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择初一至高三的年级") }
                let subject=textField(body,"subject",30)
                if (!(["语文","数学","英语","物理","化学","生物","历史","地理","道德与法治","思想政治"].contains(subject))) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择支持的学科") }
                let minutes=tutorNumber(body,"minutes")
                if (!((body.get("minutes") ?? JsonNull()) is JsonInt) || !([Int64(5),10,20].contains(minutes))) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择5、10或20分钟") }
                let goal=textField(body,"goal",30)
                if (!(["understand","practice","review"].contains(goal))) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择学习目标") }
                preferences.put("focus_subject",JsonString(subject)); preferences.put("daily_minutes",JsonInt(minutes)); preferences.put("focus_goal",JsonString(goal)); preferences.put("experience_version",JsonInt(version+1))
                tx.query("UPDATE student_profiles SET grade=?,preferences=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE user_id=?",[Some(grade),Some(preferences.toString()),user["id"]])
                profile.put("grade",JsonString(grade))
            }
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("grade",profile["grade"]); result.put("subject",JsonString(tutorText(preferences,"focus_subject"))); result.put("minutes",JsonInt(if (tutorNumber(preferences,"daily_minutes")>0) { tutorNumber(preferences,"daily_minutes") } else { 10 })); result.put("goal",JsonString(if (tutorText(preferences,"focus_goal").isEmpty()) { "understand" } else { tutorText(preferences,"focus_goal") })); result.put("version",JsonInt(tutorNumber(preferences,"experience_version"))); return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```
