### backend-cangjie/src/student_work_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

public func submitStudentWork(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let body = readJson(context,maxBytes: 8 * 1024 * 1024)
        let answer = (editText(body.get("answer") ?? JsonString(""),12000) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        let explanation = (editText(body.get("explanation") ?? JsonString(""),12000) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        let steps = manualArray(body,"steps",100)
        for (step in steps.getItems()) { editText(step,12000) }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT status FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"题目不存在或无权访问") }
            if (rowText(rows[0],"status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已归档，不能提交作答") }
            invalidateQuestionLearning(tx,id)
            tx.query("UPDATE processing_jobs SET status = 'cancelled',error = '已提交新作答，旧识别结果未应用',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE question_id = ? AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') AND status IN ('pending','running')",[Some(id)])
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET student_answer = ?,status = IF(confirmed,'confirmed','needs_confirmation'),updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",[if (answer.isEmpty()) { None } else { Some(answer) },Some(id)])
            let workId = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO student_works (id,question_id,user_id,answer,explanation,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(workId),Some(id),user["id"],Some(answer),Some(explanation)])
            for (index in 0..steps.getItems().size) {
                let text = (steps[index] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                tx.query("INSERT INTO student_steps (id,work_id,step_index,text,bbox,raw_text,normalized_text,formula,confidence,source,user_confirmed,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),?,?,?,1,'user',1,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(newId()),Some(workId),Some(index.toString()),Some(text),Some(text),Some(collapseWhitespace(text)),Some(if (text.contains("=")) { text } else { "" })])
            }
            return rowJson("student_works",tx.query("SELECT * FROM student_works WHERE id = ?",[Some(workId)]).rows[0])
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

### backend-cangjie/src/study_bank.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

// Only the public question projection leaves this module before submission.
func bankQuestion(value: JsonObject, accounts: Accounts): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject()
    for (key in ["id","subject","grade","grade_band","title","question_text","question_type","options","difficulty","knowledge_points","source_name"]) {
        result.put(key,value.get(key) ?? JsonNull())
    }
    // Reading passages stay complete in the question, while the bank lists
    // the actual subquestion so a page does not repeat twenty long articles.
    if (!tutorText(tutorObject(value,"source_metadata"),"passage_id").isEmpty()) {
        let paragraphs=jsonText(value,"question_text").split("\n\n")
        if (paragraphs.size>1) { result.put("preview_text",JsonString("阅读理解："+paragraphs[paragraphs.size-1])) }
    }
    for ((key,item) in datasetQuestionImages(value,accounts).getFields()) { result.put(key,item) }
    return result
}

func bankGradeAlias(grade: String): String {
    match (grade) {
        case "初一" => "七年级"
        case "初二" => "八年级"
        case "初三" => "九年级"
        case _ => grade
    }
}

func bankStudentGrade(db: Database, owner: String): String {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT grade FROM student_profiles WHERE user_id=?",[Some(owner)]).rows
    let grade=if (rows.isEmpty()) { "" } else { rowText(rows[0],"grade") }
    if (!(["初一","初二","初三","高一","高二","高三"].contains(grade))) { throw ApiError(422,"请先在学习设置中选择具体年级") }
    return grade
}

// Conservative exclusion: source grade labels are necessary, not sufficient.
// These rules reject obvious later-stage math; they never infer a grade for unlabelled data.
func bankLaterMath(grade: String): String {
    let college="微积分|不定积分|定积分|微分方程|偏导数|拉普拉斯|傅里叶|泰勒级数|矩阵|行列式|特征值"
    if (grade.startsWith("初")) {
        let senior=college+"|复数|虚数|共轭|导数|求导|向量|数列|对数|指数函数|椭圆|双曲线|圆锥曲线|排列组合|二项式定理|数学归纳|充分条件|必要条件|奇函数|偶函数|等差|等比|正态分布|随机变量|(^|[^a-zA-Z])(log|ln)([^a-zA-Z]|$)"
        if (grade=="初一" || grade=="初二") {
            let later=senior+"|二次函数|一元二次|反比例函数|三角函数|正弦|余弦|正切|圆周角|圆心角|相似三角形|(^|[^a-zA-Z])(sin|cos|tan)([^a-zA-Z]|$)"
            return if (grade=="初一") { later+"|勾股|全等|二次根式|分式|一次函数|因式分解|完全平方|平方差" } else { later }
        }
        return senior
    }
    return college
}

func bankFilter(subject: String, band: String, source: String, search: String, grade!: String): Array<Option<String>> {
    let params=ArrayList<Option<String>>([Some(subject),Some(subject),Some(band),Some(band),Some(source),Some(source),Some(search),Some("%"+search+"%")])
    if (!grade.startsWith("高")) { params.add(Some(grade)); params.add(Some(bankGradeAlias(grade))); params.add(Some(bankLaterMath(grade))) }
    return params.toArray()
}
let bankWhere=" FROM dataset_questions q WHERE q.usable_for_practice=1 AND q.usable_for_auto_grade=1 AND q.has_answer=1 AND (?='' OR q.subject=?) AND (?='' OR q.grade_band=?) AND (?='' OR q.dataset_source_id=?) AND (?='' OR q.question_text LIKE ?)"
func bankScopedWhere(grade: String): String {
    if (grade.startsWith("高")) { return bankWhere.replace("q WHERE","q USE INDEX (ix_bank_browse) WHERE")+" AND q.grade_band='高中'" }
    return bankWhere.replace("q WHERE","q USE INDEX (ix_bank_grade) WHERE")+" AND q.grade IN (?,?) AND (q.subject<>'数学' OR CONCAT(q.question_text,' ',COALESCE(q.options,''),' ',COALESCE(q.knowledge_points,'')) NOT REGEXP ?)"
}

func studyBank(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let form=insightQuery(context)
    let subject=queryLast(form,"subject"); let band=queryLast(form,"grade_band"); let source=queryLast(form,"source_id"); let search=queryLast(form,"search")
    if (subject.size>100 || band.size>100 || source.size>100 || search.size>300) { throw ApiError(422,"筛选条件过长") }
    let page=if (queryLast(form,"page").isEmpty()) { 1 } else { try { Int64.parse(queryLast(form,"page")) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"页码无效") } }
    if (page<1 || page>10000) { throw ApiError(422,"页码无效") }
    let seed=if (queryLast(form,"shuffle_seed").isEmpty()) { unixNow()%2147483647 } else { try { Int64.parse(queryLast(form,"shuffle_seed")) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"随机顺序参数无效") } }
    if (seed<0 || seed>2147483647) { throw ApiError(422,"随机顺序参数无效") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let grade=bankStudentGrade(db,rowText(user,"id"))
        let filters=bankFilter(subject,band,source,search,grade:grade)
        let browseWhere=bankScopedWhere(grade)
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("page",JsonInt(page)); result.put("page_size",JsonInt(20)); result.put("grade",JsonString(grade)); result.put("scope_label",JsonString(if (grade.startsWith("高")) { "高中" } else { grade }))
        let items=JsonArray()
        let sourceNames=JsonObject()
        for (sourceRow in db.query("SELECT id,name FROM dataset_sources").rows) {
            sourceNames.put(rowText(sourceRow,"id"),JsonString(rowText(sourceRow,"name")))
        }
        let paging=" LIMIT 20 OFFSET "+((page-1)*20).toString()
        // Shuffle IDs, interleaving subjects so large English datasets cannot monopolize "all".
        // The seeded per-ID key stays stable across pages and query execution plans.
        let randomKey="CRC32(CONCAT(q.id,':',"+seed.toString()+"))"
        let listing=if (subject.isEmpty()) {
            "SELECT q.* FROM dataset_questions q JOIN (SELECT id,subject_rank,random_key FROM (SELECT q.id,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY q.subject ORDER BY "+randomKey+",q.id) AS subject_rank,"+randomKey+" AS random_key"+browseWhere+") mixed ORDER BY subject_rank,random_key,id"+paging+") selected ON selected.id=q.id ORDER BY selected.subject_rank,selected.random_key,selected.id"
        } else { "SELECT q.*"+browseWhere+(if (grade.startsWith("高")) { " ORDER BY q.subject,q.id" } else { " ORDER BY q.grade,q.subject,q.id" })+paging }
        result.put("shuffle_seed",JsonInt(seed))
        for (row in db.query(listing,filters).rows) {
            let value=rowJson("dataset_questions",row)
            value.put("source_name",sourceNames.get(rowText(row,"dataset_source_id")) ?? JsonString("题库")); items.add(bankQuestion(value,accounts))
        }
        result.put("questions",items)
        // One eligibility scan supplies facets, sources and the unsearched count.
        let facetCounts=HashMap<String,JsonObject>(); let sourceCounts=HashMap<String,Int64>()
        var total: Int64=0
        for (row in db.query("SELECT q.subject,q.grade_band,q.dataset_source_id,COUNT(*) AS total"+browseWhere+" GROUP BY q.subject,q.grade_band,q.dataset_source_id",bankFilter("","","","",grade:grade)).rows) {
            let rowSubject=rowText(row,"subject"); let rowBand=rowText(row,"grade_band"); let rowSource=rowText(row,"dataset_source_id")
            let amount=Int64.parse(rowText(row,"total")); let key=rowSubject+"|"+rowBand
            let item=facetCounts.get(key) ?? JsonObject()
            item.put("subject",JsonString(rowSubject)); item.put("grade_band",JsonString(rowBand)); item.put("count",JsonInt(tutorNumber(item,"count")+amount)); facetCounts[key]=item
            sourceCounts[rowSource]=(sourceCounts.get(rowSource) ?? 0)+amount
            if ((subject.isEmpty() || subject==rowSubject) && (band.isEmpty() || band==rowBand) && (source.isEmpty() || source==rowSource)) { total+=amount }
        }
        if (!search.isEmpty()) { total=Int64.parse(rowText(db.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total"+browseWhere,filters).rows[0],"total")) }
        result.put("total",JsonInt(total))
        let facets=JsonArray(); for ((_,item) in facetCounts) { facets.add(item) }; result.put("facets",facets)
        let sources=JsonArray()
        for ((id,amount) in sourceCounts) {
            let item=JsonObject(); item.put("id",JsonString(id)); item.put("name",sourceNames.get(id) ?? JsonString("题库")); item.put("count",JsonInt(amount)); sources.add(item)
        }
        result.put("sources",sources); return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"题库暂时无法读取")
}

func studySessionRow(row: HashMap<String,Option<String>>): JsonObject {
    let value=JsonObject()
    for ((key,raw) in row) {
        if (let Some(text) <- raw) {
            if (key=="version" || key=="duration_seconds" || key=="remaining_seconds" || key=="question_count" || key=="expired") { value.put(key,JsonInt(Int64.parse(text))) }
            else if (key=="results") { value.put(key,JsonValue.fromStr(text)) }
            else { value.put(key,JsonString(text)) }
        } else { value.put(key,JsonNull()) }
    }
    return value
}

func ownedStudy(db: Database, id: String, owner: String, lock!: Bool=false): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT *,CEIL(GREATEST(0,COALESCE(TIMESTAMPDIFF(MICROSECOND,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),deadline_at),0))/1000000) AS remaining_seconds,IF(deadline_at IS NOT NULL AND deadline_at<=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),1,0) AS expired FROM study_sessions WHERE id=? AND user_id=?"+if (lock) { " FOR UPDATE" } else { "" },[Some(id),Some(owner)]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"没有找到这次练习或考试") }
    return studySessionRow(rows[0])
}

func studyItems(db: Database, id: String): JsonArray {
    let result=JsonArray()
    for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM study_session_items WHERE session_id=? ORDER BY position",[Some(id)]).rows) {
        let item=JsonObject(); item.put("id",JsonString(rowText(row,"id"))); item.put("position",JsonInt(Int64.parse(rowText(row,"position")))); item.put("snapshot",JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(row,"snapshot")))
        item.put("student_answer",if (let Some(raw) <- row["student_answer"]) { JsonValue.fromStr(raw) } else { JsonNull() })
        item.put("grading",if (let Some(raw) <- row["grading"]) { JsonValue.fromStr(raw) } else { JsonNull() }); result.add(item)
    }
    return result
}

func studyDetail(db: Database, value: JsonObject, accounts: Accounts): JsonObject {
    let items=JsonArray(); let submitted=jsonText(value,"status")=="submitted"
    for (raw in studyItems(db,jsonText(value,"id")).getItems()) {
        let item=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let snapshot=tutorObject(item,"snapshot"); let publicItem=JsonObject()
        let available=studyQuestionAvailable(db,snapshot)
        publicItem.put("id",item["id"]); publicItem.put("position",item["position"]); publicItem.put("student_answer",item["student_answer"]); publicItem.put("unavailable",JsonBool(!available))
        if (available) { publicItem.put("question",bankQuestion(snapshot,accounts)) }
        else { let question=JsonObject(); question.put("subject",snapshot["subject"]); question.put("question_type",snapshot["question_type"]); publicItem.put("question",question) }
        if (submitted) {
            let added=db.query("SELECT id FROM questions WHERE user_id=? AND status<>'archived' AND JSON_CONTAINS(tags,JSON_QUOTE(?)) LIMIT 1",[sqlValue(value["user_id"]),Some("study-item:"+jsonText(item,"id"))]).rows
            publicItem.put("mistake_id",if (added.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(rowText(added[0],"id")) })
            publicItem.put("grading",item["grading"])
            if (available) {
                publicItem.put("answer",snapshot["answer"]); publicItem.put("solution",snapshot["solution"])
                for ((key,value) in datasetSolutionImages(snapshot,accounts).getFields()) { publicItem.put(key,value) }
            }
        }
        items.add(publicItem)
    }
    value.put("items",items); value.put("question_count",JsonInt(items.getItems().size)); return value
}

func studyQuestionAvailable(db: Database, snapshot: JsonObject, lock!: Bool=false): Bool {
    if (tutorFlag(datasetImageLayout(snapshot),"review")) { return false }
    if (jsonText(snapshot,"quality_status")=="format_review") { return false }
    let rows=db.query("SELECT quality_status,source_metadata FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=?"+if (lock) { " FOR SHARE" } else { "" },[Some(jsonText(snapshot,"id"))]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { return true }
    if (rowText(rows[0],"quality_status")=="format_review") { return false }
    let metadata=(JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(rows[0],"source_metadata")) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    for (entry in tutorArray(metadata,"format_review_history").getItems()) {
        if (jsonText((entry as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"content_hash")==jsonText(snapshot,"content_hash")) { return false }
    }
    return true
}

func studyUnavailableGrade(): JsonObject {
    let grading=JsonObject(); grading.put("correct",JsonNull()); grading.put("can_update_learning",JsonBool(false)); grading.put("method",JsonString("question_unavailable")); grading.put("feedback",JsonString("此题已暂停使用，未计分")); return grading
}

func studyInteger(value: JsonValue, minimum: Int64, maximum: Int64): Int64 {
    if (!(value is JsonInt)) { throw ApiError(422,"请填写整数") }
    return paperInteger(value,minimum,maximum)
}

func studySessions(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let creating=context.request.method=="POST"; if (!creating) { requireMethod(context,"GET") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let owner=rowText(user,"id")
        if (!creating) {
            let result=JsonArray()
            for (row in db.query("SELECT s.*,(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM study_session_items i WHERE i.session_id=s.id) AS question_count FROM study_sessions s WHERE s.user_id=? ORDER BY s.created_at DESC,s.id DESC LIMIT 50",[Some(owner)]).rows) { result.add(studySessionRow(row)) }; return result
        }
        let grade=bankStudentGrade(db,owner)
        let body=readJson(context); let mode=optionalText(body,"mode","practice",20)
        if (mode!="practice" && mode!="exam") { throw ApiError(422,"请选择练习或模拟考试") }
        let subject=optionalText(body,"subject","",30); let band=optionalText(body,"grade_band","",30); let source=optionalText(body,"source_id","",36)
        let search=optionalText(body,"search","",300)
        let count=studyInteger(body.get("count") ?? JsonInt(5),1,20); let minutes=studyInteger(body.get("minutes") ?? JsonInt(15),1,180)
        let rawIds=body.get("question_ids") ?? JsonArray()
        if (!(rawIds is JsonArray)) { throw ApiError(422,"题目编号格式无效") }
        let ids=(rawIds as JsonArray).getOrThrow()
        if (ids.getItems().size>1) { throw ApiError(422,"单题练习请选择一道题") }
        let strategy=optionalText(body,"selection_strategy","random",20)
        if (strategy!="random" && strategy!="adaptive") { throw ApiError(422,"选题方式无效") }
        let selection=if (strategy=="adaptive" && mode=="practice" && ids.getItems().isEmpty()) { adaptivePracticeSelection(db,owner,subject,band,source,search,count,grade:grade) } else { JsonObject() }
        let selected=tutorArray(selection,"question_ids")
        let taskId=optionalText(body,"learning_task_id","",36)
        if (!taskId.isEmpty() && (mode!="practice" || ids.getItems().size!=1)) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择本次学习推荐的一道题") }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows=if (!taskId.isEmpty()) {
                // Old mistakes keep their original learning context after the student advances a grade.
                let task=taskRow(tx,taskId,owner)
                let plan=tutorObject(tutorObject(task,"student_view"),"recommendation")
                let requested=(editText(ids.getItems()[0],36,minimum:1,trim:true) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                if (tutorText(task,"status")!="completed" || tutorText(plan,"question_source")!="dataset" || tutorText(plan,"question_id")!=requested) { throw ApiError(409,"请从已完成的学习任务打开推荐题") }
                let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,tutorText(task,"question_id"),owner)
                if (!taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot)) { throw ApiError(409,"原题已更新，请重新学习后获取推荐") }
                let candidates=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=?",[Some(requested)]).rows
                if (candidates.isEmpty() || !usableRetrievalCandidate(tutorObject(snapshot,"question"),rowJson("dataset_questions",candidates[0]),"practice")) { throw ApiError(409,"推荐题已更新，请重新获取练习") }
                candidates
            } else if (!selected.getItems().isEmpty()) {
                let picked=ArrayList<HashMap<String,Option<String>>>()
                for (id in selected.getItems()) {
                    let params=ArrayList<Option<String>>(bankFilter(subject,band,source,search,grade:grade)); params.add(sqlValue(id))
                    for (row in tx.query("SELECT q.*"+bankScopedWhere(grade)+" AND q.id=?",params.toArray()).rows) { picked.add(row) }
                }
                picked
            } else if (ids.getItems().isEmpty()) {
                tx.query("SELECT q.*"+bankScopedWhere(grade)+" ORDER BY RAND() LIMIT "+count.toString(),bankFilter(subject,band,source,search,grade:grade)).rows
            } else {
                let params=ArrayList<Option<String>>(bankFilter(subject,band,source,search,grade:grade))
                params.add(Some((editText(ids.getItems()[0],36,minimum:1,trim:true) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()))
                tx.query("SELECT q.*"+bankScopedWhere(grade)+" AND q.id=?",params.toArray()).rows
            }
            if (rows.isEmpty() || (ids.getItems().isEmpty() && rows.size<count)) { throw ApiError(422,"当前范围可用题目不足，请减少题数或更换学科") }
            for (row in rows) {
                if (tutorFlag(datasetImageLayout(rowJson("dataset_questions",row)),"review")) { throw ApiError(422,"题图需要核对，请选择其他题目") }
            }
            let id=newId(); let title=if (mode=="exam") { (if (subject.isEmpty()) { "综合" } else { subject })+"模拟试卷" } else { (if (subject.isEmpty()) { "题库" } else { subject })+"练习" }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO study_sessions (id,user_id,mode,title,subject,grade_band,status,duration_seconds,deadline_at,version,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,'active',?,IF(?='exam',TIMESTAMPADD(SECOND,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)),NULL),1,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(id),Some(owner),Some(mode),Some(title),Some(subject),Some(band),Some((minutes*60).toString()),Some(mode),Some((minutes*60).toString())])
            var position: Int64=0
            for (row in rows) {
                let snapshot=rowJson("dataset_questions",row); let sources=tx.query("SELECT name FROM dataset_sources WHERE id=?",[row["dataset_source_id"]]).rows
                if (!selected.getItems().isEmpty()) { snapshot.put("adaptive_selection",selection) }
                snapshot.put("source_name",JsonString(if (sources.isEmpty()) { "题库" } else { rowText(sources[0],"name") }))
                tx.query("INSERT INTO study_session_items (id,session_id,position,snapshot,student_answer,grading) VALUES (?,?,?,?,NULL,NULL)",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(position.toString()),Some(snapshot.toString())]); position++
            }
            return studyDetail(tx,ownedStudy(tx,id,owner),accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"暂时无法创建练习")
}

func studyAnswer(value: JsonValue): JsonValue {
    if (value is JsonNull) { return JsonNull() }
    if (value is JsonString) { return editText(value,3000) }
    if (let Some(values) <- (value as JsonArray)) {
        if (values.getItems().size>12) { throw ApiError(422,"选项过多") }
        let result=JsonArray(); let seen=HashSet<String>()
        for (entry in values.getItems()) {
            let clean=(editText(entry,30,minimum:1,trim:true) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
            if (seen.contains(clean)) { throw ApiError(422,"选项不能重复") }; seen.add(clean); result.add(JsonString(clean))
        }
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(422,"作答格式无效")
}

func studyAnswered(value: JsonValue): Bool {
    if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) { return !stripWhitespace(text.getValue()).isEmpty() }
    if (let Some(items) <- (value as JsonArray)) { return !items.getItems().isEmpty() }
    return false
}

func gradeStudyItem(item: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    if (!studyAnswered(item["student_answer"])) {
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("correct",JsonBool(false)); result.put("can_update_learning",JsonBool(true)); result.put("feedback",JsonString("未作答")); result.put("method",JsonString("unanswered")); return result
    }
    let question=tutorObject(item,"snapshot"); let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("grade_answer"))
    input.put("text",question["question_text"]); input.put("answer",item["student_answer"]); input.put("expected",question["answer"])
    for (field in ["subject","question_type","options"]) { input.put(field,question[field]) }
    let solution=JsonArray(); for (line in jsonText(question,"solution").split("\n")) { if (!stripWhitespace(line).isEmpty()) { solution.add(JsonString(line)) } }; input.put("reference_solution",solution)
    // Existing internal grading validates model outputs and checks mathematics.
    // Session scores do not change mastery or manufacture wrong-question evidence.
    return algorithmCompute(input,timeoutSeconds:20)
}

func studySessionApi(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let method=context.request.method; let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[4]
    let submitting=parts[parts.size-1]=="submit"
    if ((submitting && method!="POST") || (!submitting && method!="GET" && method!="PATCH")) { throw ApiError(405,"请求方法不支持") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let owner=rowText(user,"id")
        if (method=="GET") { return studyDetail(db,ownedStudy(db,id,owner),accounts) }
        let body=readJson(context); let version=studyInteger(body.get("version") ?? JsonNull(),1,100000000)
        if (!submitting) {
            let raw=body.get("answers") ?? JsonNull()
            if (!(raw is JsonArray)) { throw ApiError(422,"请提交作答列表") }; let answers=(raw as JsonArray).getOrThrow()
            return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
                let session=ownedStudy(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
                if (jsonText(session,"status")!="active") { throw ApiError(409,"已经交卷，不能修改作答") }
                if (tutorNumber(session,"version")!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"作答已在其他页面更新，请重新读取") }
                if (jsonText(session,"mode")=="exam" && tutorNumber(session,"expired")==1) { throw ApiError(409,"考试时间已到，请交卷") }
                let current=studyItems(tx,id); let allowed=HashSet<String>(); let seen=HashSet<String>()
                for (entry in current.getItems()) { allowed.add(jsonText((entry as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"id")) }
                if (answers.getItems().size!=current.getItems().size) { throw ApiError(422,"作答题目与试卷不一致") }
                for (rawAnswer in answers.getItems()) {
                    if (!(rawAnswer is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(422,"作答格式无效") }
                    let answer=(rawAnswer as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let itemId=optionalText(answer,"id","",36)
                    if (!allowed.contains(itemId) || seen.contains(itemId)) { throw ApiError(422,"作答题目与试卷不一致") }; seen.add(itemId)
                    let clean=studyAnswer(answer.get("answer") ?? JsonNull())
                    tx.query("UPDATE study_session_items SET student_answer=? WHERE id=? AND session_id=?",[Some(clean.toString()),Some(itemId),Some(id)])
                }
                tx.query("UPDATE study_sessions SET version=version+1,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(id)])
                return studyDetail(tx,ownedStudy(tx,id,owner),accounts)
            })
        }
        let before=ownedStudy(db,id,owner)
        if (jsonText(before,"status")=="submitted") { return studyDetail(db,before,accounts) }
        if (tutorNumber(before,"version")!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"作答已更新，请重新读取后交卷") }
        let items=studyItems(db,id).getItems(); let results=JsonArray()
        let unavailable=HashSet<String>()
        for (entry in items) { let item=(entry as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); if (!studyQuestionAvailable(db,tutorObject(item,"snapshot"))) { unavailable.add(jsonText(item,"id")) } }
        // Bounded parallel computation outside the transaction; no locks during AI.
        var start: Int64=0
        while (start<items.size) {
            let offset=start; let size=if (items.size-start>4) { 4 } else { items.size-start }
            let jobs=Array(size,{ index =>
                let item=(items[offset+index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                let blocked=unavailable.contains(jsonText(item,"id"))
                spawn { if (blocked) { studyUnavailableGrade() } else { gradeStudyItem(item) } }
            })
            for (job in jobs) { results.add(job.get()) }; start+=size
        }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let session=ownedStudy(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            if (jsonText(session,"status")=="submitted") { return studyDetail(tx,session,accounts) }
            if (tutorNumber(session,"version")!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"作答已更新，本次判分未保存，请重新交卷") }
            var correct: Int64=0; var pending: Int64=0; var answered: Int64=0; var excluded: Int64=0
            for (index in 0..items.size) {
                let item=(items[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                let available=studyQuestionAvailable(tx,tutorObject(item,"snapshot"),lock:true)
                let grading=if (available) { (results.getItems()[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow() } else { studyUnavailableGrade() }
                if (studyAnswered(item["student_answer"])) { answered++ }
                if (!available) { excluded++ } else if (!tutorFlag(grading,"can_update_learning")) { pending++ } else if (tutorFlag(grading,"correct")) { correct++ }
                tx.query("UPDATE study_session_items SET grading=? WHERE id=? AND session_id=?",[Some(grading.toString()),Some(jsonText(item,"id")),Some(id)])
            }
            let summary=JsonObject(); summary.put("correct",JsonInt(correct)); summary.put("pending",JsonInt(pending)); if (excluded>0) { summary.put("excluded",JsonInt(excluded)) }; summary.put("answered",JsonInt(answered)); summary.put("total",JsonInt(items.size)); summary.put("score",JsonInt(correct*5)); summary.put("max_score",JsonInt((items.size-excluded)*5))
            tx.query("UPDATE study_sessions SET status='submitted',results=?,version=version+1,submitted_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(summary.toString()),Some(id)])
            return studyDetail(tx,ownedStudy(tx,id,owner),accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"练习记录暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/study_mistake.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.env.*
import std.convert.*
import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

// Resolve only server-owned snapshots. Never accept image paths supplied by a client.
func studyMistakeSource(db: Database, owner: String, body: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let sessionId=optionalText(body,"study_session_id","",36)
    let itemId=optionalText(body,"study_item_id","",36)
    if (sessionId.isEmpty() && itemId.isEmpty()) { return JsonObject() }
    let session=ownedStudy(db,sessionId,owner)
    if (tutorText(session,"status")!="submitted") { throw ApiError(409,"请先交卷再加入错题本") }
    for (raw in studyItems(db,sessionId).getItems()) {
        let item=(raw as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        if (tutorText(item,"id")!=itemId) { continue }
        let grading=tutorObject(item,"grading")
        let correct=grading.get("correct") ?? JsonNull()
        if (!(correct is JsonBool) || tutorFlag(grading,"correct")) { throw ApiError(422,"此题尚未判错") }
        let source=tutorObject(item,"snapshot")
        if (!studyQuestionAvailable(db,source)) { throw ApiError(409,"此题已暂停使用") }
        for (key in ["question_text","subject","grade"]) { body.put(key,source.get(key) ?? JsonNull()) }
        let options=datasetDisplayOptions(source)
        if (options.getItems().size>8) { throw ApiError(422,"选项数量超过支持范围") }
        for (i in 0..options.getItems().size) {
            let option=(options[i] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            let key=tutorText(option,"key")
            let explicit=tutorText(option,"label")
            let label=if (!explicit.isEmpty()) { explicit } else if (!key.isEmpty()) { key } else { ["A","B","C","D","E","F","G","H"][i] }
            option.put("label",JsonString(label))
            if (tutorText(option,"text").isEmpty()) { option.put("text",JsonString("见选项${label}配图")) }
        }
        body.put("options",options)
        let kind=tutorText(source,"question_type")
        let multiple=kind.contains("多") || ["multiple_choice","multiple-response","multiple_response","multi_choice","multi-select"].contains(kind)
        body.put("question_type",JsonString(if (options.getItems().isEmpty()) { "综合题" } else if (multiple) { "多选题" } else { "单选题" }))
        body.put("student_answer",JsonString(tutorAnswerText(item["student_answer"])))
        body.put("student_steps",JsonArray())
        return source
    }
    throw ApiError(404,"没有找到这道练习题")
}

func copyStudyFigures(db: Database, id: String, source: JsonObject, copied: ArrayList<String>): Unit {
    let paths=tutorArray(source,"image_paths")
    let layout=datasetImageLayout(source)
    let indices=tutorArray(layout,"stem")
    for (raw in tutorArray(layout,"options").getItems()) {
        for (index in (raw as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) { indices.add(index) }
    }
    let working=getWorkingDirectory().toString()
    let project=if (working.endsWith("/backend-cangjie")) { working[..working.size-16] } else { working }
    let configured=getVariable("CANGJIE_QUESTION_BANK_ROOT") ?? "data/question_bank_assets"
    let root=if (configured.startsWith("/")) { configured } else { project+"/"+configured }
    for (index in indices.getItems()) {
        let stored=(paths[Int64.parse(index.toString())] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        let absolute=if (stored.startsWith("/")) { stored } else { project+"/"+stored }
        if (!absolute.startsWith(root+"/")) { throw ApiError(422,"题库配图路径无效") }
        try (file=MediaFile(root,absolute[root.size+1..])) {
            if (file.length<=0 || file.length>64*1024*1024) { throw ApiError(422,"题库配图大小无效") }
            let bytes=Array<UInt8>(file.length,repeat:0)
            var offset: Int64=0
            while (offset<bytes.size) {
                let amount=file.readAt(bytes[offset..],offset)
                if (amount<=0) { throw ApiError(503,"题库配图读取失败") }; offset+=amount
            }
            let path="uploads/${id}/bank-${index.toString()}."+uploadedImageSuffix(bytes)
            writeMedia(path,bytes)
            copied.add(path)
            db.query("INSERT INTO question_assets (id,question_id,kind,path,sha256,bbox,description,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'question_figure',?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),'题库配图',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(path),Some(sha256(bytes))])
        }
    }
}
```

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func systemState(probe!: Bool=false): JsonObject {
    let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("system_state")); input.put("probe_provider",JsonBool(probe)); return algorithmCompute(input)
}

func systemApi(context: HttpContext, kind: String): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,if (kind=="probe") { "POST" } else { "GET" })
    if (kind=="probe") { return systemState(probe:true)["provider"] }
    if (kind=="providers") { return systemState()["provider"] }
    if (kind=="voice") { return systemState()["voice"] }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        db.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM users")
        let state=systemState(); let provider=tutorObject(state,"provider"); let result=JsonObject()
        result.put("status",JsonString("healthy")); result.put("database",JsonString("mysql")); result.put("ai",provider["last_status"]); result.put("image_recognition",JsonBool(tutorNumber(provider,"vision_entries")>0)); return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/tutor_activities.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import std.fs.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*

func teachingTemplates(): JsonObject {
    return (JsonValue.fromStr(String.fromUtf8(File.readFrom("schema/teaching_templates.json"))) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
}

func tutorStringArray(values: Array<String>): JsonArray {
    let result = JsonArray()
    for (value in values) { result.add(JsonString(value)) }
    return result
}

func tutorOptions(values: Array<(String,String)>): JsonArray {
    let result = JsonArray()
    for ((value,label) in values) {
        let item = JsonObject(); item.put("value",JsonString(value)); item.put("label",JsonString(label)); result.add(item)
    }
    return result
}

func tutorActivity(id: String, title: String, question: String, options: Array<(String,String)>,
    answers: Array<String>, hint: String, teaching: String): JsonObject {
    let result = (teachingTemplates()["activity_defaults"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    result.put("id",JsonString(id)); result.put("title",JsonString(title)); result.put("question",JsonString(question))
    result.put("options",tutorOptions(options)); result.put("correct_answers",tutorStringArray(answers))
    result.put("hint",JsonString(hint)); result.put("teaching",JsonString(teaching))
    return result
}

func tutorTopic(points: Array<String>, questionType: String): String {
    let raw = stripWhitespace(if (points.isEmpty()) { if (questionType.isEmpty()) { "错题学习" } else { questionType } } else { points[0] })
    let labels = (teachingTemplates()["question_type_labels"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    if (let Some(value) <- labels.get(raw)) { return (value as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue() }
    return if (raw.isEmpty()) { "错题学习" } else { raw }
}

func buildUnderstandingPlan(text: String, subject: String, questionType: String,
    knowledgePoints: Array<String>, referenceSteps: Array<String>): JsonArray {
    let points = ArrayList<String>(); let references = ArrayList<String>()
    let context = StringBuilder(); context.append(text)
    for (item in knowledgePoints) {
        if (!stripWhitespace(item).isEmpty()) { points.add(item); context.append(" "); context.append(item) }
    }
    for (item in referenceSteps) {
        if (!stripWhitespace(item).isEmpty()) { references.add(item); context.append(" "); context.append(item) }
    }
    let templates = teachingTemplates(); let combined = context.toString()
    if (combined.contains("正方体") && (combined.contains("展开图") || combined.contains("相对面"))) {
        return (templates["cube_plan"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow()
    }
    let prompts = (templates["guided_prompts"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    let prompt = (prompts.get(subject).getOrDefault({ => prompts["数学"] }) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
    let topic = if (points.isEmpty()) { tutorTopic([],questionType) } else { points[0] }
    let reference = if (references.isEmpty()) { prompt } else { references[0] }
    let plan = JsonArray()
    plan.add(tutorActivity("understanding-1","先抓住${topic}","完成这道题时，第一步最应该关注什么？",
        [("key",topic),("answer","只记住最后答案"),("skip","跳过题目条件直接计算")], ["key"],
        "先回到题目条件，找到与${topic}直接相关的信息。","这道题先围绕“${topic}”判断，再继续后面的步骤。"))
    plan.add(tutorActivity("understanding-2","确认下一步做法","下面哪种做法更合适？",
        [("reference",academicSummary(reference,42)),("guess","不看条件，凭印象猜一个答案")], ["reference"],prompt,"关键做法是：${reference}"))
    return plan
}

func publicTutorActivity(activity: JsonObject, attempt: Int64, feedback!: String = "", teaching!: String = ""): JsonObject {
    let result = JsonObject()
    for (key in ["id","type","title","question"]) { result.put(key,activity[key]) }
    for (key in ["options","keywords"]) { result.put(key,activity.get(key).getOrDefault({ => JsonArray() })) }
    result.put("visual",activity.get("visual").getOrDefault({ => JsonObject() }))
    result.put("attempt",JsonInt(attempt)); result.put("max_attempts",JsonInt(2))
    result.put("feedback",JsonString(feedback))
    result.put("teaching",if (teaching.isEmpty()) { activity.get("teaching").getOrDefault({ => JsonString("") }) } else { JsonString(teaching) })
    for (key in ["teaching_title","sentence_starter","input_mode","variant_id"]) {
        if (let Some(value) <- activity.get(key)) { result.put(key,value) }
    }
    let filtered = JsonObject()
    for ((key,value) in result.getFields()) {
        if (key == "options" || key == "keywords" || key == "visual" ||
            (!(value is JsonNull) && !jsonEquivalent(value,JsonString("")))) { filtered.put(key,value) }
    }
    return filtered
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/tutor_answer.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func tutorMicroPassed(source: JsonObject, answer: JsonValue, question: JsonObject): Bool {
    let expected = tutorArray(source,"correct_answers"); let input = JsonObject()
    input.put("operation",JsonString("grade_answer")); input.put("text",JsonString(if (tutorText(source,"question").isEmpty()) { jsonText(question,"question_text") } else { tutorText(source,"question") }))
    input.put("answer",answer); input.put("expected",if (tutorText(source,"type") == "multiple_choice") { expected } else if (expected.getItems().isEmpty()) { JsonString("") } else { expected[0] })
    input.put("subject",question["subject"]); input.put("question_type",source["type"]); input.put("options",tutorArray(source,"options"))
    let grade = algorithmCompute(input)
    return tutorFlag(grade,"correct") && tutorFlag(grade,"can_update_learning")
}

func tutorAnswerLabel(activity: JsonObject, answer: JsonValue): String {
    let values = if (let Some(items) <- (answer as JsonArray)) { items } else { let array = JsonArray(); array.add(answer); array }
    let text = StringBuilder(); var first = true
    for (value in values.getItems()) {
        let raw = tutorAnswerText(value); var label = raw
        for (option in tutorArray(activity,"options").getItems()) {
            let item = (option as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (tutorText(item,"value") == raw) { label = tutorText(item,"label"); break }
        }
        if (!first) { text.append("、") }; first = false; text.append(label)
    }
    return text.toString()
}

public func answerTutorActivity(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size-3]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body = readJson(context,maxBytes: 2 * 1024 * 1024)
        let answer = body.get("answer") ?? JsonString("")
        if (let Some(items) <- (answer as JsonArray)) {
            if (items.getItems().size > 100) { throw ApiError(422,"选项数量过多") }
            for (item in items.getItems()) { editText(item,12000) }
        } else { editText(answer,12000) }
        let working = (editText(body.get("working") ?? JsonString(""),12000,trim: true) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        let elapsedNumber = editNumber(body.get("elapsed_seconds") ?? JsonInt(0))
        if (elapsedNumber < 0.0 || elapsedNumber > 2147483647.0) { throw ApiError(422,"作答时长无效") }
        let elapsed = Int64(elapsedNumber)
        let unknown = editBool(body.get("unknown") ?? JsonBool(false)).toString() == "true" || tutorAnswerText(answer) == "__unknown__"
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
            let session = rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let state = tutorObject(session,"state"); session.put("state",state)
            let stage = canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage")); session.put("stage",JsonString(stage))
            if (stage != "understanding" && stage != "teach_back" && stage != "near_transfer" && stage != "far_transfer") { throw ApiError(409,"当前阶段不能继续作答") }
            let snapshot = tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"]); let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (tutorFlag(state,"question_changed") || jsonText(question,"status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let current = tutorActivityForSession(tx,session,snapshot)
            if (!(current is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(409,"当前小任务已经变化，请刷新后继续") }
            let activity = (current as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (tutorText(body,"activity_id") != jsonText(activity,"id")) { throw ApiError(409,"当前小任务已经变化，请刷新后继续") }
            let handwriting = validateTutorHandwriting(body,rowText(user,"id"),id)
            var passed = false; var feedback = ""; var transfer = JsonObject()
            let compressed = tutorText(tutorObject(state,"teaching_policy"),"mode") == "compressed"
            if (stage == "near_transfer" || stage == "far_transfer") {
                transfer = answerTutorTransfer(tx,session,snapshot,answer,unknown,elapsed); passed = tutorFlag(transfer,"passed"); feedback = tutorText(transfer,"feedback")
            } else if (stage == "teach_back") {
                let assessment = evaluateTutorTeachBack(stripWhitespace(tutorAnswerText(answer)),question,tutorStringArray(tutorKnowledge(snapshot)),tutorStringArray(tutorRowTexts(snapshot["references"])))
                passed = tutorFlag(assessment,"passed"); let count = tutorNumber(state,"teach_back_attempts")+1; state.put("teach_back_attempts",JsonInt(count))
                if (passed || count >= 2) {
                    state.put("teach_back",JsonBool(passed)); state.put("teach_back_passed",JsonBool(passed))
                    if (!passed) { state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)) }
                    session.put("stage",JsonString("near_transfer")); state.put("next_goal",JsonString("完成一道相似题"))
                    feedback = if (passed) { "讲清楚了，接着做一道相似题。" } else { "这次还少一个关键点，我先记录下来，我们继续练一道相似题。" }
                } else { feedback = jsonText(assessment,"feedback") }
            } else if (compressed && tutorText(activity,"type") == "explanation") {
                state.put("answer_reveal_acknowledged",JsonBool(true)); passed = true
                if (tutorAnswerText(answer) == "need_help") { state.put("targeted_check_pending",JsonBool(true)); feedback = "只确认一个易错点。" }
                else {
                    state.put("understanding_passed",JsonBool(true)); state.put("understood",JsonBool(true)); state.put("near_transfer",JsonNull())
                    session.put("stage",JsonString("near_transfer")); state.put("next_goal",JsonString("做一道相似题")); feedback = "好，直接练一道。"
                }
            } else if (compressed && tutorFlag(state,"targeted_check_pending")) {
                let source = tutorObject(tutorObject(state,"teaching_brief"),"check")
                passed = !unknown && tutorMicroPassed(source,answer,question)
                let attempts = tutorObject(state,"activity_attempts"); let key = tutorText(source,"id"); let count = tutorNumber(attempts,key)+1
                attempts.put(key,JsonInt(count)); state.put("activity_attempts",attempts)
                if (passed || count >= 2 || unknown) {
                    if (!passed) { state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)) }
                    state.put("targeted_check_pending",JsonBool(false)); state.put("understanding_passed",JsonBool(true)); state.put("understood",JsonBool(true))
                    state.put("near_transfer",JsonNull()); session.put("stage",JsonString("near_transfer")); state.put("next_goal",JsonString("做一道相似题"))
                    feedback = if (passed) { "答对了，练一道类似的。" } else { "记住这个条件，我们直接练一道。" }
                } else { let hint = tutorText(source,"hint"); feedback = academicSummary(if (hint.isEmpty()) { "再用刚才的关键条件判断一次。" } else { hint },70) }
            } else {
                let plan = tutorArray(state,"understanding_plan"); let index = tutorNumber(state,"understanding_index")
                if (index < 0 || index >= plan.getItems().size) { throw ApiError(409,"活动计划已变化，请刷新") }
                let source = (plan[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                if (tutorFlag(state,"understanding_confirmation")) {
                    state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)); state.put("understanding_confirmation",JsonBool(false)); state.put("understanding_index",JsonInt(index+1)); passed = true
                    feedback = "记住这个判断方法，我们继续下一个小问题。"
                } else {
                    passed = !unknown && tutorMicroPassed(source,answer,question)
                    let attempts = tutorObject(state,"activity_attempts"); let key = jsonText(source,"id"); let count = tutorNumber(attempts,key)+1
                    attempts.put(key,JsonInt(count)); state.put("activity_attempts",attempts)
                    if (passed) { state.put("understanding_index",JsonInt(index+1)); feedback = "答对了，我们继续。" }
                    else if (unknown || count >= 2) { state.put("understanding_confirmation",JsonBool(true)); state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)); feedback = "先看一个最短讲解，再确认一次就继续。" }
                    else { feedback = tutorText(source,"hint"); if (!feedback.isEmpty()) { feedback = academicSummary(feedback,70) } }
                }
                if (tutorNumber(state,"understanding_index") >= plan.getItems().size) {
                    state.put("understanding_passed",JsonBool(true)); state.put("understood",JsonBool(true)); state.put("next_goal",JsonString("用自己的话说一遍")); feedback = "已经抓住这个关键点。"
                }
            }
            if (jsonText(session,"stage") == "near_transfer") { ensureTutorVariant(tx,session,snapshot,"near") }
            state.put("activity_feedback",JsonString(feedback)); state.put("branch",JsonString("activity_${jsonText(session,"stage")}_${if (passed) { "pass" } else { "continue" }}"))
            tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage = ?,state = ?,turn_count = turn_count+1,updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",[Some(jsonText(session,"stage")),Some(state.toString()),Some(id)])
            let userMeta = JsonObject(); userMeta.put("activity_id",activity["id"]); userMeta.put("activity_type",activity["type"])
            userMeta.put("working",JsonString(if (working.isEmpty()) { "" } else { shortTitle(working,500) })); userMeta.put("handwriting_image_url",handwriting["image_url"]); userMeta.put("handwriting_recognition",handwriting["recognition"]); userMeta.put("grading",tutorObject(transfer,"grading"))
            if (let Some(consumed) <- transfer.get("attempt_consumed")) { userMeta.put("attempt_consumed",consumed) }; if (tutorFlag(transfer,"unknown")) { userMeta.put("unknown",JsonBool(true)) }
            let assistantMeta = JsonObject(); assistantMeta.put("activity_id",activity["id"]); assistantMeta.put("passed",JsonBool(passed)); assistantMeta.put("assisted",JsonBool(tutorFlag(state,"assisted")))
            for ((role,content,metadata) in [("user",tutorAnswerLabel(activity,answer),userMeta),("assistant",if (tutorText(transfer,"assistant_content").isEmpty()) { feedback } else { tutorText(transfer,"assistant_content") },assistantMeta)]) {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_turns (id,session_id,role,content,stage,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(role),Some(content),Some(jsonText(session,"stage")),Some(metadata.toString())])
            }
            let saved = rowJson("tutor_sessions",tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ?",[Some(id)]).rows[0])
            return tutorSessionPayload(tx,accounts,saved,snapshot)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/tutor_completion.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*

func cognitionFirstLabel(value: JsonValue): String {
    if (let Some(values) <- (value as JsonArray)) {
        for (item in values.getItems()) {
            var text = ""
            if (let Some(object) <- (item as JsonObject)) {
                for (key in ["name","label","code"]) { text = tutorText(object,key); if (!text.isEmpty()) { break } }
            } else { text = tutorAnswerText(item) }
            text = stripWhitespace(text); if (!text.isEmpty()) { return text }
        }
    }
    return ""
}

func completeTutorLearning(db: Database, session: JsonObject, snapshot: JsonObject, insufficient: Bool): JsonObject {
    let state=tutorObject(session,"state"); let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question")
    let owner=sqlValue(session["user_id"]); let questionId=jsonText(question,"id")
    let evidence=JsonObject()
    for (key in ["teach_back","near_transfer","far_transfer"]) { evidence.put(key,state.get(key) ?? JsonNull()) }
    evidence.put("hint_level",session["hint_level"]); evidence.put("strategy",JsonString(if (tutorFlag(state,"assisted")) { "assisted_progression" } else { "micro_activity" }))
    evidence.put("tutor_session_id",session["id"])
    let updated=applyLearningEvidence(db,jsonText(session,"user_id"),question,evidence)
    let score=editNumber(tutorObject(updated,"mastery_after")["mastery_score"])
    let days = if (insufficient) { 1 } else if (tutorFlag(state,"assisted")) { 3 } else { 15 }
    let planId=newId()
    db.query("INSERT INTO review_plans (id,user_id,question_id,due_at,interval_days,reason,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,TIMESTAMPADD(DAY,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP()),?,?,'pending',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(planId),owner,Some(questionId),Some(days.toString()),Some(days.toString()),Some(if (insufficient) { "迁移练习还不稳定，已提高复习优先级" } else { "本轮学习已完成，按掌握情况复习" })])
    state.put("completion_summary",JsonString(if (insufficient) { "这次换一种题目还不够稳定，我已经把它放到近期优先复习。" } else { "本轮已经完成。下一次复习会根据这次是否需要提示来安排。" }))
    state.put("mastery_proven",JsonBool(!insufficient && tutorFlag(state,"far_transfer")))
    session.put("stage",JsonString("finished")); session.put("status",JsonString("completed")); session.put("mastery_score",JsonFloat(score))
    db.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET status='completed',mastery_score=? WHERE id=?",[Some(score.toString()),sqlValue(session["id"])])
    let output = JsonObject(); output.put("mastery",JsonFloat(score)); output.put("interval_days",JsonInt(days)); output.put("priority",JsonString(if (insufficient) { "high" } else { "normal" }))
    recordTutorTool(db,jsonText(session,"id"),"activity_finish","update_cognition",evidence,output)
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("mastery",updated["mastery_after"]); result.put("misconception",updated["misconception_after"]); result.put("review",rowJson("review_plans",db.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE id=?",[Some(planId)]).rows[0])); return result
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/tutor_dialogue.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.base64.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func dialogueSnapshot(db: Database, id: String, owner: Option<String>): JsonObject {
    let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),owner]).rows
    if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
    let session=rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let stage=canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage")); session.put("stage",JsonString(stage))
    if (stage=="finished") { throw ApiError(409,"本次学习已经完成") }
    let questionId=jsonText(session,"question_id"); let snapshot=tutorDataSnapshot(db,questionId,owner); let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question")
    if (jsonText(question,"status")=="archived" || tutorFlag(tutorObject(session,"state"),"question_changed")) { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
    snapshot.put("session",session)
    snapshot.put("steps",queryArray(db,"student_steps","SELECT * FROM student_steps WHERE work_id=(SELECT id FROM student_works WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1) ORDER BY step_index,id",questionId))
    snapshot.put("work",firstJson(queryArray(db,"student_works","SELECT * FROM student_works WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",questionId)))
    snapshot.put("assets",queryArray(db,"question_assets","SELECT * FROM question_assets WHERE question_id=? AND kind IN ('crop','question_figure') ORDER BY created_at,id",questionId))
    snapshot.put("turns",queryArray(db,"tutor_turns","SELECT * FROM tutor_turns WHERE session_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 7",id))
    let memory=learningMemory(db,owner.getOrThrow(),question)
    snapshot.put("memory",memory["legacy_rows"]); snapshot.put("memory_snapshot",memory["snapshot"]); return snapshot
}

func dialogueContext(snapshot: JsonObject, message: String): JsonObject {
    let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question"); let analysis=tutorObject(snapshot,"analysis"); let session=tutorObject(snapshot,"session"); let state=tutorObject(session,"state")
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("memory_snapshot",snapshot["memory_snapshot"])
    for (key in ["subject","question_type","question_text","student_answer","knowledge_points"]) { result.put(key,question[key]) }
    let points=JsonArray(); let labels=HashSet<String>()
    for (container in [question,analysis]) { for (point in tutorArray(container,"knowledge_points").getItems()) { let label=point.toString(); if (!labels.contains(label) && points.getItems().size<10) { points.add(point); labels.add(label) } } }
    result.put("knowledge_points",points)
    result.put("grade",JsonString(tutorText(question,"grade")))
    result.put("final_answer",JsonString(tutorText(analysis,"final_answer"))); result.put("reference_steps",tutorStringArray(tutorRowTexts(snapshot["references"]))); result.put("student_steps",tutorStringArray(tutorRowTexts(snapshot["steps"])))
    result.put("student_message",JsonString(message)); result.put("turn_count",JsonInt(tutorNumber(session,"turn_count")+1)); result.put("hint_level",session["hint_level"])
    result.put("repeated_count",JsonInt(if (message==tutorText(state,"last_message")) { tutorNumber(state,"repeated_count")+1 } else { 0 }))
    let recent=JsonArray(); let rows=tutorArray(snapshot,"turns").getItems()
    for (offset in 0..rows.size) { let turn=(rows[rows.size-1-offset] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let item=JsonObject(); item.put("role",turn["role"]); item.put("content",turn["content"]); recent.add(item) }
    let input=JsonObject(); input.put("role",JsonString("user")); input.put("content",JsonString(message)); recent.add(input); result.put("recent_turns",recent); return result
}

func dialogueCardScore(card: JsonObject, query: String, grade: String): Int64 {
    let content=tutorObject(card,"content"); let level=tutorText(content,"grade")
    if (grade.startsWith("初") && level.startsWith("高")) { return 0 }
    let text=knowledgeLabel(query); var score: Int64=0
    for (label in [jsonText(card,"name"),jsonText(card,"code")]) {
        let key=knowledgeLabel(label); if (runeCount(key)>=2 && text.contains(key)) { score=100+runeCount(key) }
    }
    for (value in tutorArray(content,"related_knowledge").getItems()) {
        if (let Some(alias) <- (value as JsonString)) {
            let key=knowledgeLabel(alias.getValue()); if (runeCount(key)>=2 && text.contains(key) && score==0) { score=runeCount(key) }
        }
    }
    return score
}

func dialogueKnowledge(db: Database, query: String, subject: String, grade: String): JsonArray {
    let available=db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE subject=? ORDER BY code LIMIT 200",[Some(subject)]).rows
    let rows=ArrayList<HashMap<String,Option<String>>>()
    let consumed=HashSet<String>()
    for (_ in 0..3) {
        var best: Int64=0; var selected: Int64 = -1
        for (index in 0..available.size) {
            let card=rowJson("knowledge_points",available[index]); let score=dialogueCardScore(card,query,grade)
            if (!consumed.contains(jsonText(card,"id")) && score>best) { best=score; selected=index }
        }
        if (selected<0) { break }; rows.add(available[selected]); consumed.add(rowText(available[selected],"id"))
    }
    let result=JsonArray(); let seen=HashSet<String>()
    for (row in rows) {
        let card=rowJson("knowledge_points",row); card.put("retrieval_role",JsonString("anchor")); result.add(card); seen.add(jsonText(card,"code"))
    }
    // Seed relation source is the current topic, target is its prerequisite.
    for (row in rows) {
        let anchor=rowJson("knowledge_points",row)
        for (dependency in db.query("SELECT k.* FROM knowledge_relations r JOIN knowledge_points k ON r.target_id=k.id WHERE r.source_id=? AND r.relation_type='prerequisite' AND k.subject=? ORDER BY k.code LIMIT 2",[sqlValue(anchor["id"]),Some(subject)]).rows) {
            let card=rowJson("knowledge_points",dependency); let code=jsonText(card,"code")
            if (result.getItems().size>=8 || seen.contains(code)) { continue }
            card.put("retrieval_role",JsonString("prerequisite")); card.put("anchor_code",anchor["code"]); result.add(card); seen.add(code)
        }
    }
    return result
}

func tutorDialogue(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=readJson(context); let message=stripWhitespace(optionalText(body,"message","",12000))
        if (message.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"请输入你的想法") }
        let snapshot=db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx => dialogueSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"]) }); let input=JsonObject(); let domain=dialogueContext(snapshot,message)
        input.put("operation",JsonString("tutor_dialogue_prepare")); input.put("dialogue_context",domain); input.put("dialogue_memory_rows",snapshot["memory"])
        let preparation=algorithmCompute(input); let plan=tutorObject(preparation,"plan"); let knowledge=tutorText(plan,"tool_name")=="knowledge_search"
        let cards=if (knowledge) { dialogueKnowledge(db,jsonText(preparation,"knowledge_query"),jsonText(domain,"subject"),jsonText(domain,"grade")) } else { JsonArray() }
        input.put("operation",JsonString("tutor_dialogue_finish")); input.put("dialogue_preparation",preparation); input.put("dialogue_cards",cards)
        let images=JsonArray(); let seen=HashSet<String>()
        for (value in tutorArray(snapshot,"assets").getItems()) {
            let path=jsonText((value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"path")
            if (seen.contains(path)) { continue }; seen.add(path)
            try { images.add(JsonString(toBase64String(readMedia(path)))) } catch (error: ApiError) { if (error.status!=404) { throw error } }
        }
        input.put("images_base64",images); let computed=algorithmCompute(input)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let current=dialogueSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"])
            if (!jsonEquivalent(snapshot,current)) { throw ApiError(409,"题目、会话或学习记录已变化，请刷新后重试") }
            if (knowledge && !jsonEquivalent(cards,dialogueKnowledge(tx,jsonText(preparation,"knowledge_query"),jsonText(domain,"subject"),jsonText(domain,"grade")))) { throw ApiError(409,"知识卡已更新，请重试") }
            let session=tutorObject(current,"session"); let state=tutorObject(session,"state"); let stage=jsonText(session,"stage"); let graph=tutorObject(computed,"graph"); let agent=tutorObject(computed,"agent"); let tool=tutorObject(agent,"tool"); let verification=tutorObject(agent,"verification")
            state.put("last_message",JsonString(message)); state.put("repeated_count",domain["repeated_count"])
            for (key in ["branch","strategy","next_goal","mastery_proven"]) { state.put(key,graph[key]) }
            if (state.get("understanding_passed").isNone()) { state.put("understanding_passed",JsonBool(false)) }
            state.put("dialogue_stage",graph["stage"]); state.put("agent_verification",verification["status"]); state.put("agent_memory_refs",plan["memory_source_ids"])
            if (tutorText(plan,"tool_name")=="visualization") { state.put("visualization_type",tutorObject(tool,"evidence").get("visualization_type") ?? JsonNull()) }
            tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage=?,turn_count=?,hint_level=?,state=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(stage),sqlValue(domain["turn_count"]),sqlValue(graph["hint_level"]),Some(state.toString()),Some(id)])
            let userMeta=JsonObject(); userMeta.put("input_stage",JsonString(stage)); let metadata=JsonObject()
            for (key in ["branch","strategy","next_goal","mastery_proven"]) { metadata.put(key,graph[key]) }
            metadata.put("knowledge_sources",computed.get("knowledge_sources") ?? JsonArray()); metadata.put("tool_name",plan["tool_name"]); metadata.put("decision_verified",verification["status"]); metadata.put("planner_status",agent["planner_status"])
            for ((role,text,meta) in [("user",message,userMeta),("assistant",jsonText(computed,"reply"),metadata)]) { tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_turns (id,session_id,role,content,stage,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(role),Some(text),Some(stage),Some(meta.toString())]) }
            let output=tutorObject(tool,"evidence"); output.put("stage",JsonString(stage)); output.put("next_goal",graph["next_goal"]); output.put("executed",tool["executed"])
            let details=JsonObject(); details.put("memory_manifest",memoryAuditManifest(tutorObject(snapshot,"memory_snapshot"))); for (key in ["observation","plan","verification","planner_status"]) { details.put(key,agent[key]) }
            recordTutorTool(tx,id,jsonText(graph,"branch"),jsonText(plan,"tool_name"),tutorObject(tool,"consumed_input"),output,runResult:details)
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("message",computed["reply"]); result.put("stage",JsonString(stage)); result.put("hint_level",graph["hint_level"]); for ((key,value) in metadata.getFields()) { result.put(key,value) }; return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/tutor_finish.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func publicTutorSession(session: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let result=(JsonValue.fromStr(session.toString()) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    result.put("state",publicTutorProgress(tutorObject(session,"state"))); return result
}

func finishTutor(context: HttpContext,accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); readJson(context,defaultEmpty:true)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
            let session=rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); session.put("stage",JsonString(canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage"))))
            if (jsonText(session,"stage")!="finished") { throw ApiError(409,"请先完成讲清、相似题和综合应用") }
            let result=JsonObject()
            if (jsonText(session,"status")=="completed") {
                result.put("session",publicTutorSession(session)); result.put("mastery",JsonNull())
                let plans=tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? AND question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[user["id"],sqlValue(session["question_id"])]).rows
                result.put("review",if (plans.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { rowJson("review_plans",plans[0]) }); return result
            }
            let snapshot=tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"]); let state=tutorObject(session,"state"); session.put("state",state)
            if (tutorFlag(state,"question_changed") || jsonText(tutorObject(snapshot,"question"),"status")=="archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let insufficient=tutorFlag(state,"transfer_insufficient") || (state.get("far_transfer") ?? JsonNull()).toString()=="false"
            let learning=completeTutorLearning(tx,session,snapshot,insufficient)
            tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage=?,state=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(session["stage"]),Some(state.toString()),Some(id)])
            let saved=rowJson("tutor_sessions",tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=?",[Some(id)]).rows[0])
            result.put("session",publicTutorSession(saved)); result.put("mastery",learning["mastery"]); result.put("review",learning["review"]); return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/tutor_navigation.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func recordTutorTool(db: Database, sessionId: String, branch: String, name: String, input: JsonObject, output: JsonObject, runResult!: JsonObject=JsonObject()): Unit {
    let result = (JsonValue.fromStr(output.toString()) as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); result.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); for ((key,value) in runResult.getFields()) { result.put(key,value) }; let runId = newId()
    db.query("INSERT INTO agent_runs (id,session_id,agent_name,status,result,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'mindtrace_tutor','completed',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(runId),Some(sessionId),Some(result.toString())])
    db.query("INSERT INTO agent_tool_calls (id,agent_run_id,tool_name,input_json,output_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(newId()),Some(runId),Some(name),Some(input.toString()),Some(output.toString())])
}

public func navigateTutor(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, rewind: Bool): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let body = if (rewind) { JsonObject() } else { readJson(context) }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
            let session = rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let state = tutorObject(session,"state"); session.put("state",state)
            let snapshot = tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"])
            let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (jsonText(question,"status") == "archived" || tutorFlag(state,"question_changed")) { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let current = canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage"))
            let compressed = tutorText(tutorObject(state,"teaching_policy"),"mode") == "compressed"
            var target = ""
            try {
                if (rewind) { target = if (compressed && current == "near_transfer") { "understanding" } else { previousTutorStage(current) } }
                else {
                    if (tutorText(body,"target_stage") != "teach_back") { throw ApiError(422,"只支持进入讲清阶段") }
                    target = transitionTutorStage(current,"understanding_advance",tutorFlag(state,"understanding_passed"))
                }
            } catch (error: IllegalArgumentException) { throw ApiError(409,error.message) }
            let branch = if (rewind) { "rewind_${current}_to_${target}" } else { "understanding_advance" }
            let input = JsonObject(); input.put("from",JsonString(current))
            let output = JsonObject(); output.put("to",JsonString(target))
            let message = "现在请用自己的话讲一遍：用了什么规则，为什么能用，又怎样得到结论。"
            if (!rewind) {
                state.put("next_goal",JsonString("用自己的话说明规则、依据和结论"))
                input.put("event",JsonString("understanding_advance"))
                tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_turns (id,session_id,role,content,stage,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'assistant',?,'teach_back',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(message),Some(##"{"branch":"understanding_advance","explicit_transition":true}"##)])
            } else {
                if (current == "teach_back" || current == "near_transfer") {
                    state.put("teach_back",JsonNull()); state.put("teach_back_passed",JsonNull()); state.put("teach_back_attempts",JsonInt(0))
                }
                if (current == "teach_back") {
                    state.put("understanding_passed",JsonBool(true)); state.put("understood",JsonBool(true)); state.put("next_goal",JsonString("回顾已经理解的关键点"))
                } else {
                    for (key in ["near_transfer","near_confirmation","far_transfer"]) { state.put(key,JsonNull()) }
                    for (key in ["near_attempts","far_attempts"]) { state.put(key,JsonInt(0)) }
                    state.put("next_goal",JsonString(if (current == "near_transfer") { "重新用自己的话说清关键判断" } else { "重新完成一道相似题" }))
                    if (compressed && current == "near_transfer") {
                        for (key in ["understanding_passed","understood","answer_reveal_acknowledged","targeted_check_pending"]) { state.put(key,JsonBool(false)) }
                        state.put("next_goal",JsonString("重新查看答案讲解"))
                    }
                }
                let label = if (target == "understanding") { "理解" } else if (target == "teach_back") { "讲清" } else { "练习" }
                state.put("activity_feedback",JsonString("已返回“${label}”。"))
            }
            state.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); session.put("stage",JsonString(target))
            tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage = ?,state = ?,updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",[Some(target),Some(state.toString()),Some(id)])
            recordTutorTool(tx,id,branch,if (rewind) { "rewind_tutor_stage" } else { "transition_tutor_stage" },input,output)
            if (rewind) {
                let saved = rowJson("tutor_sessions",tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ?",[Some(id)]).rows[0])
                return tutorSessionPayload(tx,accounts,saved,snapshot)
            }
            let result = JsonObject(); result.put("stage",JsonString(target)); result.put("message",JsonString(message)); result.put("next_goal",state["next_goal"])
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/tutor_policy.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*

// Business decisions remain local; a model cannot choose which learning gates apply.
func normalizedTutorQuestionType(value: String, hasOptions: Bool): String {
    let raw = stripWhitespace(value).toAsciiLower()
    let normalized = match (raw) {
        case "单选题" | "选择题" => "choice"
        case "multiple-choice" | "多选题" => "multiple_choice"
        case "填空题" => "fill_blank"
        case "计算题" => "calculation"
        case "证明题" => "proof"
        case "主观题" => "subjective"
        case _ => raw
    }
    if (hasOptions && (normalized == "other" || normalized == "综合题" || normalized.isEmpty())) {
        return "choice"
    }
    return if (normalized.isEmpty()) { "other" } else { normalized }
}

func adaptiveTeachingPolicy(questionType: String, hasOptions: Bool, hasWorking: Bool,
    correctAnswer: String): JsonObject {
    let normalized = normalizedTutorQuestionType(questionType, hasOptions)
    let reliable = !stripWhitespace(correctAnswer).isEmpty()
    let shortQuestion = normalized == "choice" || normalized == "multiple_choice" || normalized == "fill_blank"
    let compressed = reliable && (shortQuestion || !hasWorking)
    let result = JsonObject()
    result.put("mode", JsonString(if (compressed) { "compressed" } else { "full" }))
    result.put("question_type", JsonString(normalized))
    result.put("show_answer_immediately", JsonBool(reliable))
    result.put("max_understanding_checks", JsonInt(if (compressed) { 1 } else { 3 }))
    result.put("skip_teach_back", JsonBool(compressed))
    result.put("skip_far_transfer", JsonBool(compressed))
    result.put("has_working", JsonBool(hasWorking))
    return result
}

// Persisted plans contain grading keys. Expose only progress, including when
// loading older sessions with additional internal fields.
func publicTutorProgress(state: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let result = JsonObject()
    for (key in ["understood", "understanding_passed", "teach_back", "teach_back_passed",
        "near_transfer", "far_transfer", "next_goal", "strategy", "branch", "mastery_proven",
        "assisted", "understanding_index", "answer_reveal_acknowledged", "targeted_check_pending",
        "transfer_insufficient", "question_changed"]) {
        if (let Some(value) <- state.getFields().get(key)) { result.put(key, value) }
    }
    return result
}
```
