### backend-cangjie/src/tutor_read.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.convert.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func tutorFlag(state: JsonObject, key: String): Bool { return (state.get(key) ?? JsonBool(false)).toString() == "true" }
func tutorText(state: JsonObject, key: String): String {
    return if (let Some(text) <- ((state.get(key) ?? JsonNull()) as JsonString)) { text.getValue() } else { "" }
}
func tutorNumber(state: JsonObject, key: String): Int64 {
    return if (let Some(value) <- ((state.get(key) ?? JsonNull()) as JsonInt)) { Int64.parse(value.toString()) } else { 0 }
}
func tutorObject(state: JsonObject, key: String): JsonObject {
    return (state.get(key) ?? JsonObject()) as JsonObject ?? JsonObject()
}
func tutorArray(state: JsonObject, key: String): JsonArray {
    return (state.get(key) ?? JsonArray()) as JsonArray ?? JsonArray()
}

func tutorActivityForSession(db: Database, session: JsonObject, snapshot: JsonObject): JsonValue {
    let state = (session["state"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let stage = jsonText(session,"stage")
    let feedback = tutorText(state,"activity_feedback")
    if (stage == "understanding" && !tutorFlag(state,"understanding_passed")) {
        if (tutorText(tutorObject(state,"teaching_policy"),"mode") == "compressed") {
            if (!tutorFlag(state,"answer_reveal_acknowledged")) {
                return JsonValue.fromStr(##"{"id":"answer-reveal","type":"explanation","title":"先看清这次错在哪","question":"","options":[],"attempt":1,"max_attempts":1,"feedback":"","teaching":""}"##)
            }
            if (tutorFlag(state,"targeted_check_pending")) {
                let check = tutorObject(tutorObject(state,"teaching_brief"),"check")
                return publicTutorActivity(check,tutorNumber(tutorObject(state,"activity_attempts"),tutorText(check,"id"))+1,feedback: feedback)
            }
            return JsonNull()
        }
        let plan = tutorArray(state,"understanding_plan").getItems()
        if (plan.isEmpty()) { return JsonNull() }
        let rawIndex = tutorNumber(state,"understanding_index")
        let index = if (rawIndex < 0) { 0 } else if (rawIndex >= plan.size) { plan.size-1 } else { rawIndex }
        let source = (plan[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        if (tutorFlag(state,"understanding_confirmation")) {
            let confirmation = tutorActivity(jsonText(source,"id")+"-confirmation","确认一下就继续",
                "解题时，需要依据题目条件一步一步检查，对吗？",[("yes","对，需要检查条件"),("unsure","我还不确定")],["yes"],"",tutorText(source,"teaching"))
            return publicTutorActivity(confirmation,1,feedback: feedback)
        }
        return publicTutorActivity(source,tutorNumber(tutorObject(state,"activity_attempts"),jsonText(source,"id"))+1,feedback: feedback)
    }
    if (stage == "teach_back") {
        let item = tutorActivity("teach-back","用一句话讲清楚","如果再遇到这种题，你会先看什么、再怎么判断？",[],[],"","")
        item.put("type",JsonString("teach_back")); item.put("sentence_starter",JsonString("我会先看……"))
        let points = tutorKnowledge(snapshot); let keywords = JsonArray(); var cube = false
        for (text in points) { if (text.contains("正方体") || text.contains("相对面")) { cube = true } }
        for (text in tutorRowTexts(snapshot["references"])) { if (text.contains("正方体") || text.contains("相对面")) { cube = true } }
        if (cube) { for (word in ["相间","Z字两端","折叠"]) { keywords.add(JsonString(word)) } }
        else { for (index in 0..points.size) { if (index >= 3) { break }; keywords.add(JsonString(points[index])) } }
        item.put("keywords",keywords)
        return publicTutorActivity(item,tutorNumber(state,"teach_back_attempts")+1,feedback: feedback)
    }
    if (stage == "near_transfer" || stage == "far_transfer") {
        if (stage == "near_transfer" && !(tutorObject(state,"near_confirmation").getFields().isEmpty())) {
            return publicTutorActivity(tutorObject(state,"near_confirmation"),1,feedback: feedback)
        }
        let transfer = if (stage == "near_transfer") { "near" } else { "far" }
        let variant = ensureTutorVariant(db,session,snapshot,transfer); let options = tutorArray(state,transfer+"_variant_options")
        let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let policyType = tutorText(tutorObject(state,"teaching_policy"),"question_type")
        let kind = if (policyType.isEmpty()) { jsonText(question,"question_type") } else { policyType }
        let activityType = if (!options.getItems().isEmpty()) { if (kind == "multiple_choice") { "multiple_choice" } else { "single_choice" } } else if (kind == "fill_blank") { "short_answer" } else { "variant" }
        let publicOptions = JsonArray()
        for (value in options.getItems()) {
            if (let Some(option) <- (value as JsonObject)) {
                let label = tutorText(option,"label"); let text = tutorText(option,"text"); let item = JsonObject()
                item.put("value",JsonString(label)); item.put("label",JsonString(if (label.isEmpty()) { text } else if (text.isEmpty()) { label } else { label+"  "+text })); publicOptions.add(item)
            }
        }
        let item = tutorActivity(transfer+"-variant-"+jsonText(variant,"id"),if (transfer == "near") { "相似题练习" } else { "换个场景试一试" },jsonText(variant,"question_text"),[],[],"",tutorText(state,"transfer_guidance"))
        item.put("type",JsonString(activityType)); item.put("variant_id",variant["id"]); item.put("options",publicOptions)
        item.put("input_mode",JsonString(if (activityType == "multiple_choice") { "multiple_choice" } else if (activityType == "single_choice") { "choice" } else { kind }))
        if (!tutorText(state,"transfer_guidance").isEmpty()) { item.put("teaching_title",JsonString("我们一步一步写")) }
        return publicTutorActivity(item,tutorNumber(state,transfer+"_attempts")+1,feedback: feedback)
    }
    return JsonNull()
}

func tutorSessionPayload(db: Database, accounts: Accounts, session: JsonObject, snapshot: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let state = (session["state"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    let result = (JsonValue.fromStr(session.toString()) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    let stage = jsonText(session,"stage")
    result.put("state",publicTutorProgress(state)); result.put("stage_status",JsonString(if (stage == "finished") { "completed" } else if (stage == "understanding" && tutorFlag(state,"understanding_passed")) { "passed" } else { "active" }))
    result.put("activity",tutorActivityForSession(db,session,snapshot)); result.put("assisted",JsonBool(tutorFlag(state,"assisted")))
    result.put("teaching_policy",state.get("teaching_policy") ?? JsonValue.fromStr(##"{"mode":"full"}"##))
    result.put("answer_reveal",state.get("answer_reveal") ?? JsonNull()); result.put("completion_summary",JsonString(tutorText(state,"completion_summary")))
    let points = tutorKnowledge(snapshot); let title = StringBuilder()
    for (index in 0..points.size) { if (index >= 2) { break }; if (index > 0) { title.append(" · ") }; title.append(points[index]) }
    result.put("knowledge_title",JsonString(if (title.toString().isEmpty()) { jsonText(question,"title") } else { title.toString() }))
    result.put("visualization_available",JsonBool(!(tutorVisualization(jsonText(question,"subject"),jsonText(question,"question_type"),points) is JsonNull)))
    let publicQuestion = JsonObject(); publicQuestion.put("id",question["id"]); publicQuestion.put("text",question["question_text"])
    let figures = db.query("SELECT path FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND kind IN ('question_figure','crop') ORDER BY (kind = 'question_figure') DESC,created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(jsonText(question,"id"))]).rows
    publicQuestion.put("figure_url",if (figures.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+rowText(figures[0],"path"))) })
    result.put("question",publicQuestion); result.put("turns",queryArray(db,"tutor_turns","SELECT * FROM tutor_turns WHERE session_id = ? ORDER BY created_at,id",jsonText(session,"id")))
    for (value in tutorArray(result,"turns").getItems()) {
        let turn = (value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let metadata = tutorObject(turn,"metadata_json"); let path = tutorText(metadata,"handwriting_image_url")
        if (!path.isEmpty()) { metadata.put("handwriting_image_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl(path))) }
    }
    return result
}

public func readTutor(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size-1]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
            let session = rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let questionId = jsonText(session,"question_id")
            let snapshot = tutorDataSnapshot(tx,questionId,user["id"])
            let state = tutorObject(session,"state"); session.put("state",state)
            session.put("stage",JsonString(canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage"))))
            if (!state.getFields().contains("understanding_passed")) { state.put("understanding_passed",JsonBool(false)) }
            if (!state.getFields().contains("assisted")) { state.put("assisted",JsonBool(false)) }
            if (tutorText(tutorObject(state,"teaching_policy"),"mode") == "compressed" && !tutorObject(state,"teaching_brief").getFields().isEmpty()) {
                for (key in ["answer_reveal_acknowledged","targeted_check_pending"]) {
                    if (!state.getFields().contains(key)) { state.put(key,JsonBool(false)) }
                }
            } else if (tutorArray(state,"understanding_plan").getItems().isEmpty()) {
                let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                state.put("understanding_plan",buildUnderstandingPlan(jsonText(question,"question_text"),jsonText(question,"subject"),jsonText(question,"question_type"),tutorKnowledge(snapshot),tutorRowTexts(snapshot["references"])))
                state.put("understanding_index",JsonInt(0)); state.put("activity_attempts",JsonObject())
            }
            tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage = ?,state = ? WHERE id = ?",[Some(jsonText(session,"stage")),Some(state.toString()),Some(id)])
            return tutorSessionPayload(tx,accounts,session,snapshot)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/tutor_start.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func tutorStrings(value: JsonValue): Array<String> {
    let result = ArrayList<String>()
    for (item in (value as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) { result.add((item as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()) }
    return result.toArray()
}

func tutorRowTexts(value: JsonValue): Array<String> {
    let result = ArrayList<String>()
    for (item in (value as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) { result.add(jsonText((item as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"text")) }
    return result.toArray()
}

func tutorKnowledge(snapshot: JsonObject): Array<String> {
    let analysis = (snapshot["analysis"] as JsonObject) ?? JsonObject()
    let points = tutorStrings(analysis.get("knowledge_points") ?? JsonArray())
    return if (points.isEmpty()) { tutorStrings((snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()["knowledge_points"]) } else { points }
}

func tutorVisualization(subject: String, kind: String, points: Array<String>): JsonValue {
    let builder = StringBuilder(); builder.append(subject); builder.append(" "); builder.append(kind)
    for (point in points) { builder.append(" "); builder.append(point) }
    let text = builder.toString().toAsciiLower()
    for ((words,visual) in [("勾股|直角三角形","right_triangle"),("圆周角|圆心角|圆","circle"),
        ("坐标|中点|两点距离","coordinate_geometry"),("三角形|全等|相似","triangle"),
        ("二次函数|抛物线","quadratic_function"),("一次函数|直线函数","linear_function"),
        ("方程|等式|移项","equation_balance"),("匀加速|速度时间|v-t","uniform_acceleration"),
        ("抛体|平抛|斜抛","projectile_motion"),("匀速|路程|s=vt","uniform_motion"),
        ("受力|合力|支持力","force_diagram"),("机械能|动能|势能|功与能","work_energy"),
        ("压强|压力","pressure"),("浮力|排液","buoyancy"),("时间轴|年代|先后顺序","history_timeline"),
        ("时空|传播|地图","timeline_map")]) {
        for (word in words.split("|")) { if (text.contains(word)) { return JsonString(visual) } }
    }
    return JsonNull()
}

func tutorCompact(value: String): String {
    let builder = StringBuilder()
    for (rune in value.runes()) {
        let one = StringBuilder(); one.append(rune)
        if (!pythonWhitespace.contains(one.toString())) { builder.append(rune) }
    }
    return builder.toString()
}

func tutorChoiceAnswer(answer: String, options: JsonArray): String {
    let text = stripWhitespace(answer); let chars = ArrayList<String>()
    for (rune in text.toAsciiUpper().runes()) { let one = StringBuilder(); one.append(rune); chars.add(one.toString()) }
    let selected = StringBuilder()
    for (index in 0..chars.size) {
        let letter = chars[index]
        if (letter.size != 1 || !"ABCDEFGH".contains(letter)) { continue }
        if ((index > 0 && "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ".contains(chars[index-1])) ||
            (index+1 < chars.size && "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ".contains(chars[index+1]))) { continue }
        for (item in options.getItems()) {
            let option = (item as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (stripWhitespace(jsonText(option,"label")).toAsciiUpper() == letter) { selected.append(letter); break }
        }
    }
    if (!selected.toString().isEmpty()) { return selected.toString() }
    let compact = tutorCompact(text)
    for (item in options.getItems()) {
        let option = (item as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        if (!compact.isEmpty() && compact == tutorCompact(jsonText(option,"text"))) { return stripWhitespace(jsonText(option,"label")).toAsciiUpper() }
    }
    return text
}

func startTutorSnapshot(db: Database, id: String, owner: Option<String>): JsonObject {
    let snapshot = diagnosisSnapshot(db,id,owner)
    if (!(snapshot["analysis"] is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(409,"请先完成题目分析，再开始学习") }
    let answer = jsonText((snapshot["analysis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"final_answer")
    if (stripWhitespace(answer).isEmpty() || answer.startsWith("需要根据完整题目")) { throw ApiError(409,"请先完成题目分析，再开始学习") }
    return snapshot
}

func initialTutorState(snapshot: JsonObject, policy: JsonObject, brief: JsonValue): JsonObject {
    let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    let analysis = (snapshot["analysis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    let points = tutorKnowledge(snapshot)
    let state = (JsonValue.fromStr(##"{"understood":false,"understanding_passed":false,"teach_back":null,"near_transfer":null,"far_transfer":null,"next_goal":"完成一个小问题","strategy":"micro_activity","branch":"opening","mastery_proven":false,"last_message":"","repeated_count":0,"assisted":false,"understanding_index":0,"activity_attempts":{},"activity_feedback":"","answer_reveal_acknowledged":false,"targeted_check_pending":false}"##) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    state.put("teaching_policy",policy); state.put("teaching_brief",brief)
    state.put("subject",question["subject"]); state.put("question_type",question["question_type"])
    state.put("visualization_type",tutorVisualization(jsonText(question,"subject"),jsonText(question,"question_type"),tutorStrings(question["knowledge_points"])))
    state.put("understanding_plan",if (brief is JsonObject) { JsonArray() } else {
        buildUnderstandingPlan(jsonText(question,"question_text"),jsonText(question,"subject"),jsonText(question,"question_type"),points,tutorRowTexts(snapshot["references"])) })
    let reveal = JsonObject(); let rawAnswer = if (question["student_answer"] is JsonNull) { "" } else { jsonText(question,"student_answer") }
    var student = if (rawAnswer.isEmpty()) { "未作答" } else { rawAnswer }
    if (let Some(item) <- (brief as JsonObject)) {
        let kind = jsonText(policy,"question_type")
        if (kind == "choice" || kind == "multiple_choice") { student = tutorChoiceAnswer(rawAnswer,(question["options"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow()) }
        for (key in ["topic","correct_answer","key_explanation","option_explanations"]) { reveal.put(key,item[key]) }
    } else {
        reveal.put("topic",JsonString(tutorTopic(points,jsonText(question,"question_type"))))
        reveal.put("correct_answer",JsonString(stripWhitespace(jsonText(analysis,"final_answer"))))
        reveal.put("key_explanation",JsonString("")); reveal.put("option_explanations",JsonArray())
    }
    reveal.put("student_answer",JsonString(student)); state.put("answer_reveal",reveal)
    return state
}

public func startTutor(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body = readJson(context)
        let id = if (let Some(value) <- body.get("question_id")) { (editText(value,36,minimum: 1) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue() } else { "" }
        let snapshot = db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx => startTutorSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"]) })
        let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let analysis = (snapshot["analysis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let expected = diagnosisFingerprint(snapshot); let steps = tutorRowTexts(snapshot["steps"])
        let policy = adaptiveTeachingPolicy(jsonText(question,"question_type"),!(question["options"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems().isEmpty(),!steps.isEmpty(),jsonText(analysis,"final_answer"))
        var brief: JsonValue = JsonNull()
        if (jsonText(policy,"mode") == "compressed") {
            let input = JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("teaching_brief")); input.put("text",question["question_text"])
            for (key in ["student_answer","options"]) { input.put(key,question[key]) }
            input.put("question_type",policy["question_type"]); input.put("correct_answer",analysis["final_answer"])
            input.put("student_steps",tutorStringArray(steps)); input.put("knowledge_points",tutorStringArray(tutorKnowledge(snapshot)))
            input.put("diagnosis",if (snapshot["diagnosis"] is JsonObject) { snapshot["diagnosis"] } else { JsonObject() })
            brief = algorithmCompute(input)["brief"]
            if (!(brief is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(503,"教学简报不完整") }
        }
        let state = initialTutorState(snapshot,policy,brief)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let current = startTutorSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"])
            if (!jsonEquivalent(diagnosisFingerprint(current),expected)) { throw ApiError(409,"题目或诊断已变化，请重新开始学习") }
            let sessionId = newId(); let opening = if (jsonText(policy,"mode") == "compressed") { "先看清正确答案和刚才选项的关键差别。" } else { "我们先只解决一个小问题。选出你认为正确的答案就可以。" }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_sessions (id,user_id,question_id,stage,hint_level,turn_count,mastery_score,state,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,'understanding',0,0,0,?,'active',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(sessionId),user["id"],Some(id),Some(state.toString())])
            tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_turns (id,session_id,role,content,stage,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'assistant',?,'understanding',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(sessionId),Some(opening),Some(##"{"branch":"opening","next_goal":"解释首错步骤"}"##)])
            let toolOutput = JsonObject(); toolOutput.put("diagnosis_id",if (let Some(diagnosis) <- (snapshot["diagnosis"] as JsonObject)) { diagnosis["id"] } else { JsonNull() })
            let runResult = JsonObject(); runResult.put("branch",JsonString("opening")); runResult.put("diagnosis_id",toolOutput["diagnosis_id"])
            let toolInput = JsonObject(); toolInput.put("question_id",JsonString(id)); let runId = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO agent_runs (id,session_id,agent_name,status,result,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'mindtrace_tutor','completed',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(runId),Some(sessionId),Some(runResult.toString())])
            tx.query("INSERT INTO agent_tool_calls (id,agent_run_id,tool_name,input_json,output_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'diagnosis_lookup',?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(newId()),Some(runId),Some(toolInput.toString()),Some(toolOutput.toString())])
            let session = rowJson("tutor_sessions",tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ?",[Some(sessionId)]).rows[0])
            return tutorSessionPayload(tx,accounts,session,snapshot)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/tutor_state.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

// The business state belongs to the Cangjie backend. Model-generated text
// cannot bypass the evidence gate or invent a transition.
public func canonicalTutorStage(stage: String): String {
    match (stage.trimAscii()) {
        case "teach_back" | "teach_back_retry" => "teach_back"
        case "near_transfer" | "near_remediation" => "near_transfer"
        case "far_transfer" | "transfer_insufficient" => "far_transfer"
        case "finished" => "finished"
        case _ => "understanding"
    }
}

public func transitionTutorStage(current: String, event: String, understandingPassed: Bool): String {
    let stage = canonicalTutorStage(current)
    if (event == "understanding_advance") {
        if (stage != "understanding") {
            throw IllegalArgumentException("当前不在理解阶段")
        }
        if (!understandingPassed) {
            throw IllegalArgumentException("需要先通过理解检查")
        }
        return "teach_back"
    }
    match ((stage, event)) {
        case ("teach_back", "teach_back_pass") => "near_transfer"
        case ("teach_back", "teach_back_fail") => "teach_back"
        case ("near_transfer", "near_pass") => "far_transfer"
        case ("near_transfer", "near_fail") => "near_transfer"
        case ("far_transfer", "far_pass") => "finished"
        case ("far_transfer", "far_fail") => "far_transfer"
        case _ => throw IllegalArgumentException("阶段 ${stage} 不能执行事件 ${event}")
    }
}

public func previousTutorStage(current: String): String {
    let stage = canonicalTutorStage(current)
    match (stage) {
        case "teach_back" => "understanding"
        case "near_transfer" => "teach_back"
        case "far_transfer" => "near_transfer"
        case _ => throw IllegalArgumentException("阶段 ${stage} 不能返回上一步")
    }
}
```

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

// All teach-back evaluation uses the original Python function. This module only
// controls persisted progress, transitions, turns and tool evidence.
func evaluateTutorTeachBack(answer: String,question: JsonObject,points: JsonArray,references: JsonArray): JsonObject {
    let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("evaluate_teach_back")); input.put("text",JsonString(answer))
    input.put("subject",question["subject"]); input.put("question_type",question["question_type"]); input.put("teach_back_points",points); input.put("reference_solution",references)
    return algorithmCompute(input)
}

func teachBackTutor(context: HttpContext,accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=readJson(context)
        let explanation=optionalText(body,"explanation","",12000)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
            let session=rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let stage=canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage"))
            if (stage!="teach_back") { throw ApiError(409,"请先完成理解检查并进入讲清阶段") }
            let snapshot=tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"]); let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question"); let state=tutorObject(session,"state"); session.put("state",state)
            if (tutorFlag(state,"question_changed") || jsonText(question,"status")=="archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let analysis=snapshot.get("analysis") ?? JsonNull()
            let points=if (analysis is JsonObject) { tutorArray((analysis as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"knowledge_points") } else { tutorArray(question,"knowledge_points") }
            let assessment=evaluateTutorTeachBack(explanation,question,points,tutorStringArray(tutorRowTexts(snapshot["references"])))
            let passed=tutorFlag(assessment,"passed"); let count=tutorNumber(state,"teach_back_attempts")+1; let assisted = !passed && count>=2
            let branch=if (passed) { "teach_back_pass" } else { "teach_back_fail" }; var feedback=jsonText(assessment,"feedback")
            state.put("teach_back",JsonBool(passed)); state.put("teach_back_passed",JsonBool(passed)); state.put("teach_back_attempts",JsonInt(count)); state.put("branch",JsonString(branch))
            state.put("next_goal",JsonString(if (passed || assisted) { "完成一道相似题" } else { "补充一个关键理由" }))
            if (assisted) { state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)) }
            session.put("stage",JsonString(stage))
            if (passed || assisted) {
                session.put("stage",JsonString("near_transfer")); ensureTutorVariant(tx,session,snapshot,"near")
                if (!passed) { feedback="这次还少一个关键点，我先记录下来，我们继续练一道相似题。" }
            }
            tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage=?,state=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(session["stage"]),Some(state.toString()),Some(id)])
            let userMeta=JsonObject(); userMeta.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); let assistantMeta=JsonObject(); assistantMeta.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); assistantMeta.put("passed",JsonBool(passed))
            for ((role,content,turnStage,metadata) in [("user",explanation,"teach_back",userMeta),("assistant",feedback,jsonText(session,"stage"),assistantMeta)]) {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_turns (id,session_id,role,content,stage,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(role),Some(content),Some(turnStage),Some(metadata.toString())])
            }
            let input=JsonObject(); input.put("explanation",JsonString(explanation)); input.put("subject",question["subject"])
            let output=JsonObject(); output.put("passed",JsonBool(passed)); output.put("stage",session["stage"]); output.put("matched_concepts",assessment["matched_concepts"])
            recordTutorTool(tx,id,branch,"evaluate_teach_back",input,output)
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("passed",JsonBool(passed)); result.put("assisted",JsonBool(assisted)); result.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); result.put("stage",session["stage"]); result.put("feedback",JsonString(feedback)); result.put("next_goal",state["next_goal"]); return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/tutor_transfer.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*

func answerTutorTransfer(db: Database, session: JsonObject, snapshot: JsonObject, answer: JsonValue, unknown: Bool, elapsed: Int64): JsonObject {
    let state = tutorObject(session,"state"); let stage = jsonText(session,"stage"); let near = stage == "near_transfer"
    let kind = if (near) { "near" } else { "far" }; let result = JsonObject(); var passed = false; var feedback = ""
    let skipFar = tutorFlag(tutorObject(state,"teaching_policy"),"skip_far_transfer")
    if (near && (state.get("near_confirmation") ?? JsonNull()) is JsonObject) {
        state.put("near_confirmation",JsonNull()); state.put("near_transfer",JsonBool(false)); state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)); passed = true
        if (skipFar) { completeTutorLearning(db,session,snapshot,true); feedback = "本轮练习完成。" }
        else { session.put("stage",JsonString("far_transfer")); state.put("next_goal",JsonString("换个场景再试一次")); ensureTutorVariant(db,session,snapshot,"far"); feedback = "继续换个场景练习。" }
    } else {
        let rows = db.query("SELECT * FROM variant_questions WHERE id = ? AND session_id = ?",[Some(tutorText(state,"${kind}_variant_id")),Some(jsonText(session,"id"))]).rows
        if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(409,"练习已变化，请刷新后继续") }
        let variant = rowJson("variant_questions",rows[0]); var help = unknown
        for (phrase in ["不会写","不知道怎么写","没有思路","没思路","教我写","请教我","不会做"]) { if (tutorAnswerText(answer).contains(phrase)) { help = true } }
        if (help) {
            let guidance = StringBuilder(); guidance.append("可以按这个顺序写："); var count: Int64 = 0
            for (step in tutorArray(variant,"reference_solution").getItems()) { if (count >= 6) { break }; let text = stripWhitespace(tutorAnswerText(step)); if (!text.isEmpty()) { count++; guidance.append("\n${count}. ${text}") } }
            if (count == 0) { count++; guidance.append("\n1. ${if (tutorText(variant,"explanation").isEmpty()) { "先找出已知条件，再列出关系式。" } else { tutorText(variant,"explanation") }}") }
            let expected = tutorAnswerText(variant["answer"])
            if (!expected.isEmpty() && !guidance.toString().contains(expected)) { guidance.append("\n${count+1}. 得到结论：${expected}") }
            state.put("transfer_guidance",JsonString(guidance.toString())); state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true))
            result.put("assistant_content",JsonString(guidance.toString())); result.put("attempt_consumed",JsonBool(false)); result.put("unknown",JsonBool(true))
            feedback = "不会写没关系，先按提示一步步写，这次不计作答次数。"
        } else {
            state.put("transfer_guidance",JsonNull())
            let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let input = JsonObject()
            input.put("operation",JsonString("grade_answer")); input.put("text",variant["question_text"]); input.put("answer",answer); input.put("expected",variant["answer"])
            input.put("reference_solution",tutorArray(variant,"reference_solution")); input.put("subject",question["subject"]); input.put("question_type",question["question_type"])
            input.put("options",tutorArray(state,"${kind}_variant_options"))
            let grade = algorithmCompute(input); result.put("grading",grade); feedback = tutorText(grade,"feedback")
            if (!tutorFlag(grade,"can_update_learning")) { state.put("last_grading",grade); result.put("attempt_consumed",JsonBool(false)) }
            else {
                passed = tutorFlag(grade,"correct"); let count = tutorNumber(state,"${kind}_attempts")+1; state.put("${kind}_attempts",JsonInt(count)); result.put("attempt_consumed",JsonBool(true))
                db.query("INSERT INTO variant_attempts (id,variant_id,user_id,answer,correct,elapsed_seconds,hint_level,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(newId()),Some(jsonText(variant,"id")),Some(jsonText(session,"user_id")),Some(tutorAnswerText(answer)),Some(if (passed) { "1" } else { "0" }),Some(elapsed.toString()),Some(tutorNumber(session,"hint_level").toString())])
                if (passed) {
                    state.put(stage,JsonBool(true))
                    if (near && !skipFar) { session.put("stage",JsonString("far_transfer")); state.put("next_goal",JsonString("换个场景再试一次")); ensureTutorVariant(db,session,snapshot,"far"); feedback = "相似题做对了，换个场景再试一次。" }
                    else { completeTutorLearning(db,session,snapshot,false); feedback = "练习完成，本轮学习结束。" }
                } else if (count >= 2) {
                    state.put(stage,JsonBool(false)); state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true))
                    if (near && !skipFar) {
                        let confirmation = tutorActivity("near-remediation-${jsonText(variant,"id")}","看懂这一步就继续","根据刚才的讲解，这道题的参考结论是什么？",[("expected",tutorAnswerText(variant["answer"])),("unsure","我还不能确定")],["expected"],"",tutorText(variant,"explanation"))
                        state.put("near_confirmation",confirmation); feedback = "先看简短讲解，确认后继续。"
                    } else { state.put("transfer_insufficient",JsonBool(true)); completeTutorLearning(db,session,snapshot,true); feedback = "本轮学习结束，已安排近期复习。" }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    result.put("passed",JsonBool(passed)); result.put("feedback",JsonString(feedback)); return result
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/tutor_variants.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import std.collection.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func tutorDataSnapshot(db: Database, questionId: String, owner: Option<String>): JsonObject {
    let questions = db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",[Some(questionId),owner]).rows
    if (questions.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"题目不存在或无权访问") }
    let snapshot = JsonObject(); snapshot.put("question",rowJson("questions",questions[0]))
    snapshot.put("analysis",firstJson(queryArray(db,"problem_analyses","SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id = ?",questionId)))
    snapshot.put("references",queryArray(db,"reference_steps","SELECT r.* FROM reference_steps r JOIN problem_analyses a ON a.id=r.analysis_id WHERE a.question_id = ? ORDER BY r.step_index,r.id",questionId))
    return snapshot
}

func tutorAnswerText(value: JsonValue): String {
    if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) { return text.getValue() }
    if (value is JsonNull) { return "" }
    if (let Some(items) <- (value as JsonArray)) {
        let result = StringBuilder(); var first = true
        for (item in items.getItems()) { if (!first) { result.append("、") }; first = false; result.append(tutorAnswerText(item)) }
        return result.toString()
    }
    if (let Some(items) <- (value as JsonObject)) {
        let result = StringBuilder(); var first = true
        for ((key,item) in items.getFields()) { if (!first) { result.append("；") }; first = false; result.append(key+"："+tutorAnswerText(item)) }
        return result.toString()
    }
    return value.toString()
}

func tutorVariantRetrieval(db: Database, snapshot: JsonObject, transfer: String): JsonObject {
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("references",JsonArray()); result.put("status",JsonString("unavailable"))
    try {
        let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question"); let mode=if (transfer=="near") { "reinforce" } else { "variation" }
        let excluded=JsonArray(); let usedTexts=HashSet<String>()
        for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM variant_questions WHERE question_id=?",[sqlValue(question["id"])]).rows) {
            let old=rowJson("variant_questions",row); usedTexts.add(jsonText(old,"question_text"))
            for (id in tutorArray(tutorObject(old,"generation_metadata"),"retrieval_ids").getItems()) { excluded.add(id) }
        }
        let preparation=retrievalPreparation(db,question,snapshot["analysis"],"variant",mode)
        let ranked=retrievalCandidates(db,question,preparation,excluded,"variant",mode); let references=JsonArray()
        for (value in tutorArray(ranked,"references").getItems()) {
            let reference=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (!usedTexts.contains(jsonText(reference,"question_text"))) { references.add(reference) }
        }
        result.put("references",references); result.put("status",JsonString("ok"))
    } catch (_: Exception) { () }
    return result
}

func ensureTutorVariant(db: Database, session: JsonObject, snapshot: JsonObject, transfer: String): JsonObject {
    let state = (session["state"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let sessionId = jsonText(session,"id")
    let existing = db.query("SELECT * FROM variant_questions WHERE id = ? AND session_id = ?",[Some(tutorText(state,transfer+"_variant_id")),Some(sessionId)]).rows
    if (!existing.isEmpty()) {
        let item=rowJson("variant_questions",existing[0]); let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question")
        let candidate=JsonObject(); for (key in ["question_text","answer","explanation","reference_solution"]) { candidate.put(key,item[key]) }
        let validation=JsonObject(); validation.put("operation",JsonString("validate_variant")); validation.put("text",question["question_text"]); validation.put("subject",question["subject"])
        validation.put("knowledge_points",tutorStringArray(tutorKnowledge(snapshot))); validation.put("variant_candidate",candidate)
        if (tutorFlag(algorithmCompute(validation),"valid")) {
            state.put(transfer+"_validated_variant_id",item["id"])
            db.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET state=? WHERE id=?",[Some(state.toString()),Some(sessionId)])
            return item
        }
        let invalidated=tutorArray(state,"invalidated_variant_ids"); invalidated.add(item["id"]); state.put("invalidated_variant_ids",invalidated)
        state.put(transfer+"_variant_id",JsonNull()); state.put(transfer+"_validated_variant_id",JsonNull())
    }
    let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let questionId = jsonText(question,"id")
    if (jsonText(question,"status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已归档，不能生成新练习") }
    if (tutorFlag(state,"question_changed")) { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化，请重新开始学习") }
    let points = tutorKnowledge(snapshot); let context = StringBuilder(); context.append(jsonText(question,"question_text"))
    for (point in points) { context.append("；"+point) }
    let diagnoses = db.query("SELECT root_misconception FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(questionId)]).rows
    if (!diagnoses.isEmpty()) { context.append("；"+rowText(diagnoses[0],"root_misconception")) }
    let input = JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("generate_variant")); input.put("text",JsonString(context.toString()))
    input.put("subject",question["subject"]); input.put("question_type",question["question_type"]); input.put("transfer_type",JsonString(transfer))
    let retrieval=tutorVariantRetrieval(db,snapshot,transfer)
    input.put("knowledge_points",tutorStringArray(points)); input.put("variant_references",retrieval["references"])
    let computed = algorithmCompute(input); let generated = (computed["variant"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    let generation = (computed["generation"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
    generation.put("retrieval_pipeline",JsonString("bge_m3_qdrant_reranker")); generation.put("retrieval_status",retrieval["status"])
    let id = newId()
    db.query("INSERT INTO variant_questions (id,question_id,session_id,transfer_type,question_text,answer,explanation,reference_solution,verified,generation_metadata,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(id),Some(questionId),Some(sessionId),Some(transfer),sqlValue(generated["question_text"]),sqlValue(generated["answer"]),sqlValue(generated["explanation"]),Some(generated["reference_solution"].toString()),sqlValue(computed["verified"]),Some(generation.toString())])
    state.put(transfer+"_variant_id",JsonString(id)); state.put(transfer+"_validated_variant_id",if (computed["verified"].toString() == "true") { JsonString(id) } else { JsonNull() })
    state.put(transfer+"_attempts",JsonInt(0)); state.put(transfer+"_variant_generation",generation); state.put(transfer+"_variant_options",generated["options"]); state.put("activity_feedback",JsonString(""))
    db.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET state = ?,updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",[Some(state.toString()),Some(sessionId)])
    let output = JsonObject(); output.put("variant_id",JsonString(id)); output.put("verified",computed["verified"])
    for ((key,value) in generation.getFields()) { output.put(key,value) }
    let runResult = (JsonValue.fromStr(output.toString()) as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); runResult.put("branch",JsonString(transfer+"_transfer_generated"))
    let toolInput = JsonObject(); toolInput.put("transfer_type",JsonString(transfer)); toolInput.put("knowledge_points",tutorStringArray(points)); toolInput.put("retrieval_pipeline",JsonString("bge_m3_qdrant_reranker"))
    let runId = newId()
    db.query("INSERT INTO agent_runs (id,session_id,agent_name,status,result,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'mindtrace_tutor','completed',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(runId),Some(sessionId),Some(runResult.toString())])
    db.query("INSERT INTO agent_tool_calls (id,agent_run_id,tool_name,input_json,output_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'generate_variant',?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(newId()),Some(runId),Some(toolInput.toString()),Some(output.toString())])
    return rowJson("variant_questions",db.query("SELECT * FROM variant_questions WHERE id = ?",[Some(id)]).rows[0])
}

public func tutorVariant(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body = readJson(context,defaultEmpty: true)
        let transfer = (editText(body.get("transfer_type") ?? JsonString("near"),10) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        if (transfer != "near" && transfer != "far") { throw ApiError(422,"迁移类型必须是 near 或 far") }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
            let session = rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let stage = canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage"))
            if (stage != transfer+"_transfer") { throw ApiError(409,"当前教学阶段不能生成这类练习") }
            session.put("stage",JsonString(stage))
            let snapshot = tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"])
            let item = ensureTutorVariant(tx,session,snapshot,transfer); let result = JsonObject()
            for (key in ["id","question_id","session_id","transfer_type","question_text","verified","created_at","updated_at"]) { result.put(key,item[key]) }
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/variant_attempt.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func variantAttempt(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=readJson(context)
        let answer=optionalText(body,"answer","",12000)
        let elapsedValue=editNumber(body.get("elapsed_seconds") ?? JsonInt(0))
        if (elapsedValue<0.0 || elapsedValue>2147483647.0) { throw ApiError(422,"作答时长无效") }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let variants=tx.query("SELECT v.* FROM variant_questions v JOIN tutor_sessions s ON s.id=v.session_id WHERE v.id=? AND s.user_id=?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (variants.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"迁移题不存在或无权访问") }
            let variant=rowJson("variant_questions",variants[0]); let sessionId=jsonText(variant,"session_id")
            let session=rowJson("tutor_sessions",tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=?",[Some(sessionId)]).rows[0])
            let state=tutorObject(session,"state"); session.put("state",state)
            let transfer=jsonText(variant,"transfer_type"); let stage=canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage")); session.put("stage",JsonString(stage))
            if (stage!="${transfer}_transfer") { throw ApiError(409,"这道练习与当前教学阶段不匹配") }
            let current=tutorText(state,"${transfer}_variant_id")
            if (!current.isEmpty() && current!=id) { throw ApiError(409,"这道练习已经变化，请刷新") }
            let snapshot=tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"]); let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question")
            if (tutorFlag(state,"question_changed") || jsonText(question,"status")=="archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let hintValue=editNumber(body.get("hint_level") ?? session["hint_level"])
            if (hintValue<0.0 || hintValue>2147483647.0) { throw ApiError(422,"提示等级无效") }
            let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("grade_answer")); input.put("text",variant["question_text"]); input.put("answer",JsonString(answer)); input.put("expected",variant["answer"])
            input.put("reference_solution",tutorArray(variant,"reference_solution")); input.put("subject",question["subject"]); input.put("question_type",question["question_type"])
            let grading=algorithmCompute(input); let decisive=tutorFlag(grading,"can_update_learning"); let correct=tutorFlag(grading,"correct")
            var feedback=jsonText(grading,"feedback"); let branch="${transfer}_transfer_${if (!decisive) { "review_required" } else if (correct) { "pass" } else { "fail" }}"
            var attempt: JsonValue=JsonNull()
            if (decisive) {
                let attemptId=newId()
                tx.query("INSERT INTO variant_attempts (id,variant_id,user_id,answer,correct,elapsed_seconds,hint_level,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(attemptId),Some(id),user["id"],Some(answer),sqlValue(grading["correct"]),Some(Int64(elapsedValue).toString()),Some(Int64(hintValue).toString())])
                attempt=rowJson("variant_attempts",tx.query("SELECT * FROM variant_attempts WHERE id=?",[Some(attemptId)]).rows[0])
                let count=tutorNumber(state,"${transfer}_attempts")+1; state.put("${transfer}_attempts",JsonInt(count)); state.put("branch",JsonString(branch))
                if (transfer=="near" && correct) { state.put("near_transfer",JsonBool(true)); session.put("stage",JsonString("far_transfer")); ensureTutorVariant(tx,session,snapshot,"far") }
                else if (transfer=="near" && count>=2) {
                    state.put("near_transfer",JsonBool(false)); state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true))
                    let explanation=if (tutorText(variant,"explanation").isEmpty()) { "先对照题目条件，再按顺序完成关键步骤。" } else { tutorText(variant,"explanation") }
                    state.put("near_confirmation",tutorActivity("near-remediation-${id}","看懂这一步就继续","根据刚才的讲解，这道题的参考结论是什么？",[("expected",tutorAnswerText(variant["answer"])),("unsure","我还不能确定")],["expected"],"",explanation))
                    feedback="先看这道题的简短讲解，再完成一个简单确认。"
                } else if (transfer=="far" && (correct || count>=2)) {
                    state.put("far_transfer",JsonBool(correct))
                    if (!correct) { state.put("transfer_insufficient",JsonBool(true)); state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)) }
                    completeTutorLearning(tx,session,snapshot,!correct)
                    if (!correct) { feedback="这次换一种题目还不稳定，本轮先结束，并提高后续复习优先级。" }
                }
                tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage=?,state=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(session["stage"]),Some(state.toString()),Some(sessionId)])
            }
            let userMeta=JsonObject(); userMeta.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); userMeta.put("variant_id",JsonString(id))
            let assistantMeta=JsonObject(); assistantMeta.put("branch",JsonString(branch))
            if (!decisive) { userMeta.put("grading",grading); userMeta.put("attempt_consumed",JsonBool(false)); assistantMeta.put("grading_verdict",grading["verdict"]) }
            else { assistantMeta.put("correct",JsonBool(correct)) }
            for ((role,content,turnStage,metadata) in [("user",answer,"${transfer}_transfer",userMeta),("assistant",feedback,jsonText(session,"stage"),assistantMeta)]) {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_turns (id,session_id,role,content,stage,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(sessionId),Some(role),Some(content),Some(turnStage),Some(metadata.toString())])
            }
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("attempt",attempt); result.put("correct",if (decisive) { JsonBool(correct) } else { JsonNull() }); result.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); result.put("stage",session["stage"]); result.put("feedback",JsonString(feedback)); result.put("grading",grading)
            if (decisive) {
                let toolInput=JsonObject(); toolInput.put("variant_id",JsonString(id)); toolInput.put("answer",JsonString(answer))
                let toolOutput=JsonObject(); toolOutput.put("correct",JsonBool(correct)); toolOutput.put("stage",session["stage"]); recordTutorTool(tx,sessionId,branch,"grade_answer",toolInput,toolOutput)
            }
            for ((name,table) in [("mastery","mastery_states"),("misconception","misconception_states")]) {
                let rows=if (decisive) { tx.query("SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[user["id"]]).rows } else { tx.query("SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE 1=0").rows }
                result.put(name,if (rows.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { rowJson(table,rows[0]) })
            }
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

### backend-cangjie/src/visualizations.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func visualizationConfig(question: JsonObject, points: JsonArray, values: JsonObject): JsonObject {
    let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("visualization_config")); input.put("subject",question["subject"])
    input.put("question_type",question["question_type"]); input.put("teach_back_points",points); input.put("visualization_values",values)
    return algorithmCompute(input)
}

func visualizationContext(db: Database, id: String, owner: String): JsonObject {
    let sessions=db.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(id),Some(owner)]).rows
    if (sessions.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
    let session=rowJson("tutor_sessions",sessions[0]); let snapshot=tutorDataSnapshot(db,jsonText(session,"question_id"),Some(owner))
    let result=JsonObject(); result.put("session",session); result.put("snapshot",snapshot); return result
}

func sessionVisualization(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let writing=context.request.method=="POST"
    if (!writing) { requireMethod(context,"GET") }
    let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=if (writing) { readJson(context,defaultEmpty:true) } else { JsonObject() }
        let values=body.get("values") ?? JsonObject()
        if (!(values is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(422,"演示参数格式不正确") }
        let observation=optionalText(body,"observation","",12000)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let visible=visualizationContext(tx,id,rowText(user,"id")); let session=tutorObject(visible,"session"); let snapshot=tutorObject(visible,"snapshot")
            let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question")
            if (writing && (tutorFlag(tutorObject(session,"state"),"question_changed") || jsonText(question,"status")=="archived")) { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let config=visualizationConfig(question,tutorStringArray(tutorKnowledge(snapshot)),(values as JsonObject).getOrThrow())
            let specValue=config["spec"]
            if (!(specValue is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(404,"这道题暂时没有匹配的互动演示") }
            let spec=(specValue as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (!writing) {
                let result=JsonObject(); let summary=JsonObject()
                for (key in ["id","title","question_text"]) { summary.put(key,question[key]) }
                result.put("question",summary); result.put("spec",spec); return result
            }
            let visualId=newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO visualization_specs (id,session_id,type,title,spec,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(visualId),Some(id),sqlValue(spec["type"]),sqlValue(spec["title"]),Some(spec.toString())])
            if (!observation.isEmpty()) {
                let interactionId=newId()
                tx.query("INSERT INTO visualization_interactions (id,visualization_id,user_id,`values`,observation,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(interactionId),Some(visualId),user["id"],Some(values.toString()),Some(observation)])
                let input=JsonObject(); input.put("type",spec["type"]); input.put("values",values)
                let output=JsonObject(); output.put("interaction_id",JsonString(interactionId)); output.put("observation",JsonString(observation)); output.put("expected_observation",spec["expected_observation"])
                recordTutorTool(tx,id,"visualization_evidence","observe_visualization",input,output)
            }
            return rowJson("visualization_specs",tx.query("SELECT * FROM visualization_specs WHERE id=?",[Some(visualId)]).rows[0])
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

func visualizationTypes(context: HttpContext): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let question=JsonObject(); question.put("subject",JsonString("数学")); question.put("question_type",JsonString("综合题"))
    return visualizationConfig(question,JsonArray(),JsonObject())["catalog"]
}

func readVisualization(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-1]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let rows=db.query("SELECT v.* FROM visualization_specs v JOIN tutor_sessions s ON s.id=v.session_id WHERE v.id=? AND s.user_id=?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
        if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"可视化不存在或无权访问") }
        let result=rowJson("visualization_specs",rows[0]); result.put("interactions",queryArray(db,"visualization_interactions","SELECT * FROM visualization_interactions WHERE visualization_id=? ORDER BY created_at,id",id)); return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

### backend-cangjie/src/voice_api.cj

```cangjie
package zhiwei

import stdx.encoding.base64.*
import stdx.encoding.json.*
import stdx.encoding.url.*
import stdx.net.http.*

func validateVoiceContext(db: Database, owner: String, questionId: String, workId: String): Unit {
    if (!questionId.isEmpty()) { paperQuestion(db,questionId,owner) }
    if (!workId.isEmpty()) {
        let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM student_works WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(workId),Some(owner)]).rows
        if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"作答记录不存在或无权访问") }
        if (!questionId.isEmpty() && rowText(rows[0],"question_id")!=questionId) { throw ApiError(422,"作答记录与题目不匹配") }
    }
}

func insertVoice(db: Database, owner: String, questionId: String, workId: String, kind: String, path: String, text: String, metadata: JsonObject, failed!: Bool=false): JsonObject {
    let id=newId(); let duration=paperInteger(metadata.get("duration_ms") ?? JsonInt(0),0,2147483647)
    db.query("INSERT INTO voice_records (id,user_id,question_id,work_id,kind,audio_path,transcript,model_name,status,duration_ms,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(id),Some(owner),if (questionId.isEmpty()) { None } else { Some(questionId) },if (workId.isEmpty()) { None } else { Some(workId) },Some(kind),Some(path),Some(text),Some(tutorText(metadata,"model")),Some(if (failed) { "failed" } else { "completed" }),Some(duration.toString())])
    return rowJson("voice_records",db.query("SELECT * FROM voice_records WHERE id=?",[Some(id)]).rows[0])
}

func transcribeVoice(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let owner=rowText(user,"id"); let form=Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? "")
        let questionId=queryLast(form,"question_id"); let workId=queryLast(form,"work_id"); validateVoiceContext(db,owner,questionId,workId)
        let upload=readImageUpload(context,audio:true); let parts=upload.filename.split("."); var suffix=".webm"
        if (parts.size>1) { let candidate="."+parts[parts.size-1].toAsciiLower(); for (allowed in [".webm",".wav",".mp3",".m4a",".ogg",".mp4"]) { if (candidate==allowed) { suffix=candidate } } }
        let path="uploads/voice/${owner}/${newId()}${suffix}"; writeMedia(path,upload.bytes)
        let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("voice_transcribe")); input.put("audio_base64",JsonString(toBase64String(upload.bytes))); input.put("audio_suffix",JsonString(suffix))
        var computed=JsonObject()
        try { computed=algorithmCompute(input) }
        catch (_: Exception) {
            let metadata=JsonObject(); metadata.put("model",JsonString("faster-whisper-small")); insertVoice(db,owner,questionId,workId,"stt",path,"",metadata,failed:true)
            throw ApiError(502,"语音转写失败，请稍后重试或使用文字输入")
        }
        let metadata=tutorObject(computed,"metadata"); let text=jsonText(computed,"text")
        let record=db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx =>
            let saved=insertVoice(tx,owner,questionId,workId,"stt",path,text,metadata)
            if (!workId.isEmpty()) { tx.query("UPDATE student_works SET explanation=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(text),Some(workId),Some(owner)]) }
            return saved
        })
        record.put("text",JsonString(text)); record.put("bytes",JsonInt(upload.bytes.size)); record.put("editable",JsonBool(true)); record.put("metadata",metadata); record.put("audio_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+path))); return record
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

func synthesizeVoice(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let owner=rowText(user,"id"); let body=readJson(context)
        let text=stripWhitespace(optionalText(body,"text","",12000))
        if (text.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(400,"朗读文本不能为空") }
        let questionId=if ((body.get("question_id") ?? JsonNull()) is JsonNull) { "" } else { optionalText(body,"question_id","",36) }; validateVoiceContext(db,owner,questionId,"")
        let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("voice_synthesize")); input.put("text",JsonString(text)); input.put("voice",JsonString(optionalText(body,"voice","zh-CN-XiaoxiaoNeural",100)))
        let computed=algorithmCompute(input); let data=fromBase64String(jsonText(computed,"audio_base64")).getOrThrow()
        if (data.isEmpty() || data.size>64*1024*1024 || sha256(data)!=jsonText(computed,"sha256")) { throw ApiError(502,"语音合成结果无效") }
        let metadata=tutorObject(computed,"metadata"); let path="uploads/voice/${owner}/tts-${newId()}.mp3"; writeMedia(path,data)
        try {
            let record=insertVoice(db,owner,questionId,"","tts",path,text,metadata)
            record.put("text",JsonString(text)); record.put("browser_fallback",JsonBool(false)); record.put("metadata",metadata); record.put("audio_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+path))); return record
        } catch (error: Exception) { try { removeMedia(path) } catch (_: Exception) {}; throw error }
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```
